-- BEGIN MACRO DEVELOPMENT STUBS
-- LuaProt/Luraph v15. Removed by the obfuscator; source runs without network dependencies.
if not LPH_OBFUSCATED then
    LP_INIT = function(callback)
        assert(callback() == true, "LP_INIT must return true")
    end
    local attribute = function(...) end
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES = attribute
    ENCRYPT = attribute
    VM = attribute
    PRESET = attribute
    TRANSFORM = attribute
    EXTRACT = attribute
    NONE, OPAL, ONYX = attribute, attribute, attribute
    FAST, BALANCED, SECURE = attribute, attribute, attribute
    GLOBALS, CONSTANTS = attribute, attribute
    CONTROL_FLOW, REWRITE_NAMECALLS = attribute, attribute

    LPH_ENCSTR = function(value, ...)
        assert(type(value) == "string", "LPH_ENCSTR expects a string literal")
        return value
    end
    LPH_STRENC = LPH_ENCSTR
    LPH_ENCNUM = function(value, ...)
        assert(type(value) == "number", "LPH_ENCNUM expects a numeric literal")
        return value
    end
    LPH_NUMENC = LPH_ENCNUM
    LPH_ENCBUF = function(value, ...)
        assert(type(value) == "string", "LPH_ENCBUF expects a string literal")
        return buffer.fromstring(value)
    end
    LPH_BUFENC = LPH_ENCBUF
end
-- END MACRO DEVELOPMENT STUBS

LPH_ATTRIBUTES(ENCRYPT("LUAPROT", "LUAPROT"))

-- BEGIN LUCID BRANDING
-- LuaProt notification style: custom. This initializer deliberately captures no main-script locals.
LP_INIT(function()
    local env = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
    if env.LucidBranding then pcall(function() env.LucidBranding:Destroy() end) end
    local previousLoader = env.LP_CUSTOM_LOADER
    local branding = { Progress = 0, Generation = 0 }
    env.LucidBranding = branding
    -- Pixel silhouette traced from the supplied sword; each rectangle is also extruded in 3D.
    local sword = {
        {13, 0, 3, 1},
        {12, 1, 4, 1},
        {11, 2, 5, 1},
        {10, 3, 5, 1},
        {9, 4, 5, 1},
        {8, 5, 5, 1},
        {2, 6, 2, 1},
        {7, 6, 5, 1},
        {2, 7, 3, 1},
        {6, 7, 5, 1},
        {3, 8, 7, 1},
        {3, 9, 6, 1},
        {4, 10, 4, 1},
        {3, 11, 6, 1},
        {2, 12, 3, 1},
        {6, 12, 4, 1},
        {0, 13, 4, 1},
        {8, 13, 2, 1},
        {0, 14, 3, 2},
    }
    local white, pink = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)
    local function create(kind, properties, parent)
        local object = Instance.new(kind)
        for key, value in pairs(properties) do object[key] = value end
        object.Parent = parent
        return object
    end
    function branding:Close()
        self.Generation += 1
        if self.Connection then self.Connection:Disconnect(); self.Connection = nil end
        if self.Gui then self.Gui:Destroy(); self.Gui = nil end
        self.Fill, self.Status, self.Percent, self.Model = nil, nil, nil, nil
    end
    function branding:Destroy()
        self:Close()
        if env.LP_CUSTOM_LOADER == self.Callback then env.LP_CUSTOM_LOADER = previousLoader end
        if env.LucidBranding == self then env.LucidBranding = nil end
    end
    function branding:MountIcon(frame)
        -- Keep library-owned themed objects alive, but hide their original glyph.
        for _, child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("GuiObject") then child.Visible = false end
        end
        local old = frame:FindFirstChild("LucidSword")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local icon = create("Frame", {
            Name = "LucidSword", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = frame.ZIndex + 1,
        }, frame)
        for _, rect in ipairs(sword) do
            create("Frame", {
                BackgroundColor3 = white, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = icon.ZIndex + 1,
                Position = UDim2.fromScale(rect[1] / 16, rect[2] / 16),
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(rect[3] / 16, rect[4] / 16),
            }, icon)
        end
        return icon
    end
    function branding:Ensure()
        if self.Gui then return end
        self.Generation += 1
        self.Progress, self.Angle = 0, 0
        self.StartedAt = os.clock()
        local gui = create("ScreenGui", {
            Name = "LucidLoader", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
            DisplayOrder = 10000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
        })
        self.Gui = gui -- Assign immediately so partial construction can always be cleaned up.
        local parent
        if type(gethui) == "function" then
            local ok, result = pcall(gethui)
            if ok then parent = result end
        end
        if parent then gui.Parent = parent else
            local ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
            if not ok then gui.Parent = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5) end
        end
        assert(gui.Parent, "Lucid loader has no GUI parent")
        local panel = create("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(350, 340), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 12, 17),
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, gui)
        create("UIScale", { Scale = math.clamp((workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X or 800) / 390, 0.4, 1) }, panel)
        create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2) }, panel)
        create("UIStroke", { Color = pink, Transparency = 0.65, Thickness = 1 }, panel)
        local viewport = create("ViewportFrame", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(65, 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 192),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(170, 150, 175),
            LightColor = white, LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1, -1, -2),
        }, panel)
        local camera = create("Camera", {
            FieldOfView = 35, CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 9),
        }, viewport)
        viewport.CurrentCamera = camera
        local world = create("WorldModel", {}, viewport)
        local model = create("Model", { Name = "LucidSword" }, world)
        self.Model = model
        for _, rect in ipairs(sword) do
            create("Part", {
                Name = "SwordPixel", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
                CanTouch = false, CanQuery = false, CastShadow = false,
                Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, Color = white,
                Size = Vector3.new(rect[3] / 4, rect[4] / 4, 0.22),
                CFrame = CFrame.new((rect[1] + rect[3] / 2 - 8) / 4, (8 - rect[2] - rect[4] / 2) / 4, 0),
            }, model)
        end
        -- Explicit origin keeps the asymmetric sword spinning around the artwork's center.
        model.WorldPivot = CFrame.new()
        create("TextLabel", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 210), Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 35),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "LUCID", TextColor3 = white,
            Font = Enum.Font.Arcade, TextSize = 27,
        }, panel)
        self.Status = create("TextLabel", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 251), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 22),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Loading Lucid", TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(189, 182, 197),
            Font = Enum.Font.Arcade, TextSize = 12, TextScaled = true,
        }, panel)
        create("UITextSizeConstraint", { MinTextSize = 8, MaxTextSize = 12 }, self.Status)
        local track = create("Frame", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 288), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 5),
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(42, 36, 47), BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ClipsDescendants = true,
        }, panel)
        self.Fill = create("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = pink, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        }, track)
        self.Percent = create("TextLabel", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 303), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 15),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "STARTING", TextColor3 = pink,
            Font = Enum.Font.Arcade, TextSize = 10,
        }, panel)
        self.Connection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            self.Angle = (self.Angle + math.min(dt, 0.1) * 1.4) % (math.pi * 2)
            model:PivotTo(CFrame.Angles(0, self.Angle, math.sin(self.Angle) * 0.07))
        end)
        local generation = self.Generation
        task.delay(45, function()
            if self.Gui == gui and self.Generation == generation then self:Fail("Loading timed out") end
        end)
    end
    function branding:Update(progress, message, stage)
        local ok, reason = pcall(function()
            self:Ensure()
            -- LuaProt callbacks may repeat. Never move the bar backwards.
            self.Progress = math.max(self.Progress, math.clamp(progress, 0, 1))
            self.Fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(self.Progress, 1)
            self.Status.Text = message
            self.Percent.Text = stage or "LOADING"
        end)
        if not ok then self:Close(); warn("[Lucid] Loader: " .. tostring(reason)) end
    end
    function branding:Start()
        self:Update(0.8, "Preparing interface and features", "STARTUP")
    end
    function branding:Complete()
        self:Update(1, "Lucid is ready", "READY")
        local gui, generation = self.Gui, self.Generation
        -- Count time already spent authenticating/loading toward the 2.5-second presentation.
        local remaining = math.max(0.25, 2.5 - (os.clock() - self.StartedAt))
        task.delay(remaining, function()
            if self.Gui == gui and self.Generation == generation then self:Close() end
        end)
    end
    function branding:WaitForMenu(isActive)
        self:Complete()
        local gui = self.Gui
        while gui and self.Gui == gui do
            if env.LucidBranding ~= self or not isActive() then
                self:Close()
                return false
            end
            task.wait()
        end
        return env.LucidBranding == self and isActive()
    end
    function branding:Fail(message)
        if not self.Gui then return end
        self.Status.Text, self.Percent.Text = message, "FAILED"
        if self.Connection then self.Connection:Disconnect(); self.Connection = nil end
        local gui, generation = self.Gui, self.Generation
        task.delay(3, function()
            if self.Gui == gui and self.Generation == generation then self:Close() end
        end)
    end
    branding.Callback = function(stepOrTime)
        if stepOrTime == 1 then
            branding:Update(0.15, "Attempting to authenticate", "AUTHENTICATION")
        elseif stepOrTime == 2 then
            branding:Update(0.4, "Connecting to LuaProt servers", "CONNECTING")
        else
            branding:Update(0.65, "Authenticated · preparing Lucid", "AUTHENTICATED")
        end
    end
    env.LP_CUSTOM_LOADER = branding.Callback
    return true
end)
-- END LUCID BRANDING

local Environment = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
local Branding = Environment.LucidBranding
local missing = {}
for name, api in pairs({
    getgenv = getgenv or false, loadstring = loadstring or false, getgc = getgc or false,
    hookfunction = hookfunction or false, hookmetamethod = hookmetamethod or false,
    getrawmetatable = getrawmetatable or false, newcclosure = newcclosure or false,
    checkcaller = checkcaller or false, getcallingscript = getcallingscript or false,
    getnamecallmethod = getnamecallmethod or false, getrenv = getrenv or false,
    getupvalue = getupvalue or debug.getupvalue or false,
    restorefunction = restorefunction or false,
    isfunctionhooked = isfunctionhooked or false,
    sethiddenproperty = sethiddenproperty or false,
    getupvalues = getupvalues or debug.getupvalues or false,
    getinfo = getinfo or debug.getinfo or false,
    getthreadidentity = getthreadidentity or getidentity or false,
    setthreadidentity = setthreadidentity or setidentity or false,
    Drawing = Drawing and Drawing.new or false,
    isfolder = isfolder or false, makefolder = makefolder or false,
    isfile = isfile or false, readfile = readfile or false,
    writefile = writefile or false, listfiles = listfiles or false,
}) do
    if not api then table.insert(missing, name) end
end
if #missing > 0 then
    if Branding then Branding:Fail("Required executor APIs are unavailable") end
    warn("[Lucid] This environment is missing required APIs: " .. table.concat(missing, ", "))
    return
end
if Environment.LokiRuntime then Environment.LokiRuntime:Unload() end
if Branding then Branding:Start() end
-- BEGIN STARTUP RAWGET HOOK
-- Install before any services, downloaded UI or feature initialization.
local startupOK, StartupRawgetCleanup = pcall(function()
    local target = getrenv().rawget
    assert(type(target) == "function", "getrenv().rawget is unavailable")
    if isfunctionhooked(target) then restorefunction(target) end
    local traceback = debug.traceback
    local original
    original = hookfunction(target, function(tbl, key, ...)
        local value = original(tbl, key, ...)
        -- Ordinary reads avoid the cost of building a traceback.
        local kind = type(value)
        if not ((key == "v" and kind == "string")
            or (key == "vx" and kind == "number")
            or ((key == "sp" or key == "cst") and kind == "boolean")) then
            return value
        end
        if traceback():find("ServiceManager", 1, true) then
            if key == "v" then return "" end
            if key == "vx" then return 0 end
            return false
        end
        return value
    end)
    return function() restorefunction(target) end
end)
if not startupOK then
    if Branding then Branding:Fail("Startup failed; check Console") end
    warn("[Lucid] Startup rawget hook: " .. tostring(StartupRawgetCleanup))
    return
end
-- END STARTUP RAWGET HOOK

local getthreadidentity = getthreadidentity or getidentity
local setthreadidentity = setthreadidentity or setidentity
local setidentity = setidentity or setthreadidentity
local cloneref = cloneref or function(value) return value end
local PreviousExports = {
    Target = Environment.get_closest_target,
    Barrel = Environment.get_gun_barrel_position,
    Restore = Environment.RestoreBulletSims,
}
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local Runtime = {
    Active = true, Connections = {}, Drawings = {}, Instances = {}, Threads = {},
    LastSweep = 0,
    Hooks = { StartupRawgetCleanup }, Cleanups = {}, Errors = {}, FailedFeatures = {},
}
Environment.LokiRuntime = Runtime

-- BEGIN SHARED SCHEDULER (max 6 connections)
local Scheduler = { ESP = {}, Combat = {}, Weapon = {}, Exploits = {}, Misc = {}, Dedicated = {} }
function Scheduler:Register(bucket, fn)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
    table.insert(self[bucket], fn)
end
local function runBucket(bucket)
    return function(dt)
        for _, fn in ipairs(bucket) do
            local ok, err = pcall(fn, dt)
            if not ok then warn("[Scheduler]", err) end
        end
    end
end
-- Use real signals directly
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(runBucket(Scheduler.ESP))
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(runBucket(Scheduler.Combat))
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(runBucket(Scheduler.Weapon))
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(runBucket(Scheduler.Exploits))
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(runBucket(Scheduler.Misc))
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(runBucket(Scheduler.Dedicated))
-- END SHARED SCHEDULER
if Branding then table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() Branding:Destroy() end) end
function Runtime:Report(label, message)
    local text = label .. ": " .. tostring(message)
    if self.Errors[text] then return end
    self.Errors[text] = true
    warn("[Lucid] " .. text)
    if self.Console then pcall(function() self.Console:Log(text, "Output") end) end
end
function Runtime:Sweep(limit)
    local now = os.clock()
    local state = self.SweepState
    if not state then
        if now - self.LastSweep < 10 then return end
        state = { Phase = 1, Index = #self.Connections }
        self.SweepState = state
        self.LastSweep = now
    end
    local lists = { self.Connections, self.Threads, self.Instances }
    -- Limit cleanup inspections and CPU time per heartbeat, including after large scans.
    local deadline = now + 0.001
    for _ = 1, limit or 64 do
        if os.clock() >= deadline then break end
        local items = lists[state.Phase]
        if not items then self.SweepState = nil; break end
        if state.Index <= 0 then
            state.Phase += 1
            state.Index = lists[state.Phase] and #lists[state.Phase] or 0
        else
            local index = state.Index
            local object = items[index]
            local keep = true
            if state.Phase == 1 then keep = object.Connected
            elseif state.Phase == 2 then keep = coroutine.status(object) ~= "dead"
            elseif object.Parent == nil then keep = pcall(function() object.Parent = nil end) end
            if not keep then
                items[index] = items[#items]
                items[#items] = nil
            end
            state.Index -= 1
        end
    end
end
-- Character readers share discovery and keep the live table even when it is empty.
function Runtime:GetEntities()
    local now = os.clock()
    if self.EntityGetter then
        if now >= (self.NextEntityRefresh or 0) then
            self.NextEntityRefresh = now + 1
            local ok, upvalues = pcall(getupvalues, self.EntityGetter)
            if ok and type(upvalues) == "table" and type(upvalues[1]) == "table" then self.Entities = upvalues[1] end
        end
        return self.Entities
    end
    if (self.EntityAttempts or 0) >= 3 or now < (self.NextEntitySearch or 0) then return nil end
    self.EntityAttempts = (self.EntityAttempts or 0) + 1
    self.NextEntitySearch = now + 10
    for _, object in ipairs(getgc(true)) do
        if type(object) ~= "table" then continue end
        local getter = rawget(object, "GetPlayerFromWorldCharacter")
        if type(getter) ~= "function" then continue end
        local ok, upvalues = pcall(getupvalues, getter)
        if not ok or type(upvalues) ~= "table" then continue end
        local api = upvalues[2]
        local characters = type(api) == "table" and api.GetCharacters
        if type(characters) == "function" then
            local found, values = pcall(getupvalues, characters)
            if found and type(values) == "table" and type(values[1]) == "table" then
                self.EntityGetter, self.Entities = characters, values[1]
            end
        end
        -- CustomMeshCharacter also stores the registry directly in this getter.
        if not self.EntityGetter and type(upvalues[1]) == "table" then
            local registry, valid = upvalues[1], true
            for _, record in pairs(registry) do
                if type(record) ~= "table" or not (record.WorldModel or record.Character) then valid = false; break end
            end
            if valid then self.EntityGetter, self.Entities = getter, registry end
        end
        if self.EntityGetter then
            self.NextEntityRefresh = now + 1
            return self.Entities
        end
    end
    if self.EntityAttempts == 3 then
        self:Report("Character data", "Character registry unavailable; reload after the game finishes loading")
    end
end
-- Chroma targets the WorldModel head; SkinWalker and the expander modify this same rig.
function Runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, name)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    model = model or character
    for _, rig in ipairs({model, character}) do
        if rig then
            local manager = self.HitboxExpander
            local cached = manager and manager:GetBodyParts(rig)
            local parts = cached and {cached[name]} or rig:GetChildren()
            for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                if part.Parent == rig and part.Name == name and part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:GetAttribute("FakeHead")
                    and not part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy") then return part end
            end
        end
    end
end
function Runtime:GetDisplayPart(part) return part end
-- Visibility is a display status; it must not change the selected aim point.
function Runtime:GetAimPosition(part)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    return part.Position
end
local function CreateVisibilityChecker(runtime, world, localPlayer, clockNow)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local cache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    runtime.VisibilityCache = cache
    local localModels, characterModels, nextLocalRefresh = {}, {}, 0
    local function cast(origin, point)
        if runtime.BulletRayTest then return runtime.BulletRayTest(origin, point, params) end
        return world:Raycast(origin, point - origin, params)
    end
    return function(target, entity, origin, exact)
        if not target or not target.Parent then return false end
        entity = entity or {}
        local now = clockNow()
        local saved = cache[target]
        if not exact and saved and now < saved.Expires and saved.Character == entity.Character and saved.Model == entity.WorldModel
            and (origin - saved.Origin).Magnitude < 0.5 and (target.Position - saved.Position).Magnitude < 0.2 then
            return saved.Visible, saved.Point
        end
        if now >= nextLocalRefresh then
            nextLocalRefresh = now + 0.25
            table.clear(localModels)
            table.clear(characterModels)
            for _, data in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
                if data.Character then characterModels[data.Character] = true end
                if data.WorldModel then characterModels[data.WorldModel] = true end
                if data.Player == localPlayer then
                    if data.Character then table.insert(localModels, data.Character) end
                    if data.WorldModel then table.insert(localModels, data.WorldModel) end
                end
            end
        end
        local ignore = table.clone(localModels)
        if world.CurrentCamera then table.insert(ignore, world.CurrentCamera) end
        if localPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignore, localPlayer.Character) end
        -- Rendered mesh characters can differ from the game's collision characters.
        if entity.WorldModel and entity.Character and entity.WorldModel ~= entity.Character then
            table.insert(ignore, entity.WorldModel)
        end
        local expander = runtime.HitboxExpander
        if expander and expander.Enabled then
            for _, entry in pairs(expander.Entries) do
                if entry.Visual and entry.Visual.Parent then table.insert(ignore, entry.Visual) end
                for _, probe in ipairs(entry.Parts) do if probe.Parent then table.insert(ignore, probe) end end
            end
        end
        local native = runtime.BulletRayParams
        if typeof(native) == "RaycastParams" then params.CollisionGroup = native.CollisionGroup end
        local model = entity.WorldModel or target.Parent
        local character = entity.Character or target.Parent
        local points = { target.Position }
        if not exact and target:IsA("BasePart") then
            local frame, size = target.CFrame, target.Size
            for _, offset in ipairs({ Vector3.new(size.X * 0.35, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-size.X * 0.35, 0, 0),
                Vector3.new(0, size.Y * 0.35, 0), Vector3.new(0, -size.Y * 0.35, 0) }) do
                table.insert(points, frame:PointToWorldSpace(offset))
            end
        end
        local function characterPart(instance)
            local parent = instance.Parent
            for _ = 1, 8 do
                if not parent then return false end
                if characterModels[parent] or parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
                parent = parent.Parent
            end
            return false
        end
        local visible, chosen = false, target.Position
        for _, point in ipairs(points) do
            local probeIgnore = table.clone(ignore)
            for _ = 1, 4 do
                params.FilterDescendantsInstances = probeIgnore
                local hit = (point - origin).Magnitude > 0.001 and cast(origin, point) or nil
                local instance = hit and hit.Instance
                if not instance or instance == target or instance:IsDescendantOf(character) or instance:IsDescendantOf(model) then
                    visible, chosen = true, point
                    break
                end
                -- Non-colliding invisible decoration is not a wall. Solid invisible walls still block.
                if instance:IsA("BasePart") and instance.Transparency >= 0.95 and not instance.CanCollide
                    and not characterPart(instance) then
                    table.insert(probeIgnore, instance)
                else break end
            end
            if visible then break end
        end
        if not exact then
            cache[target] = { Visible = visible, Point = chosen, Origin = origin, Position = target.Position,
                Character = entity.Character, Model = entity.WorldModel, Expires = now + 0.1 }
        end
        return visible, chosen
    end
end
-- Shared viewport coordinates keep targeting aligned with the visible FOV circle.
local function GetFovCenter(camera)
    if not camera then return end
    if Runtime.FovCenterProvider then
        local ok, point = pcall(Runtime.FovCenterProvider, camera)
        if ok and typeof(point) == "Vector2" and point.X == point.X and point.Y == point.Y
            and math.abs(point.X) < math.huge and math.abs(point.Y) < math.huge then return point end
    end
    return camera.ViewportSize * 0.5
end
local function ProjectFovPoint(camera, position, radius)
    if not camera or not position then return end
    local point, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(position)
    if not onScreen or point.Z <= 0 then return end
    local screen = Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y)
    local distance = (screen - GetFovCenter(camera)).Magnitude
    if distance <= math.max(0, radius or 120) then return screen, distance end
end
local function TargetPriority(camera, localPlayer, position, screenDistance, closestPlayers)
    if not closestPlayers then return screenDistance end
    local character = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
    local root = character and (character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or character:FindFirstChild("Part") or character.PrimaryPart)
    return (position - (root and root.Position or camera.CFrame.Position)).Magnitude
end
-- Clip the complete stroke, including its origin, to the circle's interior.
local function ClipLineToFov(start, finish, center, radius)
    if radius <= 0 then return end
    local delta, offset = finish - start, start - center
    local a = delta:Dot(delta)
    if a < 0.000001 then return end
    local b, c = 2 * offset:Dot(delta), offset:Dot(offset) - radius * radius
    local discriminant = b * b - 4 * a * c
    if discriminant < 0 then return end
    local root = math.sqrt(discriminant)
    local enter, leave = math.max(0, (-b - root) / (2 * a)), math.min(1, (-b + root) / (2 * a))
    if enter >= leave then return end
    return start + delta * enter, start + delta * leave
end
-- Follow the player actually displayed in the inventory panel until its next refresh.
local function GetInventorySelection(runtime)
    local model = runtime.InventoryTargetModel
    if runtime.InventoryViewerEnabled and model and model.Parent then
        return runtime.InventoryTargetName, model
    end
end
-- Visibility status must follow the local body, including while the camera is detached.
local function GetLocalVisibilityOrigin(runtime, localPlayer)
    local function eye(character, root)
        if not character or not character.Parent then return end
        local head = runtime:GetAimPart(character, character, "Head")
        if head and head.Parent then return runtime:GetAimPosition(head) end
        root = root or character:FindFirstChild("Part") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or character.PrimaryPart
        if root and root.Parent then
            local data = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("CharData")
            local state = data and data:FindFirstChild("PlayerState")
            local offsets = {Normal=4.75, Crouch=3.5, Prone=1.75, Sit=2}
            return root.Position + Vector3.new(0, offsets[state and state.Value] or 4.75, 0)
        end
    end
    for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
        if entity.Player == localPlayer then
            local origin = eye(entity.Character, entity.RootPart) or eye(entity.WorldModel, entity.RootPart)
            if origin then return origin end
        end
    end
    return eye(localPlayer.Character)
end
-- Imported detector: group 3441839, moderator rank 99 or higher.
local function CreateModeratorDetector(runtime, players, notify)
    local manager = { Enabled = false, Duration = 5, Epoch = 0, Seen = {} }
    local added, removed
    local function disconnect()
        if added then added:Disconnect(); added = nil end
        if removed then removed:Disconnect(); removed = nil end
        table.clear(manager.Seen)
    end
    local function inspect(player)
        if player == players.LocalPlayer or manager.Seen[player] then return end
        local ticket, epoch = {}, manager.Epoch
        manager.Seen[player] = ticket
        local function current()
            return manager.Enabled and epoch == manager.Epoch and manager.Seen[player] == ticket
                and player.Parent == players
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local rank
            for attempt = 1, 3 do
                if not current() then return end
                local ok, value = pcall(player.GetRankInGroup, player, 3441839)
                if not current() then return end
                if ok and type(value) == "number" then rank = value; break end
                if attempt < 3 then task.wait(0.5 * attempt) end
            end
            if not rank then manager.Seen[player] = nil; return end
            if rank >= 99 then notify(player.Name .. " Is a moderator!", manager.Duration) end
        end)
    end
    function manager:SetEnabled(value)
        self.Epoch += 1
        self.Enabled = value
        disconnect()
        if not value then return end
        added = runtime:Connect(players.PlayerAdded, inspect)
        removed = runtime:Connect(players.PlayerRemoving, function(player) self.Seen[player] = nil end)
        for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do inspect(player) end
    end
    function manager:Destroy() self:SetEnabled(false) end
    return manager
end
-- BEGIN VISIBLE BODY TARGETS
-- Prefer the configured part, then head/torso and exposed limbs on the nearest rig.
local function SelectVisibleBodyTarget(runtime, entities, localPlayer, camera, fov, preferred, origin, isTeammate, closestPlayers, inventoryName, resolveShifted, includeExpanded)
    if not camera or typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" or not runtime.IsTargetVisible then return end
    local names = { preferred or "Head", "Head", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso",
        "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "RightLowerArm", "LeftHand", "RightHand",
        "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
        "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }
    local candidates = {}
    for _, entity in pairs(entities or {}) do
        local player = entity.Player
        if not player or player == localPlayer or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
            or entity.IsDead or entity.Dead or (isTeammate and isTeammate(player)) then continue end
        if inventoryName and player.Name ~= inventoryName then continue end
        local model = entity.WorldModel or entity.Character
        local character = entity.Character or model
        if not model or not model.Parent or not character then continue end
        -- Build once per rig; never include expander probes or SkinWalker proxy parts.
        local byName = {}
        for _, rig in ipairs(model == character and {model} or {model, character}) do
            local manager = runtime.HitboxExpander
            local cached = manager and manager:GetBodyParts(rig)
            for _, part in pairs(cached or rig:GetChildren()) do
                if not byName[part.Name] and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Parent == rig
                    and not part:GetAttribute("FakeHead") and not part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy")
                    and not part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider") and not part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual") then
                    byName[part.Name] = part
                end
            end
        end
        local parts, seen, distance = {}, {}, math.huge
        for _, name in ipairs(names) do
            local part = byName[name]
            if not part or seen[part] then continue end
            seen[part] = true
            local position = runtime:GetDisplayPart(part).Position
            local screen, radius = ProjectFovPoint(camera, position, fov)
            if screen then
                table.insert(parts, part)
                distance = math.min(distance, TargetPriority(camera, localPlayer, position, radius, closestPlayers))
            end
        end
        local expander = includeExpanded and runtime.HitboxExpander
        local entry = expander and expander.Enabled and expander.Entries and expander.Entries[model]
        if entry and not entry.Removed then
            for _, probe in ipairs(entry.Parts) do
                if probe.Parent ~= model or not probe.CanQuery then continue end
                local position = probe.Position
                local screen, radius = ProjectFovPoint(camera, position, fov)
                if screen then
                    table.insert(parts, probe)
                    distance = math.min(distance, TargetPriority(camera, localPlayer, position, radius, closestPlayers))
                end
            end
        end
        if #parts > 0 then
            table.insert(candidates, { Parts = parts, Distance = distance, Entity = entity,
                Root = entity.RootPart or character:FindFirstChild("Part") or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") })
        end
    end
    table.sort(candidates, function(a, b) return a.Distance < b.Distance end)
    for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
        for _, part in ipairs(candidate.Parts) do
            -- Shared short-lived visibility cache avoids repeating rays for every shot/frame.
            local visible, point = runtime.IsTargetVisible(part, candidate.Entity, origin, part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider") == true)
            point = point or runtime:GetAimPosition(part)
            if visible and ProjectFovPoint(camera, point, fov) then
                return part, candidate.Entity.Player, candidate.Root, candidate.Entity, point
            end
        end
        -- A blocked rig can still have a part visible from a valid manipulated origin.
        -- Try direct body parts first, then preserve the successful shift for the shot.
        if resolveShifted then
            for _, part in ipairs(candidate.Parts) do
                local visible, point, shifted, offset = resolveShifted(part, candidate.Entity, origin)
                point = point or runtime:GetAimPosition(part)
                if visible and shifted and ProjectFovPoint(camera, point, fov) then
                    return part, candidate.Entity.Player, candidate.Root, candidate.Entity, point, shifted, offset
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
-- END VISIBLE BODY TARGETS
Runtime.IsTargetVisible = CreateVisibilityChecker(Runtime, workspace, game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer, os.clock)
function Runtime:Call(label, callback, ...)
    if not self.Active then return end
    local result = table.pack(pcall(callback, ...))
    if not result[1] then self:Report(label, result[2]) end
    return table.unpack(result, 1, result.n)
end
function Runtime:Init(name, callback)
    local success = self:Call(name, callback)
    if not success then self.FailedFeatures[name] = true end
end
-- Events do not consume return values; avoid allocating a results table on every frame.
function Runtime:Handle(label, callback, ...)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    if not self.Active then return end
    local success, message = pcall(callback, ...)
    if not success then self:Report(label, message) end
end
function Runtime:Connect(signal, callback)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local connection = signal:Connect(function(...)
        self:Handle("Feature event", callback, ...)
    end)
    table.insert(self.Connections, connection)
    return connection
end
function Runtime:Once(signal, callback)
    local connection = signal:Once(function(...)
        self:Handle("Feature event", callback, ...)
    end)
    table.insert(self.Connections, connection)
    return connection
end
local sweepElapsed = 0
Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
    sweepElapsed += dt
    if sweepElapsed >= 10 or Runtime.SweepState then
        sweepElapsed = 0
        Runtime:Sweep()
    end
end)
function Runtime:Unload()
    if not self.Active or self.Unloading then return end
    self.Unloading = true
    -- Let each toggle restore the state it owns before disconnecting its events.
    if self.Toggles then
        for _, toggle in pairs(self.Toggles) do
            if toggle.Value then toggle:SetValue(false) end
        end
    end
    self.Active = false
    self.SweepState = nil
    for _, connection in ipairs(self.Connections) do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
    for _, thread in ipairs(self.Threads) do
        if thread ~= coroutine.running() then pcall(task.cancel, thread) end
    end
    for index = #self.Hooks, 1, -1 do pcall(self.Hooks[index]) end
    for _, cleanup in ipairs(self.Cleanups) do pcall(cleanup) end
    for _, object in ipairs(self.Drawings) do pcall(function() object:Remove() end) end
    for _, object in ipairs(self.Instances) do pcall(function() object:Destroy() end) end
    if self.UIExit then pcall(self.UIExit) end
    if Environment.LokiRuntime == self then Environment.LokiRuntime = nil end
end
local executorHook = hookfunction
local hookfunction = function(target, replacement)
    local original = executorHook(target, replacement)
    table.insert(Runtime.Hooks, function() executorHook(target, original) end)
    return original
end
local executorMetaHook = hookmetamethod
local hookmetamethod = function(target, method, replacement)
    local original = executorMetaHook(target, method, replacement)
    table.insert(Runtime.Hooks, function() executorMetaHook(target, method, original) end)
    return original
end
local NativeDrawing = Drawing
local Drawing = { new = function(kind)
    local object = NativeDrawing.new(kind)
    table.insert(Runtime.Drawings, object)
    return object
end }
local NativeInstance = Instance
local Instance = { new = function(...)
    local object = NativeInstance.new(...)
    table.insert(Runtime.Instances, object)
    return object
end }
local NativeTask = task
local task = setmetatable({}, { __index = NativeTask })
for _, method in ipairs({ "spawn", "defer", "delay" }) do
    task[method] = function(first, ...)
        local args = table.pack(...)
        local callback, delay
        if method == "delay" then
            delay, callback = first, args[1]
            table.remove(args, 1)
            args.n -= 1
        else callback = first end
        local guarded = function()
            Runtime:Call("Feature task", callback, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
        end
        local thread
        if method == "delay" then thread = NativeTask.delay(delay, guarded)
        else thread = NativeTask[method](guarded) end
        table.insert(Runtime.Threads, thread)
        return thread
    end
end

local LIBRARY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cueshut/vitremake/2e16adeaaa1aa417a782fee84c621e9e4ba4b830/vitality.lua"
-- BEGIN UI LOADER
local function LoadVitality(fetch, compile, environment)
    local previous = environment.Library
    local function valid(ui)
        if type(ui) ~= "table" or type(ui.Animation) ~= "table" then return false end
        for _, method in ipairs({ "Window", "Exit", "Tween", "CreateColorpicker", "Create",
            "Connect", "Button", "Notification", "Watermark", "Console", "KeybindList" }) do
            if type(ui[method]) ~= "function" then return false end
        end
        return true
    end
    local ok, body = pcall(fetch)
    if not ok then return nil, "UI source could not be read: " .. tostring(body) end
    if type(body) ~= "string" or not body:find("%S") then
        return nil, "UI source is empty; check HTTP access or use the supplied Vitality cache file"
    end
    -- Vitality assigns Library globally. Keep its internal closures bound to this load.
    local compiled, chunk, reason = pcall(compile, "local Library\n" .. body, "LokiVitality")
    if not compiled then return nil, "Compilation failed: " .. tostring(chunk) end
    if type(chunk) ~= "function" then
        return nil, "Compilation failed: " .. tostring(reason or "executor returned no compiled function")
    end
    local executed, result = pcall(chunk)
    local exported = environment.Library
    if executed and valid(result) then return result end
    if executed and result == nil and exported ~= previous and valid(exported) then
        return exported
    end
    -- Release a fresh partial export on failure, never an unrelated existing library.
    if exported ~= previous and type(exported) == "table" and type(exported.Exit) == "function" then
        pcall(exported.Exit, exported)
    end
    if not executed then return nil, "Initialization failed: " .. tostring(result) end
    return nil, "UI did not provide the required Vitality API (returned " .. type(result) .. ")"
end
-- END UI LOADER
-- BEGIN UI SOURCE
local function FetchVitality(urls, httpGet, requestAPI, files)
    local function usable(body)
        return type(body) == "string" and body:find("Library.Window =", 1, true)
            and body:find("return Library", 1, true)
    end
    local cached, body = pcall(files.Read)
    if cached and usable(body) then return body end
    local failures = {}
    for _, url in ipairs(urls) do
        if type(requestAPI) == "function" then
            local ok, response = pcall(requestAPI, { Url = url, Method = "GET" })
            if ok and type(response) == "table" and (response.StatusCode == 200 or response.StatusCode == nil)
                and response.Success ~= false and usable(response.Body) then return response.Body end
            table.insert(failures, "request: " .. (ok and "empty/invalid response" or tostring(response)))
        end
        local ok, response = pcall(httpGet, url)
        if ok and usable(response) then return response end
        table.insert(failures, "HttpGet: " .. (ok and "empty/invalid response" or tostring(response)))
    end
    error("Vitality could not be downloaded from either pinned host. Place vitality.lua in Loki/" ..
        files.Name .. " to load it locally. " .. table.concat(failures, "; "), 0)
end
-- END UI SOURCE
local cacheName = "vitality-2e16adeaaa1aa417a782fee84c621e9e4ba4b830.lua"
local cachePath = "Loki/" .. cacheName
local uiSource
local Vitality, uiError = LoadVitality(function()
    uiSource = FetchVitality({ LIBRARY_URL,
        "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/cueshut/vitremake@2e16adeaaa1aa417a782fee84c621e9e4ba4b830/vitality.lua",
    }, function(url) return game:HttpGet(url, true) end,
        request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request), {
            Name = cacheName,
            Read = function() if isfile(cachePath) then return readfile(cachePath) end end,
        })
    return uiSource
end, loadstring, Environment)
if not Vitality then
    Runtime:Report("UI load", uiError)
    Runtime:Unload()
    return
end
-- BEGIN UI LIFETIME
local function BindUILifetime(UI, runtime)
    local exit, exited = UI.Exit, false
    runtime.UIExit = function()
        if exited then return end
        exited = true
        exit(UI)
    end
    UI.Exit = function() runtime:Unload() end
end
-- END UI LIFETIME
BindUILifetime(Vitality, Runtime)
-- Cache only after compilation, execution and API validation succeed.
pcall(function()
    if not isfolder("Loki") then makefolder("Loki") end
    writefile(cachePath, uiSource)
end)
if not Runtime.Active then
    pcall(Runtime.UIExit)
    return
end

-- Default menu accent; Vitality uses Alpha = 0 for full opacity.
local menuAccent = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)
Vitality.Theme.Accent = menuAccent
Vitality.Presets.Vitality.Accent = menuAccent

-- BEGIN ANIMATION POLICY
local function ApplyAnimationPolicy(UI, runtime)
    local tween = UI.Tween
    UI.Animation.Time = 0
    UI.Tween = function(self, properties, _, rawItem)
        return tween(self, properties, TweenInfo.new(0), rawItem)
    end
    -- Popup visibility changes complete immediately, keeping their debounce callbacks intact.
    UI.FadeDescendants = function(self, visible, callback)
        self.Instance.Visible = visible
        if callback then callback() end
    end
    local createPicker = UI.CreateColorpicker
    UI.CreateColorpicker = function(self, data)
        data = table.clone(data)
        -- The RGBA readout shows opacity (1 - Alpha), so Alpha=1 displays 0.00.
        data.Default, data.Alpha = menuAccent, 1
        local picker, items = createPicker(self, data)
        local allowed = (data.Section and data.Section.Name == "Player ESP")
            or data.Flag == "CustomCrosshairColor" or data.Flag == "CustomCrosshairColor2"
        if not allowed then
            picker.Animate = false
            local update = picker.UpdateAnimation
            picker.UpdateAnimation = function(self)
                self.Animate = false
                return update(self) -- Cancels any previously started animation thread.
            end
            for _, name in ipairs({ "SettingsTab", "SettingsPage" }) do
                if items[name] then items[name].Instance.Visible = false end
            end
        end
        table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function()
            picker.Animate = false
            picker:UpdateAnimation()
        end)
        return picker, items
    end
    -- Remove the menu's animation settings, including when a saved config is loaded.
    for _, method in ipairs({ "Dropdown", "Slider" }) do
        local create = UI[method]
        UI[method] = function(self, params)
            params = params or {}
            local name = params.Name or params.name
            local animationSetting = name == "Easing Style" or name == "Easing Direction" or name == "Tweening Speed"
            if animationSetting then
                params = table.clone(params)
                params.Callback = function() UI.Animation.Time = 0 end
                if name == "Tweening Speed" then params.Default = 0 end
            end
            local control = create(self, params)
            if animationSetting then control:SetVisibility(false) end
            return control
        end
    end
end
-- END ANIMATION POLICY
ApplyAnimationPolicy(Vitality, Runtime)

-- BEGIN CLICK TARGETS
local function FixControlClicks(UI)
    local createObject = UI.Create
    UI.Create = function(self, class, properties)
        local item = createObject(self, class, properties)
        -- The pinned library mistakes a property value of "Text" for a key
        -- and clears this literal label on TextButtons (including dropdown rows).
        if class == "TextButton" and properties and properties.Text == "Text" then item.Instance.Text = "Text" end
        return item
    end
    -- Use one native activation handler (mouse, touch and keyboard), after the press.
    local function createWithActivation(create, self, params, handlers)
        local connect = UI.Connect
        UI.Connect = function(item, signal, callback)
            if signal == "MouseButton1Down" and item.Instance and item.Instance:IsA("GuiButton") then
                signal = "Activated"
            end
            local connection = connect(item, signal, callback)
            if handlers and signal == "Activated" then
                table.insert(handlers, { Item = item, Connection = connection })
            end
            return connection
        end
        local result = table.pack(pcall(create, self, params))
        UI.Connect = connect
        if not result[1] then error(result[2], 0) end
        return table.unpack(result, 2, result.n)
    end
    local createPicker = UI.CreateColorpicker
    UI.CreateColorpicker = function(self, data)
        local picker, items = createWithActivation(createPicker, self, data)
        local window, swatch = items.ColorpickerWindow, items.ColorpickerButton
        local isOver = window.IsMouseOverFrame
        -- A swatch press is not an outside click. Keep the popup's native dismissal.
        window.IsMouseOverFrame = function(item)
            return isOver(item) or swatch:IsMouseOverFrame()
        end
        swatch.Instance.Active, swatch.Instance.Selectable = true, true
        return picker, items
    end
    local createButton = UI.Button
    UI.Button = function(self, params)
        local handlers = {}
        local button = createWithActivation(createButton, self, params, handlers)
        local realButton = button.Items.RealButton.Instance
        for _, handler in ipairs(handlers) do
            if handler.Item.Instance == realButton then handler.Connection:Disconnect() end
        end
        -- Cover the existing button, including its text and border, without resizing it.
        local target = UI:Create("TextButton", {
            Name = "LokiButtonClickTarget", Parent = button.Items.Button.Instance,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
            Active = true, Selectable = true, ZIndex = realButton.ZIndex + 1,
        })
        target:Connect("Activated", function() button:Press() end)
        button.Items.ClickTarget = target
        button.Items.RealButton.Instance.Active = true
        button.Items.RealButton.Instance.Selectable = true
        button.Items.Text.Instance.Active = false
        return button
    end
end
-- END CLICK TARGETS
FixControlClicks(Vitality)

-- BEGIN UI BRIDGE (also exercised by tests/check.py)
local function CreateBridge(UI, runtime)
    local Toggles, Options = {}, {}
    local Bridge = { Unloaded = false }
    local Control = {}
    Control.__index = Control
    local Section = {}
    Section.__index = Section
    local keyListHosts = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local trackedKeyList
    local function trackKeyList()
        local list = UI.KeyList
        if not list or list == trackedKeyList then return end
        trackedKeyList = list
        local add, createToggle = list.Add, UI.Toggle
        local function keybindToggle(self, params)
            if keyListHosts[coroutine.running()] and type(params.Flag) == "string"
                and params.Flag:match("ShowInKeybindsList$") then
                params = table.clone(params)
                local host = keyListHosts[coroutine.running()]
                params.Default = host.ListKeybinds == true
                if host.ForceInKeybindsList then
                    local callback = params.Callback
                    params.Callback = function() if callback then callback(true) end end
                end
            end
            return createToggle(self, params)
        end
        UI.Toggle = keybindToggle
        local function addRow(self, ...)
            -- Native row creation yields; keep ownership local to this coroutine.
            local host = keyListHosts[coroutine.running()]
            local row = add(self, ...)
            if host then
                local status, mode, visibility = row.SetStatus, row.SetMode, row.SetVis
                local listed = host.ListKeybinds == true
                row.SetStatus = function(item)
                    local enabled = listed and host.Value == true and runtime.Active ~= false
                    status(item, enabled)
                    if item.NewKey then item.NewKey.Instance.Visible = enabled end
                    mode(item, enabled and "enabled" or "off")
                end
                row.SetMode = function(item)
                    mode(item, host.Value == true and "enabled" or "off")
                end
                row.SetVis = function(item, value)
                    listed = host.ForceInKeybindsList or value == true
                    visibility(item, listed)
                    item:SetStatus()
                end
                host:OnChanged(function() row:SetStatus() end)
                row:SetStatus()
            end
            return row
        end
        list.Add = addRow
        table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function()
            if list.Add == addRow then list.Add = add end
            if UI.Toggle == keybindToggle then UI.Toggle = createToggle end
        end)
    end

    local function publish(control, value)
        local changed = not control.Initialized or control.Value ~= value
        control.Value, control.Initialized = value, true
        if changed then
            if control.Callback then runtime:Call(control.Id, control.Callback, value) end
            for _, callback in ipairs(control.Listeners) do runtime:Call(control.Id, callback, value) end
        end
    end
    local function newControl(section, id, params, registry)
        assert(not Toggles[id] and not Options[id], "Duplicate control flag: " .. id)
        local control = setmetatable({
            Id = id, Section = section, Callback = params.Callback,
            Value = params.Default, Listeners = {},
        }, Control)
        registry[id] = control
        return control
    end
    function Control:OnChanged(callback)
        table.insert(self.Listeners, callback)
        return self
    end
    function Control:SetValue(value)
        if runtime.Active == false then self.Value = value; return end
        if runtime.Unloading or not self.Widget then publish(self, value); return end
        self.Widget:Set(value)
        -- Vitality defers toggle callbacks; update backend state immediately for keybinds/unload.
        publish(self, self.Widget.Value)
    end
    function Control:AddColorPicker(id, params)
        if runtime.Active == false then return self end
        params = table.clone(params)
        params.Default, params.Transparency = menuAccent, 1
        local control = newControl(self.Section, id, params, Options)
        control.Widget = self.Widget:Colorpicker({
            Flag = id, Name = params.Title or params.Text or id,
            Default = params.Default, Alpha = params.Transparency or 0,
            Callback = function(color) publish(control, color) end,
        })
        return self -- Linoria addon chaining keeps the toggle/label as the host.
    end
    function Control:AddSettings()
        local settings = self.Widget:Settings()
        self.Widget.SettingsItem = nil
        settings.Window, settings.Page, settings.Name = self.Widget.Window, self.Widget.Page, (self.Widget.Name or self.Id) .. " Settings"
        settings:SetOpen(false)
        local row = self.Widget.Items.Toggle.Instance
        local gear = UI:Create("TextButton", {
            Name = "LucidFovSettings", Parent = row,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "⚙", FontFace = UI.Font, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = UI.Theme["Inactive Text"],
            AutoButtonColor = false, Active = true, Selectable = true, ZIndex = row.ZIndex + 2,
        }):AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Inactive Text"})
        local open = false
        runtime:Connect(gear.Instance.Activated, function()
            open = not open
            settings:SetOpen(open)
        end)
        return setmetatable({Widget = settings}, Section)
    end
    function Control:AddKeyPicker(id, params)
        if runtime.Active == false then return self end
        local control = newControl(self.Section, id, params, Options)
        local toggleHost = Toggles[self.Id] == self
        local previous, initializing = self.Value == true, true
        local key = params.Default or "None"
        if type(key) == "string" and key ~= "None" then
            key = Enum.KeyCode[key] or Enum.UserInputType[key] or "None"
        end
        self.ListKeybinds = true
        self.ForceInKeybindsList = true
        trackKeyList()
        local thread = coroutine.running()
        local previousHost = keyListHosts[thread]
        keyListHosts[thread] = self
        local ok, widget = pcall(self.Widget.Keybind, self.Widget, {
            Flag = id, Name = params.Text or id, Default = key,
            Mode = params.Mode or "Toggle",
            Callback = function(state)
                if initializing or state == previous then return end
                previous = state
                -- Native Toggle/Hold/Always supply the requested state. Setting the
                -- host directly also handles a mouse toggle changed since the last key press.
                if toggleHost then self:SetValue(state == true)
                elseif params.Callback then runtime:Call(id, params.Callback, state) end
            end,
        })
        keyListHosts[thread] = previousHost
        if not ok then error(widget, 0) end
        control.Widget = widget
        -- Match the native key-button menu from the entire feature row as well.
        local row = self.Widget.Items and self.Widget.Items.Toggle
        local button = widget.Items and widget.Items.KeyButton
        if row and type(row.Connect) == "function" and type(widget.SetOpen) == "function" then
            row:Connect("MouseButton2Down", function()
                -- A click on the native key button already has its own handler.
                if button and type(button.IsMouseOverFrame) == "function" and button:IsMouseOverFrame() then return end
                if runtime.Active ~= false then widget:SetOpen(not widget.IsOpen) end
            end)
        end
        if button and button.Instance then
            button.Instance.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 16)
        end
        initializing = false
        if toggleHost then
            local setMode = widget.SetMode
            -- Config loads use SetMode too; Always must activate without a key press.
            if setMode then
                widget.SetMode = function(item, ...)
                    if item.Mode == "Always" then item.Toggled = true end
                    return setMode(item, ...)
                end
            end
            local function syncHost()
                -- Feature prerequisites can reject activation. Read the final host value,
                -- including any nested reset, and publish it back to the native key/list.
                previous = self.Value == true
                widget.Toggled = previous
                if setMode then setMode(widget) end
            end
            self:OnChanged(syncHost)
            syncHost()
            if widget.Mode == "Always" then self:SetValue(true) end
        end
        return self
    end
    function Section:AddGear()
        local items = self.Widget.Items
        local frame, content = items.Section.Instance, items.Content.Instance
        local originalSize, originalAutomaticSize = frame.Size, frame.AutomaticSize
        local gear = UI:Create("TextButton", {
            Name = "LucidSectionSettings", Parent = items.Header.Instance,
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -5, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "⚙", FontFace = UI.Font, TextSize = 16, TextColor3 = UI.Theme["Inactive Text"],
            AutoButtonColor = false, Active = true, Selectable = true, ZIndex = items.Header.Instance.ZIndex + 2,
        }):AddToTheme({TextColor3 = "Inactive Text"})
        local open = false
        local function update()
            content.Visible = open
            frame.AutomaticSize = open and originalAutomaticSize or Enum.AutomaticSize.None
            frame.Size = open and originalSize or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 31)
        end
        runtime:Connect(gear.Instance.Activated, function() open = not open; update() end)
        update()
        return self
    end
    function Section:AddToggle(id, params)
        local control = newControl(self, id, params, Toggles)
        if runtime.Active == false then return control end
        control.Widget = self.Widget:Toggle({
            Name = params.Text or id, Flag = id, Default = params.Default == true,
            Tooltip = params.Tooltip, Callback = function(value)
                if control.Widget then publish(control, control.Widget.Value)
                else publish(control, value) end
            end,
        })
        publish(control, params.Default == true)
        return control
    end
    function Section:AddSlider(id, params)
        local control = newControl(self, id, params, Options)
        if runtime.Active == false then return control end
        control.Widget = self.Widget:Slider({
            Name = params.Text or id, Flag = id, Default = params.Default or params.Min or 0,
            Min = params.Min, Max = params.Max, Suffix = params.Suffix or "",
            Decimals = (params.Rounding or 0) > 0 and 10 ^ -params.Rounding or 0,
            Tooltip = params.Tooltip, Callback = function(value) publish(control, value) end,
        })
        return control
    end
    function Section:AddDropdown(id, params)
        local items = params.Values or {}
        local value = params.Default
        if type(value) == "number" then value = items[value] end
        if value == nil then value = params.Multi and {} or items[1] end
        local control = newControl(self, id, params, Options)
        if runtime.Active == false then return control end
        control.Widget = self.Widget:Dropdown({
            Name = params.Text or id, Flag = id, Items = items, Default = value,
            Multi = params.Multi or false, Tooltip = params.Tooltip,
            Callback = function(selected) publish(control, selected) end,
        })
        return control
    end
    function Section:AddInput(id, params)
        local control = newControl(self, id, params, Options)
        if runtime.Active == false then return control end
        control.Widget = self.Widget:Textbox({
            Name = params.Text or id, Flag = id, Default = params.Default or "",
            Placeholder = params.Placeholder or "", Finished = params.Finished or false,
            Numeric = params.Numeric or false, Callback = function(value) publish(control, value) end,
        })
        return control
    end
    function Section:AddButton(text, callback)
        if runtime.Active == false then return end
        if type(text) == "table" then callback, text = text.Func, text.Text end
        return self.Widget:Button({ Name = text, Callback = function() runtime:Call(text, callback) end })
    end
    function Section:AddLabel(text)
        if runtime.Active == false then return setmetatable({ Section = self, Listeners = {} }, Control) end
        return setmetatable({ Section = self, Widget = self.Widget:Label({ Name = text }) }, Control)
    end
    function Bridge:Section(page, name, side)
        if runtime.Active == false then return setmetatable({}, Section) end
        return setmetatable({ Widget = page:Section({ Name = name, Side = side }) }, Section)
    end
    function Bridge:Notify(text, time)
        if runtime.Active == false then return end
        UI:Notification({ Name = tostring(text), Time = time or 4 })
    end
    function Bridge:SetWatermarkVisibility(value)
        if runtime.Active ~= false then UI.WatermarkObject:SetVisibility(value) end
    end
    function Bridge:SetWatermark(text)
        if runtime.Active ~= false then UI.WatermarkObject:SetSubText(text) end
    end
    function Bridge:Unload() self.Unloaded = true; runtime:Unload() end
    runtime.Toggles = Toggles
    return Bridge, Toggles, Options
end
-- END UI BRIDGE

-- Avoid a full tooltip hit-test walk on every heartbeat; native hover events select the active entries.
local function ApplyMenuPerformancePolicy(UI, runtime, getWindow)
    local pending = {}
    local function rendered(target)
        while target and target:IsA("GuiObject") do
            if not target.Visible then return false end
            target = target.Parent
        end
        return target ~= nil and target:IsA("LayerCollector") and target.Enabled
    end
    UI.WatchTooltip = function(_, object, enter, leave, step)
        local entry = { Object = object, Enter = enter, Leave = leave, Step = step, State = false }
        local function setHovered(value)
            if value == entry.State then return end
            entry.State = value
            UI:SafeCall(value and enter or leave)
            entry.Expires = os.clock() + 0.2
            pending[entry] = true
        end
        UI:Connect(object.Instance.MouseEnter, function()
            if rendered(object.Instance) then setHovered(true) end
        end)
        UI:Connect(object.Instance.MouseLeave, function() setHovered(false) end)
        return entry
    end
    runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
        if not next(pending) then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        for entry in pairs(pending) do
            local target = entry.Object.Instance
            if entry.State and (not target or not target.Parent or not rendered(target)) then
                entry.State = false
                UI:SafeCall(entry.Leave)
                entry.Expires = now + 0.2
            end
            if entry.Step then UI:SafeCall(entry.Step, dt, entry.State) end
            if not entry.State and (not entry.Step or now >= entry.Expires) then pending[entry] = nil end
        end
    end)
    -- Keep console/floating window scrollbars functional while the main menu is closed.
    local updateScrollbars, nextScroll = UI.UpdateScrollbars, 0
    UI.UpdateScrollbars = function(self)
        local now = os.clock()
        if now < nextScroll then return end
        local window = getWindow()
        nextScroll = now + (window and window.IsOpen and 1 / 30 or 0.2)
        return updateScrollbars(self)
    end
    table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function() table.clear(pending) end)
end

-- BEGIN FOV EFFECTS
local function CreateFovEffects(runtime, drawings, flags, parent)
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name, gui.IgnoreGuiInset, gui.ResetOnSpawn = "LucidFovFill", true, false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 0
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Name, fill.BorderSizePixel, fill.Active = "Fill", 0, false
    fill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    fill.Visible = false
    fill.Parent = gui
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius, corner.Parent = UDim.new(0.5, 0), fill
    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Parent = fill
    local effects = {Gui = gui, Fill = fill, Gradient = gradient, Glow = {}, Rotation = 0, Destroyed = false}
    table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function() effects:Destroy() end)
    gui.Parent = parent
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local ring = drawings.new("Circle")
        ring.Filled, ring.NumSides, ring.Visible = false, 100, false
        ring.Thickness, ring.ZIndex = 2, 0
        effects.Glow[i] = ring
    end
    function effects:Update(camera, dt, center)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        local visible = runtime.Active and flags.FovVisible and camera ~= nil
        gui.Enabled = visible
        local glow = math.clamp(flags.FovBloom or 0, 0, 1)
        for _, ring in ipairs(self.Glow) do ring.Visible = visible and glow > 0 end
        fill.Visible = visible and flags.FovFill == true
        if not visible then return end
        local center, radius = center or GetFovCenter(camera), flags.FovSize or 120
        local color, opacity = flags.FovColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), flags.FovTransparency or 1
        for i, ring in ipairs(self.Glow) do
            ring.Position, ring.Radius = center, radius + (flags.FovThickness or 1) * 0.5 + i * 1.5
            ring.Color = color
            ring.Transparency = opacity * glow * (1 - i / 9) ^ 2 * 0.5
        end
        if not fill.Visible then return end
        fill.Position, fill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(center.X, center.Y), UDim2.fromOffset(radius * 2, radius * 2)
        fill.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(flags.FovFillTransparency or 0.8, 0, 1)
        local first = flags.FovFillColor1 or color
        local second = flags.FovFillColor2 or Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 200)
        if self.Color1 ~= first or self.Color2 ~= second then
            self.Color1, self.Color2 = first, second
            gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(first, second)
        end
        gradient.Enabled = flags.FovFillGradient == true
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = gradient.Enabled and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or first
        self.Rotation = (self.Rotation + math.min(math.max(dt or 0, 0), 0.1) * (flags.FovFillSpeed or 45)) % 360
        gradient.Rotation = self.Rotation
    end
    function effects:Destroy()
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Destroyed = true
        gui:Destroy()
        for _, ring in ipairs(self.Glow) do pcall(function() ring:Remove() end) end
    end
    return effects
end
-- END FOV EFFECTS

-- BEGIN HAND OFFSETS
local function CreateHandOffsets(runtime, getFolder)
    local manager = {Enabled = false, Offset = Vector3.zero, Entries = {}, Connections = {}}
    local folder
    local function apply(offset, entry, desired)
        local delta = desired - entry.Applied
        if delta.Magnitude == 0 then return end
        offset.Value += delta
        entry.Applied = desired
    end
    local function restore(offset, entry)
        pcall(apply, offset, entry, Vector3.zero)
        manager.Entries[offset] = nil
    end
    local function eligible(offset)
        local stats = offset.Parent
        return offset.Name == "Offset" and (offset:IsA("Vector3Value") or offset:IsA("CFrameValue")) and stats and stats.Name == "Stats"
            and stats.Parent and stats.Parent.Parent == folder
    end
    local function add(offset)
        if not manager.Enabled or not eligible(offset) then return end
        local entry = manager.Entries[offset] or {Applied = Vector3.zero}
        manager.Entries[offset] = entry
        apply(offset, entry, manager.Offset)
    end
    function manager:Clear()
        for _, connection in ipairs(self.Connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(self.Connections)
        for offset, entry in pairs(self.Entries) do restore(offset, entry) end
        folder = nil
    end
    function manager:Refresh()
        local current = self.Enabled and getFolder() or nil
        if current ~= folder then
            self:Clear()
            folder = current
            if folder then
                for _, offset in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do add(offset) end
                table.insert(self.Connections, runtime:Connect(folder.DescendantAdded, add))
                table.insert(self.Connections, runtime:Connect(folder.DescendantRemoving, function(offset)
                    local entry = self.Entries[offset]
                    if entry then restore(offset, entry) end
                end))
            end
        end
        for offset, entry in pairs(self.Entries) do
            if not eligible(offset) then restore(offset, entry)
            else apply(offset, entry, self.Offset) end
        end
    end
    function manager:SetEnabled(value)
        self.Enabled = value == true
        self:Refresh()
    end
    function manager:SetAxis(axis, value)
        if type(value) ~= "number" or value ~= value then return end
        value = math.clamp(value, -5, 5)
        local p = self.Offset
        if axis == "X" then self.Offset = Vector3.new(value, p.Y, p.Z)
        elseif axis == "Y" then self.Offset = Vector3.new(p.X, value, p.Z)
        elseif axis == "Z" then self.Offset = Vector3.new(p.X, p.Y, value)
        else return end
        self:Refresh()
    end
    table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function() manager.Enabled = false; manager:Clear() end)
    return manager
end
-- END HAND OFFSETS

-- BEGIN OVERLAY POSITION HELPERS
local function RegisterOverlayPosition(UI, runtime, key, position, anchor)
    local binding = { Connections = {} }
    local function pose(pos, point)
        return {pos.X.Scale, pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Scale, pos.Y.Offset, point.X, point.Y}
    end
    local function valid(value)
        if type(value) ~= "table" then return false end
        for i = 1, 6 do
            local number = value[i]
            if type(number) ~= "number" or number ~= number or math.abs(number) == math.huge then return false end
        end
        return true
    end
    UI.Flags[key] = pose(position, anchor)
    local function apply(value)
        if not valid(value) then return end
        UI.Flags[key] = {value[1], value[2], value[3], value[4], value[5], value[6]}
        if binding.Frame then
            binding.Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(value[5], value[6])
            binding.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(value[1], value[2], value[3], value[4])
        end
    end
    UI.SetFlags[key] = apply
    function binding:Detach()
        for _, connection in ipairs(self.Connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(self.Connections)
        self.Frame = nil
    end
    function binding:Attach(frame)
        self:Detach()
        self.Frame = frame
        apply(UI.Flags[key])
        local function changed()
            UI.Flags[key] = pose(frame.Position, frame.AnchorPoint)
        end
        for _, property in ipairs({"Position", "AnchorPoint"}) do
            table.insert(self.Connections, runtime:Connect(frame:GetPropertyChangedSignal(property), changed))
        end
    end
    table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function()
        binding:Detach()
        if UI.SetFlags[key] == apply then UI.SetFlags[key] = nil end
    end)
    return binding
end
-- END OVERLAY POSITION HELPERS

ApplyMenuPerformancePolicy(Vitality, Runtime, function() return Window end)

    Window = Vitality:Window({ Name = "Lucid" })
    if Branding then Branding:MountIcon(Window.Items["Icon"].Instance) end
    Vitality.MenuKeybind = tostring(Enum.KeyCode.RightShift)
    Vitality.WatermarkObject = Vitality.WatermarkObject or Vitality:Watermark({ Name = "Lucid", SubName = "private", Auto = false })
    Vitality.WatermarkObject.Auto = false
    Vitality.ConsoleObject = Vitality.ConsoleObject or Vitality:Console({
        Name = "Console", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(420, 320), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 262, 0, 150),
    })
    Vitality.KeyList = Vitality.KeyList or Vitality:KeybindList({ Name = "Keybind" })
    Runtime.Console = Vitality.ConsoleObject
end)
if not initialized or not Runtime.Active then
    if Runtime.Active then Runtime:Report("UI startup", startupError) end
    Runtime:Unload()
    return
end

    SnaplineBloom = 0,

    aimbot = {
        autofire = {
            enabled = false,
            distance = 1000,
            manipulation = false
        },
        exploits = {
            manipulation = {
                enabled = false,
                mode = "Automatic",
                manip_dist = 3,
                manipulation_steps = 4,
                offsetX = 0,
                offsetY = 0,
                offsetZ = 0
            }
        }
    }
}

local function MergeDefaults(target, defaults)
    for key, value in pairs(defaults) do
        if target[key] == nil then
            target[key] = type(value) == "table" and MergeDefaults({}, value) or value
        elseif type(value) == "table" and type(target[key]) == "table" then
            MergeDefaults(target[key], value)
        end
    end
    return target
end
local flags = MergeDefaults(type(Environment.flags) == "table" and Environment.flags or {}, completeFlags)
local FLAGS = flags
Environment.flags, Environment.FLAGS = flags, flags

-- BEGIN INVENTORY PANEL
local function CreateInventoryPanel(UI)
    local panel = { Width = 300, Height = 116, Rows = {}, Objects = {}, Destroyed = false }
    local function create(class, properties, theme)
        local item = UI:Create(class, properties)
        panel.Objects[item.Instance] = true
        if theme then item:AddToTheme(theme) end
        return item
    end
    panel.Root = create("Frame", {
        Name = "LokiInventory", Parent = UI.Holder.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(panel.Width, panel.Height), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(25, 25),
        BackgroundColor3 = UI.Theme.Background,
    }, { BackgroundColor3 = "Background" })
    panel.Layer = UI:RegisterWindow(panel.Root)
    panel.Scale = create("UIScale", { Parent = panel.Root.Instance, Scale = 1 })
    create("UIStroke", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, Thickness = 1, Color = UI.Theme["Outline 1"],
    }, { Color = "Outline 1" })
    local accent = create("Frame", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
    }, { BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
    panel.Header = create("TextButton", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
