        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32),
    })
    create("TextLabel", {
        Parent = panel.Header.Instance, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        FontFace = UI.BoldFont or UI.Font, TextSize = UI.FontSize, Text = "Lucid",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 1, 0),
    }, { TextColor3 = "Accent" })
    create("TextLabel", {
        Parent = panel.Header.Instance, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        FontFace = UI.Font, TextSize = UI.FontSize, Text = "Inventory Viewer",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 1, 0),
    }, { TextColor3 = "Text" })
    panel.Target = create("TextLabel", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        FontFace = UI.Font, TextSize = UI.FontSize - 1, Text = "Awaiting target",
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 36), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 24),
        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
    }, { TextColor3 = "Inactive Text" })
    panel.List = create("ScrollingFrame", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 64),
        Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -72), CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        ScrollBarThickness = 4, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
    }, { BackgroundColor3 = "Content", ScrollBarImageColor3 = "Accent" })
    for index = 1, 24 do
        panel.Rows[index] = create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = panel.List.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false,
            FontFace = UI.Font, TextSize = UI.FontSize - 1, Text = "",
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, (index - 1) * 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 24),
        }, { BackgroundColor3 = "Content", TextColor3 = "Text" })
    end
    function panel:SetScale(value) self.Scale.Instance.Scale = value end
    function panel:SetPosition(value) self.Root.Instance.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(value.X, value.Y) end
    function panel:Update(target, entries)
        self.Target.Instance.Text = target and (target .. "'s inventory") or "Awaiting target"
        local count = math.min(#entries, #self.Rows)
        if count == 0 then
            entries = { target and "No gear detected" or "Aim at a player to view inventory" }
            count = 1
        end
        self.Height = 72 + math.min(count, 10) * 24
        self.Root.Instance.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(self.Width, self.Height)
        self.List.Instance.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, count * 24)
        for index, row in ipairs(self.Rows) do
            row.Instance.Visible = index <= count
            if index <= count then row.Instance.Text = entries[index] end
        end
    end
    function panel:Destroy()
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Destroyed = true
        for index = #UI.WindowLayers, 1, -1 do
            if UI.WindowLayers[index].Frame == self.Root then table.remove(UI.WindowLayers, index) end
        end
        for index = #UI.Layers, 1, -1 do
            if UI.Layers[index] == self.Layer then table.remove(UI.Layers, index) end
        end
        for index = #UI.ThemingStuff, 1, -1 do
            if self.Objects[UI.ThemingStuff[index].Item] then table.remove(UI.ThemingStuff, index) end
        end
        for object in pairs(self.Objects) do UI.ThemeMap[object] = nil end
        UI.ThemeIndex = nil
        self.Root.Instance:Destroy()
        self.Layer.Instance:Destroy()
    end
    return panel
end
-- END INVENTORY PANEL

-- BEGIN EFFECT HELPERS
local function CameraAlignedPitch(cameraFrame, pitch)
    local forward = Vector3.new(cameraFrame.LookVector.X, 0, cameraFrame.LookVector.Z)
    if forward.Magnitude < 0.0001 then
        local right = Vector3.new(cameraFrame.RightVector.X, 0, cameraFrame.RightVector.Z)
        forward = right.Magnitude > 0.0001 and Vector3.yAxis:Cross(right.Unit) or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
    end
    local yawFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cameraFrame.Position, cameraFrame.Position + forward.Unit, Vector3.yAxis)
    -- A small horizontal component preserves heading at the otherwise singular vertical pitch.
    return yawFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.clamp(pitch, -math.rad(89), math.rad(89)), 0, 0)
end
local function CrosshairAppearance(cfg, time, rotation)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local mode = cfg.AnimationMode or "Off"
    local phase = time * (cfg.AnimationSpeed or 1) * math.pi * 2
    local color = cfg.Color
    if mode == "Color Wave" or mode == "Wave + Pulse" then
        color = color:Lerp(cfg.Color2 or color, (math.sin(phase) + 1) / 2)
    end
    local size = cfg.Size
    if mode == "Pulse" or mode == "Wave + Pulse" then
        size *= 1 + math.sin(phase) * (cfg.PulseAmount or 0.25)
    end
    return color, size, cfg.SpinEnabled and math.rad(rotation) or 0
end
local tracerAppearanceCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local tracerAppearanceFields = {
    "BeamColor", "BeamWidth", "BeamEndWidth", "BeamTransparency", "BeamBrightness",
    "BeamLightEmission", "BeamLightInfluence", "BeamSegments", "BeamFaceCamera",
    "BeamTexture", "BeamTextureMode", "BeamTextureLength",
}
local function ApplyTracerAppearance(beam, cfg)
    local previous = tracerAppearanceCache[beam]
    local changed = not previous
    if previous then
        for _, field in ipairs(tracerAppearanceFields) do
            if previous[field] ~= cfg[field] then changed = true; break end
        end
    end
    if not changed then return end
    previous = previous or {}
    for _, field in ipairs(tracerAppearanceFields) do previous[field] = cfg[field] end
    tracerAppearanceCache[beam] = previous
    beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(cfg.BeamColor)
    beam.Width0 = cfg.BeamWidth
    beam.Width1 = cfg.BeamEndWidth
    beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(math.clamp(cfg.BeamTransparency, 0, 1))
    beam.Brightness = cfg.BeamBrightness
    beam.LightEmission = cfg.BeamLightEmission
    beam.LightInfluence = cfg.BeamLightInfluence
    beam.Segments = cfg.BeamSegments
    beam.FaceCamera = cfg.BeamFaceCamera
    beam.Texture = cfg.BeamTexture == "Streak" and "rbxassetid://6079934154" or ""
    beam.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode[cfg.BeamTextureMode]
    beam.TextureLength = cfg.BeamTextureLength
    beam.TextureSpeed = 0
end
local function CreateTracerGlow(core, parent)
    local layers = {}
    local previous
    local effect = {}
    function effect:Update(cfg)
        local strength = cfg.BeamGlowEnabled and math.clamp(cfg.BeamGlowStrength or 0.6, 0, 1) or 0
        if strength <= 0 then
            for _, beam in ipairs(layers) do beam:Destroy() end
            table.clear(layers)
            previous = nil
            return
        end
        if #layers == 0 then
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
                beam.Name = "LucidTracerGlow" .. i
                beam.Attachment0, beam.Attachment1 = core.Attachment0, core.Attachment1
                beam.LightEmission, beam.LightInfluence, beam.Brightness = 1, 0, 1
                beam.Texture, beam.FaceCamera = "", true
                beam.Parent = parent
                layers[i] = beam
            end
        end
        local color, width, endWidth = cfg.BeamColor, cfg.BeamWidth, cfg.BeamEndWidth
        local opacity = 1 - math.clamp(cfg.BeamTransparency, 0, 1)
        if previous and previous.Color == color and previous.Width == width and previous.EndWidth == endWidth
            and previous.Opacity == opacity and previous.Strength == strength and previous.Segments == cfg.BeamSegments then return end
        previous = {Color = color, Width = width, EndWidth = endWidth, Opacity = opacity, Strength = strength, Segments = cfg.BeamSegments}
        for i, beam in ipairs(layers) do
            local padding = 0.06 * i * strength
            local scale = 1 + i * 0.7
            beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color)
            beam.Width0 = width * scale + padding
            beam.Width1 = endWidth > 0 and endWidth * scale + padding or 0
            beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1 - opacity * strength * 0.22 * (1 - (i - 1) / 3))
            beam.Segments = cfg.BeamSegments
        end
    end
    function effect:Destroy()
        for _, beam in ipairs(layers) do beam:Destroy() end
        table.clear(layers)
        previous = nil
    end
    return effect
end
local function GetScopedVisualOrigin(camera, gunPlugin, horizontalFraction)
    local query = gunPlugin and gunPlugin.Functions and gunPlugin.Functions.IsScoped
    if not camera or not query or type(query.Invoke) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, scoped = pcall(query.Invoke, query)
    if not ok or scoped ~= true then return end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(viewport.X * (horizontalFraction or 0.94), viewport.Y * 0.62)
    local depth = math.max(1, math.abs(camera.NearPlaneZ or -0.1) + 0.1)
    return ray.Origin + ray.Direction * depth
end
local function CaptureBulletTraceOrigin(runtime, fallback)
    -- Capture the left-edge position once per shot. The tracer then stays in
    -- world space along the bullet path, independent of later camera motion.
    if type(runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin) == "function" then
        local ok, position = pcall(runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin, 0.06)
        if ok and typeof(position) == "Vector3" then return position end
    end
    if type(runtime.GetGunBarrel) == "function" then
        local ok, position = pcall(runtime.GetGunBarrel)
        if ok and typeof(position) == "Vector3" then return position end
    end
    return fallback
end
local function CaptureKillAuraTraceOrigin(runtime, localPlayer, camera)
    local muzzle = CaptureBulletTraceOrigin(runtime)
    if typeof(muzzle) == "Vector3" then return muzzle end
    local function handPosition(model)
        local hand = model and model:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
        if not hand or not hand:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        local bone = hand:FindFirstChild("RightHandBone")
        if bone and (bone:IsA("Bone") or bone:IsA("Attachment")) then return bone.WorldPosition end
        return runtime:GetDisplayPart(hand).Position
    end
    for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
        if entity.Player == localPlayer then
            local position = handPosition(entity.WorldModel) or handPosition(entity.Character)
            if position then return position end
        end
    end
    return handPosition(localPlayer.Character) or (camera and camera.CFrame.Position)
end
local function StartBulletTrace(runtime, origin, shotOrigin, direction)
    if type(runtime.BeginBulletTrace) ~= "function" or typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3"
        or typeof(shotOrigin) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(direction) ~= "Vector3" or direction.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    -- A provisional visual ray appears before the simulator can yield. Its endpoint
    -- is replaced with the native result; this never feeds back into shot physics.
    local ok, finish = pcall(runtime.BeginBulletTrace, origin, shotOrigin + direction.Unit * 1000)
    if not ok then runtime:Report("Bullet tracer start", finish); return end
    if type(finish) == "function" then return finish end
end
local function DispatchBulletTrace(runtime, origin, result, resimulation, finish)
    -- Replayed paths are not new shots; rendering must never change the shot result.
    if resimulation or not runtime.BulletTrace or type(result) ~= "table" then return end
    local path = result.Resimulation
    local last = type(path) == "table" and path[#path]
    local endpoint = result.Instance and result.Position or (last and last.End)
    if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(endpoint) ~= "Vector3" then return end
    if (endpoint - origin).Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
    if finish then runtime:Call("Bullet tracer endpoint", finish, endpoint)
    else runtime:Call("Bullet tracer", runtime.BulletTrace, origin, endpoint) end
end
local threeLineDirections = {
    Vector2.new(-math.sqrt(3) / 2, -0.5), Vector2.new(math.sqrt(3) / 2, -0.5), Vector2.new(0, 1),
}
local crossDirections = { Vector2.new(-1, 0), Vector2.new(1, 0), Vector2.new(0, 1), Vector2.new(0, -1) }
local function CrosshairDirections(style)
    return style == "Three Lines" and threeLineDirections or crossDirections
end
local function CreateRefreshGate(interval)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local gate = { Next = -math.huge }
    function gate:Ready(now, force)
        if not force and now < self.Next then return false end
        self.Next = now + interval
        return true
    end
    return gate
end
-- A coroutine yields after each expensive probe. Never drain the whole search in one frame.
local function CreateFrameJob(callback, maxSteps, maxSeconds)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local thread = coroutine.create(callback)
    return function(...)
        if coroutine.status(thread) == "dead" then return true end
        local deadline = os.clock() + maxSeconds
        for _ = 1, maxSteps do
            if os.clock() >= deadline then break end
            local ok, result = coroutine.resume(thread, ...)
            if not ok then error(result, 0) end
            if coroutine.status(thread) == "dead" then return true, result end
        end
        return false
    end
end
local function SelectCrosshairTarget(camera, parts, center, fov, closestPlayers, localPlayer, inventoryModel)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local nearest, nearestPoint, distance = nil, nil, math.huge
    for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
        if not part.Parent or (inventoryModel and not part:IsDescendantOf(inventoryModel)) then continue end
        local position = Runtime:GetDisplayPart(part).Position
        local screen, offset = ProjectFovPoint(camera, position, fov)
        if screen then
            local priority = TargetPriority(camera, localPlayer, position, offset, closestPlayers)
            if priority < distance then
                nearest, nearestPoint, distance = part, screen, priority
            end
        end
    end
    return nearest, nearestPoint
end
-- BEGIN SKIN JOINT HELPERS
local function FindRealHead(model)
    local expanded = Runtime.HitboxExpander and Runtime.HitboxExpander.Entries[model]
    if expanded and expanded.Head.Parent == model and not expanded.Head:GetAttribute("FakeHead") then return expanded.Head end
    if not model then return nil end
    for _, part in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
        if part.Name == "Head" and part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:GetAttribute("FakeHead")
            and not part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy") then return part end
    end
end
local function CreateSkinWalker(onPoseChanged)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local manager = { Entries = {} }
    function manager:Remove(model)
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if not entry then return end
        local restoredFrame
        pcall(function()
            if entry.Neck.Parent then entry.Neck.C0 = entry.C0 end
            if entry.Head.Parent and entry.Torso.Parent then
                restoredFrame = entry.Torso.CFrame * entry.C0 * entry.Neck.Transform * entry.Neck.C1:Inverse()
                entry.Head.CFrame = restoredFrame
            end
        end)
        self.Entries[model] = nil
        if restoredFrame and onPoseChanged then onPoseChanged(model, restoredFrame) end
    end
    function manager:Clear()
        for model in pairs(self.Entries) do self:Remove(model) end
    end
    function manager:GetPose(model, offset)
        local head = FindRealHead(model)
        local neck = head and head:FindFirstChild("Neck")
        local torso = neck and neck.Part0
        if not head or not neck or not neck:IsA("Motor6D") or not torso then self:Remove(model); return false end
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if entry and (entry.Head ~= head or entry.Neck ~= neck or entry.Torso ~= torso) then self:Remove(model); entry = nil end
        if not entry then
            entry = { Head=head, Neck=neck, Torso=torso, C0=neck.C0 }
            self.Entries[model] = entry
        end
        -- Chroma moves the real neck. Selection, visibility and expander now share that pose.
        local vertical = torso.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(Vector3.new(0, offset.Y, 0))
        local c0 = entry.C0 + Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z) + vertical
        return torso.CFrame * c0 * neck.Transform * neck.C1:Inverse(), c0, entry
    end
    function manager:Apply(model, offset)
        local frame, c0, entry = self:GetPose(model, offset)
        if not frame then return false end
        entry.Neck.C0, entry.Head.CFrame = c0, frame
        if onPoseChanged then onPoseChanged(model, frame) end
        return true
    end
    return manager
end
local function CreateSkinAuto(skin, visible, clockNow)
    local manager = { States = {}, NextIndex = 1 }
    local offsets, added = {}, {}
    local function add(x, y, z)
        local key = string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f", x, y, z)
        if added[key] then return end
        added[key] = true
        table.insert(offsets, Vector3.new(x, y, z))
    end
    add(0, 0, 0)
    -- Try broad escape directions early, then fill every half-stud gap.
    for _, distance in ipairs({ 0.5, 1.5, 2.5 }) do
        for x = -1, 1 do
            for y = -1, 1 do
                for z = -1, 1 do add(x * distance, y * distance, z * distance) end
            end
        end
    end
    local fine = {}
    for x = -2.5, 2.5, 0.5 do
        for y = -2.5, 2.5, 0.5 do
            for z = -2.5, 2.5, 0.5 do table.insert(fine, Vector3.new(x, y, z)) end
        end
    end
    table.sort(fine, function(a, b)
        if a.Magnitude ~= b.Magnitude then return a.Magnitude < b.Magnitude end
        if a.Y ~= b.Y then return a.Y > b.Y end
        if a.X ~= b.X then return a.X < b.X end
        return a.Z < b.Z
    end)
    for _, offset in ipairs(fine) do add(offset.X, offset.Y, offset.Z) end
    function manager:Clear()
        table.clear(self.States)
        self.NextIndex = 1
    end
    function manager:GetOffset(model)
        local state = self.States[model]
        return state and state.Offset or Vector3.zero
    end
    function manager:Update(models, height, origin)
        local now, seen = clockNow(), {}
        local baseOffset = Vector3.new(0, height, 0)
        for _, model in ipairs(models) do
            local frame, _, entry = skin:GetPose(model, baseOffset)
            if not frame then continue end
            seen[model] = true
            local state = self.States[model]
            if not state or state.Head ~= entry.Head then
                state = { Head = entry.Head, Offset = Vector3.zero, NextCheck = 0, NextBaseline = 0, Cursor = 1 }
                self.States[model] = state
            end
            if not state.Anchor or (frame.Position - state.Anchor).Magnitude > 0.25
                or (origin - state.Origin).Magnitude > 0.5
                or (frame.LookVector - state.Look).Magnitude > 0.1 then
                -- Revalidate promptly without restarting a blocked player's search on every step.
                state.NextCheck = 0
                state.Anchor, state.Origin, state.Look = frame.Position, origin, frame.LookVector
            end
        end
        for model in pairs(self.States) do if not seen[model] then self.States[model] = nil end end
        if #models == 0 then return end
        local deadline = clockNow() + 0.001
        -- Every visit performs at most one ray; blocked players keep their search progress.
        for _ = 1, 48 do
            if clockNow() >= deadline then break end
            self.NextIndex = (self.NextIndex - 1) % #models + 1
            local model = models[self.NextIndex]
            self.NextIndex += 1
            local state = self.States[model]
            if not state then continue end
            local checking = now >= state.NextCheck
            local baseline = not checking and not state.Searching and state.Offset.Magnitude > 0 and now >= state.NextBaseline
            if not checking and not baseline and not state.Searching then continue end
            local offset = checking and state.Offset or (baseline and Vector3.zero or offsets[state.Cursor])
            local frame = skin:GetPose(model, offset + baseOffset)
            local clear = frame and visible(frame.Position, origin, model)
            if checking then
                state.NextCheck = now + 0.05
                if clear then
                    state.Searching = false
                else
                    state.Offset, state.Searching = Vector3.zero, true
                end
            elseif baseline then
                state.NextBaseline = now + 0.25
                if clear then state.Offset, state.Cursor = Vector3.zero, 1 end
            elseif clear then
                state.Offset, state.Searching = offset, false
                state.NextCheck, state.NextBaseline = now + 0.05, now + 0.25
            else
                state.Cursor += 1
                if state.Cursor > #offsets then
                    state.Cursor, state.Searching, state.NextCheck = 1, false, now + 0.1
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return manager
end
-- END SKIN JOINT HELPERS
-- Chroma's native-sized head probes; only the selected target receives a shell.
local function CreateHeadProbeOffsets(size, expandedSize)
    local radius = math.max(0, math.max(expandedSize.X / size.X, expandedSize.Y / size.Y, expandedSize.Z / size.Z) * 0.5 - 0.5)
    local offsets = {}
    if radius > 3 then
        -- Dense large-radius shells can contain thousands of native-sized heads.
        -- Keep broad coverage with a bounded, evenly distributed surface sample.
        local function add(x, y, z)
            table.insert(offsets, Vector3.new(x * radius * size.X, y * radius * size.Y, z * radius * size.Z))
        end
        add(1, 0, 0); add(-1, 0, 0); add(0, 1, 0); add(0, -1, 0); add(0, 0, 1); add(0, 0, -1)
        local count, angle = 58, math.pi * (3 - math.sqrt(5))
        for index = 0, count - 1 do
            local y = 1 - 2 * (index + 0.5) / count
            local horizontal = math.sqrt(math.max(0, 1 - y * y))
            add(math.cos(index * angle) * horizontal, y, math.sin(index * angle) * horizontal)
        end
        return offsets
    end
    local steps = math.floor(radius * 2)
    local outer, inner = (radius + 0.5) ^ 2, math.max(0, radius - 0.5) ^ 2
    -- Solve the two Z surface intervals for each X/Y pair instead of visiting
    -- every interior cell. The final predicate preserves Chroma's shell exactly.
    for x = -radius, radius, 1 do
        for y = -radius, radius, 1 do
            local xy = x * x + y * y
            if xy > outer then continue end
            local edge = math.sqrt(outer - xy)
            local first = math.max(0, math.ceil(radius - edge - 1e-10))
            local last = math.min(steps, math.floor(radius + edge + 1e-10))
            local function emit(a, b)
                for index = a, b do
                    local z = -radius + index
                    local distance = math.sqrt(xy + z * z) - radius
                    if distance <= 0.5 and distance >= -0.5 then
                        table.insert(offsets, Vector3.new(x * size.X, y * size.Y, z * size.Z))
                    end
                end
            end
            if xy >= inner then emit(first, last)
            else
                local gap = math.sqrt(inner - xy)
                local low = math.min(last, math.floor(radius - gap + 1e-10))
                emit(first, low)
                emit(math.max(first, low + 1, math.ceil(radius + gap - 1e-10)), last)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(offsets, function(a, b) return a.Magnitude < b.Magnitude end)
    return offsets
end
local function GetPlayerESPBounds(model, expander)
    local entry = expander and expander.Entries[model]
    if not entry then return model:GetBoundingBox() end
    -- GetBoundingBox scans the added probes too; GetPivot has the same orientation.
    local pivot = model:GetPivot()
    local minX, minY, minZ, maxX, maxY, maxZ
    for _, part in ipairs(entry.ESPParts) do
        if not part.Parent or not part:IsDescendantOf(model) then continue end
        local x, y, z, r00, r01, r02, r10, r11, r12, r20, r21, r22 = pivot:ToObjectSpace(part.CFrame):GetComponents()
        local half = part.Size * 0.5
        local ex = math.abs(r00) * half.X + math.abs(r01) * half.Y + math.abs(r02) * half.Z
        local ey = math.abs(r10) * half.X + math.abs(r11) * half.Y + math.abs(r12) * half.Z
        local ez = math.abs(r20) * half.X + math.abs(r21) * half.Y + math.abs(r22) * half.Z
        if minX then
            minX, minY, minZ = math.min(minX, x - ex), math.min(minY, y - ey), math.min(minZ, z - ez)
            maxX, maxY, maxZ = math.max(maxX, x + ex), math.max(maxY, y + ey), math.max(maxZ, z + ez)
        else minX, minY, minZ, maxX, maxY, maxZ = x - ex, y - ey, z - ez, x + ex, y + ey, z + ez end
    end
    if minX then
        return pivot * CFrame.new((minX + maxX) * 0.5, (minY + maxY) * 0.5, (minZ + maxZ) * 0.5),
            Vector3.new(maxX - minX, maxY - minY, maxZ - minZ)
    end
    return pivot, Vector3.zero
end
local function AdvanceTweakPitch(phase, dt, speed)
    -- A sine easing tween between both poles, with continuous phase when speed changes.
    local duration = 0.5 - math.clamp(tonumber(speed) or 1, 0.1, 1) * 0.38
    phase = (phase + math.max(0, dt) / duration) % 2
    return phase, math.rad(89) * math.cos(math.pi * phase)
end
local function LocalChamAppearance(color, transparency, parent, material, brightness)
    material = material or Enum.Material.ForceField
    if material == Enum.Material.Neon then
        local function dim(value, factor)
            factor = factor or 0.65
            return Color3.new(
                math.clamp(value.R * factor, 0, 1),
                math.clamp(value.G * factor, 0, 1),
                math.clamp(value.B * factor, 0, 1))
        end
        color = dim(color, brightness)
    end
    return { Color = color, Transparency = transparency, Parent = parent, Material = material }
end
local function CreateGunGlow(world)
    local entries, parts, unsupported = {}, {}, false
    local sprite = "\137\080\078\071\013\010\026\010\000\000\000\013\073\072\068\082\000\000\000\032\000\000\000\032\008\006\000\000\000\115\122\122\244\000\000\002\069\073\068\065\084\120\218\205\151\061\079\002\065\016\134\241\003\014\063\114\001\073\046\010\005\049\230\098\008\141\033\134\130\138\196\144\016\042\019\043\019\010\059\203\043\108\041\104\108\108\236\105\252\001\116\086\118\118\254\045\124\215\060\107\150\229\064\042\239\138\167\153\204\204\059\051\059\123\031\133\197\098\081\216\192\142\199\174\199\030\248\118\063\110\173\198\054\194\190\216\254\031\248\069\109\044\100\027\113\087\184\040\074\016\120\088\123\049\165\144\181\069\172\019\119\187\182\194\070\164\044\014\196\161\056\018\199\112\132\237\000\159\192\043\196\159\070\106\001\190\184\219\113\025\001\035\022\138\138\168\138\019\168\098\011\241\057\036\198\157\072\106\017\127\137\007\142\176\017\168\137\072\156\138\186\104\064\029\091\132\079\197\041\036\216\084\068\090\001\086\220\118\029\210\101\132\088\083\092\136\088\092\066\140\173\137\079\068\076\232\076\195\045\098\169\000\127\225\220\206\067\058\058\035\185\017\106\139\043\113\045\186\112\141\173\141\079\147\152\154\083\132\157\196\210\098\022\060\241\125\206\205\021\055\227\061\023\045\209\017\061\209\023\003\049\132\001\182\030\062\045\098\234\094\017\037\111\049\087\010\176\163\063\102\132\103\036\106\211\169\017\025\137\059\113\047\198\112\143\109\132\079\151\152\115\114\156\144\179\236\079\193\022\096\187\183\163\175\112\142\077\186\049\009\111\196\045\130\143\034\017\079\144\096\027\227\115\067\076\139\028\017\057\237\081\216\041\252\020\144\214\125\141\101\138\025\105\159\196\015\136\077\196\179\120\129\103\108\009\062\183\196\116\200\209\032\231\202\020\010\206\230\151\120\144\132\078\247\109\206\117\068\119\070\096\042\094\197\076\188\193\012\219\020\159\049\049\061\114\216\041\132\104\216\093\088\041\192\142\255\148\107\117\069\039\119\140\120\130\144\017\157\139\119\152\099\123\197\231\145\152\062\057\046\200\089\241\150\113\169\128\128\071\106\149\237\141\185\094\003\150\044\097\212\051\004\063\196\039\124\096\155\225\147\016\051\032\071\076\206\042\026\129\091\128\187\128\118\251\027\060\096\186\092\179\049\203\246\066\167\239\008\127\193\039\182\055\124\158\136\025\146\227\146\156\246\054\252\046\098\046\010\200\252\008\114\179\132\153\094\195\076\031\068\153\063\138\115\241\050\202\252\117\156\233\007\073\230\159\100\185\248\040\205\252\179\060\023\063\038\185\248\053\203\197\207\233\191\254\158\127\003\010\223\122\253\009\036\197\250\000\000\000\000\073\069\078\068\174\066\096\130"
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType, params.IgnoreWater = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
    local images, samples, halos, buckets = {}, {}, {}, {}
    local effect = {}
    function effect:Clear()
        for _, image in ipairs(images) do image:Remove() end
        table.clear(images); table.clear(entries); table.clear(parts)
        table.clear(samples); table.clear(halos); table.clear(buckets)
    end
    function effect:Sync(desired, enabled)
        if not enabled then self:Clear(); return end
        parts = desired
        for part in pairs(entries) do
            if not desired[part] or not part.Parent then entries[part] = nil end
        end
        for part in pairs(desired) do
            if part.Parent and not entries[part] then entries[part] = {NextRay = 0} end
        end
    end
    function effect:Update(color, strength, width, camera)
        strength, width = math.clamp(strength or 0.6, 0, 1), math.clamp(width or 0.04, 0.01, 0.2)
        for _, image in ipairs(images) do image.Visible = false end
        if unsupported or not camera or strength <= 0 then return end
        table.clear(samples)
        local now = os.clock()
        for part, entry in pairs(entries) do
            if not part.Parent then entries[part] = nil; continue end
            local opacity = (1-part.Transparency)*(1-part.LocalTransparencyModifier)
            if opacity <= 0 then continue end
            local frame, size = part:GetRenderCFrame(), part.Size
            local position = frame.Position
            if not part:IsDescendantOf(camera) then
                local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
                if now >= entry.NextRay or not entry.Origin or (origin-entry.Origin).Magnitude > 0.5
                    or not entry.Position or (position-entry.Position).Magnitude > 0.5 then
                    entry.NextRay, entry.Origin, entry.Position = now+0.1, origin, position
                    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {camera}
                    local hit = world:Raycast(origin, position-origin, params)
                    entry.Clear = not hit or parts[hit.Instance] == true
                end
                if not entry.Clear then continue end
            end
            local length, breadth, axis = size.X, math.max(size.Y,size.Z), frame.RightVector
            if size.Y > length then length, breadth, axis = size.Y, math.max(size.X,size.Z), frame.UpVector end
            if size.Z > length then length, breadth, axis = size.Z, math.max(size.X,size.Y), frame.LookVector end
            local count = math.clamp(math.ceil(length/math.max(breadth,0.1)),1,16)
            -- Keep the original radial halo, following each part's rendered long axis.
            local extent = math.min(math.max(breadth, length/count)*0.5,1)+width*4
            for index = 1, count do
                local offset = count == 1 and 0 or ((index-1)/(count-1)-0.5)*math.max(0,length-breadth)
                local point = position+axis*offset
                local screen, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point)
                if not onScreen or screen.Z <= 0 then continue end
                local edge = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point+camera.CFrame.RightVector*extent)
                local radius = math.clamp(math.abs(edge.X-screen.X),6,96)
                table.insert(samples,{X=screen.X,Y=screen.Y,Radius=radius,Opacity=opacity})
            end
        end
        if #samples == 0 then return end
        -- Nearby samples share a halo. Every source contributes, including guns with
        -- many tiny pieces; the drawing pool stays bounded without dropping parts.
        local spacing = 4
        repeat
            table.clear(halos); table.clear(buckets)
            for _, sample in ipairs(samples) do
                local key = math.floor(sample.X/spacing)..":"..math.floor(sample.Y/spacing)
                local halo = buckets[key]
                if not halo then
                    halo = {X=sample.X,Y=sample.Y,Radius=sample.Radius,Opacity=sample.Opacity}
                    buckets[key] = halo; table.insert(halos,halo)
                else
                    local dx,dy = sample.X-halo.X,sample.Y-halo.Y
                    halo.Radius = math.max(halo.Radius,math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)+sample.Radius)
                    halo.Opacity = math.max(halo.Opacity,sample.Opacity)
                end
            end
            spacing *= 2
        until #halos <= 128
        -- Match the soft sprite's radial falloff when compensating overlapping halos.
        -- A receiver made of many meshes should not glow brighter than the barrel.
        for index, halo in ipairs(halos) do
            local density = 1
            for otherIndex, other in ipairs(halos) do
                if otherIndex == index then continue end
                local dx,dy = halo.X-other.X,halo.Y-other.Y
                local distance = (dx*dx+dy*dy)/(other.Radius*other.Radius)
                if distance < 1 then density += math.exp(-4*distance)*other.Opacity/math.max(halo.Opacity,0.001) end
            end
            local image = images[index]
            if not image then
                local ok, message = pcall(function()
                    image = NativeDrawing.new("Image")
                    image.Data, image.Visible, image.ZIndex = sprite, false, 5
                end)
                if not ok then
                    if image then pcall(function() image:Remove() end) end
                    self:Clear(); unsupported = true
                    Runtime:Report("Gun Glow",message); return
                end
                images[index] = image
            end
            image.Position = Vector2.new(halo.X-halo.Radius,halo.Y-halo.Radius)
            image.Size = Vector2.new(halo.Radius*2,halo.Radius*2)
            image.Color = color
            image.Transparency = 1-(1-halo.Opacity*strength*0.14)^(1/density)
            image.Visible = true
        end
    end
    return effect
end
local CreateLocalChams
local function CreateHitboxExpander(runtime, world, localPlayer, clockNow)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    clockNow = clockNow or os.clock
    local manager = { Entries = {}, Geometries = {}, Enabled = false, Radius = 5, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251),
        RealAimParts = { "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm",
            "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg" } }
    manager.VisualChams = CreateLocalChams(nil)
    function manager:RefreshVisuals()
        local desired = {}
        for _, entry in pairs(self.Entries) do
            local style = LocalChamAppearance(self.Color, 0)
            style.ForceVisible = true
            desired[entry.Visual] = style
        end
        self.VisualChams:Sync(desired)
    end
    local function strip(head, keepNeck)
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetDescendants()) do
            if (child:IsA("JointInstance") and not (keepNeck and child.Name == "Neck" and child:IsA("Motor6D")))
                or child:IsA("Constraint") or child:IsA("WeldConstraint") or child:IsA("Script")
                or child:IsA("LocalScript") or child:IsA("ModuleScript") or child:IsA("Decal")
                or child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or child:IsA("BasePart")
                or child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or child:IsA("Trail") or child:IsA("Beam") or child:IsA("Light")
                or child:IsA("Sound") or child:IsA("Fire") or child:IsA("Smoke") or child:IsA("Sparkles")
                or child:IsA("BillboardGui") or child:IsA("SurfaceGui") or child:IsA("Highlight") then child:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    function manager:Geometry(size, expandedSize)
        for _, geometry in ipairs(self.Geometries) do
            if geometry.Size == size and geometry.ExpandedSize == expandedSize then return geometry end
        end
        local offsets, frames = CreateHeadProbeOffsets(size, expandedSize), {}
        for index, offset in ipairs(offsets) do frames[index] = CFrame.new(offset) end
        local geometry = { Size = size, ExpandedSize = expandedSize, Offsets = offsets, Frames = frames }
        if #self.Geometries >= 4 then table.remove(self.Geometries, 1) end
        table.insert(self.Geometries, geometry)
        return geometry
    end
    function manager:Remove(model)
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if not entry then return end
        entry.Removed = true
        self.Entries[model] = nil
        self:RefreshVisuals()
        for _, connection in ipairs(entry.BodyConnections or {}) do connection:Disconnect() end
        entry.Visual:Destroy()
        entry.Template:Destroy()
        for _, part in ipairs(entry.Parts) do part:Destroy() end
        self.Entries[model] = nil
    end
    function manager:Clear()
        for model in pairs(self.Entries) do self:Remove(model) end
    end
    function manager:SetColor(color)
        self.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)
        self:RefreshVisuals()
    end
    function manager:Configure(enabled, radius)
        radius = tonumber(radius) or self.Radius
        if radius ~= radius or radius == math.huge or radius == -math.huge then radius = self.Radius end
        radius = math.clamp(radius, 1, 20)
        if self.Enabled == enabled and self.Radius == radius then return end
        self:Clear()
        self.Enabled, self.Radius = enabled, radius
    end
    function manager:Sync(entities, teamCheck, targetPlayer)
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        local selectedModel
        for _, data in pairs(entities or {}) do
            local player, model = data.Player, data.WorldModel or data.Character
            if not player or player ~= targetPlayer or player == localPlayer or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
                or data.Dead or data.IsDead or not model or not model.Parent then continue end
            if teamCheck then
                local squad = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName")
                if (localPlayer.Team and player.Team == localPlayer.Team)
                    or (squad and squad ~= "" and squad == player:GetAttribute("SquadName")) then continue end
            end
            local entry = self.Entries[model]
            local head = entry and entry.Head.Parent == model and entry.Head or FindRealHead(model)
            if not head then continue end
            local realNeck = head:FindFirstChild("Neck")
            if entry and (entry.Head ~= head or entry.RealNeck ~= realNeck or entry.HeadSize ~= head.Size) then
                self:Remove(model); entry = nil
            end
            if not entry then
                -- Clean one native head template; probe cloning never rescans descendants.
                local template = head:Clone()
                if not template then continue end
                strip(template, true)
                template.Name = "Head"
                template.Anchored, template.CanCollide, template.CanQuery, template.CanTouch = true, false, true, false
                template.Massless, template.CastShadow = true, false
                template.Transparency, template.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1, 0
                template:SetAttribute("FakeHead", true)
                template:SetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider", true)
                template:SetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual", nil)
                template:SetAttribute("StretchedHead", nil)
                -- A clean shell cannot inherit custom head meshes, textures or overlays.
                local visual = Instance.new("Part")
                visual.Name, visual.Shape = "LokiHitboxVisual", Enum.PartType.Ball
                local scale = math.max(1, self.Radius * 2 / math.max(head.Size.X, head.Size.Y, head.Size.Z, 0.001))
                visual.Size = head.Size * scale
                visual.MaterialVariant = ""
                visual.Color, visual.Material = self.Color, Enum.Material.ForceField
                visual.Transparency, visual.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0, 0
                visual.Anchored, visual.CanCollide, visual.CanQuery, visual.CanTouch = true, false, false, false
                visual.Massless, visual.CastShadow = true, false
                visual:SetAttribute("FakeHead", true)
                visual:SetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual", true)
                visual:SetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider", nil)
                local bodyParts = {}
                local function rememberBody(part)
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:GetAttribute("FakeHead") and not part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy")
                        and not part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider") and not part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual") then bodyParts[part.Name] = part end
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do rememberBody(part) end
                local espParts = {}
                for _, part in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:GetAttribute("FakeHead") and not part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy") then
                        table.insert(espParts, part)
                    end
                end
                local frame = runtime:GetDisplayPart(head).CFrame
                visual.CFrame, visual.Parent = frame, model
                local geometry = self:Geometry(head.Size, visual.Size)
                entry = { Head = head, HeadSize = head.Size, Visual = visual, Template = template,
                    Parts = {}, Joints = {}, AnchoredParts = {}, ESPParts = espParts, BodyParts = bodyParts, BodyConnections = {},
                    RealNeck = realNeck, Offsets = geometry.Offsets, OffsetFrames = geometry.Frames, Cursor = 1, Frame = frame }
                self.Entries[model] = entry
                table.insert(entry.BodyConnections, runtime:Connect(model.ChildAdded, rememberBody))
                table.insert(entry.BodyConnections, runtime:Connect(model.ChildRemoved, function(part)
                    if bodyParts[part.Name] == part then bodyParts[part.Name] = nil end
                end))
                self:RefreshVisuals()
            end
            selectedModel = model
            break
        end
        for model in pairs(self.Entries) do if model ~= selectedModel then self:Remove(model) end end
    end
    function manager:GetBodyParts(model)
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if not entry then return end
        return entry.BodyParts
    end
    function manager:GetTopPoint(model)
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if not self.Enabled or not entry or not entry.Visual.Parent then return end
        local frame, half = entry.Visual:GetRenderCFrame(), entry.Visual.Size * 0.5
        local right, up, look = frame.RightVector, frame.UpVector, frame.LookVector
        local height = math.sqrt((right.Y * half.X)^2 + (up.Y * half.Y)^2 + (look.Y * half.Z)^2)
        if height <= 0 then return frame.Position end
        return frame.Position + (right * (right.Y * half.X^2) + up * (up.Y * half.Y^2) + look * (look.Y * half.Z^2)) / height
    end
    function manager:Build(entry, model)
        local deadline = clockNow() + 0.00035
        -- Construction runs on Heartbeat, with a small clone/time budget per step.
        for _ = 1, 4 do
            if clockNow() >= deadline then return end
            local offset = entry.Offsets[entry.Cursor]
            if not offset then return end
            local offsetFrame = entry.OffsetFrames[entry.Cursor]
            local part = entry.Template:Clone()
            if not part then return end
            local neck, real = part:FindFirstChild("Neck"), entry.RealNeck
            local jointed = neck and neck:IsA("Motor6D") and real and real:IsA("Motor6D") and real.Part0
            part.Anchored = not jointed
            if jointed then
                neck.Part0, neck.Part1, neck.C0 = real.Part0, part, real.C0 * offsetFrame
                table.insert(entry.Joints, { Joint = neck, Offset = offset, Frame = offsetFrame })
            else
                table.insert(entry.AnchoredParts, { Part = part, Frame = offsetFrame })
            end
            part.CFrame, part.Parent = entry.Frame * offsetFrame, model
            table.insert(entry.Parts, part)
            entry.Cursor += 1
        end
    end
    function manager:GetVisiblePoint(model, entity, origin, camera)
        if not self.Enabled or not camera or type(runtime.IsTargetVisible) ~= "function" then return end
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if not entry or entry.Removed then return end
        local function visiblePoint(part)
            if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or not part.Parent or part:GetAttribute("FakeHead")
                or part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy") or part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider")
                or part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual") then return end
            local projected, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if not onScreen or projected.Z <= 0 then return end
            local visible, point = runtime.IsTargetVisible(part, entity, origin)
            if visible then return point, part end
        end
        local point, part = visiblePoint(entry.Head)
        if point then return point, part end
        for _, name in ipairs(self.RealAimParts) do
            point, part = visiblePoint(model:FindFirstChild(name))
            if point then return point, part end
        end
    end
    function manager:UpdateModelPose(model, frame)
        local entry = self.Entries[model]
        if not self.Enabled or not entry then return end
        if not model.Parent or entry.Head.Parent ~= model or not entry.Visual.Parent then self:Remove(model); return end
        frame = frame or runtime:GetDisplayPart(entry.Head).CFrame
        local neck = entry.RealNeck
        local neckFrame = neck and neck:IsA("Motor6D") and neck.Parent and neck.C0
        if neckFrame and entry.NeckFrame ~= neckFrame then
            entry.NeckFrame = neckFrame
            for _, record in ipairs(entry.Joints) do record.Joint.C0 = neckFrame * record.Frame end
        end
        if frame ~= entry.Frame then
            entry.Frame, entry.Visual.CFrame = frame, frame
            for _, record in ipairs(entry.AnchoredParts) do record.Part.CFrame = frame * record.Frame end
        end
    end
    function manager:UpdatePose()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        for model, entry in pairs(self.Entries) do
            self:UpdateModelPose(model)
        end
        if self.VisualChams:NeedsRefresh() then self:RefreshVisuals() end
    end
    function manager:BuildPending()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        for model, entry in pairs(self.Entries) do
            if not entry.Removed and model.Parent and entry.Cursor <= #entry.Offsets then
                self:Build(entry, model)
                break
            end
        end
    end
    return manager
end

-- Reversible, incremental rendering reductions; no geometry/collision removal.
local function CreateGameOptimizer(world, clockNow)
    clockNow = clockNow or os.clock
    local manager = { Enabled = false, Saved = {}, Pending = {},
        Seen = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }), Queued = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }
    function manager:Set(object, property, value)
        if not object then return false end
        local ok, before = pcall(function() return object[property] end)
        if not ok or before == value then return false end
        local written = pcall(function() object[property] = value end)
        if not written then return false end
        local saved = self.Saved[object]
        if not saved then saved = {}; self.Saved[object] = saved end
        if not saved[property] then saved[property] = { Before = before, Applied = value }
        else saved[property].Applied = value end
        return true
    end
    function manager:Restore(object)
        local saved = self.Saved[object]
        if not saved then return end
        for property, state in pairs(saved) do
            pcall(function()
                -- Preserve a newer change made by another feature or the game.
                if object[property] == state.Applied then object[property] = state.Before end
            end)
        end
        self.Saved[object] = nil
    end
    function manager:Queue(object)
        if not self.Enabled or self.Seen[object] or self.Queued[object] then return end
        self.Queued[object] = true
        table.insert(self.Pending, object)
    end
    function manager:Departing(object)
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        self:Restore(object)
        self.Seen[object] = nil
    end
    function manager:Apply(object)
        -- Feature-owned guides keep their ForceField material while optimization is on.
        if object:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider") or object:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual") then return end
        if object:IsA("BasePart") and not object:IsA("Terrain") then
            -- Simplify built-in surface textures without stripping the asset's colors.
            self:Set(object, "Material", Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
            self:Set(object, "MaterialVariant", "")
        elseif object:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            -- Keep ColorMap and tint; remove only fine surface-detail maps.
            self:Set(object, "NormalMap", "")
            self:Set(object, "RoughnessMap", "")
            self:Set(object, "MetalnessMap", "")
        end
    end
    function manager:Step()
        if not self.Enabled or #self.Pending == 0 then return end
        local deadline = clockNow() + 0.001
        for _ = 1, 100 do
            if clockNow() >= deadline then break end
            local object = table.remove(self.Pending)
            if not object then break end
            self.Queued[object] = nil
            if self.Seen[object] then continue end
            if object ~= world and not object:IsDescendantOf(world) then continue end
            self.Seen[object] = true
            self:Apply(object)
            for _, child in ipairs(object:GetChildren()) do self:Queue(child) end
        end
    end
    function manager:SetEnabled(enabled)
        if self.Enabled == enabled then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled
        if enabled then
            self:Queue(world)
        else
            for object in pairs(self.Saved) do self:Restore(object) end
            table.clear(self.Pending); table.clear(self.Queued); table.clear(self.Seen)
        end
    end
    return manager
end
-- BEGIN LOCAL CHAMS
CreateLocalChams = function(folder)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local manager = { Entries = {}, Visibility = {}, Sources = {}, Materials = {}, VisibilityValues = {}, VisibilityConnections = {}, Hidden = {}, ForcedTransparency = {}, TransparencyConnections = {} }
    local prefix = "LucidChamBase"
    local properties = {"Material", "MaterialVariant", "Color", "Transparency", "TextureID", "TextureId"}
    local function rememberMaterial(part)
        local saved = {}
        if part:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            local parent = part.Parent
            if parent and parent:GetAttribute("LucidChamSurfaceStorage") then parent = parent.Parent end
            saved.Parent = parent
            return saved
        end
        for _, property in ipairs(properties) do
            local supported = (part:IsA("BasePart") and property ~= "TextureId" and (property ~= "TextureID" or part:IsA("MeshPart")))
                or ((part:IsA("Decal") or part:IsA("Texture")) and property == "Transparency")
                or (part:IsA("SpecialMesh") and property == "TextureId")
            if supported then
                local value = part:GetAttribute(prefix .. property)
                if value == nil then
                    value = part[property]
                    part:SetAttribute(prefix .. property, property == "Material" and value.Name or value)
                elseif property == "Material" then value = Enum.Material[value] or part.Material end
                saved[property] = value
            end
        end
        return saved
    end
    local function restoreMaterial(part, saved)
        pcall(function()
            if saved.Material then
                part.Material, part.Color, part.Transparency = saved.Material, saved.Color, saved.Transparency
                if saved.TextureID ~= nil then part.TextureID = saved.TextureID end
                if saved.MaterialVariant ~= nil then part.MaterialVariant = saved.MaterialVariant end
            elseif saved.Parent then
                local storage = saved.Parent:FindFirstChild("LucidChamSurfaceStorage")
                -- Only return appearances still held by our storage. Native removal
                -- or Destroy() must never be undone by trying to reparent that object.
                if storage and storage:GetAttribute("LucidChamSurfaceStorage") then
                    if part.Parent == storage then part.Parent = saved.Parent end
                    if #storage:GetChildren() == 0 then storage:Destroy() end
                end
            elseif saved.Transparency ~= nil then part.Transparency = saved.Transparency
            else part.TextureId = saved.TextureId end
            for _, property in ipairs(properties) do part:SetAttribute(prefix .. property, nil) end
        end)
    end
    -- Native controllers can overwrite an existing part without adding/removing it.
    function manager:NeedsRefresh()
        for part, appearance in pairs(self.Appearances or {}) do
            local saved = self.Materials[part]
            if saved and not part.Parent then self.Dirty = true; return true end
            if part.Parent and saved then
                if saved.Material then
                    if part.Material ~= appearance.Material or part.Color ~= appearance.Color or part.MaterialVariant ~= ""
                        or (saved.TextureID ~= nil and part.TextureID ~= "") then self.Dirty = true; return true end
                elseif saved.Transparency ~= nil and part.Transparency ~= 1 then self.Dirty = true; return true
                elseif saved.TextureId ~= nil and part.TextureId ~= "" then self.Dirty = true; return true end
            end
        end
        return self.Dirty
    end
    function manager:RemoveSource(adornee)
        local source = self.Sources[adornee]
        if not source then return end
        for _, connection in ipairs(source.Connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        self.Sources[adornee] = nil
    end
    function manager:Source(adornee)
        local source = self.Sources[adornee]
        if source then return source end
        source = { Parts = {}, Connections = {} }
        self.Sources[adornee] = source
        local function remember(part)
            if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("SpecialMesh") or part:IsA("SurfaceAppearance")
                or part:IsA("Decal") or part:IsA("Texture") then
                source.Parts[part] = true
                self.Dirty = true
            end
        end
        remember(adornee)
        for _, part in ipairs(adornee:GetDescendants()) do remember(part) end
        table.insert(source.Connections, Runtime:Connect(adornee.DescendantAdded, remember))
        table.insert(source.Connections, Runtime:Connect(adornee.DescendantRemoving, function(part)
            if source.Parts[part] then
                -- Our own move into nested storage also raises this event. Retain
                -- that entry until Sync can check its final ancestry after the move.
                if not self.Hidden[part] then source.Parts[part] = nil end
                self.Dirty = true
            end
        end))
        return source
    end
    function manager:Remove(adornee)
        local highlight = self.Entries[adornee]
        if highlight then highlight:Destroy(); self.Entries[adornee] = nil end
    end
    function manager:Sync(desired, visibility)
        self.Dirty = false
        visibility = table.clone(visibility or {})
        local materialTargets, priorities = {}, {}
        for adornee in pairs(self.Sources) do
            if not desired[adornee] or not desired[adornee].Material or not adornee.Parent then self:RemoveSource(adornee) end
        end
        for adornee in pairs(self.Entries) do
            if not desired[adornee] or desired[adornee].Material or not adornee.Parent then self:Remove(adornee) end
        end
        for adornee, appearance in pairs(desired) do
            if not adornee.Parent then continue end
            if appearance.Material then
                local source = self:Source(adornee)
                local priority = adornee:IsA("BasePart") and 2 or 1
                for part in pairs(source.Parts) do
                    local originalParent = self.Hidden[part]
                    local parent = part.Parent
                    if not originalParent and parent and parent:GetAttribute("LucidChamSurfaceStorage") then originalParent = parent.Parent end
                    if not part.Parent or (part ~= adornee and not part:IsDescendantOf(adornee)) then
                        source.Parts[part] = nil
                        continue
                    end
                    if not appearance.Filter or appearance.Filter(part, originalParent) then
                        if not priorities[part] or priority >= priorities[part] then
                            materialTargets[part], priorities[part] = appearance, priority
                        end
                        if appearance.ForceVisible and part:IsA("BasePart") and visibility[part] == nil then visibility[part] = 0 end
                    end
                end
            else
                local highlight = self.Entries[adornee]
                if not highlight then
                    highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                    highlight.Adornee = adornee
                    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 1
                    self.Entries[adornee] = highlight
                end
                highlight.Parent = appearance.Parent or folder
                highlight.DepthMode = appearance.DepthMode or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
                highlight.Enabled = true
                highlight.FillColor = appearance.Color
                highlight.FillTransparency = appearance.Transparency
            end
        end
        -- Native camera code can hide the head with Transparency as well as LTM.
        -- Enforce only explicit ForceVisible opacity, and disconnect before restoration.
        local forced = {}
        for part, appearance in pairs(materialTargets) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and appearance.ForceVisible and appearance.Transparency ~= nil then
                forced[part] = appearance.Transparency
            end
        end
        self.ForcedTransparency = forced
        for part, connection in pairs(self.TransparencyConnections) do
            if forced[part] == nil or self.FrameManaged then connection:Disconnect(); self.TransparencyConnections[part] = nil end
        end
        for part in pairs(forced) do
            if not self.FrameManaged and part.GetPropertyChangedSignal and not self.TransparencyConnections[part] then
                self.TransparencyConnections[part] = Runtime:Connect(part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"), function()
                    local wanted = self.ForcedTransparency[part]
                    if wanted ~= nil and part.Transparency ~= wanted then part.Transparency = wanted end
                end)
            end
        end
        for part, saved in pairs(self.Materials) do
            if not materialTargets[part] then restoreMaterial(part, saved); self.Materials[part] = nil; self.Hidden[part] = nil end
        end
        for part, appearance in pairs(materialTargets) do
            -- Native material callbacks can destroy a later item in this snapshot.
            if not part.Parent then materialTargets[part] = nil; continue end
            local saved = self.Materials[part]
            if not saved then
                saved = rememberMaterial(part)
                self.Materials[part] = saved
            end
            if saved.Material then
                if part.Material ~= appearance.Material then part.Material = appearance.Material end
                if part.MaterialVariant ~= "" then part.MaterialVariant = "" end
                if part.Color ~= appearance.Color then part.Color = appearance.Color end
                local transparency = appearance.Transparency or saved.Transparency
                if (saved.Transparency < 1 or appearance.ForceVisible) and part.Transparency ~= transparency then part.Transparency = transparency end
                if saved.TextureID ~= nil and part.TextureID ~= "" then part.TextureID = "" end
            elseif saved.Parent then
                local storage
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    if not part.Parent or not saved.Parent.Parent then return end
                    self.Hidden[part] = saved.Parent
                    storage = saved.Parent:FindFirstChild("LucidChamSurfaceStorage")
                    if not storage or not storage:GetAttribute("LucidChamSurfaceStorage") then
                        storage = Instance.new("Folder")
                        storage.Name = "LucidChamSurfaceStorage"
                        storage:SetAttribute("LucidChamSurfaceStorage", true)
                        storage.Parent = saved.Parent
                    end
                    -- Adding storage can invoke native cleanup synchronously.
                    -- Recheck before touching an appearance whose Parent may be locked.
                    if part.Parent and part.Parent ~= storage then part.Parent = storage end
                end)
                if not ok or not part.Parent then
                    materialTargets[part], self.Materials[part], self.Hidden[part] = nil, nil, nil
                    for _, source in pairs(self.Sources) do source.Parts[part] = nil end
                    if storage and #storage:GetChildren() == 0 then pcall(function() storage:Destroy() end) end
                end
            elseif saved.Transparency ~= nil then
                if part.Transparency ~= 1 then part.Transparency = 1 end
            elseif part.TextureId ~= "" then part.TextureId = "" end
        end
        self.Appearances = materialTargets
        self.FrameParts = {}
        if self.FrameManaged then
            for part, appearance in pairs(materialTargets) do
                local saved = self.Materials[part]
                if appearance.FrameUpdate and saved and saved.Material then self.FrameParts[part] = appearance end
            end
        end
        self.VisibilityValues = visibility
        for part, saved in pairs(self.Visibility) do
            if visibility[part] == nil or not part.Parent then
                local connection = self.VisibilityConnections[part]
                if connection then connection:Disconnect(); self.VisibilityConnections[part] = nil end
                pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = saved end)
                self.Visibility[part] = nil
            end
        end
        for part, value in pairs(visibility) do
            if part.Parent then
                if self.Visibility[part] == nil then
                    self.Visibility[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
                    if not self.FrameManaged and part.GetPropertyChangedSignal then
                        self.VisibilityConnections[part] = Runtime:Connect(part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalTransparencyModifier"), function()
                            local wanted = self.VisibilityValues[part]
                            if wanted ~= nil and part.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= wanted then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = wanted end
                        end)
                    end
                end
                if part.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= value then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = value end
            end
        end
    end
    -- Run once after native camera/viewmodel updates, never from property-change callbacks.
    function manager:Step()
        for part, appearance in pairs(self.FrameParts or {}) do
            if not part.Parent then self.FrameParts[part] = nil; self.Dirty = true; continue end
            if part.Material ~= appearance.Material then part.Material = appearance.Material end
            if part.Color ~= appearance.Color then part.Color = appearance.Color end
            if part.MaterialVariant ~= "" then part.MaterialVariant = "" end
            local saved = self.Materials[part]
            if saved and saved.TextureID ~= nil and part.TextureID ~= "" then part.TextureID = "" end
        end
        for part, value in pairs(self.ForcedTransparency) do
            if part.Parent and part.Transparency ~= value then part.Transparency = value end
        end
        for part, value in pairs(self.VisibilityValues) do
            if part.Parent and part.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= value then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = value end
        end
    end
    function manager:Clear() self:Sync({}) end
    return manager
end
-- Remember only vehicles the local player has entered; never rescan the world on a timer.
local function CreateEnteredVehicleTracker(world, player)
    local tracker = { Vehicles = {}, Drivers = {}, Connections = {}, Dirty = false }
    local function connect(signal, callback)
        local connection = Runtime:Connect(signal, callback)
        table.insert(tracker.Connections, connection)
        return connection
    end
    function tracker:Remember(vehicle)
        if not vehicle or not vehicle:IsDescendantOf(world) or self.Vehicles[vehicle] then return end
        self.Vehicles[vehicle] = true
        self.Dirty = true
    end
    local function watchDriver(driver)
        if not driver:IsA("ObjectValue") or driver.Name ~= "Driver" or tracker.Drivers[driver] then return end
        local config = driver.Parent
        local vehicle = config and config.Parent
        if not config or config.Name ~= "Configuration" or not vehicle or not vehicle:IsA("Model") then return end
        local function changed()
            if driver.Value == player and driver.Parent == config and config.Parent == vehicle then tracker:Remember(vehicle) end
        end
        tracker.Drivers[driver] = Runtime:Connect(driver:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"), changed)
        changed()
    end
    function tracker:PollSeat()
        local character = player.Character
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local seat = humanoid and humanoid.SeatPart
        if not seat then return end
        local ancestor, fallback = seat.Parent, nil
        while ancestor and ancestor ~= world do
            if ancestor:IsA("Model") then
                fallback = fallback or ancestor
                if ancestor:FindFirstChild("Chassis") or ancestor:FindFirstChild("Configuration") then
                    self:Remember(ancestor)
                    return
                end
            end
            ancestor = ancestor.Parent
        end
        self:Remember(fallback)
    end
    function tracker:Prune()
        for vehicle in pairs(self.Vehicles) do
            if not vehicle:IsDescendantOf(world) then
                self.Vehicles[vehicle] = nil
                self.Dirty = true
            end
        end
    end
    connect(world.DescendantAdded, watchDriver)
    connect(world.DescendantRemoving, function(object)
        local connection = tracker.Drivers[object]
        if connection then connection:Disconnect(); tracker.Drivers[object] = nil end
        if tracker.Vehicles[object] then tracker.Vehicles[object] = nil; tracker.Dirty = true end
    end)
    -- The game's driver reference is Configuration.Driver (also used by vehicle movement).
    for _, vehicle in ipairs(world:GetChildren()) do
        local config = vehicle:FindFirstChild("Configuration")
        local driver = config and config:FindFirstChild("Driver")
        if driver then watchDriver(driver) end
    end
    tracker:PollSeat()
    function tracker:Destroy()
        for _, connection in ipairs(self.Connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        for _, connection in pairs(self.Drivers) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(self.Connections)
        table.clear(self.Drivers)
        table.clear(self.Vehicles)
    end
    return tracker
end
-- END LOCAL CHAMS
-- END EFFECT HELPERS

-- BEGIN MANIPULATION HELPERS
-- Callback capture order can change between native controller builds.
local function ReadOriginCodec(values)
    if type(values) ~= "table" then return end
    local libraries, state, position, token, multiplier
    for _, value in pairs(values) do
        if typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
            if position then return end
            position = value
        elseif type(value) == "table" then
            if rawget(value, "BinaryUtil") then libraries = true end
            if rawget(value, "ThisIsNotAHoneyPotDontChangeThis") ~= nil then state = true end
        elseif type(value) == "string" then token = true
        elseif type(value) == "number" then
            if multiplier or value < 1 or value > 255 or value ~= math.floor(value) then return end
            multiplier = value
        end
    end
    if libraries and state and position and token then return multiplier end
end
local function IsNativeOriginState(value)
    return type(value) == "table" and rawget(value, "ThisIsNotAHoneyPotDontChangeThis") ~= nil
        and type(rawget(value, "Cameras")) == "table" and typeof(rawget(value, "lRP")) == "Vector3"
end
-- Reference capture: [10] native pivot, [11] encoded position, [12] scaled position.
-- Fit a scalar using all three axes so a zero coordinate cannot cause division by zero.
local function ReadNativeOriginSample(sample, encoded)
    if type(sample) ~= "table" or typeof(encoded) ~= "Vector3" then return end
    local function scale(pivot, scaled)
        if typeof(pivot) ~= "CFrame" or typeof(scaled) ~= "Vector3" then return end
        local position = pivot.Position
        local norm = position:Dot(position)
        if norm ~= norm or norm < 0.000001 or norm == math.huge then return end
        local ratio = position:Dot(scaled) / norm
        if ratio ~= ratio or ratio < 0.5 or ratio > 255.5 then return end
        local multiplier = math.floor(ratio + 0.5)
        local residual = (scaled - position * multiplier).Magnitude
        if multiplier >= 1 and multiplier <= 255 and residual == residual and residual <= 0.001 then return multiplier end
    end
    local function matches(value)
        if typeof(value) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
        local difference = (value - encoded).Magnitude
        return difference == difference and difference <= 0.001
    end
    if matches(rawget(sample, 11)) then
        local multiplier = scale(rawget(sample, 10), rawget(sample, 12))
        if multiplier then return multiplier end
    end
    local pivots, vectors, full = {}, {}, false
    local inspected = 0
    for _, value in pairs(sample) do
        if typeof(value) == "CFrame" then
            if #pivots < 4 then table.insert(pivots, value) end
        elseif typeof(value) == "Vector3" then
            if matches(value) then full = true elseif #vectors < 12 then table.insert(vectors, value) end
        end
        inspected += 1
        if inspected >= 96 then break end
    end
    if not full then return end
    local found
    for _, pivot in ipairs(pivots) do
        for _, scaled in ipairs(vectors) do
            local candidate = scale(pivot, scaled)
            if candidate then
                if found and found ~= candidate then return end -- Ambiguous samples must never guess a scale.
                found = candidate
            end
        end
    end
    return found
end
local function CreateOriginCodecResolver(runtime, clockNow)
    local resolver = { NextSearch = 0, NextCapture = 0 }
    function resolver:Cancel()
        local state, wrapper = self.State, self.Wrapper
        if not wrapper then return end
        -- Preserve subsequent owners and raw writes if another feature changed the table.
        if getmetatable(state) == wrapper then setmetatable(state, self.PreviousMeta) end
        if rawget(state, "lRP") == nil then rawset(state, "lRP", self.LastPosition) end
        self.Wrapper, self.PreviousMeta = nil, nil
    end
    function resolver:Capture(encoded)
        if type(debug.getstack) ~= "function" then return end
        -- Executors add different hook/pcall frames. Accept only a validated native triple.
        for level = 2, 16 do
            local ok, stack = pcall(debug.getstack, level)
            if not ok or type(stack) ~= "table" then continue end
            local multiplier = ReadNativeOriginSample(stack, encoded)
            if multiplier then return multiplier end
            local inspected = 0
            for _, sample in pairs(stack) do
                multiplier = ReadNativeOriginSample(sample, encoded)
                if multiplier then return multiplier end
                inspected += 1
                if inspected >= 96 then break end
            end
        end
    end
    function resolver:Observe(state)
        if type(debug.getstack) ~= "function" then self.Status = "executor stack API unavailable"; return end
        if not IsNativeOriginState(state) then self.Status = "controller state unavailable"; return end
        local previous = getmetatable(state)
        if previous ~= nil and type(previous) ~= "table" then self.Status = "controller metatable is protected"; return end
        -- Do not install an observer whose protected metatable cannot be restored.
        if previous and rawget(previous, "__metatable") ~= nil then self.Status = "controller metatable is protected"; return end
        local wrapper = previous and table.clone(previous) or {}
        self.State, self.PreviousMeta, self.LastPosition = state, previous, rawget(state, "lRP")
        local priorIndex, priorNewIndex = previous and previous.__index, previous and previous.__newindex
        wrapper.__index = function(object, key)
            if key == "lRP" then return self.LastPosition end
            if type(priorIndex) == "function" then return priorIndex(object, key) end
            if type(priorIndex) == "table" then return priorIndex[key] end
        end
        wrapper.__newindex = function(object, key, value)
            if key ~= "lRP" then
                if type(priorNewIndex) == "function" then return priorNewIndex(object, key, value) end
                if type(priorNewIndex) == "table" then priorNewIndex[key] = value; return end
                rawset(object, key, value); return
            end
            self.LastPosition = value
            -- Capture errors must not escape into the native controller's update.
            local ok, multiplier = pcall(self.Capture, self, value)
            self:Cancel()
            rawset(object, key, value)
            if ok and multiplier then self.Multiplier, self.Status = multiplier, "ready"
            else self.Status = "native position sample unavailable" end
        end
        local installed = pcall(setmetatable, state, wrapper)
        if not installed then self.PreviousMeta = nil; return end
        self.Wrapper, self.CaptureStarted, self.Status = wrapper, clockNow(), "waiting for controller position update"
        rawset(state, "lRP", nil)
    end
    function resolver:Update()
        local now = clockNow()
        local state = runtime.OriginCodecState
        if state ~= self.State then
            self:Cancel()
            self.State, self.Multiplier, self.ConfigStamp = state, nil, nil
        end
        if state then
            local stamp = rawget(state, "LastConfigUpdateTimestamp")
            if stamp ~= self.ConfigStamp then self:Cancel(); self.Multiplier, self.ConfigStamp = nil, stamp end
            if self.Multiplier then return self.Multiplier end
            if self.Wrapper and now - self.CaptureStarted >= 1 then self:Cancel(); self.NextCapture = now + 0.5 end
            if not self.Wrapper and now >= self.NextCapture then
                self.NextCapture = now + 0.1
                self:Observe(state)
            end
        else
            self.Status = "controller state not found"
        end
        -- Keep looking for the native callback while a state observer is pending or unsupported.
        -- A discovered state must not strand executors without a usable stack API.
        -- Retry discovery when injection precedes controller creation. Bound inspections per frame.
        if not self.Objects and now >= self.NextSearch then
            self.NextSearch = now + 1
            local ok, objects = pcall(getgc, true)
            if ok and type(objects) == "table" then self.Objects, self.Cursor = objects, 1 end
        end
        if self.Objects then
            for _ = 1, 512 do
                local object = self.Objects[self.Cursor]
                if object == nil then self.Objects = nil; break end
                self.Cursor += 1
                if not runtime.OriginCodecState and IsNativeOriginState(object) then
