                    runtime.OriginCodecState = object
                end
                if type(object) == "function" then
                    local ok, values = pcall(getupvalues, object)
                    if ok and ReadOriginCodec(values) then
                        runtime.OriginCodecReader, self.Objects = object, nil
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function resolver:Destroy()
        self:Cancel()
        self.Objects, self.State, self.Multiplier = nil, nil, nil
    end
    return resolver
end
local function CreateManipulationOriginChecker(runtime, world, localPlayer)
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.IgnoreWater = true
    local clearance = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
    return function(origin, shifted)
        local delta = shifted - origin
        if delta.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return true end
        local ignore = {}
        local native = runtime.BulletRayParams
        if typeof(native) == "RaycastParams" then
            params.CollisionGroup = native.CollisionGroup
            if native.FilterType == Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude then
                for _, object in ipairs(native.FilterDescendantsInstances) do table.insert(ignore, object) end
            end
        end
        if localPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignore, localPlayer.Character) end
        if world.CurrentCamera then table.insert(ignore, world.CurrentCamera) end
        for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            if entity.Player == localPlayer then
                if entity.Character then table.insert(ignore, entity.Character) end
                if entity.WorldModel then table.insert(ignore, entity.WorldModel) end
            end
        end
        -- Sweep a small volume all the way to the new origin. This includes Terrain
        -- and sloped ground; looking from beneath a surface cannot validate a shift.
        for _ = 1, 4 do
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
            local hit = world:Blockcast(CFrame.new(origin), clearance, delta, params)
                or world:Raycast(shifted, -delta, params)
            if not hit then return true end
            local object = hit.Instance
            if object and object:IsA("BasePart") and object.Transparency >= 0.95 and not object.CanCollide then
                table.insert(ignore, object)
            else return false end
        end
        return false
    end
end
local function CreateManipulation(runtime, getSettings, getCamera, clockNow)
    local manager = { Cache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), PreviewCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}),
        Remaining = 0, PreviewRemaining = 0 }
    local corners = {
        Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
        Vector3.new(0, 1, 1).Unit, Vector3.new(0, 1, -1).Unit, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
        Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-1, 1, 0).Unit,
        Vector3.new(1, 1, 0).Unit, Vector3.new(-1, -1, 0).Unit, Vector3.new(1, -1, 0).Unit,
    }
    local function number(value, default, low, high)
        if type(value) ~= "number" or value ~= value then return default end
        return math.clamp(value, low, high)
    end
    function manager:Clear()
        self.Display, self.PreviewDisplay = nil, nil
        table.clear(self.Cache); table.clear(self.PreviewCache)
        self.Multiplier = nil
        if runtime.OriginCodecResolver then runtime.OriginCodecResolver:Cancel() end
    end
    function manager:BeginFrame()
        self.Display, self.PreviewDisplay = nil, nil
        self.PreviewRemaining = 16 -- Display search cannot consume a shot's search budget.
        self.Remaining = 64 -- Shared across Silent Aim, Rage Bot and Triggerbot, including failed candidates.
        self.Multiplier = nil
        if not getSettings().enabled then
            self.WaitingSince = nil
            if runtime.OriginCodecResolver then runtime.OriginCodecResolver:Cancel() end
            return
        end
        if runtime.OriginCodecReader then
            local ok, values = pcall(getupvalues, runtime.OriginCodecReader)
            if ok then self.Multiplier = ReadOriginCodec(values) end
        end
        local resolver = runtime.OriginCodecResolver
        if not self.Multiplier and resolver then self.Multiplier = resolver:Update()
        elseif self.Multiplier and resolver then resolver:Cancel() end
        if self.Multiplier then self.WaitingSince = nil
        else
            self.WaitingSince = self.WaitingSince or clockNow()
            if clockNow() - self.WaitingSince >= 5 then
                runtime:Report("Manipulation", "Shifted shots paused: " .. ((resolver and resolver.Status) or "native origin configuration unavailable"))
            end
        end
    end
    function manager:Resolve(part, entity, origin, preview)
        local settings = getSettings()
        if not part or not part.Parent or typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then return end
        if not settings.enabled then
            local visible, point = runtime.IsTargetVisible(part, entity, origin)
            return origin, point or part.Position, visible, Vector3.zero
        end
        local budgetKey = preview and "PreviewRemaining" or "Remaining"
        local displayKey = preview and "PreviewDisplay" or "Display"
        local cache = preview and self.PreviewCache or self.Cache
        self[displayKey] = { Target = part, Visible = false, Offset = Vector3.zero }
        local camera = getCamera()
        if not camera then return end
        local function check(offset, requireVisible)
            -- A normal shot needs no origin codec. Only nonzero offsets require a valid scale.
            if offset.Magnitude > 0.001 and not self.Multiplier then return end
            if self[budgetKey] <= 0 then return end
            self[budgetKey] -= 1
            local shifted = origin + offset
            if offset.Magnitude > 0.001 and (not runtime.IsOriginShiftClear or not runtime.IsOriginShiftClear(origin, shifted)) then return end
            local visible, point = runtime.IsTargetVisible(part, entity, shifted, true)
            if visible or requireVisible == false then
                self[displayKey] = { Target = part, Visible = visible == true, Offset = offset }
                local aimPoint = point or (runtime.GetAimPosition and runtime:GetAimPosition(part)) or part.Position
                return shifted, aimPoint, visible == true, offset
            end
        end
        if settings.mode == "Manual" then
            local forward = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            local right = math.abs(forward.Y) > 0.9999 and Vector3.xAxis or forward:Cross(Vector3.yAxis).Unit
            local up = right:Cross(forward).Unit
            return check(right * number(settings.offsetX, 0, -4, 4)
                + up * number(settings.offsetY, 0, -4, 4) + forward * number(settings.offsetZ, 0, -4, 4), false)
        end
        local direct, point, visible, offset = check(Vector3.zero)
        if direct then return direct, point, visible, offset end
        if not self.Multiplier then return end
        local distance = number(settings.manip_dist, 3, 0, 11)
        local steps = math.floor(number(settings.manipulation_steps, 4, 1, 16))
        local rings = math.ceil(distance * steps)
        local saved = cache[part]
        local now = clockNow()
        if not saved or saved.Distance ~= distance or saved.Steps ~= steps or saved.Entity ~= entity then
            saved = { Index = 1, Origin = origin, Point = part.Position, Entity = entity, Distance = distance, Steps = steps }
            cache[part] = saved
        end
        -- Recheck the chosen offset every frame; never fire using stale visibility or a previous target's offset.
        if saved.Offset then
            direct, point, visible, offset = check(saved.Offset)
            if direct then return direct, point, visible, offset end
            saved.Offset, saved.Index = nil, 1
        end
        if distance <= 0 or (saved.RetryAt and now < saved.RetryAt) then return end
        while saved.Index <= rings * #corners and self[budgetKey] > 0 do
            local index = saved.Index - 1
            saved.Index += 1
            offset = corners[index % #corners + 1] * math.min(distance, (math.floor(index / #corners) + 1) / steps)
            direct, point, visible = check(offset)
            if direct then
                saved.Offset = offset
                return direct, point, visible, offset
            end
        end
        if saved.Index > rings * #corners then saved.Index, saved.RetryAt = 1, now + 0.05 end
    end
    return manager
end
local function GetDisplayVisibility(runtime, settings, target, entity, origin)
    if not origin then return false, "No Character", nil, Vector3.zero end
    local visible, point = runtime.IsTargetVisible(target, entity, origin)
    local manipulation = runtime.Manipulation
    local exploits = settings.aimbot and settings.aimbot.exploits
    if manipulation and exploits and exploits.manipulation and exploits.manipulation.enabled then
        local _, manipPoint, manipVisible, offset = manipulation:Resolve(target, entity, origin, true)
        local normalStatus = visible and "Visible" or "Not Visible"
        local manipStatus = manipVisible and "Manip Visible" or manipulation.Multiplier and "Manip Blocked" or "Manip Loading"
        return visible or manipVisible == true, normalStatus .. " | " .. manipStatus, manipPoint or point, offset
    end
    return visible, visible and "Visible" or "Not Visible", point, Vector3.zero
end
-- END MANIPULATION HELPERS

-- BEGIN SHOT PACKET HELPERS
-- Field order comes from the supplied GunController and Chroma's gun_fire decoder.
-- Editing a copy preserves every unrelated packet field and avoids VM register assumptions.
local function ShotVector(packet, offset)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    return Vector3.new(buffer.readf32(packet, offset), buffer.readf32(packet, offset + 4), buffer.readf32(packet, offset + 8))
end
local function WriteShotVector(packet, offset, value)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    buffer.writef32(packet, offset, value.X)
    buffer.writef32(packet, offset + 4, value.Y)
    buffer.writef32(packet, offset + 8, value.Z)
end
local function FiniteShotVector(value)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    if typeof(value) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
    local magnitude = value.Magnitude
    return magnitude == magnitude and magnitude > 0.001 and magnitude < math.huge
end
local function ShotSpread(seed, accuracy)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local random = Random.new(seed)
    return Vector3.new(random:NextNumber() - random:NextNumber(), random:NextNumber() - random:NextNumber(),
        random:NextNumber() - random:NextNumber()) / accuracy
end
local function ReadShotPacket(packet)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    if typeof(packet) ~= "buffer" then return nil end
    local length = buffer.len(packet)
    if length < 103 or length > 4096 then return nil end
    local tokenLength = buffer.readu16(packet, 4)
    local directionOffset = 38 + tokenLength
    local dotOffset = directionOffset + 53 -- Chroma layout is independent of buffer capacity.
    if dotOffset + 12 > length then return nil end
    local direction = ShotVector(packet, directionOffset)
    local accuracy = buffer.readf64(packet, dotOffset + 4)
    if not FiniteShotVector(direction) or direction.Magnitude > 2 or accuracy ~= accuracy
        or accuracy <= 0 or accuracy >= math.huge then return nil end
    return { Id = buffer.readi32(packet, 0), Timestamp = buffer.readf64(packet, 6 + tokenLength), Direction = direction, Accuracy = accuracy,
        TimestampOffset = 6 + tokenLength, OriginOffset = 18 + tokenLength, DirectionOffset = directionOffset, DotOffset = dotOffset }
end
local function BuildShotPacket(packet, origin, target, noSpread, pellets, originOffset, multiplier)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    if not target and not noSpread then return nil end
    local data = ReadShotPacket(packet)
    if not data or type(pellets) ~= "number" or pellets < 1 or pellets > 256 or pellets ~= math.floor(pellets) then return nil end
    local shifted = originOffset and originOffset.Magnitude > 0.001
    if shifted and (not target or not FiniteShotVector(originOffset) or type(multiplier) ~= "number"
        or multiplier < 1 or multiplier > 255 or multiplier ~= math.floor(multiplier)) then return nil end
    local direction = data.Direction
    if target then
        if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(target) ~= "Vector3" then return nil end
        local offset = target - origin
        if not FiniteShotVector(offset) then return nil end
        direction = offset.Unit
    end
    if noSpread then direction -= ShotSpread(data.Id * pellets + 1, data.Accuracy) end
    if not FiniteShotVector(direction) then return nil end
    local copy = buffer.create(buffer.len(packet))
    buffer.copy(copy, 0, packet)
    if shifted then
        local encoded = ShotVector(packet, data.OriginOffset) + originOffset * multiplier
        if encoded.Magnitude ~= encoded.Magnitude or encoded.Magnitude == math.huge then return nil end
        WriteShotVector(copy, data.OriginOffset, encoded)
    end
    for _, offset in ipairs({0, 28, 40}) do WriteShotVector(copy, data.DirectionOffset + offset, direction) end
    local pitch, yaw = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, direction):ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
    buffer.writef64(copy, data.DirectionOffset + 12, pitch)
    buffer.writef64(copy, data.DirectionOffset + 20, yaw)
    buffer.writef32(copy, data.DotOffset, 1)
    -- Use the serialized f32 values for local simulation as well as the server packet.
    direction = ShotVector(copy, data.DirectionOffset)
    local pending = {}
    for pellet = 1, pellets do
        local spread = ShotSpread(data.Id * pellets + pellet, data.Accuracy)
        table.insert(pending, { Id=data.Id, Pellet=pellet, Native = (data.Direction + spread).Unit, Directed = (direction + spread).Unit, Offset = shifted and originOffset or nil })
    end
    return copy, pending
end
local function TakeShotDirection(pending, direction, now, origin)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    for index = #pending, 1, -1 do if now > pending[index].Expires then table.remove(pending, index) end end
    if not FiniteShotVector(direction) then return direction end
    local unit = direction.Unit
    for index, shot in ipairs(pending) do
        if (unit - shot.Native).Magnitude < 0.0001 then
            table.remove(pending, index)
            shot.Consumed = true
            return shot.Directed, shot.Offset, shot
        end
    end
    if typeof(origin) == "Vector3" then
        for index, shot in ipairs(pending) do
            local nativeOrigin = shot.Origin and (shot.Origin - (shot.Offset or Vector3.zero))
            if nativeOrigin and (origin - nativeOrigin).Magnitude <= 1 then
                table.remove(pending, index)
                shot.Consumed = true
                return shot.Directed, shot.Offset, shot
            end
        end
    end
    return direction
end
-- Chroma's route decoder and low-level fire upvalue, independent of Cipher.new().
local function DecodeChromaRoute(route)
    if type(route) ~= "string" then return end
    if route:match("gun_fire$") or route:match("bullet_hole$") then return route end
    if #route == 0 or #route > 512 or #route % 2 ~= 0 or route:find("[^%x]") then return end
    local state, factor = 8186584168865098, 16384 + 4887
    return (route:gsub("%x%x", function(pair)
        local low = state % 274877906944
        local high = (state - low) / 274877906944
        local remainder = high % 128
        local encoded = tonumber(pair, 16)
        local decoded = ((encoded + (high - remainder) / 128) * (2 * remainder + 1)) % 256
        state = ((low * factor + high) + encoded) + decoded
        return string.char(decoded)
    end))
end
local function ChromaFireTarget(network, readUpvalue)
    if type(network) ~= "table" or type(network.Fire) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, first, second = pcall(readUpvalue, network.Fire, 1)
    if ok then
        if type(first) == "function" then return first end
        if type(second) == "function" then return second end
    end
    return network.Fire
end
local function CreateChromaPredictor(clockNow)
    local history = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function intercept(origin, target, velocity, speed)
        local delta = target - origin
        local a, b, c = speed * speed - velocity:Dot(velocity), -2 * delta:Dot(velocity), -delta:Dot(delta)
        if math.abs(a) < 0.0001 then
            if math.abs(b) < 0.0001 then return end
            local t = -c / b
            return t > 0 and t or nil
        end
        local discriminant = b * b - 4 * a * c
        if discriminant < 0 then return end
        local root = math.sqrt(discriminant)
        local t1, t2 = (-b + root) / (2 * a), (-b - root) / (2 * a)
        if t1 > 0 and t2 > 0 then return math.min(t1, t2) end
        return t1 > 0 and t1 or t2 > 0 and t2 or nil
    end
    return function(key, origin, point, rootPosition, velocity, speed, drop, ping, airborne, gravity, instant)
        if type(speed) ~= "number" or speed ~= speed or speed <= 0 or speed >= math.huge then return point end
        if typeof(velocity) ~= "Vector3" or velocity.Magnitude ~= velocity.Magnitude or velocity.Magnitude == math.huge then velocity = Vector3.zero end
        local now = clockNow()
        if instant then velocity = Vector3.zero
        elseif key then
            local state = history[key]
            if not state then state = { Samples = {}, Next = 0 }; history[key] = state end
            local samples = state.Samples
            local last = samples[#samples]
            if last and last.Magnitude > 2 and velocity.Magnitude > 2 and last.Unit:Dot(velocity.Unit) < 0.7 then
                table.clear(samples); state.Next = 0
            end
            -- Packet and display prediction can run in the same frame; sample once.
            if now >= state.Next then
                table.insert(samples, velocity)
                if #samples > 6 then table.remove(samples, 1) end
                state.Next = now + 1 / 60
            end
            local sum, weight = Vector3.zero, 0
            for index, sample in ipairs(samples) do sum += sample * index; weight += index end
            if weight > 0 then velocity = sum / weight end
        end
        local base = typeof(rootPosition) == "Vector3" and rootPosition or point
        local t = intercept(origin, base, velocity, speed)
        if not t then return point end
        local latency = type(ping) == "number" and ping == ping and math.clamp(ping * 0.5, 0, t) or 0
        local latencyPosition = base + velocity * latency
        local duration = intercept(origin, latencyPosition, velocity, speed) or t
        local predicted = latencyPosition + velocity * duration + (point - base)
        if airborne then predicted -= Vector3.yAxis * (0.5 * gravity * duration * duration) end
        return predicted + Vector3.yAxis * (drop * duration * duration)
    end
end
local function FindShotCall(args)
    local route = type(args[1]) == "string" and 1 or type(args[2]) == "string" and 2
    if not route then return end
    for index = route + 1, math.min(args.n, route + 2) do
        if typeof(args[index]) == "buffer" then return route, index end
    end
end
local function CorrectShotReport(packet, shot)
    if not shot or not shot.Consumed or typeof(packet) ~= "buffer" or buffer.len(packet) < 55 then return end
    local copy = buffer.create(buffer.len(packet))
    buffer.copy(copy, 0, packet)
    local start = ShotVector(copy, 5)
    -- Native layout: id(4), pellet(1), origin(12), direction(12), spread(12), timing(2), maximum(12).
    local distance = (ShotVector(copy, 43) - start).Magnitude
    if shot.Offset then start += shot.Offset; WriteShotVector(copy, 5, start) end
    WriteShotVector(copy, 17, shot.Directed)
    WriteShotVector(copy, 43, start + shot.Directed * distance)
    return copy
end
-- Instant simulation finishes before its physical flight time. Match the supplied
-- source's timestamp compensation and 0.26s early-report allowance at the packet boundary.
local function CreateInstantReports()
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local state = { Fires = {}, Flights = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}), NextInstant = 0 }
    local function finite(value)
        return type(value) == "number" and value == value and math.abs(value) < math.huge
    end
    function state:CanInstant(now)
        return now > self.NextInstant
    end
    function state:RecordFire(packet, now, instant)
        local shot = ReadShotPacket(packet)
        if not shot or not finite(shot.Timestamp) then return end
        local count, oldestKey, oldest = 0, nil, math.huge
        for id, fire in pairs(self.Fires) do
            if now > fire.Expires then self.Fires[id] = nil
            else
                count += 1
                if fire.Expires < oldest then oldestKey, oldest = id, fire.Expires end
            end
        end
        if count >= 1024 then self.Fires[oldestKey] = nil end
        local offset = instant and 0.8 or 0
        self.Fires[shot.Id] = {Timestamp = shot.Timestamp - offset, Offset = offset, Expires = now + 120}
        if offset > 0 then
            local copy = buffer.create(buffer.len(packet))
            buffer.copy(copy, 0, packet)
            buffer.writef64(copy, shot.TimestampOffset, shot.Timestamp - offset)
            return copy
        end
    end
    function state:RecordFlight(result, started)
        local thread = coroutine.running()
        self.Flights[thread] = nil
        if type(result) ~= "table" or type(result.Resimulation) ~= "table" then return end
        local duration = 0
        for _, step in ipairs(result.Resimulation) do
            if type(step) ~= "table" or not finite(step.Time) or step.Time < 0 then return end
            duration += step.Time
        end
        if not finite(duration) or duration <= 0 then return end
        self.Flights[thread] = {Duration = duration, Started = started}
    end
    function state:Correct(packet, now, replay)
        local thread = coroutine.running()
        local flight = self.Flights[thread]
        self.Flights[thread] = nil
        if typeof(packet) ~= "buffer" or buffer.len(packet) < 149 then return end
        local fire = self.Fires[buffer.readi32(packet, 0)]
        if not fire or now > fire.Expires or fire.Offset == 0 then return end
        if flight and now - flight.Started > 120 then return end
        local offset, length = 135, buffer.len(packet)
        for _ = 1, 3 do
            if offset + 2 > length then return end
            offset += 2 + buffer.readu16(packet, offset)
        end
        if offset + 8 > length then return end
        local timestamp = buffer.readf64(packet, offset)
        if not finite(timestamp) then return end
        local duration = flight and flight.Duration or 0
        -- Chroma decodes the transmitted float32 deltas, so use those exact times
        -- when available rather than accumulating float64 rounding differences.
        if flight and typeof(replay) == "buffer" and buffer.len(replay) >= 26 then
            local count = buffer.readu16(replay, 24)
            if count > 0 and count <= 20000 and 26 + count * 4 <= buffer.len(replay) then
                local total = 0
                for index = 1, count do
                    local delta = buffer.readf32(replay, 26 + (index - 1) * 4)
                    if not finite(delta) or delta < 0 then total = nil; break end
                    total += delta
                end
                if total and finite(total) and total > 0 then duration = total end
            end
        end
        -- Pair the source's fire/impact backdating even if a paced shot or a failed
        -- custom simulation had to use native physics. Only accelerated flights add duration.
        local corrected = timestamp - fire.Offset + duration
        local copy = buffer.create(length)
        buffer.copy(copy, 0, packet)
        buffer.writef64(copy, offset, corrected)
        -- Same integrity expression as GunController, using the corrected impact time.
        local delay = 0
        if flight then
            buffer.writeu16(copy, 41, math.abs(corrected - fire.Timestamp - duration) * 100000)
            if duration > 0.255 then
                delay = math.max(0, duration - 0.26)
                self.NextInstant = math.max(self.NextInstant, now + delay)
            else
                self.NextInstant = math.max(self.NextInstant, now + duration)
            end
        end
        return copy, delay
    end
    function state:Clear()
        table.clear(self.Fires)
        table.clear(self.Flights)
        self.NextInstant = 0
    end
    return state
end
local function CreateInstantCooldown()
    local state = {Enabled = false, Shots = 0, Until = 0, LastShot = nil, Seen = {}, Pending = {}}
    function state:Reset()
        self.Shots, self.Until, self.LastShot = 0, 0, nil
        table.clear(self.Seen)
        table.clear(self.Pending)
    end
    function state:SetEnabled(enabled)
        self.Enabled = enabled == true
        self:Reset()
    end
    function state:Remaining(now)
        if self.Until > 0 and now >= self.Until then
            self.Shots, self.Until, self.LastShot = 0, 0, nil
        elseif self.Until == 0 and self.LastShot and now - self.LastShot >= 3 then
            self.Shots, self.LastShot = 0, nil
        end
        return math.max(0, self.Until - now)
    end
    function state:CanInstant(now)
        return not self.Enabled or self:Remaining(now) == 0
    end
    function state:RecordFire(key, now, pellets)
        if not self.Enabled then return true end
        local previous = self.Seen[key]
        if previous and now <= previous.Expires then return previous.Allowed end
        local allowed = self:CanInstant(now)
        if allowed then
            self.Shots += 1
            self.LastShot = now
            if self.Shots >= 5 then self.Until = now + 3 end
        end
        if type(pellets) ~= "number" or pellets ~= math.floor(pellets) or pellets < 1 or pellets > 256 then pellets = 1 end
        for index = #self.Pending, 1, -1 do
            if now > self.Pending[index].Expires then table.remove(self.Pending, index) end
        end
        if #self.Pending >= 512 then table.remove(self.Pending, 1) end
        table.insert(self.Pending, {Allowed = allowed, Remaining = pellets, Expires = now + 2})
        local count, oldestKey, oldest = 0, nil, math.huge
        for id, item in pairs(self.Seen) do
            if now > item.Expires then self.Seen[id] = nil
            else
                count += 1
                if item.Expires < oldest then oldestKey, oldest = id, item.Expires end
            end
        end
        if count >= 512 then self.Seen[oldestKey] = nil end
        self.Seen[key] = {Allowed = allowed, Expires = now + 30}
        return allowed
    end
    function state:TakeSimulation(now)
        if not self.Enabled then return true end
        while #self.Pending > 0 do
            local shot = self.Pending[1]
            if now > shot.Expires then table.remove(self.Pending, 1)
            else
                shot.Remaining -= 1
                if shot.Remaining <= 0 then table.remove(self.Pending, 1) end
                return shot.Allowed
            end
        end
        return self:CanInstant(now)
    end
    return state
end
local function CreateInstantCooldownIndicator(runtime, drawings, world, flags, cooldown, clockNow)
    local text = drawings.new("Text")
    text.Center, text.Outline, text.Size, text.Transparency = true, true, 16, 1
    text.Font, text.ZIndex = 2, 50
    local indicator = {Text = text, Destroyed = false, NextUpdate = 0}
    function indicator:Update(force)
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        local now = clockNow()
        if not force and now < self.NextUpdate then return end
        self.NextUpdate = now + 0.05
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        text.Visible = runtime.Active and flags.instant_bullet and flags.instant_bullet_cooldown and camera ~= nil
        if not text.Visible then return end
        text.Position = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X * 0.5, camera.ViewportSize.Y * 0.5 + 80)
        local remaining = cooldown.Enabled and cooldown:Remaining(now) or 0
        if remaining > 0 then
            text.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
            text.Text = "instant cooldown"
        else
            text.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
            text.Text = "instant on"
        end
    end
    function indicator:Destroy()
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Destroyed = true
        text.Visible = false
        text:Remove()
    end
    table.insert(runtime.Cleanups, function() indicator:Destroy() end)
    indicator:Update(true)
    return indicator
end
-- Native game modules (including TracerUtil) must run at game identity, as in Chroma.
local function CallGameFunction(callback, ...)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local identity = getthreadidentity()
    setthreadidentity(2)
    local result = table.pack(pcall(callback, ...))
    setthreadidentity(identity)
    if not result[1] then error(result[2], 0) end
    return table.unpack(result, 2, result.n)
end
-- END SHOT PACKET HELPERS

-- BEGIN SHOT ALERT HELPERS
local function ShotMapLabelPoint(label, mapImage)
    -- Closed maps can report zero pixel sizes. Compose scale coordinates through
    -- nested containers before falling back to current absolute geometry.
    local point = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    local current = label
    while current and current ~= mapImage do
        if current:IsA("GuiObject") then
            local position, size, anchor = current.Position, current.Size, current.AnchorPoint
            if not position or not size or not anchor or position.X.Offset ~= 0 or position.Y.Offset ~= 0
                or (size.X.Offset ~= 0 and point.X ~= anchor.X)
                or (size.Y.Offset ~= 0 and point.Y ~= anchor.Y) then point = nil; break end
            point = Vector2.new(position.X.Scale + (point.X - anchor.X) * size.X.Scale,
                position.Y.Scale + (point.Y - anchor.Y) * size.Y.Scale)
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end
    if point and current == mapImage then return point end
    local common = mapImage.Parent
    local parent = label.Parent
    while parent and parent ~= common do parent = parent.Parent end
    if common and common:IsA("GuiObject") and parent == common and mapImage:IsA("GuiObject") then
        local position, size, anchor = mapImage.Position, mapImage.Size, mapImage.AnchorPoint
        if position.X.Offset == 0 and position.Y.Offset == 0 and size.X.Offset == 0 and size.Y.Offset == 0
            and size.X.Scale > 0 and size.Y.Scale > 0 then
            local relative = ShotMapLabelPoint(label, common)
            if relative then
                return Vector2.new((relative.X - position.X.Scale) / size.X.Scale + anchor.X,
                    (relative.Y - position.Y.Scale) / size.Y.Scale + anchor.Y)
            end
        end
    end
    local size = mapImage.AbsoluteSize
    if size.X > 0 and size.Y > 0 then
        local center = label.AbsolutePosition + label.AbsoluteSize * 0.5 - mapImage.AbsolutePosition
        return Vector2.new(center.X / size.X, center.Y / size.Y)
    end
    if label.Parent == mapImage and label.Position.X.Offset == 0 and label.Position.Y.Offset == 0 then
        return Vector2.new(label.Position.X.Scale, label.Position.Y.Scale)
    end
end
local function ShotMapLabelName(label, mapImage)
    local generic = {
        label=true, textlabel=true, textbutton=true, text=true, name=true, title=true,
        poi=true, pois=true, pointofinterest=true, location=true, frame=true, folder=true,
        imagelabel=true, imagebutton=true, container=true, labels=true, maplabels=true,
        mapimage=true, map=true, dropshadow=true, shadow=true, templates=true, template=true,
        marker=true, icon=true, button=true, small=true, medium=true, large=true,
        blip=true, blips=true, locations=true,
    }
    local function clean(value)
        if type(value) ~= "string" then return end
        local name = value:gsub("<[^>]*>", ""):gsub("[_\r\n]", " "):gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local key = name:lower():gsub("[%s%p%d]", "")
        if key == "" or generic[key] or key:match("^blip") or name:match("^%u%u?$") then return end
        return name
    end
    local name = clean(label.Text)
    if name then return name end
    -- Default text such as 'Label' is not a POI. Recover its name from game metadata.
    local current = label
    while current and current ~= mapImage do
        for _, key in ipairs({"POIName", "LocationName", "DisplayName"}) do
            name = clean(current:GetAttribute(key))
            local value = not name and current:FindFirstChild(key)
            if value and value:IsA("StringValue") then name = clean(value.Value) end
            if name then return name end
        end
        name = clean(current.Name)
        if name then
            return name:gsub("(%l)(%u)", "%1 %2")
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end
end
local function ShotMapGridCoordinate(shot, mapImage, labels)
    local letters, numbers = {}, {}
    for label in pairs(labels) do
        if not label.Parent then continue end
        local text = label.Text:gsub("<[^>]*>", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local letter = text:match("^%u%u?$")
        local number = text:match("^%d+$")
        if not letter and not number then continue end
        local point = ShotMapLabelPoint(label, mapImage)
        if not point then continue end
        local entries = letter and letters or numbers
        local value = letter or tostring(tonumber(number))
        -- Grid labels are often repeated on opposite map edges. Keep one center per cell.
        local entry = entries[value]
        if entry then
            entry.Point = (entry.Point * entry.Count + point) * (1 / (entry.Count + 1))
            entry.Count += 1
        else
            entries[value] = { Text=value, Point=point, Count=1 }
        end
    end
    local function axis(entries)
        local count, minX, maxX, minY, maxY = 0, math.huge, -math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge
        for _, entry in pairs(entries) do
            count += 1
            minX, maxX = math.min(minX, entry.Point.X), math.max(maxX, entry.Point.X)
            minY, maxY = math.min(minY, entry.Point.Y), math.max(maxY, entry.Point.Y)
        end
        if count < 2 then return end
        local key = maxX - minX >= maxY - minY and "X" or "Y"
        if math.max(maxX - minX, maxY - minY) < 0.001 then return end
        local best, distance
        for _, entry in pairs(entries) do
            local delta = math.abs(entry.Point[key] - shot[key])
            if not distance or delta < distance or (delta == distance and entry.Point[key] < best.Point[key]) then
                best, distance = entry, delta
            end
        end
        return best.Text, key
    end
    local letter, letterAxis = axis(letters)
    local number, numberAxis = axis(numbers)
    if letter and number and letterAxis ~= numberAxis then return letter .. " " .. number end
end
local function DescribeShotLocation(position, mapImage, blipUtil, labels, gridLabels)
    if mapImage and blipUtil then
        local ok, point = pcall(function() return blipUtil:ToBlipSpace(position) end)
        if ok and typeof(point) == "UDim2" then
            local size = mapImage.AbsoluteSize
            if (point.X.Offset == 0 or size.X > 0) and (point.Y.Offset == 0 or size.Y > 0) then
                local shot = Vector2.new(point.X.Scale + (size.X > 0 and point.X.Offset / size.X or 0),
                    point.Y.Scale + (size.Y > 0 and point.Y.Offset / size.Y or 0))
                local best, distance
                for label in pairs(labels) do
                    if not label.Parent then continue end
                    local name = ShotMapLabelName(label, mapImage)
                    local location = ShotMapLabelPoint(label, mapImage)
                    if location then
                        local delta = (location - shot).Magnitude
                        if not distance or delta < distance or (delta == distance and not best and name) then best, distance = name, delta end
                    end
                end
                if best then return "near " .. best end
                local grid = ShotMapGridCoordinate(shot, mapImage, gridLabels or labels)
                if grid then return "at " .. grid end
            end
        end
    end
    return string.format("at X %.0f, Z %.0f", position.X, position.Z)
end
local function ShotWeaponName(value)
    local seen = {}
    for _ = 1, 8 do
        if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
            if seen[value] then return end
            seen[value] = true
            if value:IsA("ObjectValue") then
                value = value.Value
            else
                local display = value:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
                local name = display and display:IsA("StringValue") and display.Value or value.Name
                return type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and name:gsub("%.item$", "") or nil
            end
        elseif type(value) == "table" then
            if seen[value] then return end
            seen[value] = true
            value = value.Name or value.WeaponName or value.WeaponData or value.Weapon or value.Instance
        elseif type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
            return value:gsub("%.item$", "")
        else
            return
        end
    end
end
local function CreateShotWeaponFilter()
    local manager = { Selected = {}, Count = 0 }
    local function key(name)
        return (name or "Unknown Weapon"):lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", ""):gsub("%.item$", "")
    end
    function manager:SetSelection(value)
        table.clear(self.Selected)
        self.Count = 0
        local function add(name)
            if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return end
            local normalized = key(name)
            if not self.Selected[normalized] then self.Selected[normalized] = true; self.Count += 1 end
        end
        if type(value) == "table" then
            for name, selected in pairs(value) do
                if type(name) == "number" then add(selected) elseif selected then add(name) end
            end
        elseif type(value) == "string" then add(value) end
    end
    function manager:Matches(name)
        return self.Selected[key(name)] == true
    end
    return manager
end
local function CreateShotObserver(localPlayer, isEnabled, describe, notify, weaponName)
    return function(data)
        if not isEnabled() or type(data) ~= "table" then return end
        local origin = data.Origin
        local position = typeof(origin) == "CFrame" and origin.Position or typeof(origin) == "Vector3" and origin
        if not position then return end
        local shooter = data.Shooter
        local who = shooter == localPlayer and "You"
            or typeof(shooter) == "Instance" and shooter.Name
            or type(shooter) == "string" and shooter ~= "" and shooter or "Unknown shooter"
        local weapon = weaponName and weaponName(data) or nil
        notify(who .. " fired " .. (weapon or "a gun") .. " " .. describe(position))
    end
end
local function CreateShotBlips(localPlayer, makeView, clockNow, settings)
    local manager = { Entries = {} }
    function manager:Clear()
        for _, entry in ipairs(self.Entries) do entry.View.Root:Destroy() end
        table.clear(self.Entries)
    end
    function manager:Add(data, origin)
        if type(data) ~= "table" or typeof(data.Origin) ~= "CFrame" then return end
        local shooter = data.Shooter
        if typeof(shooter) ~= "Instance" or not shooter:IsA("Player") or shooter == localPlayer then return end
        local position = data.Origin.Position
        if origin and (position - origin).Magnitude < settings.MinimumDistance then return end
        local now, duration = clockNow(), settings.Duration
        for _, entry in ipairs(self.Entries) do
            if entry.Shooter == shooter and entry.Expires > now
                and (entry.Position - position).Magnitude <= settings.CombineDistance then
                entry.Count += 1
                entry.Position = entry.Position:Lerp(position, 0.5)
                entry.Expires = now + duration
                return
            end
        end
        if #self.Entries >= 64 then table.remove(self.Entries, 1).View.Root:Destroy() end
        local view = makeView(position)
        if view then table.insert(self.Entries, { Shooter = shooter, Position = position,
            Count = 1, Expires = now + duration, View = view }) end
    end
    function manager:Update(project)
        local now = clockNow()
        for i = #self.Entries, 1, -1 do
            local entry = self.Entries[i]
            local remaining = entry.Expires - now
            if remaining <= 0 or not entry.View.Root.Parent then
                entry.View.Root:Destroy()
                table.remove(self.Entries, i)
            else
                entry.View.Root.Position = project(entry.Position)
                local text = string.format("%s %.1fs (%d)", entry.Shooter.Name, remaining, entry.Count)
                entry.View.Label.Text = text
                if entry.View.Shadow then entry.View.Shadow.Text = text end
                local fade = 1 - math.clamp(remaining / 0.3, 0, 1)
                for _, visual in ipairs(entry.View.Visuals) do
                    local object = visual.Object
                    for property, original in pairs(visual.Values) do object[property] = original + (1 - original) * fade end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return manager
end
local function ObserveShotAction(route, observer, installHook, readUpvalues, readInfo)
    assert(type(route) == "table", "GunFire route is unavailable")
    -- The current game exposes the decoded event directly. Subscribe without
    -- depending on the private GunFire handler's source name or callback layout.
    local signal = route.OnClientEvent
    if signal and type(signal.Connect) == "function" then
        local connection = signal:Connect(function(...) pcall(observer, ...) end)
        return function() connection:Disconnect() end
    end
    local callback = route.Action
    if type(callback) ~= "function" then
        assert(type(route.SetAction) == "function", "GunFire route has no callback API")
        local seen, matches, remaining = {}, {}, 256
        local function inspect(value, depth)
            if remaining <= 0 then return end
            remaining -= 1
            local kind = type(value)
            if (kind ~= "function" and kind ~= "table") or seen[value] or depth > 4 then return end
            seen[value] = true
            if kind == "function" then
                local ok, info = pcall(readInfo, value)
                local source = ok and (type(info) == "table" and (info.source or info.short_src) or type(info) == "string" and info)
                local normalized = type(source) == "string" and source:lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
                if normalized and normalized:find("gunfirehandler", 1, true) then
                    matches[value] = true
                    return
                end
                local found, values = pcall(readUpvalues, value)
                if found and type(values) == "table" then
                    for _, child in pairs(values) do
                        inspect(child, depth + 1)
                        if remaining <= 0 then break end
                    end
                end
            else
                for _, child in pairs(value) do
                    inspect(child, depth + 1)
                    if remaining <= 0 then break end
                end
            end
        end
        -- Read the existing callback graph. Never call a setter, replace route tables,
        -- or feed already decoded data through the game's decoding adapter again.
        inspect(route.SetAction, 0)
        local count = 0
        for candidate in pairs(matches) do callback = candidate; count += 1 end
        assert(count == 1 and remaining > 0,
            "GunFire callback could not be identified uniquely; the game's route was left unchanged")
    end
    local active, original = true, nil
    original = installHook(callback, function(...)
        if active then pcall(observer, ...) end
        return original(...)
    end)
    return function() active = false end
end
-- END SHOT ALERT HELPERS

-- BEGIN LOCAL FIRE SOUND
local function IsLocalFireSound(sound, localPlayer, camera, entities, weaponModels)
    if not sound:IsA("Sound") then return false end
    local name = sound.Name:lower()
    local namedFire = name == "fire" or name == "shoot" or name == "shot" or name:find("firesound", 1, true) ~= nil or name:find("gunshot", 1, true) ~= nil
    local parent = sound.Parent
    while parent do
        if parent.Name:lower():find("firesound", 1, true) then namedFire = true; break end
        parent = parent.Parent
    end
    if not namedFire then return false end
    if camera and sound:IsDescendantOf(camera) then return true end
    local character = localPlayer.Character
    if character and sound:IsDescendantOf(character) then
        local tool = sound.Parent
        while tool and tool ~= character do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") or tool.Name == "Weapon" or tool.Name == "CurrentWeapon" then return true end
            tool = tool.Parent
        end
    end
    if entities then
        for _, entity in pairs(entities) do
            if entity.Player == localPlayer then
                local model = entity.WorldModel
                if model and sound:IsDescendantOf(model) then return true end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, model in ipairs(weaponModels or {}) do
        if model and model.Parent and sound:IsDescendantOf(model) then return true end
    end
    return false
end
-- END LOCAL FIRE SOUND




-- BEGIN TARGET INDICATOR HELPERS
local function SelectIndicatorTarget(camera, localPlayer, entities, players, settings, preferred)
    if not camera then return end
    local center = GetFovCenter(camera)
    local inventoryName = GetInventorySelection(Runtime)
    local best, bestPart, bestPlayer, distance
    local seen = {}
    local function consider(player, entity)
        if not player or seen[player] then return end
        seen[player] = true
        if player == localPlayer or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead") then return end
        if inventoryName and player.Name ~= inventoryName then return end
        if entity.IsDead or entity.Dead then return end
        if settings.TeamCheck then
            if localPlayer.Team and player.Team == localPlayer.Team then return end
            local squad = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName")
            if squad and squad == player:GetAttribute("SquadName") then return end
        end
        local model = entity.WorldModel or entity.Character or player.Character
        if not model or not model.Parent then return end
        local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") or not head.Parent then return end
        local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local health = model:GetAttribute("Health")
        if (humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0) or (type(health) == "number" and health <= 0) then return end
        local point, visible = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
        if not visible or point.Z <= 0 then return end
        local delta = (Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y) - center).Magnitude
        if delta > (settings.FovSize or 120) then return end
        if head == preferred then
            best, bestPart, bestPlayer, distance = entity, head, player, -1
        elseif not distance or delta < distance then
            best, bestPart, bestPlayer, distance = entity, head, player, delta
        end
    end
    for _, entity in pairs(entities or {}) do consider(entity.Player, entity) end
    for _, player in ipairs(players) do
        if not seen[player] then consider(player, { Player = player, Character = player.Character }) end
    end
    return bestPlayer, bestPart, best
end
-- CurrentSelectedObject points to the inventory slot, whose Value is the equipped item.
local function ReadIndicatorGun(player, entity)
    entity = entity or {}
    local function name(value)
        local seen = {}
        for _ = 1, 8 do
            if typeof(value) ~= "Instance" then
                if type(value) == "table" then return value.Name end
                if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then return value:gsub("%.item$", "") end
                return nil
            end
            if seen[value] then return nil end
            seen[value] = true
            if value:IsA("ObjectValue") then
                value = value.Value
            else
                local display = value:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
                local text = display and display:IsA("StringValue") and display.Value or value.Name
                return text:gsub("%.item$", "")
            end
        end
    end
    local selectedObject = player:FindFirstChild("CurrentSelectedObject")
    if selectedObject and selectedObject:IsA("ObjectValue") then
        return name(selectedObject) or "Fists"
    end
    local character = entity.WorldModel or entity.Character or player.Character
    local tool = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if tool then return name(tool) end
    local inventory = player:FindFirstChild("GunInventory")
    if inventory then
        local selected = inventory:FindFirstChild("SelectedSlot") or inventory:FindFirstChild("CurrentSlot")
        local slot = entity.CurrentSlot or player:GetAttribute("CurrentSlot") or player:GetAttribute("SelectedSlot")
        if selected and selected:IsA("ValueBase") then
            if selected:IsA("ObjectValue") then return name(selected) or "Fists" end
            slot = selected.Value
        end
        if type(slot) == "number" or type(slot) == "string" then
            local item = inventory:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot)) or inventory:FindFirstChild("Slot" .. tostring(slot))
            return name(item) or "Fists"
        end
        for _, item in ipairs(inventory:GetChildren()) do
            if item:IsA("ObjectValue") and (item:GetAttribute("Equipped") == true or item:GetAttribute("Selected") == true) then
                return name(item) or "Fists"
            end
        end
    end
    return name(player:GetAttribute("EquippedWeapon") or player:GetAttribute("WeaponName")
        or entity.EquippedWeapon or entity.WeaponData) or "Unknown"
end
local function BindMenuDrag(frame, handle, isMenuOpen)
    local dragging, startInput, startPosition, pointer
    local connections = {}
    local function connect(signal, callback)
        table.insert(connections, Runtime:Connect(signal, callback))
    end
    connect(handle.InputBegan, function(input)
        if not isMenuOpen() then return end
        local kind = input.UserInputType
        if kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        dragging, startInput, startPosition, pointer = true, input.Position, frame.Position, input
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if not isMenuOpen() or not frame.Visible or not frame.Parent then dragging = false; return end
        local touch = pointer.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        if (touch and input ~= pointer) or (not touch and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement) then return end
        local delta = input.Position - startInput
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPosition.X.Scale, startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
            startPosition.Y.Scale, startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end)
    connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
        if input == pointer or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
    return function()
        dragging = false
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(connections)
    end
end
local function CreateTargetIndicator(UI, isMenuOpen, visibilitySettings)
    local panel = { Objects = {}, Destroyed = false }
    local function create(class, properties, theme)
        local item = UI:Create(class, properties)
        panel.Objects[item.Instance] = true
        if theme then item:AddToTheme(theme) end
        return item
    end
    panel.Root = create("Frame", {
        Name = "LokiTargetIndicator", Parent = UI.Holder.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 24, 0.65, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 114), Visible = false,
    }, { BackgroundColor3 = "Background" })
    targetIndicatorPosition:Attach(panel.Root.Instance)
    panel.Layer = UI:RegisterWindow(panel.Root)
    create("UIStroke", { Parent = panel.Root.Instance, Thickness = 1 }, { Color = "Outline 1" })
    create("Frame", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
    }, { BackgroundColor3 = "Accent" })
    panel.Avatar = create("ImageLabel", {
        Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 18), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 56), Image = "",
    })
    local function label(y, size, theme)
        return create("TextLabel", {
            Parent = panel.Root.Instance, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(78, y), Size = UDim2.new(1, -88, 0, 20),
            FontFace = UI.Font, TextSize = size, Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        }, { TextColor3 = theme })
    end
    panel.Name = label(12, UI.FontSize, "Text")
    panel.Distance = label(34, UI.FontSize - 1, "Inactive Text")
    panel.Gun = label(56, UI.FontSize - 1, "Accent")
    panel.Visibility = label(78, UI.FontSize - 1, nil)
    panel.DragHandle = create("TextButton", {
        Name = "TargetIndicatorDrag", Parent = panel.Root.Instance, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Active = true, ZIndex = 10,
    })
    local stopDrag = BindMenuDrag(panel.Root.Instance, panel.DragHandle.Instance, isMenuOpen)
    function panel:Update(player, distance, gun, visible)
        local menuOpen = isMenuOpen()
        self.Root.Instance.Visible = player ~= nil or menuOpen
        self.DragHandle.Instance.Visible = menuOpen
        if not player then
            self.UserId = nil
            self.Avatar.Instance.Image = ""
            self.Name.Instance.Text = "Target Indicator"
            self.Distance.Instance.Text = "Drag to move"
            self.Gun.Instance.Text = "No Target"
            self.Visibility.Instance.Text = ""
            self.Visibility.Instance.Visible = false
            return
        end
        if self.UserId ~= player.UserId then
            self.UserId = player.UserId
            self.Avatar.Instance.Image = string.format("rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=%d&w=150&h=150", player.UserId)
        end
        local display = player.DisplayName
        self.Name.Instance.Text = display and display ~= player.Name and (display .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")") or player.Name
        self.Distance.Instance.Text = string.format("Distance: %.0f m", distance)
        self.Gun.Instance.Text = "Gun: " .. gun
        self.Visibility.Instance.Visible = not visibilitySettings or visibilitySettings.Enabled ~= false
        self.Visibility.Instance.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 78)
        self.Visibility.Instance.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
        self.Visibility.Instance.Text = string.format("%s | %s | %.1fm", player.Name,
            visible and "VISIBLE" or "NOT VISIBLE", distance)
        self.Visibility.Instance.TextColor3 = visible and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
    end
    function panel:Destroy()
        if self.Destroyed then return end
        self.Destroyed = true
        stopDrag()
        targetIndicatorPosition:Detach()
        for index = #UI.WindowLayers, 1, -1 do
            if UI.WindowLayers[index].Frame == self.Root then table.remove(UI.WindowLayers, index) end
        end
        for index = #UI.Layers, 1, -1 do
            if UI.Layers[index] == self.Layer then table.remove(UI.Layers, index) end
        end
        for index = #UI.ThemingStuff, 1, -1 do
            if self.Objects[UI.ThemingStuff[index].Item] then table.remove(UI.ThemingStuff, index) end
        end
        for object in pairs(self.Objects) do UI.ThemeMap[object] = nil end
        UI.ThemeIndex = nil
        self.Root.Instance:Destroy()
        self.Layer.Instance:Destroy()
    end
    return panel
end
-- END TARGET INDICATOR HELPERS

-- BEGIN CAMERA / RAGE HELPERS
local function SelectRageTarget(runtime, localPlayer, origin, settings)
    if not origin or localPlayer:GetAttribute("Dead") or localPlayer:GetAttribute("StartLoading") then return end
    local candidates = {}
    for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
        -- One partially streamed player must not interrupt the remaining candidates.
        local ok, candidate = pcall(function()
            local player = entity.Player
            if not player or player == localPlayer or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
                or player:GetAttribute("StartLoading") or entity.IsDead or entity.Dead then return end
            if settings.TeamCheck then
                local squad = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName")
                if (localPlayer.Team and player.Team == localPlayer.Team)
                    or (squad and squad ~= "" and squad == player:GetAttribute("SquadName")) then return end
            end
            local model = entity.WorldModel or entity.Character
            local character = entity.Character or model
            if not model or not model.Parent or not character or not character.Parent then return end
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
            local part = runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, settings.AimPart or "Head")
                or runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, "Head")
            if not part then return end
            return { Part = part, Player = player, Entity = entity, Model = model, Character = character,
                Distance = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude }
        end)
        if ok and candidate then table.insert(candidates, candidate) end
    end
    table.sort(candidates, function(a, b) return a.Distance < b.Distance end)
    for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
        local tried = {}
        for _, name in ipairs({ settings.AimPart or "Head", "Head", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "LowerTorso" }) do
            local part = runtime:GetAimPart(candidate.Character, candidate.Model, name)
            if not part or tried[part] or part:GetAttribute("FakeHead") or part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider")
                or part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual") or part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy") then continue end
            tried[part] = true
            local visible, point, shifted, offset
            if runtime.Manipulation and settings.aimbot.exploits.manipulation.enabled then
                shifted, point, visible, offset = runtime.Manipulation:Resolve(part, candidate.Entity, origin)
            else
                visible, point = runtime.IsTargetVisible(part, candidate.Entity, origin)
            end
            if visible then
                local entity = candidate.Entity
                local root = entity.RootPart or candidate.Character:FindFirstChild("Part")
                    or candidate.Model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                return part, candidate.Player, root, entity, point or part.Position, offset
            end
        end
    end
end
local function ResolveNativeFireRequest()
    local system = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
    local controller = system and system:FindFirstChild("GunController")
    local events = controller and controller:FindFirstChild("Events")
    local module = events and events:FindFirstChild("GunPlugin")
    assert(module, "GunPlugin unavailable; wait until the gun system finishes loading")
    local plugin = CallGameFunction(require, module)
    local request = plugin.Functions and plugin.Functions.AttemptShootGun
    if request and type(request.Invoke) == "function" then return request.Invoke, request end
    assert(type(plugin.AttemptShootGun) == "function", "GunPlugin.AttemptShootGun unavailable")
    return plugin.AttemptShootGun, plugin
end
local function CreateMountainLODRemover(runtime, world)
    local manager = { Enabled = false, Hidden = {} }
    function manager:Restore()
        for object, parent in pairs(self.Hidden) do
            pcall(function()
                if object.Parent == nil and parent.Parent then object.Parent = parent end
            end)
        end
        table.clear(self.Hidden)
    end
    function manager:AttachCamera()
        if self.Added then self.Added:Disconnect(); self.Added = nil end
        self:Restore()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local function remove(object)
            if not self.Enabled or object.Name ~= "MountainLOD" or not object:IsDescendantOf(camera) then return end
            self.Hidden[object] = object.Parent
            object.Parent = nil
        end
        self.Added = runtime:Connect(camera.DescendantAdded, remove)
        for _, object in ipairs(camera:GetDescendants()) do remove(object) end
    end
    function manager:SetEnabled(enabled)
        if self.Enabled == enabled then return end
        self.Enabled = enabled
        self:AttachCamera()
    end
    function manager:Destroy()
        self:SetEnabled(false)
        if self.CameraChanged then self.CameraChanged:Disconnect() end
    end
    manager.CameraChanged = runtime:Connect(world:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"), function() manager:AttachCamera() end)
    return manager
end
local function CreateFreecam(runtime, world, input, actions, player, cameraService, menuOpen)
    local manager = { Enabled = false, Speed = 2.5, Keys = {}, Mouse = Vector2.zero, Focused = true,
        StreamBusy = false, NextStream = 0, Generation = 0 }
    local moveAction, mouseAction = "LokiFreecamMove_" .. tostring(runtime), "LokiFreecamMouse_" .. tostring(runtime)
    local fireAction = "LokiFreecamFire_" .. tostring(runtime)
    function manager:ReleaseCharacter()
        for root, saved in pairs(self.HeldRoots or {}) do
            pcall(function() root.Anchored = saved.Anchored end)
        end
        for humanoid, saved in pairs(self.HeldHumanoids or {}) do
            pcall(function()
                if humanoid.WalkSpeed == 0 then humanoid.WalkSpeed = saved.WalkSpeed end
                if humanoid.AutoRotate == false then humanoid.AutoRotate = saved.AutoRotate end
            end)
        end
        self.HeldRoots, self.HeldHumanoids = {}, {}
    end
    function manager:HoldCharacter(force)
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if force or now >= (self.NextCharacter or 0) or self.HeldCharacter ~= player.Character then
            self.NextCharacter, self.HeldCharacter = now + 0.25, player.Character
            local roots, humanoids = {}, {}
            local function add(model, root)
                root = root or (model and (model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Part") or model.PrimaryPart))
                if root and root:IsA("BasePart") and root.Parent then roots[root] = true end
                local humanoid = model and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then humanoids[humanoid] = true end
            end
            add(player.Character)
            for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
                if entity.Player == player then add(entity.Character, entity.RootPart) end
            end
            self.HeldRoots, self.HeldHumanoids = self.HeldRoots or {}, self.HeldHumanoids or {}
            for root, saved in pairs(self.HeldRoots) do
                if not roots[root] then pcall(function() root.Anchored = saved.Anchored end); self.HeldRoots[root] = nil end
            end
            for humanoid, saved in pairs(self.HeldHumanoids) do
                if not humanoids[humanoid] then
                    pcall(function() humanoid.WalkSpeed, humanoid.AutoRotate = saved.WalkSpeed, saved.AutoRotate end)
                    self.HeldHumanoids[humanoid] = nil
                end
            end
            for root in pairs(roots) do
                if not self.HeldRoots[root] then self.HeldRoots[root] = { Anchored = root.Anchored, Frame = root.CFrame } end
            end
            for humanoid in pairs(humanoids) do
                if not self.HeldHumanoids[humanoid] then self.HeldHumanoids[humanoid] = { WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed, AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate } end
            end
        end
        for root, saved in pairs(self.HeldRoots or {}) do
            if root.Parent then
                root.Anchored, root.CFrame = true, saved.Frame
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity, root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        for humanoid in pairs(self.HeldHumanoids or {}) do
            if humanoid.Parent then
                humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                humanoid.WalkSpeed, humanoid.AutoRotate, humanoid.Jump = 0, false, false
            end
        end
    end
    function manager:RestoreCamera()
        local saved, camera = self.SavedCamera, self.Camera
        if camera and saved then
            pcall(function()
                camera.CameraType, camera.CameraSubject = saved.Type, saved.Subject
                camera.CFrame, camera.Focus = saved.Frame, saved.Focus
            end)
        end
        self.Camera, self.SavedCamera = nil, nil
    end
    function manager:AttachCamera()
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if self.Camera == camera then return end
        self:RestoreCamera()
        if not camera then return end
        self.Camera = camera
        self.SavedCamera = { Type = camera.CameraType, Subject = camera.CameraSubject, Frame = camera.CFrame, Focus = camera.Focus }
        if not self.Position then
            local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            self.Position = camera.CFrame.Position
            self.Pitch, self.Yaw = math.asin(math.clamp(look.Y, -1, 1)), math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
        end
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
    end
    function manager:AcceptInput()
        return self.Enabled and self.Focused and not menuOpen() and not input:GetFocusedTextBox()
    end
    function manager:SetEnabled(enabled)
        if self.Enabled == enabled then return end
        self.Enabled, runtime.FreecamActive = enabled, enabled
        self.Generation += 1
        table.clear(self.Keys); self.Mouse, self.FireHeld = Vector2.zero, false
        if not enabled then
            actions:UnbindAction(moveAction); actions:UnbindAction(mouseAction); actions:UnbindAction(fireAction)
            self:ReleaseCharacter()
            self:RestoreCamera()
            if self.MouseSaved then
                input.MouseBehavior, input.MouseIconEnabled = self.MouseSaved.Behavior, self.MouseSaved.Icon
            end
            if self.Reserved then
                pcall(cameraService.Release, cameraService, "LokiFreecam")
                self.Reserved = false
            end
            self.Position, self.MouseSaved, self.StreamPosition = nil, nil, nil
            return
        end
        self.MouseSaved = { Behavior = input.MouseBehavior, Icon = input.MouseIconEnabled }
        self.NextStream = 0
        if cameraService then self.Reserved = pcall(cameraService.Reserve, cameraService, "LokiFreecam") end
        self:AttachCamera()
        self:HoldCharacter(true)
        actions:BindActionAtPriority(moveAction, function(_, state, event)
            if not self:AcceptInput() then table.clear(self.Keys); return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
            self.Keys[event.KeyCode] = state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin or state == Enum.UserInputState.Change
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
        end, false, Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 1,
            Enum.KeyCode.W, Enum.KeyCode.A, Enum.KeyCode.S, Enum.KeyCode.D, Enum.KeyCode.Space, Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift)
        actions:BindActionAtPriority(mouseAction, function(_, _, event)
            if not self:AcceptInput() then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
            self.Mouse += Vector2.new(-event.Delta.Y, -event.Delta.X)
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
        end, false, Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 1, Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
        actions:BindActionAtPriority(fireAction, function(_, state)
            self.FireHeld = self:AcceptInput() and (state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin or state == Enum.UserInputState.Change)
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
        end, false, Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 1, Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
    end
    function manager:StepShooting()
        if not self:AcceptInput() or not self.FireHeld or self.FireBusy or not runtime.FireFreecamShot then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now < (self.NextShot or 0) then return end
        self.NextShot, self.FireBusy = now + 0.05, true
        local generation = self.Generation
        local ok, reason = pcall(runtime.FireFreecamShot)
        self.FireBusy = false
        if not ok and generation == self.Generation then runtime:Report("Freecam shot", reason) end
    end
    function manager:Step(dt)
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        self:HoldCharacter()
        self:AttachCamera()
        local camera = self.Camera
        if not camera then return end
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        local active = self:AcceptInput()
        input.MouseBehavior = active and Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter or self.MouseSaved.Behavior
        input.MouseIconEnabled = not active and self.MouseSaved.Icon or false
        if active then
            -- Source controls: WASD, Space up, Left Shift down; speed is frame-rate independent.
            local key = Enum.KeyCode
            local function down(code) return self.Keys[code] and 1 or 0 end
            local movement = Vector3.new(down(key.D) - down(key.A), down(key.Space) - down(key.LeftShift), down(key.S) - down(key.W))
            if movement.Magnitude > 1 then movement = movement.Unit end
            local sensitivity = math.pi / 512
            self.Pitch = math.clamp(self.Pitch + self.Mouse.X * sensitivity, -math.rad(89.9), math.rad(89.9))
            self.Yaw = (self.Yaw + self.Mouse.Y * sensitivity) % (2 * math.pi)
            local orientation = CFrame.fromOrientation(self.Pitch, self.Yaw, 0)
            self.Position += orientation:VectorToWorldSpace(movement) * self.Speed * 60 * math.clamp(dt, 0, 0.1)
        else table.clear(self.Keys) end
        self.Mouse = Vector2.zero
        camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(self.Position) * CFrame.fromOrientation(self.Pitch, self.Yaw, 0)
        camera.Focus = camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -20)
        -- Keep remote scenery available without the source's nine requests every frame.
        local now = os.clock()
        if world.StreamingEnabled and not self.StreamBusy and now >= self.NextStream
            and (not self.StreamPosition or (self.Position - self.StreamPosition).Magnitude >= 128) then
            self.StreamBusy, self.NextStream, self.StreamPosition = true, now + 1, self.Position
            local position, generation = self.Position, self.Generation
            task.spawn(function()
                if self.Enabled and self.Generation == generation then pcall(player.RequestStreamAroundAsync, player, position) end
                self.StreamBusy = false
            end)
        end
    end
    function manager:Destroy()
        self:SetEnabled(false)
        runtime.FreecamActive = nil
    end
    runtime:Connect(input.WindowFocused, function() manager.Focused = true end)
    runtime:Connect(input.WindowFocusReleased, function()
        manager.Focused = false; table.clear(manager.Keys); manager.Mouse, manager.FireHeld = Vector2.zero, false
    end)
    return manager
end
-- END CAMERA / RAGE HELPERS

-- BEGIN ESP STYLE HELPERS
local function ESPFontIndex(font)
    return ({System = 0, UI = 1, Plex = 2, Monospace = 3})[font] or 3
end
local function CreateESPBox(drawing)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local style = { Corners = {}, CornerOutlines = {} }
    local function create(kind, zindex)
        local object = drawing.new(kind)
        object.Visible, object.ZIndex = false, zindex
        if kind == "Square" then object.Filled = false end
        return object
    end
    style.Outline = create("Square", 1)
    style.Box = create("Square", 2)
    for i = 1, 8 do
        style.CornerOutlines[i] = create("Line", 1)
        style.Corners[i] = create("Line", 2)
    end
    function style:Hide()
        self.Box.Visible, self.Outline.Visible = false, false
        for i = 1, 8 do
            self.Corners[i].Visible, self.CornerOutlines[i].Visible = false, false
        end
    end
    function style:Update(minimum, maximum, settings, color)
        self:Hide()
        if not settings.BoxEnabled then return end
        local size = maximum - minimum
        if size.X <= 0 or size.Y <= 0 then return end
        local thickness, opacity = settings.BoxThickness or 1, 1 - (settings.BoxTransparency or 0)
        local outlined = settings.BoxOutlineEnabled == true
        local outlineColor = settings.BoxOutlineColor or Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        if settings.BoxType == "Full Box" then
            for _, object in ipairs({self.Outline, self.Box}) do
                object.Position, object.Size, object.Transparency = minimum, size, opacity
            end
            self.Outline.Color, self.Outline.Thickness = outlineColor, thickness + 2
            self.Outline.Visible = outlined
            self.Box.Color, self.Box.Thickness, self.Box.Visible = color, thickness, true
            return
        end
        -- Short, balanced brackets at all four corners.
        local horizontal, vertical = math.min(size.X / 4, 18), math.min(size.Y / 5, 18)
        local anchors = {minimum, Vector2.new(maximum.X, minimum.Y),
            Vector2.new(minimum.X, maximum.Y), maximum}
        for corner, anchor in ipairs(anchors) do
            local x = (corner == 1 or corner == 3) and 1 or -1
            local y = corner <= 2 and 1 or -1
            for axis = 1, 2 do
                local index = (corner - 1) * 2 + axis
                local finish = anchor + (axis == 1 and Vector2.new(horizontal * x, 0) or Vector2.new(0, vertical * y))
                local line, outline = self.Corners[index], self.CornerOutlines[index]
                line.From, line.To, line.Color = anchor, finish, color
                line.Thickness, line.Transparency, line.Visible = thickness, opacity, true
                outline.From, outline.To, outline.Color = anchor, finish, outlineColor
                outline.Thickness, outline.Transparency, outline.Visible = thickness + 2, opacity, outlined
            end
        end
    end
    function style:Destroy()
        self.Box:Remove()
        self.Outline:Remove()
        for i = 1, 8 do self.Corners[i]:Remove(); self.CornerOutlines[i]:Remove() end
    end
    return style
end
-- A native UIGradient provides a spatial blend; Rotation moves it around the box.
local function CreateESPFill(drawing, instances, parent)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local fill = { Solid = drawing.new("Square") }
    fill.Solid.Visible, fill.Solid.Filled, fill.Solid.ZIndex = false, true, 0
    function fill:Hide()
        self.Solid.Visible = false
        if self.Frame then self.Frame.Visible = false end
    end
    function fill:Update(minimum, maximum, settings, now)
        self:Hide()
        local size = maximum - minimum
        if not settings.BoxEnabled or not settings.BoxFillEnabled or size.X <= 0 or size.Y <= 0 then return end
        if not settings.BoxFillAnimated then
            self.Solid.Position, self.Solid.Size = minimum, size
            self.Solid.Color, self.Solid.Transparency = settings.BoxFillColor, settings.BoxFillTransparency
            self.Solid.Visible = true
            return
        end
        if not self.Frame then
            self.Frame = instances.new("Frame")
            self.Frame.Name, self.Frame.BorderSizePixel = "LokiESPGradient", 0
            self.Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            self.Frame.Active, self.Frame.Visible = false, false
            self.Gradient = instances.new("UIGradient")
            self.Gradient.Parent = self.Frame
            self.Frame.Parent = parent
        end
        if self.Color1 ~= settings.BoxFillColor or self.Color2 ~= settings.BoxFillColor2 then
            self.Color1, self.Color2 = settings.BoxFillColor, settings.BoxFillColor2
            self.Gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(self.Color1, self.Color2)
        end
        self.Frame.Position, self.Frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(minimum.X, minimum.Y), UDim2.fromOffset(size.X, size.Y)
        -- Keep the existing fill opacity setting consistent between both renderers.
