        self.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - settings.BoxFillTransparency
        self.Gradient.Rotation = (now * 60) % 360
        self.Frame.Visible = true
    end
    function fill:Destroy()
        self.Solid:Remove()
        if self.Frame then self.Frame:Destroy(); self.Frame, self.Gradient = nil, nil end
    end
    return fill
end
-- END ESP STYLE HELPERS

-- BEGIN BARREL SNAPLINE HELPERS
local function GetSnaplineObjectFrame(obj)
    if not obj then return end
    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj:GetRenderCFrame() end
    local frame
    if obj:IsA("Bone") then
        frame = obj.TransformedWorldCFrame
    elseif obj:IsA("Attachment") then
        frame = obj.WorldCFrame
        local boneName = obj:GetAttribute("Bone")
        local bone = type(boneName) == "string" and obj.Parent and obj.Parent:FindFirstChild(boneName, true)
        if not bone or not bone:IsA("Bone") then bone = obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Bone") end
        if bone then frame = bone.TransformedWorldCFrame * bone.WorldCFrame:Inverse() * frame end
    end
    if not frame then return end
    -- WorldCFrame/Position omit the visual interpolation of moving world rigs.
    -- Apply that render delta to the marker, preserving its animation and muzzle offset.
    local root = obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if root then frame = root:GetRenderCFrame() * root.CFrame:Inverse() * frame end
    return frame
end
-- Clip against the actual world (including Terrain), independently of bullet hooks.
local function CreateMuzzleCache(resolve, getContext, clockNow)
    local cache = {}
    return function(preferRendered, preferWorld)
        local camera, viewmodel, character, item = getContext()
        if not camera then return end
        local key = (preferRendered and 1 or 0) + (preferWorld and 2 or 0)
        local saved, now = cache[key], clockNow()
        if saved and saved.Camera == camera and saved.Viewmodel == viewmodel and saved.Character == character
            and saved.Item == item and now < saved.Expires then
            if not saved.Marker then return end
            if saved.Marker.Parent then
                local frame = GetSnaplineObjectFrame(saved.Marker)
                if frame then return frame.Position, saved.Marker, saved.ThirdPerson end
            end
        end
        local position, marker, thirdPerson = resolve(preferRendered, preferWorld)
        cache[key] = { Camera = camera, Viewmodel = viewmodel, Character = character, Item = item,
            Marker = marker, ThirdPerson = thirdPerson, Expires = now + 0.25 }
        return position, marker, thirdPerson
    end
end
local function CreateSnaplineClipper(runtime, world, localPlayer, clockNow)
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType, params.IgnoreWater = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true
    clockNow = clockNow or os.clock
    local baseIgnore, nextRefresh, previousCamera, previousCharacter = {}, 0
    return function(origin, target, marker, targetPart)
        if not origin or not target then return end
        local delta = target - origin
        if delta.Magnitude < 0.001 then return target, false end
        local now = clockNow()
        if now >= nextRefresh or previousCamera ~= world.CurrentCamera or previousCharacter ~= localPlayer.Character then
            nextRefresh, previousCamera, previousCharacter = now + 0.25, world.CurrentCamera, localPlayer.Character
            table.clear(baseIgnore)
            local function remember(object)
                if object and object ~= world and object ~= world.Terrain then table.insert(baseIgnore, object) end
            end
            remember(world.CurrentCamera); remember(localPlayer.Character)
            for _, data in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do remember(data.Character); remember(data.WorldModel) end
        end
        local ignore = table.clone(baseIgnore)
        local function add(object) if object and object ~= world and object ~= world.Terrain then table.insert(ignore, object) end end
        if marker then
            add(marker:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model") or marker:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart"))
        end
        -- Exclude the target itself, not an arbitrary shared scene container.
        if targetPart then add(targetPart) end
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
        local hit = world:Raycast(origin, delta, params)
        if hit then
            -- Stop just before the surface so the beam cannot poke through it.
            local distance = math.max(0, (hit.Position - origin).Magnitude - 0.02)
            return origin + delta.Unit * distance, true
        end
        return target, false
    end
end
local function IsSnaplinePathClear(clipSegment, camera, origin, target, marker, targetPart)
    if not camera or not origin or not target then return false end
    local _, blocked = clipSegment(origin, target, marker, targetPart)
    if blocked then return false end
    -- A clear muzzle ray alone can still sit behind a hill in the camera's view.
    -- Hide the whole line when either its target or its body is occluded.
    for _, fraction in ipairs({0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1}) do
        local _, hidden = clipSegment(camera.CFrame.Position, origin:Lerp(target, fraction), marker, targetPart)
        if hidden then return false end
    end
    return true
end
local function UpdateBarrelSnapline(line, camera, origin, target, settings, crosshairPosition)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    if not settings.SnaplineEnabled or not camera or not target then line.Visible = false; return end
    if not ProjectFovPoint(camera, target, settings.FovSize) then line.Visible = false; return end
    local finish = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(target)
    local mode = settings.SnaplineOrigin or "Gun Barrel"
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local start
    if mode == "Crosshair" then
        start = crosshairPosition or Vector2.new(viewport.X * 0.5, viewport.Y * 0.5)
    elseif mode == "Bottom of Screen" then
        start = Vector2.new(viewport.X * 0.5, viewport.Y)
    elseif mode == "Top of Screen" then
        start = Vector2.new(viewport.X * 0.5, 0)
    else
        if not origin then line.Visible = false; return end
        local point = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(origin)
        -- Viewmodel hands/muzzles can straddle the camera plane during ADS/recoil.
        -- Clip the real world segment, then its screen projection; never substitute the camera center.
        local near = math.max(0.01, math.abs(camera.NearPlaneZ or -0.1))
        if point.Z < near then
            if finish.Z <= near then line.Visible = false; return end
            origin = origin:Lerp(target, (near - point.Z) / (finish.Z - point.Z))
            point = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(origin)
        end
        local dx, dy = finish.X - point.X, finish.Y - point.Y
        local enter, leave = 0, 1
        local function clip(p, q)
            if p == 0 then return q >= 0 end
            local t = q / p
            if p < 0 then enter = math.max(enter, t) else leave = math.min(leave, t) end
            return enter <= leave
        end
        if not (clip(-dx, point.X) and clip(dx, viewport.X - point.X)
            and clip(-dy, point.Y) and clip(dy, viewport.Y - point.Y)) then line.Visible = false; return end
        start = Vector2.new(point.X + dx * enter, point.Y + dy * enter)
    end
    local endpoint = Vector2.new(finish.X, finish.Y)
    local thickness = (settings.SnaplineThickness or 1) + (settings.SnaplineBloom or 0) * 3
    -- Screen-edge origins extend outside the circle; their target still has to be inside it.
    if mode ~= "Top of Screen" and mode ~= "Bottom of Screen" then
        start, endpoint = ClipLineToFov(start, endpoint, GetFovCenter(camera),
            (settings.FovSize or 120) - thickness * 0.5)
        if not start then line.Visible = false; return end
    end
    line.From, line.To = start, endpoint
    line.Color = settings.SnaplineColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    line.Thickness = thickness
    line.Transparency = settings.SnaplineTransparency or 1
    line.Visible = true
end
local function CreateAttachedGunSnapline()
    local manager = {}
    function manager:Hide()
        if self.Overlay then self.Overlay.Visible = false end
    end
    function manager:Destroy()
        if self.Overlay then self.Overlay:Remove(); self.Overlay = nil end
    end
    function manager:Update(marker, target, camera, settings, targetPoint)
        if not settings.SnaplineEnabled or not camera then self:Hide(); return end
        local originFrame, targetFrame = GetSnaplineObjectFrame(marker), GetSnaplineObjectFrame(target)
        if not originFrame or not targetFrame then self:Hide(); return end
        -- Project the current rendered muzzle each frame so the whole line can be
        -- clipped to the screen-space FOV, including in first person and ADS.
        if not self.Overlay then self.Overlay = Drawing.new("Line"); self.Overlay.ZIndex = 20 end
        UpdateBarrelSnapline(self.Overlay, camera, originFrame.Position, targetPoint or targetFrame.Position, settings)
    end
    return manager
end
-- END BARREL SNAPLINE HELPERS

-- BEGIN FEATURE ENGINE
Runtime:Init("Player / vehicle ESP engine", function()
    local flags = getgenv().flags
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    local function get_entity_list()
        return Runtime:GetEntities()
    end

    local function IsTeammate(player)
        if not player then return true end
        if player == LocalPlayer then return true end

        local success1, mySquad = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        local success2, theirSquad = pcall(function() return player:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        if success1 and success2 and mySquad and theirSquad and mySquad == theirSquad then
            return true
        end

        local successRank1, myRank = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        local successRank2, theirRank = pcall(function() return player:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        if successRank1 and successRank2 and myRank and theirRank and myRank > 0 and theirRank > 0 then
            return true
        end
        
        return false
    end

    local function GetLerpColor(color1, color2)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local t = (math.sin(tick() * 3) + 1) / 2
        return color1:Lerp(color2, t)
    end

    local function GetWaveColor(color1, color2, index, speed, frequency)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        speed = speed or 4
        frequency = frequency or 0.4
        local t = (math.sin(tick() * speed - (index * frequency)) + 1) / 2
        return color1:Lerp(color2, t)
    end

    local Cache = {}

    local vehicleESPState = {
        Active = {},
        WorldModelCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "v"}),
    }

    local SkeletonBones = {
        {"Head", "UpperTorso"},
        {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
        {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"},
        {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
        {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
        {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
        {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"},
        {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
        {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"},
        {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
        {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
        {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
        {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"},
        {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
    }

    local function Init(ent)
        local boxStyle = CreateESPBox(Drawing)

        local boxFill = CreateESPFill(Drawing, Instance, Vitality.Holder.Instance)

        local name = Drawing.new("Text")
        name.Visible = false
        name.Center = true
        name.Outline = true
        name.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        name.Font = ESPFontIndex(flags.ESPFont)
        name.Size = flags.NameSize
        name.Color = flags.NameColor
        name.Transparency = 1 - flags.NameTransparency

        local dist = Drawing.new("Text")
        dist.Visible = false
        dist.Center = true
        dist.Outline = true
        dist.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        dist.Font = ESPFontIndex(flags.ESPFont)
        dist.Size = flags.DistanceSize
        dist.Color = flags.DistanceColor
        dist.Transparency = 1 - flags.DistanceTransparency

        local weapon = Drawing.new("Text")
        weapon.Visible = false
        weapon.Center, weapon.Outline, weapon.Font = true, true, ESPFontIndex(flags.ESPFont)
        weapon.Size, weapon.Color = 13, flags.NameColor

        local skeleton = {}
        for i = 1, #SkeletonBones do
            local boneLine = Drawing.new("Line")
            boneLine.Visible = false
            boneLine.Thickness = flags.SkeletonThickness
            boneLine.Color = flags.SkeletonColor
            table.insert(skeleton, boneLine)
        end

        Cache[ent] = {
            BoxStyle = boxStyle,
            BoxFill = boxFill,
            Name = name,
            Dist = dist,
            Weapon = weapon,
            WeaponChars = {},
            WeaponNext = 0,
            Skeleton = skeleton,
            NameChars = {},
            DistChars = {}
        }
    end

    local function createVehicleBillboard(targetPart)
        if vehicleESPState.Active[targetPart] then
            return vehicleESPState.Active[targetPart]
        end

        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "VehicleESP_Main"
        billboard.Adornee = targetPart
        billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 90)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
        billboard.Enabled = false

        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.Name = "VehicleNameText"
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = flags.VehicleNameColor
        nameLabel.TextSize = flags.VehicleNameSize
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        nameLabel.Text = ""
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        nameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        nameLabel.Visible = false
        nameLabel.Parent = billboard

        local distLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        distLabel.Name = "VehicleDistText"
        distLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        distLabel.TextColor3 = flags.VehicleDistanceColor
        distLabel.TextSize = flags.VehicleDistanceSize
        distLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        distLabel.Text = ""
        distLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        distLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20)
        distLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        distLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        distLabel.Visible = false
        distLabel.Parent = billboard

        local healthLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        healthLabel.Name = "VehicleHealthText"
        healthLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        healthLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        healthLabel.TextSize = 13
        healthLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        healthLabel.Text = ""
        healthLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        healthLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 40)
        healthLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        healthLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        healthLabel.Visible = false
        healthLabel.Parent = billboard

        billboard.Parent = targetPart

        local data = {
            billboard = billboard,
            nameLabel = nameLabel,
            distLabel = distLabel,
            healthLabel = healthLabel,

            NameChars = {},
            DistChars = {}
        }

        vehicleESPState.Active[targetPart] = data

        return data
    end

    local healthMatchGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.75)
    local healthWorldModels = {}
    local nextHealthMatch = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    vehicleESPState.WorldModelCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function getVehicleHealth(targetPart)
        local cached = vehicleESPState.WorldModelCache[targetPart]
        if cached and cached.Parent then
            local states = cached:FindFirstChild("States")
            local health = states and states:GetAttribute("Health")
            if type(health) == "number" then return health end
        end
        vehicleESPState.WorldModelCache[targetPart] = nil
        local now = os.clock()
        if now < (nextHealthMatch[targetPart] or -math.huge) then return nil end
        nextHealthMatch[targetPart] = now + 0.75
        if healthMatchGate:Ready(now) then
            table.clear(healthWorldModels)
            for _, model in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if model.Name == "WorldModel" and model:IsA("Model") and model:FindFirstChild("States") then
                    table.insert(healthWorldModels, model)
                end
            end
        end
        local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, 150
        for _, model in ipairs(healthWorldModels) do
            if model.Parent then
                local frame = model:GetBoundingBox()
                local distance = (frame.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude
                if distance < nearestDistance then nearest, nearestDistance = model, distance end
            end
        end
        if nearest then
            vehicleESPState.WorldModelCache[targetPart] = nearest
            return nearest:FindFirstChild("States"):GetAttribute("Health")
        end
        return nil
    end


    local playerCacheGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.25)
    local playerEntitySet = {}
    local playerESPVisible = false
    local function hidePlayerData(data)
        data.BoxStyle:Hide()
        data.BoxFill:Hide()
        data.Name.Visible, data.Dist.Visible, data.Weapon.Visible = false, false, false
        for _, field in ipairs({ "NameChars", "DistChars", "WeaponChars", "Skeleton" }) do
            for _, drawing in pairs(data[field]) do drawing.Visible = false end
        end
    end
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not flags.ESPEnabled then
            if playerESPVisible then
                for _, data in pairs(Cache) do hidePlayerData(data) end
                playerESPVisible = false
                playerCacheGate.Next = -math.huge
            end
            return
        end
        Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not Camera then
            if playerESPVisible then for _, data in pairs(Cache) do hidePlayerData(data) end end
            playerESPVisible = false
            return
        end
        playerESPVisible = true
        local font = ESPFontIndex(flags.ESPFont)
        local fontWidth = font == 3 and 0.6 or 0.55
        if playerCacheGate:Ready(os.clock()) then
            table.clear(playerEntitySet)
            for _, ent in pairs(get_entity_list() or {}) do
                if ent and ent.Player and ent.Player ~= LocalPlayer then
                    playerEntitySet[ent] = true
                    if not Cache[ent] then Init(ent) end
                end
            end
        end
        do
            local entitySet = playerEntitySet

            for ent, data in pairs(Cache) do
                data.Name.Font, data.Dist.Font, data.Weapon.Font = font, font, font
                data.Weapon.Visible = false
                for _, character in pairs(data.WeaponChars) do character.Visible = false end
                if not entitySet[ent] then
                    data.BoxStyle:Destroy()
                    data.BoxFill:Destroy()
                    data.Name:Remove()
                    data.Dist:Remove()
                    data.Weapon:Remove()
                    for _, character in pairs(data.WeaponChars) do character:Remove() end
                    for _, cObj in pairs(data.NameChars) do cObj:Remove() end
                    for _, cObj in pairs(data.DistChars) do cObj:Remove() end
                    for _, s in pairs(data.Skeleton) do s:Remove() end
                    Cache[ent] = nil
                    continue
                end

                local model = ent.WorldModel
                local player = ent.Player

                if not player or player == LocalPlayer or not flags.ESPEnabled or not model or not model.Parent then
                    hidePlayerData(data)
                    continue
                end

                if flags.teamcheck_esp and IsTeammate(player) then
                    hidePlayerData(data)
                    continue
                end

                local root = model:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') or ent.RootPart
                if not root then
                    hidePlayerData(data)
                    continue
                end

                local cf, size = GetPlayerESPBounds(model, Runtime.HitboxExpander)
                local pts = {}

                for x = -1, 1, 2 do
                    for y = -1, 1, 2 do
                        for z = -1, 1, 2 do
                            local w = cf.Position
                                + cf.RightVector * size.X / 2 * x
                                + cf.UpVector * size.Y / 2 * y
                                + cf.LookVector * size.Z / 2 * z

                            local v, ok = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(w)
                            if ok then
                                pts[#pts + 1] = Vector2.new(v.X, v.Y)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if #pts < 2 then
                    hidePlayerData(data)
                    continue
                end

                local min, max = pts[1], pts[1]
                for _, p in pairs(pts) do
                    min = Vector2.new(math.min(min.X, p.X), math.min(min.Y, p.Y))
                    max = Vector2.new(math.max(max.X, p.X), math.max(max.Y, p.Y))
                end

                min = Vector2.new(math.floor(min.X + 0.5), math.floor(min.Y + 0.5))
                max = Vector2.new(math.floor(max.X + 0.5), math.floor(max.Y + 0.5))
                local boxColor = flags.BoxAnimated and GetLerpColor(flags.BoxColor, flags.BoxColor2) or flags.BoxColor

                data.BoxFill:Update(min, max, flags, os.clock())

                data.BoxStyle:Update(min, max, flags, boxColor)

                local topWorldPos = cf.Position + Vector3.new(0, size.Y / 2 + 0.5, 0)
                local bottomWorldPos = cf.Position - Vector3.new(0, size.Y / 2 + 0.5, 0)
                
                local topVec, topVis = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(topWorldPos)
                local botVec, botVis = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(bottomWorldPos)

                if flags.NameEnabled and topVis then
                    local textStr = ('[%s]'):format(player.Name)
                    if flags.NameAnimated then
                        data.Name.Visible = false
                        local charWidth = flags.NameSize * fontWidth
                        local totalWidth = #textStr * charWidth
                        local startX = topVec.X - (totalWidth / 2)

                        for i = 1, #textStr do
                            local charObj = data.NameChars[i]
                            if not charObj then
                                charObj = Drawing.new("Text")
                                charObj.Center = false
                                charObj.Outline = true
                                charObj.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                charObj.Font = font
                                data.NameChars[i] = charObj
                            end
                            charObj.Font = font
                            charObj.Text = textStr:sub(i, i)
                            charObj.Size = flags.NameSize
                            charObj.Transparency = 1 - flags.NameTransparency
                            charObj.Color = GetWaveColor(flags.NameColor, flags.NameColor2, i, 5, 0.4)
                            charObj.Position = Vector2.new(startX + ((i - 1) * charWidth), topVec.Y - 16)
                            charObj.Visible = true
                        end
                        for i = #textStr + 1, #data.NameChars do
                            if data.NameChars[i] then data.NameChars[i].Visible = false end
                        end
                    else
                        for _, cObj in pairs(data.NameChars) do cObj.Visible = false end
                        data.Name.Position = Vector2.new(topVec.X, topVec.Y - 16)
                        data.Name.Text = textStr
                        data.Name.Color = flags.NameColor
                        data.Name.Size = flags.NameSize
                        data.Name.Transparency = 1 - flags.NameTransparency
                        data.Name.Visible = true
                    end
                else
                    data.Name.Visible = false
                    for _, cObj in pairs(data.NameChars) do cObj.Visible = false end
                end

                if flags.DistanceEnabled and botVis then
                    local distStr = ('[%d M]'):format(math.floor((Camera.CFrame.Position - root.Position).Magnitude / 3.5461))
                    if flags.DistanceAnimated then
                        data.Dist.Visible = false
                        local charWidth = flags.DistanceSize * fontWidth
                        local totalWidth = #distStr * charWidth
                        local startX = botVec.X - (totalWidth / 2)

                        for i = 1, #distStr do
                            local charObj = data.DistChars[i]
                            if not charObj then
                                charObj = Drawing.new("Text")
                                charObj.Center = false
                                charObj.Outline = true
                                charObj.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                charObj.Font = font
                                data.DistChars[i] = charObj
                            end
                            charObj.Font = font
                            charObj.Text = distStr:sub(i, i)
                            charObj.Size = flags.DistanceSize
                            charObj.Transparency = 1 - flags.DistanceTransparency
                            charObj.Color = GetWaveColor(flags.DistanceColor, flags.DistanceColor2, i, 5, 0.4)
                            charObj.Position = Vector2.new(startX + ((i - 1) * charWidth), botVec.Y + 4)
                            charObj.Visible = true
                        end
                        for i = #distStr + 1, #data.DistChars do
                            if data.DistChars[i] then data.DistChars[i].Visible = false end
                        end
                    else
                        for _, cObj in pairs(data.DistChars) do cObj.Visible = false end
                        data.Dist.Position = Vector2.new(botVec.X, botVec.Y + 4)
                        data.Dist.Text = distStr
                        data.Dist.Color = flags.DistanceColor
                        data.Dist.Size = flags.DistanceSize
                        data.Dist.Transparency = 1 - flags.DistanceTransparency
                        data.Dist.Visible = true
                    end
                else
                    data.Dist.Visible = false
                    for _, cObj in pairs(data.DistChars) do cObj.Visible = false end
                end

                if flags.WeaponEnabled and botVis then
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now >= data.WeaponNext then
                        data.Weapon.Text = ReadIndicatorGun(player, ent)
                        data.WeaponNext = now + 0.2
                    end
                    local offset = flags.DistanceEnabled and (flags.DistanceSize + 8) or 4
                    if flags.WeaponAnimated then
                        local text = data.Weapon.Text or ""
                        local charWidth = 13 * fontWidth
                        local startX = botVec.X - #text * charWidth / 2
                        for i = 1, #text do
                            local character = data.WeaponChars[i]
                            if not character then
                                character = Drawing.new("Text")
                                character.Center, character.Outline, character.Font = false, true, font
                                character.Size = 13
                                data.WeaponChars[i] = character
                            end
                            character.Font = font
                            character.Text = text:sub(i, i)
                            character.Color = GetWaveColor(flags.WeaponColor, flags.WeaponColor2, i, 5, 0.4)
                            character.Position = Vector2.new(startX + (i - 1) * charWidth, botVec.Y + offset)
                            character.Visible = true
                        end
                    else
                        data.Weapon.Position = Vector2.new(botVec.X, botVec.Y + offset)
                        data.Weapon.Color = flags.WeaponColor
                        data.Weapon.Visible = true
                    end
                end

                if flags.SkeletonEnabled then
                    for i, bone in ipairs(SkeletonBones) do
                        local partA = model:FindFirstChild(bone[1])
                        local partB = model:FindFirstChild(bone[2])
                        local line = data.Skeleton[i]

                        if partA and partB then
                            local posA, visA = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(partA.Position)
                            local posB, visB = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(partB.Position)

                            if visA and visB then
                                line.From = Vector2.new(posA.X, posA.Y)
                                line.To = Vector2.new(posB.X, posB.Y)
                                line.Color = flags.SkeletonColor
                                line.Thickness = flags.SkeletonThickness
                                line.Transparency = 1 - flags.SkeletonTransparency
                                line.Visible = true
                            else
                                line.Visible = false
                            end
                        else
                            line.Visible = false
                        end
                    end
                else
                    for _, line in pairs(data.Skeleton) do line.Visible = false end
                end
            end
        end
    end)


    local vehicleScanTimer = 0
    local vehicleScanInterval = 0.75

    local function rememberVehicle(part)
        if part.Name == "Chassis" and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Parent and part.Parent:IsA("Model") then
            createVehicleBillboard(part)
        end
    end
    Runtime.RefreshVehicleESP = function()
        if not flags.VehicleESPEnabled then return end
        -- One initial/enable scan includes nested containers; streaming uses events.
        for _, part in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if part.Name == "Chassis" then rememberVehicle(part) end
        end
    end
    Runtime:Connect(Workspace.DescendantAdded, function(part)
        if flags.VehicleESPEnabled and part.Name == "Chassis" then rememberVehicle(part) end
    end)
    if flags.VehicleESPEnabled then Runtime.RefreshVehicleESP() end

    local function hideVehicleData(data)
        if not data then
            return
        end

        if data.billboard then
            data.billboard.Enabled = false
        end

        if data.nameLabel then
            data.nameLabel.Visible = false
        end

        if data.distLabel then
            data.distLabel.Visible = false
        end

        if data.healthLabel then
            data.healthLabel.Visible = false
        end

        for _, charObj in pairs(data.NameChars or {}) do
            charObj.Visible = false
        end

        for _, charObj in pairs(data.DistChars or {}) do
            charObj.Visible = false
        end
    end

    local function cleanupVehicles()
        for chassis, data in pairs(vehicleESPState.Active) do
            if not chassis or not chassis.Parent then

                if data then
                    if data.billboard then
                        pcall(function()
                            data.billboard:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end

                    for _, charObj in pairs(data.NameChars or {}) do
                        pcall(function()
                            charObj:Remove()
                        end)
                    end

                    for _, charObj in pairs(data.DistChars or {}) do
                        pcall(function()
                            charObj:Remove()
                        end)
                    end
                end

                vehicleESPState.Active[chassis] = nil
            end
        end

        for key, worldModel in pairs(vehicleESPState.WorldModelCache) do
            if not worldModel or not worldModel.Parent then
                vehicleESPState.WorldModelCache[key] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function updateVehicleESP()
        Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera or Camera

        if not Camera then return end
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local origin

        if root then
            origin = root.Position
        else
            origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
        end

        for targetPart, data in pairs(vehicleESPState.Active) do

            if not targetPart or not targetPart.Parent then
                hideVehicleData(data)
                continue
            end

            local distance = (targetPart.Position - origin).Magnitude

            if not flags.VehicleESPEnabled or distance > (flags.VehicleRenderDistance or 10000) then
                hideVehicleData(data)
                continue
            end

            local displayDist = distance / 3.5461
            local hasContent = false
            
            local viewportPosition, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            
            if not onScreen then
                hideVehicleData(data)
                continue
            end

            local currentYOffset = 0
            local defaultSize = 14

            if flags.VehicleNameEnabled then
                local textStr = "[Vehicle]"

                for _, charObj in pairs(data.NameChars) do
                    charObj.Visible = false
                end

                data.nameLabel.Text = textStr
                data.nameLabel.TextColor3 = flags.VehicleNameColor
                data.nameLabel.TextSize = flags.VehicleNameSize or defaultSize
                data.nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentYOffset)
                data.nameLabel.Visible = true

                currentYOffset = currentYOffset + 15
                hasContent = true
            else
                data.nameLabel.Visible = false
                for _, charObj in pairs(data.NameChars) do
                    charObj.Visible = false
                end
            end

            if flags.VehicleDistanceEnabled then
                local distStr = string.format("[%d M]", math.floor(displayDist))

                for _, charObj in pairs(data.DistChars) do
                    charObj.Visible = false
                end

                data.distLabel.Text = distStr
                data.distLabel.TextColor3 = flags.VehicleDistanceColor
                data.distLabel.TextSize = flags.VehicleDistanceSize or defaultSize
                data.distLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentYOffset)
                data.distLabel.Visible = true

                currentYOffset = currentYOffset + 15
                hasContent = true
            else
                data.distLabel.Visible = false
                for _, charObj in pairs(data.DistChars) do
                    charObj.Visible = false
                end
            end

            if flags.VehicleHealthEnabled then
                local health = getVehicleHealth(targetPart)

                if health then
                    data.healthLabel.Text = string.format("HP: %.0f", health)
                    data.healthLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
                else
                    data.healthLabel.Text = "HP: N/A"
                    data.healthLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                end

                data.healthLabel.TextSize = 13
                data.healthLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, currentYOffset)
                data.healthLabel.Visible = true

                hasContent = true
            else
                data.healthLabel.Visible = false
            end

            data.billboard.Enabled = hasContent
        end
    end

    local vehicleLabelGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.1)
    local vehicleESPVisible = false
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not flags.VehicleESPEnabled then
            if vehicleESPVisible then
                for _, data in pairs(vehicleESPState.Active) do hideVehicleData(data) end
                vehicleESPVisible = false
            end
            return
        end
        vehicleESPVisible = true
        vehicleScanTimer += dt
        if vehicleScanTimer >= vehicleScanInterval then
            cleanupVehicles()
            vehicleScanTimer = 0
        end
        if vehicleLabelGate:Ready(os.clock()) then updateVehicleESP() end
    end)
end)
Runtime:Init("Player ESP controls", function()
    GroupBoxes.PlayerESP:AddToggle("WeaponEnabled", {
        Text = "Equipped Weapon", Default = flags.WeaponEnabled,
        Callback = function(value) flags.WeaponEnabled = value end,
    }):AddColorPicker("WeaponColor", {
        Title = "Weapon Color", Default = flags.WeaponColor,
        Callback = function(value) flags.WeaponColor = value end,
    }):AddColorPicker("WeaponColor2", {

Runtime:Init("Grave ESP", function()
    Players = Players or game:GetService("Players")
    LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    RunService = game:GetService('RunService')
    
    graveESP = graveESP or {
        Enabled = false,
        ShowName = false,
        ShowDistance = false,
        Color1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251),
        Size = 14,
        RenderDistance = 10000,
        Active = {},
        Font = 2,
    }
    
    local Functions = {}
    do
        function Functions:FadeOutOnDist(drawingsList, distance, maxDistance)
            local transparency = math.max(0.1, 1 - (distance / maxDistance))
            for _, obj in ipairs(drawingsList) do
                obj.Transparency = transparency
            end
        end
    end

    local function getPrimaryPart(model)
        if model.PrimaryPart then
            return model.PrimaryPart
        end
        for _, part in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                return part
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getGravePlayerName(model)
        local nameLower = model.Name:lower()
        if nameLower:find("zombie") or nameLower:find("npc") or nameLower:find("dummy") then
            return nil
        end

        if model:GetAttribute("OwnerName") then
            return model:GetAttribute("OwnerName")
        end
        if model:GetAttribute("DisplayName") then
            return model:GetAttribute("DisplayName")
        end
        
        for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            local descNameLower = descendant.Name:lower()
            if descNameLower:find("zombie") or descNameLower:find("npc") then
                return nil
            end

            if descendant:GetAttribute("DisplayName") then
                return descendant:GetAttribute("DisplayName")
            end
            if descendant:GetAttribute("OwnerName") then
                return descendant:GetAttribute("OwnerName")
            end
            if descendant:IsA("StringValue") and (descendant.Name == "Owner" or descendant.Name == "Player") then
                return descendant.Value
            end
        end

        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if model.Name == player.Name or model.Name == player.DisplayName then
                return player.Name
            end
        end

        if model.Name == "Default" or model.Name == "Model" or model.Name == "PlayerGrave" then
            return nil
        end

        return model.Name
    end

    local function cleanupGraveData(data)
        if data and data.charDrawings then
            for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do
                pcall(function() obj:Remove() end)
            end
            data.charDrawings = {}
        end
    end

    local function createGraveESP(model)
        if graveESP.Active[model] then return end
        
        local playerName = getGravePlayerName(model)
        if not playerName then return end

        local part = getPrimaryPart(model)
        if not part then return end

        graveESP.Active[model] = {
            model = model,
            part = part,
            playerName = playerName,
            charDrawings = {},
            lastText = ""
        }
    end
    
    local function scanGraves()
        if not graveESP.Enabled then return end
        
        local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("WorldModels") and workspace.WorldModels:FindFirstChild("PlayerGraves")
        if folder then
            local children = folder:GetChildren()
            for i = 1, #children do
                local model = children[i]
                if model:IsA("Model") and not graveESP.Active[model] then
                    createGraveESP(model)
                end
            end
        end
        
        local children = workspace:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #children do
            local obj = children[i]
            if obj:IsA("Model") and (obj.Name:find("Grave") or obj:FindFirstChild("PlayerGrave")) and not graveESP.Active[obj] then
                createGraveESP(obj)
            end
        end
    end
    
    local function updateGraveESP()
        Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera or Camera
        if not Camera then return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local InitializerPos = (char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position) or Camera.CFrame.Position
        
        for model, data in pairs(graveESP.Active) do
            local part = data.part
            
            if not model or not model.Parent or not part or not part.Parent or not model:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                cleanupGraveData(data)
                graveESP.Active[model] = nil
            else
                local dist = (part.Position - InitializerPos).Magnitude
                local displayDist = dist / 2.56
                
                if graveESP.Enabled and dist <= graveESP.RenderDistance then
                    local targetWorldPos = part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
                    local pos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetWorldPos)
                    
                    if onScreen then
                        local text = ''
                        if graveESP.ShowName or graveESP.ShowDistance then
                            if graveESP.ShowName then
                                text = data.playerName
                            end
                            if graveESP.ShowDistance then
                                local distText = string.format('[%.0fm]', displayDist)
                                text = (text ~= '' and (text .. ' ' .. distText)) or distText
                            end
                        end
                        
                        if text == '' then
                            for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do obj.Visible = false end
                        else
                            if text ~= data.lastText then
                                cleanupGraveData(data)
                                data.lastText = text
                                for i = 1, #text do
                                    local charObj = Drawing.new("Text")
                                    charObj.Visible = false
                                    charObj.Center = true 
                                    charObj.Outline = true
                                    charObj.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                    charObj.Size = graveESP.Size
                                    charObj.Font = graveESP.Font
                                    table.insert(data.charDrawings, charObj)
                                end
                            end
                            
                            local charSpacing = graveESP.Size * 0.55
                            local totalWidth = #text * charSpacing
                            local startX = pos.X - (totalWidth / 2) + (charSpacing / 2)
                            local startY = pos.Y
                            
                                
                            for i, charObj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do
                                charObj.Visible = graveESP.Enabled
                                charObj.Size = graveESP.Size
                                charObj.Font = graveESP.Font
                                charObj.Text = text:sub(i, i)
                                charObj.Position = Vector2.new(startX + ((i - 1) * charSpacing), startY)
                            charObj.Color = graveESP.Color1
                            end
                            
                            Functions:FadeOutOnDist(data.charDrawings, dist, graveESP.RenderDistance)
                        end
                    else
                        for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do obj.Visible = false end
                    end
                else
                    for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do obj.Visible = false end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    local graveScanInterval = 1.0
    local lastGraveScan = 0
    
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        local now = os.clock()
        if graveESP.Enabled and (graveESP.ShowName or graveESP.ShowDistance) then
            if now - lastGraveScan >= graveScanInterval then
                scanGraves()
                lastGraveScan = now
            end
            updateGraveESP()
        else
            for _, data in pairs(graveESP.Active) do
                for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings or {}) do
                    obj.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    GroupBoxes.Grave:AddToggle("GraveNameToggle", {
        Text = 'Grave Name',    
        Default = false,    
        Callback = function(state)
            graveESP.ShowName = state
            graveESP.Enabled = graveESP.ShowName or graveESP.ShowDistance
            if graveESP.Enabled then
                task.defer(scanGraves)
            else
                for _, data in pairs(graveESP.Active) do
                    cleanupGraveData(data)
                end
            end
        end,    
    }):AddColorPicker("GraveESPColor1", {
        Title = 'Grave Color 1',    
        Default = graveESP.Color1,    
        Callback = function(value)
            graveESP.Color1 = value
        end,    
    })


    GroupBoxes.Grave:AddToggle('GraveDistanceToggle', {
        Text = 'Grave Distance',    
        Default = false,    
        Callback = function(state)
            graveESP.ShowDistance = state
            graveESP.Enabled = graveESP.ShowName or graveESP.ShowDistance
            if graveESP.Enabled then
                task.defer(scanGraves)
            else
                for _, data in pairs(graveESP.Active) do
                    cleanupGraveData(data)
                end
            end
        end,    
    })

    GroupBoxes.Grave:AddSlider('GraveESPRenderDistance', {
        Text = 'Grave ESP Distance',    
        Min = 50,    
        Max = 10000,    
        Default = graveESP.RenderDistance,    
        Rounding = 0,    
        Callback = function(value)
            graveESP.RenderDistance = value
        end,    
    })
end)

    local function get_entity_list()
        return Runtime:GetEntities()
    end

    local fovcircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
    fovcircle.Visible = false

    fovcircle.NumSides = 100
    fovcircle.Radius = flags.FovSize or 120
    fovcircle.Position = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
    local overlayParent
    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local ok, result = pcall(gethui)
        if ok then overlayParent = result end

        overlayParent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or game:GetService("CoreGui")

    snapline.Visible = false
    snapline.Color = flags.SnaplineColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    snapline.Thickness = flags.SnaplineThickness or 1

    local ConfigService = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("CustomCharacter", 10):WaitForChild("ConfigService", 10))

    local function get_current_gun(plr)
        return plr and ReadIndicatorGun(plr) or "Fists"
    end

    local function IsTeammate(player)
        if not player then return true end
        if player == LocalPlayer then return true end

        local success1, mySquad = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        local success2, theirSquad = pcall(function() return player:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        if success1 and success2 and mySquad and theirSquad and mySquad == theirSquad then
            return true
        end

        local successRank1, myRank = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        local successRank2, theirRank = pcall(function() return player:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        if successRank1 and successRank2 and myRank and theirRank and myRank > 0 and theirRank > 0 then
            return true
        end
        
        return false
    end

    local function predict_priv9(origin, pos, vel, speed, drop)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local dist = (origin - pos).Magnitude
        local t = dist / speed
        return pos + (vel * t) + Vector3.yAxis * (drop * t * t)
    end

    local instantReports = CreateInstantReports()
    local instantCooldown = CreateInstantCooldown()
    instantCooldown:SetEnabled(flags.instant_bullet_cooldown)
    local instantIndicator = CreateInstantCooldownIndicator(Runtime, Drawing, Workspace, flags, instantCooldown, os.clock)
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function() instantIndicator:Update() end)
    local chromaPrediction = CreateChromaPredictor(os.clock)
    local function full_prediction_silent(target_position, target_collider, shotOrigin, entity, instantPolicy)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not target_position then return end

        local currentgun = get_current_gun(LocalPlayer)
        local gun = aftermath.gundata:FindFirstChild(currentgun)
        local stats = gun and gun:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local bullet_settings = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("BulletSettings")

        local proj_speed, proj_drop

        if bullet_settings then
            local bullet_speed = bullet_settings:FindFirstChild("BulletSpeed")
            local bullet_gravity = bullet_settings:FindFirstChild("BulletGravity")
            proj_speed = tonumber(bullet_speed and bullet_speed.Value) or tonumber(aftermath.sv_config.sv_default_bullet_speed.Value) or 1500
            local gravityValue = bullet_gravity and bullet_gravity.Value
            local defaultGravity = tonumber(aftermath.sv_config.sv_default_bullet_gravity.Value) or 0
            if type(gravityValue) == "boolean" then
                proj_drop = gravityValue and defaultGravity or 0
            else proj_drop = tonumber(gravityValue) or defaultGravity end
        else
            proj_speed = tonumber(aftermath.sv_config.sv_default_bullet_speed.Value) or 1500
            proj_drop = tonumber(aftermath.sv_config.sv_default_bullet_gravity.Value) or 0
        end

        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local origin = shotOrigin or camera and camera.CFrame.Position
        if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then return target_position end
        local velocity = target_collider and target_collider.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local player, character = entity and entity.Player, entity and entity.Character
        if player then
            local car = player:FindFirstChild("Car")
            local chassis = car and car.Value and car.Value:FindFirstChild("Chassis")
            local charData = player:FindFirstChild("CharData")
            local charVelocity = charData and charData:FindFirstChild("Velocity")
            velocity = chassis and chassis.AssemblyLinearVelocity or charVelocity and charVelocity.Value or velocity
        end
        if flags.ResolveY and typeof(velocity) == "Vector3" then velocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z) end
        local pingOK, ping = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() end)
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        local airborne = humanoid and humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air or false
        return chromaPrediction(player or target_collider, origin, target_position, target_collider and target_collider.Position,
            velocity, proj_speed, proj_drop, pingOK and ping or 0, airborne, Workspace.Gravity,
            flags.instant_bullet and instantReports:CanInstant(os.clock())
                and (instantPolicy == true or (instantPolicy == nil and instantCooldown:CanInstant(os.clock()))))
    end

    -- BEGIN SNAPLINE SERVICES
    -- World weapons can be outside both the camera and the character hierarchy.
    local snaplineMeshService, snaplineWeaponService, snaplineGunPlugin
    do
        local first = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")
        local plugins, guns = first:FindFirstChild("GunSystemPlugins"), first:FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
        local meshModule = plugins and plugins:FindFirstChild("CustomMeshCharacter")
        local weaponModule = guns and guns:FindFirstChild("WeaponWorldModelService")
        local controller = guns and guns:FindFirstChild("GunController")
        local events = controller and controller:FindFirstChild("Events")
        local pluginModule = events and events:FindFirstChild("GunPlugin")
        if pluginModule then
            local ok, plugin = pcall(require, pluginModule)
            if ok then snaplineGunPlugin = plugin end
        end
        if meshModule then
            local ok, service = pcall(require, meshModule)
            if ok then snaplineMeshService = service end
        end
        if weaponModule then
            local ok, service = pcall(require, weaponModule)
            if ok then snaplineWeaponService = service end
        end
    end
    -- END SNAPLINE SERVICES

    local function findBarrelPart(container, targetName, descendants)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not container or not targetName then return nil end
        descendants = descendants or container:GetDescendants()
        local function usable(obj)
            return obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Attachment") or obj:IsA("Bone")
        end
        -- Some exporters keep Meshes/Foo as a literal part name, others create folders.
        for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
            if usable(obj) and obj.Name == targetName then return obj end
        end
        for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
            if usable(obj) and obj.Name:lower() == targetName:lower() then return obj end
        end
        if targetName:find("/", 1, true) then
            local parts = string.split(targetName, "/")
            for _, root in ipairs(descendants) do
                if root.Name:lower() == parts[1]:lower() then
                    local current = root
                    for index = 2, #parts do
                        local child
                        for _, candidate in ipairs(current:GetChildren()) do
                            if candidate.Name:lower() == parts[index]:lower() then child = candidate; break end
                        end
                        current = child
                        if not current then break end
                    end
                    if current and usable(current) then return current end
                end
            end
            -- Flattened world models may keep only the leaf mesh name.
            for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
                if usable(obj) and obj.Name:lower() == parts[#parts]:lower() then return obj end
            end
        end
    end

    local function getObjectPosition(obj)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local frame = GetSnaplineObjectFrame(obj)
        return frame and frame.Position
    end

    local function getSnaplineCharacters()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local rigs, seen = {}, {}
        local function add(rig)
            if rig and not seen[rig] then seen[rig] = true; table.insert(rigs, rig) end
        end
        local query = snaplineGunPlugin and snaplineGunPlugin.Functions and snaplineGunPlugin.Functions.GetWorldCharacter
        if query and type(query.Invoke) == "function" then
            local ok, rig = pcall(query.Invoke, query)
            if ok then add(rig) end
        end
        if snaplineMeshService and type(snaplineMeshService.GetWorldCharacterFromPlayer) == "function" then
            local ok, rig = pcall(snaplineMeshService.GetWorldCharacterFromPlayer, snaplineMeshService, LocalPlayer)
            if ok then add(rig) end
        end
        for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            if type(entity) == "table" and entity.Player == LocalPlayer then add(entity.WorldModel); add(entity.Character) end
        end
        add(LocalPlayer.Character)
        return rigs
    end

    local function getSnaplineCharacter()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        return getSnaplineCharacters()[1]
    end

    local snaplineCamera, snaplineThirdPerson
    local function isSnaplineThirdPerson(camera, rigs)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not camera then return false end
        if snaplineCamera ~= camera then snaplineCamera, snaplineThirdPerson = camera, nil end
        -- Native state accounts for scopes, custom camera offsets and moved character heads.
        local functions = snaplineGunPlugin and snaplineGunPlugin.Functions
        local thirdPerson = functions and functions.IsThirdPerson
        if thirdPerson and type(thirdPerson.Invoke) == "function" then
            local ok, active = pcall(thirdPerson.Invoke, thirdPerson)
            if ok and active == true then snaplineThirdPerson = true; return true end
        end
        for _, name in ipairs({"IsViewModelActive", "IsFirstPerson", "IsThirdPerson"}) do
            local query = functions and functions[name]
            if query and type(query.Invoke) == "function" then
                local ok, value = pcall(query.Invoke, query)
                if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then
                    if name == "IsThirdPerson" then snaplineThirdPerson = value else snaplineThirdPerson = not value end
                    return snaplineThirdPerson
                end
            end
        end
        if not camera.CFrame then return false end
        local distance
        for _, rig in ipairs(rigs) do
            local head = rig:FindFirstChild("Head", true)
            local position = getObjectPosition(head)
            if position then distance = (camera.CFrame.Position - position).Magnitude; break end
        end
        if not distance and camera.Focus then distance = (camera.CFrame.Position - camera.Focus.Position).Magnitude end
        -- Deadband avoids mode changes every frame around the old two-stud threshold.
        if distance then
            if distance <= 1 then snaplineThirdPerson = false
            elseif distance >= 3 then snaplineThirdPerson = true end
        end
        if snaplineThirdPerson == nil then snaplineThirdPerson = false end
        return snaplineThirdPerson
    end

    local function getSnaplineViewModel(camera)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local query = snaplineGunPlugin and snaplineGunPlugin.Functions and snaplineGunPlugin.Functions.GetGunViewModel
        if query and type(query.Invoke) == "function" then
            local ok, model = pcall(query.Invoke, query)
            if ok and typeof(model) == "Instance" and model.Parent then return model end
        end
        return camera:FindFirstChild("CurrentWeapon")
    end

    local function getNativeSnaplineMuzzle(container)
        if not container then return end
        local weapon = container:FindFirstChild("Weapon") or container
        local main = weapon:FindFirstChild("Main")
        if main and (main:IsA("Attachment") or main:IsA("Bone")) then return main end
        local primary = container:IsA("Model") and container.PrimaryPart
        if not primary and weapon:IsA("Model") then primary = weapon.PrimaryPart end
        local reference = primary and primary:FindFirstChild("Main")
        if reference and reference:IsA("Attachment") then
            local boneName = reference:GetAttribute("Bone")
            local bone = type(boneName) == "string" and primary:FindFirstChild(boneName, true)
            -- GunController copies this attachment to a separate Main part. That
            -- part can lag the animated mesh; evaluate its source bone and offset
            -- directly instead of sampling the previous copied/interpolated pose.
            if bone and bone:IsA("Bone") and (not main or (main:IsA("BasePart")
                and main:GetAttribute("Bone") == boneName)) then return reference end
        end
        if main and main:IsA("BasePart") then return main end
        if reference and (reference:IsA("Attachment") or reference:IsA("Bone")) then return reference end
    end

    local function resolve_gun_barrel_position(preferRendered, preferWorld)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return nil end
        local ok, gun = pcall(get_current_gun, LocalPlayer)
        if not ok or gun == "Fists" then gun = ReadIndicatorGun(LocalPlayer) end
        local targetName = type(gun) == "string" and BarrelMap[gun:gsub("%.item$", "")] or nil
        local function frontPosition(obj)
            return getObjectPosition(obj) and obj
        end
        local function position(marker, thirdPerson)
            local frame = GetSnaplineObjectFrame(marker)
            return frame and frame.Position, marker, thirdPerson
        end
        local function resolve(container, weaponRoot)
            if not container then return end
            if weaponRoot then
                -- Use the same Main marker as the native muzzle flash and tracer.
                local main = getNativeSnaplineMuzzle(container)
                if main and frontPosition(main) then return main end
            end
            local descendants = container:GetDescendants()
            -- Pointer identifies the model actually rendered, even during inventory swaps.
            local pointer = container:FindFirstChild("Pointer")
            local reference = pointer and pointer:IsA("ObjectValue") and pointer.Value
            local mappedName = reference and BarrelMap[reference.Name:gsub("%.item$", "")] or targetName
            local function attachmentPosition(objects)
                for _, name in ipairs({"muzzleattachment", "muzzle", "muzzlepoint", "firepoint", "shootpoint", "bulletorigin", "tipattachment", "tip", "barrelattachment"}) do
                    for _, obj in ipairs(objects) do
                        if (obj:IsA("Attachment") or obj:IsA("Bone")) and obj.Name:lower():gsub("[_%s%-]", "") == name then
                            local position = frontPosition(obj)
                            if position then return position end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            local mapped = findBarrelPart(container, mappedName, descendants)
            -- Non-native exports still prefer muzzle attachments over mesh pivots.
            local attachment = attachmentPosition(descendants)
            if attachment then return attachment end
            if mapped and (mapped:IsA("Attachment") or mapped:IsA("Bone")) then
                return frontPosition(mapped)
            end
            for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
                local name = obj.Name:lower()
                if (obj:IsA("Attachment") or obj:IsA("Bone")) and (name:find("muzzle") or name:find("barrel")) then
                    local position = frontPosition(obj)
                    if position then return position end
                end
            end
            local function usable(obj)
                if not obj or not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
                local name = obj.Name:lower()
                return not (name:find("handle") or name:find("hand") or name:find("grip") or name:find("stock")
                    or name:find("magazine") or name == "mag" or name:match("^mag[_%s%-]")
                    or name:find("clip") or name == "main")
            end
            for _, name in ipairs({"Muzzle", "MuzzleAttachment", "Barrel", "Tip", "Frontsight", "Front", "barrel_low", "barrel_lp001", "BarrelThingy", "ChainSaw_Muzzle"}) do
                local obj = findBarrelPart(container, name, descendants)
                if usable(obj) then
                    local position = frontPosition(obj)
                    if position then return position end
                end
            end
            if usable(mapped) then return frontPosition(mapped) end
            for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
                local name = obj.Name:lower()
                if usable(obj) and (name:find("muzzle") or name:find("barrel")) then
                    local position = frontPosition(obj)
                    if position then return position end
                end
            end
            -- Keep the legacy mesh fallback for exports without a native marker.
            if mapped then return frontPosition(mapped) end
        end
        local rigs = getSnaplineCharacters()
        if not preferWorld and not isSnaplineThirdPerson(camera, rigs) then
            local marker = resolve(getSnaplineViewModel(camera), true)
            if marker then return position(marker, false) end
            for _, model in ipairs(camera:GetChildren()) do
                if model:IsA("Model") or model:IsA("Folder") then
                    marker = resolve(model, true)
                    if marker then return position(marker, false) end
                end
            end
            return
        end
        -- Match the native controller's selected world-weapon lookup for each local rig.
        if snaplineWeaponService and type(snaplineWeaponService.GetSelectedWeapons) == "function" then
            for _, rig in ipairs(rigs) do
                local ok, selected = pcall(snaplineWeaponService.GetSelectedWeapons, snaplineWeaponService, LocalPlayer, rig)
                if ok and type(selected) == "table" then
                    for _, record in ipairs(selected) do
                        local weaponModel = record.WeaponModel
                        local marker = weaponModel and resolve(weaponModel.Instance, true)
                        if marker then return position(marker, true) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, rig in ipairs(rigs) do
            local weapon = rig:FindFirstChild("Weapon") or rig:FindFirstChild("CurrentWeapon")
            local marker = weapon and resolve(weapon, true) or resolve(rig)
            if marker then return position(marker, true) end
        end
    end

    local get_gun_barrel_position = CreateMuzzleCache(resolve_gun_barrel_position, function()
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        return camera, camera and camera:FindFirstChild("CurrentWeapon"), LocalPlayer.Character, get_current_gun(LocalPlayer)
    end, os.clock)

    local function barrelFovCenter(camera)
        if not flags.FovFollowBarrel or Runtime.FreecamActive then return end
        if isSnaplineThirdPerson(camera, getSnaplineCharacters()) then return end
        local _, marker = get_gun_barrel_position(true)
        local frame = marker and marker.Parent and GetSnaplineObjectFrame(marker)
        if not frame then return end
        local point = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(frame.Position + frame.LookVector * 1000)
        if point.Z > 0 then return Vector2.new(point.X, point.Y) end
    end
    Runtime.FovCenterProvider = barrelFovCenter
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        if Runtime.FovCenterProvider == barrelFovCenter then Runtime.FovCenterProvider = nil end
    end)

    local function get_closest_target(fov_size, aimpart, visibleOrigin, allowManipulation, includeExpanded)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local ermm_part, plr_instance, collider, targetEntity
        Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not Camera then return nil, nil, nil end
        local inventoryName = GetInventorySelection(Runtime)
        if visibleOrigin then
            local resolveShifted
            if allowManipulation and flags.aimbot.exploits.manipulation.enabled and Runtime.Manipulation then
                resolveShifted = function(part, entity, origin)
                    local shifted, point, visible, offset = Runtime.Manipulation:Resolve(part, entity, origin)
                    return visible, point, shifted, offset
                end
            end
            return SelectVisibleBodyTarget(Runtime, get_entity_list(), LocalPlayer, Camera, fov_size, aimpart,
                visibleOrigin, flags.TeamCheck and IsTeammate or nil, flags.TargetClosestPlayers, inventoryName, resolveShifted, includeExpanded)
        end
        local maximum_distance = math.huge

        local list = get_entity_list()
        if not list then return nil, nil, nil end

        for userid, v in pairs(list) do
            local player = v.Player
            if not (player and player ~= LocalPlayer) or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
                or v.IsDead or v.Dead then continue end

            if flags.TeamCheck and IsTeammate(player) then continue end
            if inventoryName and player.Name ~= inventoryName then continue end

            local worldmodel = v.WorldModel or v.Character
            local character = v.Character or worldmodel
            if not (worldmodel and worldmodel.Parent and character) then continue end
            local root = v.RootPart or character:FindFirstChild("Part")
                or worldmodel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            local targetPart = Runtime:GetAimPart(character, worldmodel, aimpart)

            if not targetPart then continue end

            local position = Runtime:GetDisplayPart(targetPart).Position
            local screen, offset = ProjectFovPoint(Camera, position, fov_size)
            if not screen then continue end
            local distance = TargetPriority(Camera, LocalPlayer, position, offset, flags.TargetClosestPlayers)

            if distance <= maximum_distance then
                targetEntity = v
                plr_instance = player
                ermm_part = targetPart
                collider = root
                maximum_distance = distance
            end
        end

        return ermm_part, plr_instance, collider, targetEntity
    end

    local function shotOrigin()
        local entities = Runtime:GetEntities()
        for _, entity in pairs(entities or {}) do
            if entity.Player == LocalPlayer then
                local character = entity.Character
                local root = entity.RootPart or (character and character:FindFirstChild("Part"))
                if root then
                    local charData = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("CharData")
                    local state = charData and charData:FindFirstChild("PlayerState")
                    local offsets = {Normal=4.75, Crouch=3.5, Prone=1.75, Sit=2}
                    return root.Position + Vector3.yAxis * (offsets[state and state.Value] or 4.75)
                end
            end
        end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        return camera and camera.CFrame.Position
    end
    local manipulationSettings = flags.aimbot.exploits.manipulation
    local manipulation = CreateManipulation(Runtime, function() return manipulationSettings end,
        function() return Workspace.CurrentCamera end, os.clock)
    Runtime.Manipulation = manipulation
    Runtime.GetShotOrigin = shotOrigin
    Runtime.IsOriginShiftClear = CreateManipulationOriginChecker(Runtime, Workspace, LocalPlayer)
    local originResolver = CreateOriginCodecResolver(Runtime, os.clock)
    Runtime.OriginCodecResolver = originResolver
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function() manipulation:BeginFrame() end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        manipulation:Clear()
        originResolver:Destroy()
        Runtime.Manipulation, Runtime.OriginCodecReader, Runtime.FireManipulatedTarget, Runtime.GetShotOrigin = nil, nil, nil, nil
        Runtime.OriginCodecResolver, Runtime.OriginCodecState, Runtime.IsOriginShiftClear = nil, nil, nil
    end)
    GroupBoxes.Manipulation:AddToggle("ManipulationEnabled", {
        Text = "Manipulation", Default = manipulationSettings.enabled,
        Tooltip = "Shift the shot origin for Silent Aim, Triggerbot and Rage Bot. Requires native origin configuration; server validation still applies.",
        Callback = function(value)
            manipulationSettings.enabled = value; manipulation:Clear()
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Manipulation:AddDropdown("ManipulationMode", {
        Text = "Mode", Values = {"Automatic", "Manual"}, Default = manipulationSettings.mode,
        Callback = function(value) manipulationSettings.mode = value; manipulation:Clear() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Manipulation:AddSlider("ManipulationDistance", {
        Text = "Automatic Distance", Default = manipulationSettings.manip_dist, Min = 0, Max = 11, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value) manipulationSettings.manip_dist = value; manipulation:Clear() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Manipulation:AddSlider("ManipulationSteps", {
        Text = "Search Steps Per Stud", Default = manipulationSettings.manipulation_steps, Min = 1, Max = 16, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(value) manipulationSettings.manipulation_steps = value; manipulation:Clear() end,
    })
    for _, axis in ipairs({"X", "Y", "Z"}) do
        GroupBoxes.Manipulation:AddSlider("Manipulation" .. axis, {
            Text = "Manual " .. axis, Default = manipulationSettings["offset" .. axis], Min = -4, Max = 4, Rounding = 1,
            Callback = function(value) manipulationSettings["offset" .. axis] = value; manipulation:Clear() end,
        })
    end

    local function get_silent_aim_point(part, entity)
        -- Selection is independent of the visibility indicator, including with the expander enabled.
        if not part or not part.Parent then return end
        return Runtime:GetAimPosition(part)
    end

    -- BEGIN SILENT CAMERA INPUT
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        Runtime.InstantFrameTime = StatsService.FrameTime
    end)
    local silentPoint, silentOffset
    local shotOverride
    local function resolveShotTarget(origin, allowManipulation, instantPolicy)
        if shotOverride then return shotOverride.Point, allowManipulation and shotOverride.Offset or nil end
        if flags.RageBotEnabled then return silentPoint, allowManipulation and silentOffset or nil end
        if not flags.Enabled or flags.AimType ~= "Silent Aim" then return end
        local part, _, collider, entity, visiblePoint, selectedOrigin, selectedOffset = get_closest_target(flags.FovSize or 120, flags.AimPart,
            (flags.SilentBestVisiblePart or (Runtime.HitboxExpander and Runtime.HitboxExpander.Enabled)) and origin or nil, allowManipulation, true)
        local point = visiblePoint or get_silent_aim_point(part, entity)
        local offset, predictedOrigin = selectedOffset, selectedOrigin
        if allowManipulation and manipulationSettings.enabled and part and not selectedOrigin then
            local ok, shifted, shiftedPoint, _, shiftedOffset = pcall(manipulation.Resolve, manipulation, part, entity, origin)
            if ok and shifted then
                point, offset, predictedOrigin = shiftedPoint or point, shiftedOffset, shifted
            elseif not ok then Runtime:Report("Manipulation target", shifted) end
        end
        if point then return full_prediction_silent(point, collider, predictedOrigin or origin, entity, instantPolicy) or point, offset end
    end
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if flags.RageBotEnabled or shotOverride then return end
        if not flags.Enabled or flags.AimType ~= "Silent Aim" then silentPoint, silentOffset = nil, nil; return end
