        local ok, point, offset = pcall(resolveShotTarget, shotOrigin(), true)
        silentPoint, silentOffset = nil, nil
        if ok then silentPoint, silentOffset = point, offset else Runtime:Report("Silent aim target", point) end
    end)
    local pendingShots, reportedShots = {}, {}
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        silentPoint, silentOffset, shotOverride = nil, nil, nil
        table.clear(pendingShots); table.clear(reportedShots); instantReports:Clear(); instantCooldown:Reset()
    end)
    -- END SILENT CAMERA INPUT

    local fovBinding = "LucidFov_" .. tostring(Runtime)
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(fovBinding, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 3, function(dt)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local center = GetFovCenter(camera)
        fovEffects:Update(camera, dt, center)
        fovcircle.Visible = flags.FovVisible and camera ~= nil
        if not camera then return end
        fovcircle.Position = center
        fovcircle.Radius = flags.FovSize or 120
        fovcircle.Color = flags.FovColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        fovcircle.Thickness = flags.FovThickness or 1
        fovcircle.Transparency = flags.FovTransparency or 1
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(fovBinding) end)
    -- BEGIN SNAPLINE TARGET
    local function getSnaplineTarget(camera)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        -- Recheck projection every frame: movement, zoom and FOV changes invalidate old locks.
        local inventoryName = GetInventorySelection(Runtime)
        local closest, maximum = nil, math.huge
        for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            -- A stale/partially streamed record must not hide all other players' lines.
            local ok, part, distance = pcall(function()
                local player = entity.Player
                if not player or player == LocalPlayer or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
                    or entity.IsDead or entity.Dead or (flags.TeamCheck and IsTeammate(player)) then return end
                if inventoryName and player.Name ~= inventoryName then return end
                local world = entity.WorldModel
                local character = entity.Character
                if world and not world.Parent then world = nil end
                if character and not character.Parent then character = nil end
                world = world or character
                if not world then return end
                local target = Runtime:GetAimPart(character or world, world, flags.AimPart or "Head")
                if not target then return end
                local frame = GetSnaplineObjectFrame(target)
                if not frame then return end
                local screen, offset = ProjectFovPoint(camera, frame.Position, flags.FovSize)
                if not screen then return end
                return target, TargetPriority(camera, LocalPlayer, frame.Position, offset, flags.TargetClosestPlayers)
            end)
            if ok and part and distance < maximum then closest, maximum = part, distance end
        end
        return closest
    end
    -- END SNAPLINE TARGET

    -- BEGIN BARREL SNAPLINE RENDER
    local attachedSnapline = CreateAttachedGunSnapline()
    local scopedOrigin
    local function getScopedVisualOrigin(horizontalFraction)
        return GetScopedVisualOrigin(Workspace.CurrentCamera, snaplineGunPlugin, horizontalFraction)
    end
    Runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin = getScopedVisualOrigin
    local function getScopedSnaplineOrigin(camera)
        local position = GetScopedVisualOrigin(camera, snaplineGunPlugin)
        if not position then return end
        if not scopedOrigin then
            scopedOrigin = Instance.new("Part")
            scopedOrigin.Name, scopedOrigin.Size = "LucidScopedOrigin", Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01)
            scopedOrigin.Transparency, scopedOrigin.Anchored = 1, true
            scopedOrigin.CanCollide, scopedOrigin.CanTouch, scopedOrigin.CanQuery = false, false, false
            scopedOrigin.CastShadow = false
        end
        scopedOrigin.Parent = camera
        scopedOrigin.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
        return scopedOrigin
    end
    local function hideSnapline()
        snapline.Visible = false
        attachedSnapline:Hide()
    end
    local function updateAimCamera()
        if flags.RageBotEnabled or Runtime.FreecamActive or not flags.Enabled or flags.AimType ~= "Aimbot" then return end
        local part, _, collider = get_closest_target(flags.FovSize or 120, flags.AimPart)
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if part and camera then
            local point = Runtime:GetAimPosition(part)
            local predicted = full_prediction_silent(point, collider) or point
            camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(camera.CFrame.Position, predicted)
        end
    end
    local function drawSnapline()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera or not flags.SnaplineEnabled then hideSnapline(); return end
        local part = getSnaplineTarget(camera)
        if not part then hideSnapline(); return end
        -- Migrate saved hand selections to the visible gun muzzle.
        local mode = flags.SnaplineOrigin or "Gun Barrel"
        if mode == "Hands" then mode = "Gun Barrel"; flags.SnaplineOrigin = mode end
        local frame = GetSnaplineObjectFrame(part)
        local target = frame and frame.Position
        local expander = Runtime.HitboxExpander
        target = expander and expander:GetTopPoint(part.Parent) or target
        if mode == "Gun Barrel" then
            local marker = getScopedSnaplineOrigin(camera)
            if not marker then local _; _, marker = get_gun_barrel_position(true) end
            -- Sample the rendered muzzle/head after native animation, then clip to the FOV.
            snapline.Visible = false
            attachedSnapline:Update(marker, part, camera, flags, target)
            return
        end
        attachedSnapline:Hide()
        local crosshairPosition
        if mode == "Crosshair" then
            crosshairPosition = camera.ViewportSize * 0.5
        end
        UpdateBarrelSnapline(snapline, camera, nil, target, flags, crosshairPosition)
    end
    local snaplineBinding = "LucidSnapline_" .. tostring(Runtime)
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(snaplineBinding, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 2, function()
        if not Runtime.Active then hideSnapline(); return end
        -- Sample after native gun positioning; never rotate the rig in this pass.
        local aimed, aimReason = pcall(updateAimCamera)
        if not aimed then Runtime:Report("Aim camera", aimReason) end
        local ok, reason = pcall(drawSnapline)
        if not ok then hideSnapline(); Runtime:Report("Snapline render", reason) end
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(snaplineBinding)
        hideSnapline()
        attachedSnapline:Destroy()
        if scopedOrigin then scopedOrigin:Destroy(); scopedOrigin = nil end
        if Runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin == getScopedVisualOrigin then Runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin = nil end
    end)
    -- END BARREL SNAPLINE RENDER


    if getgenv().RestoreBulletSims then
        getgenv().RestoreBulletSims()
    end

    local WeaponDataUtil = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GunSystem", 10):WaitForChild("GunLibrary", 10):WaitForChild("WeaponDataUtil", 10))
    local TracerUtil = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GunSystem", 10):WaitForChild("GunLibrary", 10):WaitForChild("TracerUtil", 10))
    local BulletModule = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GunSystem", 10):WaitForChild("GunLibrary", 10):WaitForChild("BulletModule", 10))
    local BulletSims, seenSims = {}, {}
    local function addSimulator(callback)
        if type(callback) == "function" and not seenSims[callback] then
            seenSims[callback] = true
            table.insert(BulletSims, callback)
        end
    end
    addSimulator(BulletModule.BulletSimulationAsync)
    for _, callback in getgc(true) do
        if IsNativeOriginState(callback) then Runtime.OriginCodecState = callback end
        if type(callback) ~= "function" then continue end
        local readOK, upvalues = pcall(getupvalues, callback)
        if not readOK or type(upvalues) ~= "table" then continue end
        -- Executor source labels may be stripped; validate codec structure independently.
        if ReadOriginCodec(upvalues) then Runtime.OriginCodecReader = callback end
        local ok, info = pcall(getinfo, callback)
        if not ok or type(info) ~= "table" or type(info.source) ~= "string"
            or not info.source:find("GunController", 1, true) then continue end
        local hasWeaponData, hasTracers, hasParams = false, false, false
        for _, value in pairs(upvalues) do
            if value == WeaponDataUtil then hasWeaponData = true end
            if value == TracerUtil then hasTracers = true end
            if typeof(value) == "RaycastParams" then hasParams = true end
        end
        if (hasWeaponData and hasTracers and hasParams) or (info.nups == 12 and upvalues[2] == 58) then
            addSimulator(callback)
        end
    end

    local function callTracer(method, ...)
        local result = table.pack(pcall(CallGameFunction, method, TracerUtil, ...))
        if not result[1] then Runtime:Report("Native tracer", result[2]); return nil end
        return table.unpack(result, 2, result.n)
    end
    -- Native simulation also reaches these methods; protect that path, not just Instant Bullet.
    for _, method in ipairs({"CreateTracer", "RemoveTracer"}) do
        local original
        original = hookfunction(TracerUtil[method], newcclosure(function(...)
            return CallGameFunction(original, ...)
        end))
    end

    local function getBulletSettings(p31)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local v32
        if p31 then
            v32 = WeaponDataUtil:GetWeaponData(p31)
        else
            v32 = p31
        end
        if v32 then v32 = v32.Stats end
        if v32 then v32 = v32.BulletSettings end
        local v33 = v32 and v32.BulletGravity or nil
        local v34 = v32 and v32.BulletSpeed or nil
        -- GunController treats BulletGravity.Value as a toggle, including numeric values.
        local gravity = v33 and v33.Value and ConfigService.server():get("sv_default_bullet_gravity") or 0
        local settings = {
            ["MaxDistance"] = v32 and v32.MaxDistance and v32.MaxDistance.Value + 4 or 0,
            ["Speed"] = v34 and v34.Value or ConfigService.server():get("sv_default_bullet_speed"),
            ["Gravity"] = tonumber(gravity) or 0
        }
        assert(type(settings.MaxDistance) == "number" and settings.MaxDistance > 0 and settings.MaxDistance < math.huge,
            "Unsupported Instant Bullet range; using native simulation")
        assert(type(settings.Speed) == "number" and settings.Speed > 0 and settings.Speed < math.huge,
            "Unsupported Instant Bullet speed; using native simulation")
        assert(type(settings.Gravity) == "number" and settings.Gravity >= 0 and settings.Gravity < math.huge,
            "Unsupported Instant Bullet gravity; using native simulation")
        return settings
    end

    local v_u_15 = getupvalue(BulletModule.RayTest, 1)
    Runtime.BulletRayParams = v_u_15
    Runtime.BulletRayTest = function(origin, point, params) return BulletModule:RayTest(origin, point, params) end

    local BulletSimulationAsync = function(p51, p52, p53, p54, p55, ...)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local v56 = p54 and p54.Tracer and p54.Tracer.Enabled or false
        local v57 = p54 and p54.Tracer and p54.Tracer.Offset or Vector3.zero
        local v58 = p54 and p54.RaycastParams or v_u_15
        local v59 = WeaponDataUtil:GetWeaponData(p53)
        local v60 = v59 and v59.BulletTrail and (v59.Name or "Tracer") or "Tracer"
        local v61 = p52.Unit
        local v62 = {
            ["Position"] = p51,
            ["Normal"] = -v61,
            ["Material"] = Enum.Material.Air,
            ["Distance"] = 0,
            ["Resimulation"] = {}
        }
        local v63 = getBulletSettings(p53)
        local v64 = CFrame.new
        if p55 then p51 = p55[1].Start or p51 end
        local v65 = v64(p51)
        local v66 = 0
        local v67
        if v56 and not p55 then
            v67 = callTracer(TracerUtil.CreateTracer, v60, v59 and v59.BulletTrail)
        else
            v67 = nil
        end

        local CanInstant = flags.instant_bullet and instantReports:CanInstant(os.clock())
        local frameTime = Runtime.InstantFrameTime
        if type(frameTime) ~= "number" or frameTime ~= frameTime or frameTime <= 0 or frameTime >= math.huge then
            frameTime = 1 / 60
        end
        -- Chroma steps with Stats.FrameTime. Bound the replay length to the native
        -- GunController limit of 20,000 entries, even on very fast render frames.
        frameTime = math.max(frameTime, v63.MaxDistance / v63.Speed / 19999)
        local v68 = 1
        local v69 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        -- Instant flights complete synchronously, as in Chroma. Their step count
        -- is bounded above; frame waits would add latency to long-range reports.
        while true do
            local v70
            if p55 then
                local v71 = p55[v68]
                if not v71 then return v62 end
                v70 = v71.Time
            elseif CanInstant then
                v70 = frameTime
            else
                v70 = RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end

            local v72 = v66 + v70
            v68 = v68 + 1
            local v73 = -(v63.Gravity * v72 ^ 2)
            local v74 = Vector3.new(0, v73, 0)
            local v75 = v63.Speed * v70
            local v76 = v65.Position
            local v77 = v63.MaxDistance - v62.Distance
            local v78
            if v77 <= v75 then
                v78 = true
            else
                v77 = v75
                v78 = false
            end
            v65 = v65 * CFrame.new(v61 * v77)
            local v79 = v65.Position
            local v80 = (v79 - v76).Unit
            local v81 = v76 + v69
            local v82 = v79 + v74
            local v83 = BulletModule:RayTest(v81, v82, v58)
            if p54 and p54.RaycastCallback then v83 = p54.RaycastCallback(v83, v81, v82) end
            local v84 = { ["Time"] = v70, ["Start"] = v81, ["End"] = v82 }
            local v85 = v62.Resimulation
            table.insert(v85, v84)
            local v86 = p55 and v83.Instance and workspace:Raycast(v83.Position, v81 - v83.Position, v58)
            if v86 then
                v83.Instance = v86.Instance
                v83.Position = v86.Position
                v83.Normal = v86.Normal
            end
            if v83.Instance then
                if v67 then
                    local v87 = CFrame.new(v83.Position)
                    local v88 = v72 * 4
                    local v89 = v57:Lerp(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), math.clamp(v88, 0, 1) ^ 2)
                    local v90 = v87.Position + v89
                    v67:PivotTo(CFrame.new(v90, v90 + v80))
                    callTracer(TracerUtil.RemoveTracer, v60, v67)
                end
                v62.Distance = v62.Distance + v83.Distance
                v62.Position = v83.Position
                v62.Normal = v83.Normal
                v62.Material = v83.Material
                v62.Instance = v83.Instance
                v84.End = v83.Position
                return v62
            end
            if v67 then
                local v91 = v65 + v74
                local v92 = v72 * 4
                local v93 = v57:Lerp(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), math.clamp(v92, 0, 1) ^ 2)
                local v94 = v91.Position + v93
                v67:PivotTo(CFrame.new(v94, v94 + v80))
            end
            v62.Distance = v62.Distance + v77
            v62.Position = v82
            if v78 then
                if v67 then
                    callTracer(TracerUtil.RemoveTracer, v60, v67)
                end
                return v62
            end
            v66 = v72
            v69 = v74
        end
    end

    -- BEGIN FIRE DISPATCH
    for _, BulletSim in BulletSims do
        if isfunctionhooked(BulletSim) then restorefunction(BulletSim) end
        local original
        original = hookfunction(BulletSim, newcclosure(function(Origin, Direction, ...)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
            local replay = select(3, ...)
            instantReports.Flights[coroutine.running()] = nil
            local burstAllowed = not replay and flags.instant_bullet and instantCooldown:TakeSimulation(os.clock())
            if not replay then
                local offset
                Direction, offset = TakeShotDirection(pendingShots, Direction, os.clock(), Origin)
                if offset then Origin += offset end
            end
            if not flags.instant_bullet and not Runtime.BulletTraceActive then
                return CallGameFunction(original, Origin, Direction, ...)
            end
            -- Snapshot the displayed muzzle before either simulator can yield or move the gun.
            local traceOrigin, finishTrace = Origin, nil
            if Runtime.BulletTraceActive and not replay then
                traceOrigin = CaptureBulletTraceOrigin(Runtime, Origin)
                finishTrace = StartBulletTrace(Runtime, traceOrigin, Origin, Direction)
            end
            -- Consume only a direction committed to a fire packet; leave replay directions intact.
            local returned
            if burstAllowed and instantReports:CanInstant(os.clock()) and not replay then
                local started = os.clock()
                returned = table.pack(pcall(BulletSimulationAsync, Origin, Direction, ...))
                if returned[1] then
                    local result = returned[2]
                    instantReports:RecordFlight(result, started)
                    DispatchBulletTrace(Runtime, traceOrigin, result, replay, finishTrace)
                    return table.unpack(returned, 2, returned.n)
                end
                Runtime:Report("Instant Bullet", returned[2])
            end
            -- Normal shots and all replay passes keep the game's own physics/timing.
            returned = table.pack(CallGameFunction(original, Origin, Direction, ...))
            DispatchBulletTrace(Runtime, traceOrigin, returned[1], replay, finishTrace)
            return table.unpack(returned, 1, returned.n)
        end))
    end
    -- END FIRE DISPATCH

    getgenv().RestoreBulletSims = function()
        for _, v in BulletSims do
            restorefunction(v)
        end
    end

    -- BEGIN FIRE PACKET
    local Network
    local packetOK, packetError = pcall(function()
        local libraries = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GunSystem", 10):WaitForChild("SharedLibraries", 10)
        Network = require(libraries:WaitForChild("Network", 10))
    end)
    if not packetOK then Runtime:Report("Chroma network", packetError) end
    -- BEGIN RAGE FIRE
    local rageInput = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local rageFocused, rageBusy, nextRageShot = true, false, 0
    local rageRequest, rageOwner, ragePart, ragePlayer
    local function clearRageTarget()
        ragePart, ragePlayer, Runtime.RageTargetPart = nil, nil, nil
    end
    Runtime.GetRageTarget = function() return ragePart, ragePlayer end
    Runtime:Connect(rageInput.WindowFocused, function() rageFocused = true end)
    Runtime:Connect(rageInput.WindowFocusReleased, function() rageFocused = false; clearRageTarget(); silentPoint = nil end)
    local function updateRage(origin)
        -- Keep a committed shot target intact if the native firing request yields.
        if rageBusy or shotOverride then return end
        silentPoint, silentOffset = nil, nil
        clearRageTarget()
        if not flags.RageBotEnabled or not origin or not rageFocused or Window.IsOpen
            or Runtime.FreecamActive or rageInput:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        Camera = camera
        local part, player, root, entity, point, offset = SelectRageTarget(Runtime, LocalPlayer, origin, flags)
        if not point then return end
        silentPoint, silentOffset = full_prediction_silent(point, root, offset and origin + offset) or point, offset
        ragePart, ragePlayer, Runtime.RageTargetPart = part, player, part
        local now = os.clock()
        if not rageRequest or now < nextRageShot then return end
        -- Commit the same target used by the fire-packet hook before requesting a native shot.
        rageBusy = true
        local committed = { Point = silentPoint, Origin = origin, Offset = offset }
        shotOverride = committed
        local ok, accepted = pcall(CallGameFunction, rageRequest, rageOwner)
        if shotOverride == committed then shotOverride = nil end
        rageBusy = false
        if ok and accepted ~= false then
            nextRageShot = os.clock() + math.clamp(Runtime.TriggerDelay or 0.08, 0.01, 0.5)
        elseif not ok then
            Runtime:Report("Rage Bot", accepted)
            Toggles.RageBotEnabled:SetValue(false)
        end
    end
    GroupBoxes.Rage:AddToggle("RageBotEnabled", {
        Text = "Rage Bot", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "360-degree Silent Aim with automatic native fire and optional Manipulation. Uses Team Check and Triggerbot Shot Delay; ammo, cooldowns and server hit validation still apply.",
        Callback = function(value)
            flags.RageBotEnabled = false
            silentPoint, nextRageShot = nil, 0
            clearRageTarget()
            if not value then rageRequest, rageOwner = nil, nil; return end
            local ok, request, owner = pcall(function()
                assert(#BulletSims > 0, "Native bullet simulator unavailable")
                return ResolveNativeFireRequest()
            end)
            if not ok then
                Runtime:Report("Rage Bot", request)
                Toggles.RageBotEnabled:SetValue(false)
                return
            end
            rageRequest, rageOwner, flags.RageBotEnabled = request, owner, true
        end,
    }):AddKeyPicker("RageBotKeybind", {
        Default = "None", Mode = "Toggle", ShowInKeybindsList = true, ForceInKeybindsList = true, Text = "Rage Bot",
        Callback = function() Toggles.RageBotEnabled:SetValue(not Toggles.RageBotEnabled.Value) end,
    })
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        flags.RageBotEnabled = false
        rageRequest, rageOwner, Runtime.GetRageTarget = nil, nil, nil
        clearRageTarget(); silentPoint = nil
    end)
    -- END RAGE FIRE

    -- Resolve Instances from Loki's heartbeat, outside a native GunController call.
    local fireOrigin, firePellets
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        fireOrigin, firePellets = nil, nil
        if not flags.Enabled and not flags.RageBotEnabled and not flags.instant_bullet then return end
        local origin = shotOrigin()
        local gun = aftermath.gundata:FindFirstChild(get_current_gun(LocalPlayer))
        local stats = gun and gun:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local pellets = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("Pellets")
        fireOrigin, firePellets = origin, pellets and pellets.Value or 1
        if flags.RageBotEnabled then updateRage(origin) end
    end)
    Runtime.FireManipulatedTarget = function(part, entity)
        if shotOverride or not manipulationSettings.enabled or Runtime.FreecamActive then return false end
        local origin = shotOrigin()
        local shifted, point, visible, offset = manipulation:Resolve(part, entity, origin)
        if not shifted or not visible then return false end
        local root = entity and (entity.RootPart or (entity.Character and entity.Character:FindFirstChild("Part")))
        local predicted = full_prediction_silent(point, root, shifted) or point
        local gun = aftermath.gundata:FindFirstChild(get_current_gun(LocalPlayer))
        local stats = gun and gun:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local pellets = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("Pellets")
        if not stats or #BulletSims == 0 then return false end
        local request, owner = ResolveNativeFireRequest()
        local committed = { Point = predicted, Origin = origin, Offset = offset, Pellets = pellets and pellets.Value or 1 }
        shotOverride = committed
        local ok, accepted = pcall(CallGameFunction, request, owner)
        if shotOverride == committed then shotOverride = nil end
        if not ok then Runtime:Report("Manipulation Triggerbot", accepted) end
        return ok and accepted ~= false
    end
    Runtime.FireFreecamShot = function()
        local manager, camera = Runtime.Freecam, Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if shotOverride or not manager or not manager:AcceptInput() or not camera then return false end
        local origin = shotOrigin()
        if not origin then return false end
        local ignore = { camera }
        if LocalPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignore, LocalPlayer.Character) end
        for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            if entity.Player == LocalPlayer then
                if entity.Character then table.insert(ignore, entity.Character) end
                if entity.WorldModel then table.insert(ignore, entity.WorldModel) end
            end
        end
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType, params.IgnoreWater, params.FilterDescendantsInstances = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude, true, ignore
        if typeof(Runtime.BulletRayParams) == "RaycastParams" then params.CollisionGroup = Runtime.BulletRayParams.CollisionGroup end
        local ray = camera.CFrame.LookVector * 10000
        local hit = Workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, ray, params)
        local point = hit and hit.Position or camera.CFrame.Position + ray
        local gun = aftermath.gundata:FindFirstChild(get_current_gun(LocalPlayer))
        local stats = gun and gun:FindFirstChild("Stats")
        local pellets = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("Pellets")
        if not stats or #BulletSims == 0 then return false end
        local request, owner = ResolveNativeFireRequest()
        local committed = { Point = point, Origin = origin, Pellets = pellets and pellets.Value or 1 }
        shotOverride = committed
        local ok, accepted = pcall(CallGameFunction, request, owner)
        if shotOverride == committed then shotOverride = nil end
        if not ok then Runtime:Report("Freecam firing", accepted) end
        return ok and accepted ~= false
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() Runtime.FireFreecamShot = nil end)
    local fireTarget = ChromaFireTarget(Network, getupvalue)
    if fireTarget then
        local originalFire
        originalFire = hookfunction(fireTarget, newcclosure(function(...)
            local args = table.pack(...)
            local routeIndex, packetIndex = FindShotCall(args)
            local function forward() return CallGameFunction(originalFire, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)) end
            if not Runtime.Active or not routeIndex then return forward() end
            local route, packet = args[routeIndex], args[packetIndex]
            local decoded = DecodeChromaRoute(route)
            if not decoded then return forward() end
            local now, reportCount = os.clock(), 0
            for key, shot in pairs(reportedShots) do
                if now > shot.ReportExpires then reportedShots[key] = nil else reportCount += 1 end
            end
            if decoded:match("bullet_hole$") and buffer.len(packet) >= 55 then
                local key = buffer.readi32(packet, 0) .. ":" .. buffer.readu8(packet, 4)
                local shot = reportedShots[key]
                local corrected, delay = instantReports:Correct(packet, now, args[packetIndex + 2])
                packet = corrected or packet
                if shot and shot.Consumed then
                    packet = CorrectShotReport(packet, shot) or packet
                    reportedShots[key] = nil
                end
                args[packetIndex] = packet
                if delay and delay > 0 then task.wait(delay) end
                return forward()
            end
            if not decoded:match("gun_fire$") then return forward() end
            local instantPolicy = flags.instant_bullet and instantCooldown:CanInstant(now)
            if flags.instant_bullet then
                local data = ReadShotPacket(packet)
                if data and data.Timestamp == data.Timestamp and math.abs(data.Timestamp) < math.huge then
                    local pellets = shotOverride and shotOverride.Pellets or firePellets or 1
                    instantPolicy = instantCooldown:RecordFire(data.Id .. ":" .. tostring(data.Timestamp), now, pellets)
                    instantIndicator:Update(true)
                end
            end
            packet = instantReports:RecordFire(packet, now, instantPolicy) or packet
            args[packetIndex] = packet
            if #BulletSims == 0 or not flags.Enabled and not flags.RageBotEnabled and not shotOverride then return forward() end
            local committed = shotOverride
            local origin = committed and committed.Origin or fireOrigin or shotOrigin()
            local targetOK, target, offset = pcall(resolveShotTarget, origin, true, instantPolicy)
            if not targetOK then Runtime:Report("Silent aim packet target", target); return forward() end
            local pellets = committed and committed.Pellets or firePellets or 1
            if not manipulationSettings.enabled then offset = nil end
            if offset and origin then origin += offset end
            local ok, copy, shots = pcall(BuildShotPacket, packet, origin, target, flags.NoSpread, pellets, offset, manipulation.Multiplier)
            if not ok or not copy or reportCount + #shots > 1024 then return forward() end
            local previous, owned = {}, {}
            for _, shot in ipairs(shots) do
                local key = shot.Id .. ":" .. shot.Pellet
                previous[key], owned[shot] = reportedShots[key], true
                shot.Origin, shot.Expires, shot.ReportExpires = origin, now + 2, now + 30
                table.insert(pendingShots, shot); reportedShots[key] = shot
            end
            args[packetIndex] = copy
            local returned = table.pack(pcall(forward))
            if not returned[1] or returned[2] == false then
                for index = #pendingShots, 1, -1 do
                    if owned[pendingShots[index]] then table.remove(pendingShots, index) end
                end
                for _, shot in ipairs(shots) do
                    local key = shot.Id .. ":" .. shot.Pellet
                    if reportedShots[key] == shot then reportedShots[key] = previous[key] end
                end
            end
            for index = #pendingShots, 1, -1 do
                if now > pendingShots[index].Expires then table.remove(pendingShots, index) end
            end
            if not returned[1] then error(returned[2], 0) end
            return table.unpack(returned, 2, returned.n)
        end))
    else Runtime:Report("Silent aim", "Chroma fire function unavailable; check executor getupvalue support and GunSystem.Network") end
    if #BulletSims == 0 then Runtime:Report("Silent aim", "Native bullet simulator unavailable") end
    -- END FIRE PACKET


    Runtime.CrosshairEntities = get_entity_list
    Runtime.GetGunBarrel = get_gun_barrel_position
    local function getWorldGunBarrel() return get_gun_barrel_position(true, true) end
    Runtime.GetWorldGunBarrel = getWorldGunBarrel
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        if Runtime.GetWorldGunBarrel == getWorldGunBarrel then Runtime.GetWorldGunBarrel = nil end
    end)
    Runtime.GetAimTarget = get_closest_target
    getgenv().get_closest_target = get_closest_target
    getgenv().get_gun_barrel_position = get_gun_barrel_position


    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("aimbotenabled", {
        Text = "Enable Aimbot",
        Default = flags.Enabled,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.Enabled = State
            silentPoint = nil
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("TargetClosestPlayers", {
        Text = "Target Closest Players", Default = flags.TargetClosestPlayers,
        Tooltip = "Prioritize players nearest to your character inside the FOV. Off: prioritize the FOV center. Applies to aim, snap lines and crosshair targeting.",
        Callback = function(value)
            flags.TargetClosestPlayers = value
            silentPoint, silentOffset = nil, nil
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("SilentBestVisiblePart", {
        Text = "Best Visible Part", Default = flags.SilentBestVisiblePart,
        Tooltip = "Silent Aim: choose a visible target part, head, torso or limb inside the FOV. With Manipulation on, also try valid shifted origins for blocked parts.",
        Callback = function(value)
            flags.SilentBestVisiblePart = value
            silentPoint, silentOffset = nil, nil
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("teamchecktoggle", {
        Text = "Team Check",
        Default = flags.TeamCheck or false,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.TeamCheck = State
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle("instantbulletoggle", {
        Text = "Instant Bullet",
        Default = flags.instant_bullet,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.instant_bullet = State
            instantCooldown:Reset()
            instantIndicator:Update(true)
        end
    })
    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle("InstantBulletCooldown", {
        Text = "instant cooldown", Default = flags.instant_bullet_cooldown,
        Tooltip = "Show green instant on when ready. After five fired shots, show red instant cooldown and use normal bullets for three seconds. Pellets count as one shot.",
        Callback = function(value)
            flags.instant_bullet_cooldown = value
            instantCooldown:SetEnabled(value)
            instantIndicator:Update(true)
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddDropdown("aimtypedropdown", {
        Values = {"Silent Aim", "Aimbot"},
        Default = 1,
        Text = "Aim Type",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.AimType = Value
            silentPoint = nil
        end
    })

    local fovToggle = GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("fovcirc", {
        Text = "Enable FOV Circle",
        Default = flags.FovVisible,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.FovVisible = State
            if fovcircle then fovcircle.Visible = State end
        end
    })
    local fovSettings = fovToggle:AddSettings()
    fovSettings:AddLabel("Outline Color"):AddColorPicker("FovColor", {
        Text = "FOV Color",
        Default = flags.FovColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Title = "FOV Color",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.FovColor = Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            if fovcircle then fovcircle.Color = flags.FovColor end
        end
    })


    fovSettings:AddSlider("fovbloom", {
        Text = "FOV Glow Intensity", Min = 0.0, Max = 1, Default = flags.FovBloom or 0, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(Value) flags.FovBloom = Value end
    })

    fovSettings:AddToggle("FovFill", {
        Text = "FOV Fill", Default = flags.FovFill,
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFill = value end,
    }):AddColorPicker("FovFillColor1", {
        Title = "FOV Fill First Color", Default = flags.FovFillColor1,
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFillColor1 = value end,
    })
    fovSettings:AddSlider("FovFillTransparency", {
        Text = "FOV Fill Transparency", Min = 0, Max = 1, Default = flags.FovFillTransparency, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFillTransparency = value end,
    })
    fovSettings:AddToggle("FovFillGradient", {
        Text = "FOV Fill Gradient", Default = flags.FovFillGradient,
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFillGradient = value end,
    }):AddColorPicker("FovFillColor2", {
        Title = "FOV Gradient Second Color", Default = flags.FovFillColor2,
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFillColor2 = value end,
    })
    fovSettings:AddSlider("FovFillSpeed", {
        Text = "FOV Gradient Speed", Min = 0, Max = 180, Default = flags.FovFillSpeed, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFillSpeed = value end,
    })

    fovSettings:AddSlider("fovslider", {
        Text = "FOV Size", 
        Min = 1, 
        Max = 500, 
        Default = flags.FovSize or 120, 
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.FovSize = Value
            if fovcircle then fovcircle.Radius = Value end
        end
    })

    fovSettings:AddSlider("thickness", {
        Text = "FOV Thickness", 
        Min = 1, 
        Max = 10, 
        Default = flags.FovThickness or 1, 
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.FovThickness = Value
            if fovcircle then fovcircle.Thickness = Value end
        end
    })

    fovSettings:AddSlider("fovtransparency", {
        Text = "FOV Transparency", Min = 0.0, Max = 1, Default = flags.FovTransparency or 1, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(Value) flags.FovTransparency = Value end
    })

    fovSettings:AddToggle("FovFollowBarrel", {
        Text = "Follow Gun Barrel", Default = flags.FovFollowBarrel,
        Tooltip = "Center the FOV on the rendered muzzle direction. Target checks follow the same circle. Falls back to screen center without a gun or in freecam.",
        Callback = function(value) flags.FovFollowBarrel = value end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("snaplinetoggle", {
        Text = "Snap Line",
        Default = flags.SnaplineEnabled or false,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.SnaplineEnabled = State
            if not State then hideSnapline() end
        end
    }):AddColorPicker("SnaplineColor", {
        Text = "Snap Line Color",
        Default = flags.SnaplineColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        Title = "Snap Line Color",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.SnaplineColor = Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            if snapline then snapline.Color = flags.SnaplineColor end
        end
    })

    -- BEGIN SNAPLINE ORIGIN CONTROL
    if flags.SnaplineOrigin == "Hands" then flags.SnaplineOrigin = "Gun Barrel" end
    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddDropdown("SnaplineOrigin", {
        Text = "Snap Line Origin",
        Values = {"Crosshair", "Gun Barrel", "Bottom of Screen", "Top of Screen"},
        Default = flags.SnaplineOrigin or "Gun Barrel",
        Callback = function(Value) flags.SnaplineOrigin = Value == "Hands" and "Gun Barrel" or Value end
    })
    -- END SNAPLINE ORIGIN CONTROL

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddSlider("snaplinethickness", {
        Text = "Snap Line Thickness", Min = 1, Max = 10, Default = flags.SnaplineThickness or 1, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.SnaplineThickness = Value
            if snapline then snapline.Thickness = Value end
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddSlider("snaplinetransparency", {
        Text = "Snap Line Transparency", Min = 0.0, Max = 1, Default = flags.SnaplineTransparency or 1, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(Value) flags.SnaplineTransparency = Value end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddSlider("snaplinebloom", {
        Text = "Snap Line Glow Intensity", Min = 0.0, Max = 1, Default = flags.SnaplineBloom or 0, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(Value) flags.SnaplineBloom = Value end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddDropdown("aimpartdropdown", {
        Values = {
            "Head",
            "UpperTorso",
            "LowerTorso",
            "LeftUpperArm",
            "LeftLowerArm",
            "RightLowerArm",
            "RightLowerLeg",
            "LeftLowerLeg"
        },
        Default = 1,
        Text = "Target Part",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.AimPart = Value
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("SkinWalker", function()
    local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local CustomMeshCharacter = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):WaitForChild("GunSystemPlugins", 10):WaitForChild("CustomMeshCharacter", 10))

    if not Maid then
        Maid = {
            Tasks = {},
            AddTask = function(self, task)
                table.insert(self.Tasks, task)
                return task
            end
        }
    end

    local NeckStretchAmount = 0
    local NeckStretchEnabled = false
    local NeckStretchTeamCheck = false
    local NeckStretchType = "Manual"

    local StretchX = 1
    local StretchY = 1
    local StretchZ = 1

    local OffsetX = 0
    local OffsetY = 0
    local OffsetZ = 0

    local function get_entity_list()
        return Runtime:GetEntities() or (CustomMeshCharacter.GetCharacters and CustomMeshCharacter:GetCharacters()) or {}
    end

    local cachedRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    cachedRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    cachedRayParams.IgnoreWater = true
    local cachedIgnore, localWorldModels = {}, {}

    local function IsVisibleAtPosition(testPosition, OriginPos, extraIgnore)
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        OriginPos = OriginPos or (camera and camera.CFrame.Position) or Vector3.new()
        
        table.clear(cachedIgnore)
        if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character then
            table.insert(cachedIgnore, LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        if camera then table.insert(cachedIgnore, camera) end
        for _, model in ipairs(localWorldModels) do
            if model.Parent then table.insert(cachedIgnore, model) end
        end
        if typeof(extraIgnore) == 'table' then
            for _, v in ipairs(extraIgnore) do 
                if typeof(v) == 'Instance' then 
                    table.insert(cachedIgnore, v) 
                end 
            end
        elseif typeof(extraIgnore) == 'Instance' then 
            table.insert(cachedIgnore, extraIgnore) 
        end
        
        cachedRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = cachedIgnore
        
        local direction = testPosition - OriginPos
        if direction.Magnitude == 0 then return true, nil end
        
        local result = workspace:Raycast(OriginPos, direction, cachedRayParams)
        if not result then return true, nil end
        return false, result
    end

    local function IsTeammate(player)
        if not player then return true end
        if player == LocalPlayer then return true end

        local success1, mySquad = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        local success2, theirSquad = pcall(function() return player:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        if success1 and success2 and mySquad and mySquad ~= "" and mySquad == theirSquad then
            return true
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Team and not LocalPlayer.Neutral and not player.Neutral and player.Team == LocalPlayer.Team then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end

    local function GetPlayerCharacters(Player)
        local characters = {}
        if Player then
            if CustomMeshCharacter and CustomMeshCharacter.GetCharacters then
                local allChars = CustomMeshCharacter:GetCharacters()
                for _, Data in ipairs(allChars) do
                    if Data.Player == Player then
                        table.insert(characters, Data.WorldModel)
                    end
                end
            else
                local character = Player.Character
                if character then
                    table.insert(characters, character)
                end
            end
        end
        return characters
    end

    local skin = CreateSkinWalker(function(model, frame)
        local expander = Runtime.HitboxExpander
        if expander then expander:UpdateModelPose(model, frame) end
    end)
    Runtime.SkinWalker = skin
    local function restoreStretched() skin:Clear() end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, restoreStretched)
    local auto = CreateSkinAuto(skin, function(position, origin, model)
        return IsVisibleAtPosition(position, origin, model)
    end, os.clock)
    local function eligible(data)
        local player, model = data.Player, data.WorldModel or data.Character
        if not player or player == LocalPlayer or not player.Parent or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
            or data.IsDead or data.Dead or (NeckStretchTeamCheck and IsTeammate(player))
            or not model or not model.Parent then return end
        return model
    end
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not NeckStretchEnabled or NeckStretchAmount <= 0 or NeckStretchType ~= "Auto" then return end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then auto:Clear(); return end
        local models, seen = {}, {}
        table.clear(localWorldModels)
        for _, data in pairs(get_entity_list()) do
            if data.Player == LocalPlayer then
                local model = data.WorldModel
                if model then table.insert(localWorldModels, model) end
            end
            local model = eligible(data)
            if model and not seen[model] then seen[model] = true; table.insert(models, model) end
        end
        auto:Update(models, NeckStretchAmount * 0.8, camera.CFrame.Position)
    end)

    local skinGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.25)
    local skinEntities = {}
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not NeckStretchEnabled or NeckStretchAmount <= 0 then return end
        if not workspace.CurrentCamera then return end
        if skinGate:Ready(os.clock()) then
            skinEntities = get_entity_list()
        end
        local seen = {}
        for _, data in pairs(skinEntities) do
            local model = eligible(data)
            if not model then continue end
            local x, y, z = OffsetX, OffsetY, OffsetZ
            if NeckStretchType == "Auto" then
                local offset = auto:GetOffset(model)
                x, y, z = offset.X, offset.Y, offset.Z
            end
            if skin:Apply(model, Vector3.new(x, y + NeckStretchAmount * 0.8, z)) then seen[model] = true end
        end
        for model in pairs(skin.Entries) do if not seen[model] then skin:Remove(model) end end
    end)

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddToggle("NeckStretchEnabled", {
        Text = "SkinWalker",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(State)
            NeckStretchEnabled = State
            auto:Clear()
            skinGate.Next = -math.huge
            if not State then restoreStretched() end
        end
    }):AddKeyPicker("SkinWalkerKeyPicker", {
        Text = "SkinWalker", Default = "None", Mode = "Toggle", ShowInKeybindsList = true,
        Callback = function()
            Toggles.NeckStretchEnabled:SetValue(not Toggles.NeckStretchEnabled.Value)
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddDropdown("NeckStretchType", {
        Text = "Type",
        Values = {"Manual", "Auto"},
        Default = "Manual",
        Callback = function(Value)
            NeckStretchType = Value
            auto:Clear()
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddToggle("NeckStretchTeamCheck", {
        Text = "Team Check",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(State)
            NeckStretchTeamCheck = State
            auto:Clear()
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddSlider("NeckStretchAmount", {
        Text = "Float Height",
        Min = 0,
        Max = 3,
        Default = 0,
        Rounding = 1,
        Suffix = "s",
        Callback = function(Value)
            NeckStretchAmount = tonumber(Value) or 0
            auto:Clear()
            if NeckStretchAmount <= 0 then restoreStretched() end
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddSlider("OffsetX", {
        Text = "Neck Offset X",
        Min = -2.5,
        Max = 2.5,
        Default = 0,
        Rounding = 1,
        Suffix = "s",
        Callback = function(Value)
            OffsetX = tonumber(Value) or 0
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddSlider("OffsetY", {
        Text = "Neck Offset Y",
        Min = -2.5,
        Max = 2.5,
        Default = 0,
        Rounding = 1,
        Suffix = "s",
        Callback = function(Value)
            OffsetY = tonumber(Value) or 0
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddSlider("OffsetZ", {
        Text = "Neck Offset Z",
        Min = -2.5,
        Max = 2.5,
        Default = 0,
        Rounding = 1,
        Suffix = "s",
        Callback = function(Value)
            OffsetZ = tonumber(Value) or 0
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Hitbox expander", function()
    local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local runService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local meshCharacters = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):WaitForChild("GunSystemPlugins", 10):WaitForChild("CustomMeshCharacter", 10))
    local enabled, radius = false, 5
    local manager = CreateHitboxExpander(Runtime, workspace, player)
    Runtime.HitboxExpander = manager
    local refresh, selection = CreateRefreshGate(0.25), CreateRefreshGate(0.05)
    local lastTarget
    local function sync(force)
        if not selection:Ready(os.clock(), force) then return end
        local entities = Runtime:GetEntities() or meshCharacters:GetCharacters() or {}
        local settings = Environment.flags or {}
        local target
        -- Honor a crosshair lock when aim assistance is off; otherwise use the
        -- same FOV and aim-part selector as Aimbot/Silent Aim.
        local locked = not settings.Enabled and Runtime.CrosshairTarget
        if settings.RageBotEnabled and Runtime.GetRageTarget then
            local _, selected = Runtime.GetRageTarget()
            target = selected
        elseif locked and locked.Parent then
            for _, entity in pairs(entities) do
                if entity.WorldModel == locked.Parent or entity.Character == locked.Parent then
                    target = entity.Player
                    break
                end
            end
        elseif type(Runtime.GetAimTarget) == "function" then
            local ok, part, selected = pcall(Runtime.GetAimTarget, settings.FovSize or 120, settings.AimPart or "Head")
            if ok and part and part.Parent then target = selected end
            if not ok then Runtime:Report("Hitbox target", part) end
        end
        if target ~= lastTarget or refresh:Ready(os.clock()) then
            lastTarget = target
            manager:Sync(entities, settings.teamcheck_esp, target)
        end
    end
    Runtime:Connect(runService.RenderStepped, function()
        if not enabled then return end
        sync()
        manager:UpdatePose()
    end)
    Runtime:Connect(runService.Heartbeat, function() manager:BuildPending() end)
    if meshCharacters.CharacterAdded then Runtime:Connect(meshCharacters.CharacterAdded, function() refresh.Next = -math.huge end) end
    if meshCharacters.CharacterRemoved then
        Runtime:Connect(meshCharacters.CharacterRemoved, function(_, _, model) if model then manager:Remove(model) end end)
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() manager:Clear(); table.clear(manager.Geometries) end)
    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddToggle("HitboxExpander", {
        Text = "Hitbox Expander", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Adds native-sized head query probes with a ForceField shell. Large radii use lower probe density for performance. Server hit validation still applies.",
        Callback = function(value)
            enabled = value
            manager:Configure(enabled, radius)
            refresh.Next = -math.huge
            if enabled then sync(true) end
        end,
    }):AddKeyPicker("HitboxExpanderKeyPicker", {
        Text = "Hitbox Expander", Default = "None", Mode = "Toggle", ShowInKeybindsList = true, ForceInKeybindsList = true,
        Callback = function()
            Toggles.HitboxExpander:SetValue(not Toggles.HitboxExpander.Value)
        end,
    }):AddColorPicker("HitboxExpanderColor", {
        Text = "ForceField Color", Default = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251),
        Callback = function(color) manager:SetColor(color) end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Skin:AddSlider("HitboxRadius", {
        Text = "Hitbox Radius", Min = 1, Max = 20, Default = radius, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value)
            radius = tonumber(value) or radius
            manager:Configure(enabled, radius)
            refresh.Next = -math.huge
            if enabled then sync(true) end
        end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Inventory viewer", function()
    local CorePlayers = game:GetService("Players")
    local HeartbeatService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local PointerService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local ClientPlayer = CorePlayers.LocalPlayer
    local CurrentViewport = workspace.CurrentCamera

    local MeshHandler = require(game.ReplicatedFirst:WaitForChild("GunSystemPlugins", 10):WaitForChild("CustomMeshCharacter", 10))
    local UserRoster = require(game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("CustomCharacter", 10):WaitForChild("PlayerList", 10))

    local panelMultiplier = 1.21
    local deltaXOffset, deltaYOffset = 0, 0
    local inspector = nil
    local activeSignals = {}

    local function IsTeammate(player)
        if not player then return true end
        if player == ClientPlayer then return true end

        local success1, mySquad = pcall(function() return ClientPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        local success2, theirSquad = pcall(function() return player:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        if success1 and success2 and mySquad and theirSquad and mySquad == theirSquad then
            return true
        end

        local successRank1, myRank = pcall(function() return ClientPlayer:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        local successRank2, theirRank = pcall(function() return player:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        if successRank1 and successRank2 and myRank and theirRank and myRank > 0 and theirRank > 0 then
            return true
        end
        
        return false
    end

    local function fetchPlayerNameFromWorld(worldEntity)
        local mappedChar = MeshHandler:GetCharacterFromWorldCharacter(worldEntity)
        local rosterNode = mappedChar and UserRoster:GetPlayerFromCharacter(mappedChar)
        return rosterNode and rosterNode.Name or nil
    end

    local function pullPlayerLoadout(targetName)
        local targetUser = CorePlayers:FindFirstChild(targetName)
        local storageBin = targetUser and targetUser:FindFirstChild("GunInventory")
        if not storageBin then return {} end

        local entries = {}
        for _, itemNode in ipairs(storageBin:GetChildren()) do
            if itemNode:IsA("ObjectValue") and itemNode.Value then
                local weaponName = itemNode.Value.Name
                local opticNode = itemNode:FindFirstChild("AttachmentReticle")
                local opticDescription = (opticNode and opticNode.Value and tostring(opticNode.Value)) or "Standard"
                
                local clip = itemNode:FindFirstChild("BulletsInMagazine")
                local stockpile = itemNode:FindFirstChild("BulletsInReserve")
                
                local lineString = string.format("[%s] %s", weaponName, opticDescription)
                if clip and stockpile and clip:IsA("IntValue") and stockpile:IsA("IntValue") then
                    lineString = string.format("%s (%d/%d)", lineString, clip.Value, stockpile.Value)
                end

                table.insert(entries, lineString)
            end
        end
        return entries
    end

    local function locateTargettedUser()
        local minimumDistance = math.huge
        local chosenTargetName, chosenModel = nil, nil
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return nil end
        local midpoint = GetFovCenter(camera)

        for _, worldEntity in ipairs(MeshHandler:GetWorldCharacters()) do
            if worldEntity and worldEntity.Parent then
                local entityName = fetchPlayerNameFromWorld(worldEntity)
                if entityName and entityName ~= ClientPlayer.Name then
                    local userObj = CorePlayers:FindFirstChild(entityName)
                    if not userObj or userObj:GetAttribute("Dead") then continue end
                    if (flags.teamcheck_inventory or flags.TeamCheck) and IsTeammate(userObj) then continue end
                    local humanoid = worldEntity:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local health = worldEntity:GetAttribute("Health")
                    if (humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0) or (type(health) == "number" and health <= 0) then continue end
                    local targetPart = Runtime:GetAimPart(worldEntity, worldEntity, flags.AimPart or "Head")
                    if not targetPart or not ProjectFovPoint(camera, Runtime:GetAimPosition(targetPart), flags.FovSize) then continue end

                    local originPart = worldEntity:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if originPart then
                        local screenCoords, visibleCheck = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(originPart.Position)
                        if visibleCheck and screenCoords.Z > 0 then
                            local distanceOffset = (Vector2.new(screenCoords.X, screenCoords.Y) - midpoint).Magnitude
                            distanceOffset = TargetPriority(camera, ClientPlayer, originPart.Position, distanceOffset, flags.TargetClosestPlayers)
                            if distanceOffset < minimumDistance then
                                minimumDistance = distanceOffset
                                chosenTargetName, chosenModel = entityName, worldEntity
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return chosenTargetName, chosenModel
    end
    Runtime.GetGazeTarget = locateTargettedUser

    local function repositionPanel()
        if not inspector then return end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
        inspector:SetPosition(Vector2.new(
            math.max(0, viewport.X - inspector.Width * panelMultiplier - 25 + deltaXOffset),
            math.max(0, 25 + deltaYOffset)
        ))
    end
    local function shutdownInspector()
        Runtime.InventoryTargetName, Runtime.InventoryTargetModel = nil, nil
        Runtime.InventoryViewerEnabled = false
        for _, connection in ipairs(activeSignals) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(activeSignals)
        if inspector then inspector:Destroy(); inspector = nil end
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, shutdownInspector)
    local function initializeInspector()
        shutdownInspector()
        inspector = CreateInventoryPanel(Vitality)
        Runtime.InventoryViewerEnabled = true
        inspector:SetScale(panelMultiplier)
        repositionPanel()
        local dragging, dragStart, anchor = false, nil, nil
        table.insert(activeSignals, Runtime:Connect(inspector.Header.Instance.InputBegan, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
                anchor = inspector.Root.Instance.AbsolutePosition
            end
        end))
        table.insert(activeSignals, Runtime:Connect(PointerService.InputEnded, function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end))
        table.insert(activeSignals, Runtime:Connect(PointerService.InputChanged, function(input)
            if not dragging or not inspector then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local position = anchor + Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y) - dragStart
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not camera then return end
                local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
                inspector:SetPosition(Vector2.new(
                    math.clamp(position.X, 0, math.max(0, viewport.X - inspector.Width * panelMultiplier)),
                    math.clamp(position.Y, 0, math.max(0, viewport.Y - inspector.Height * panelMultiplier))
                ))
            end
        end))
        local elapsed = 0.15
        table.insert(activeSignals, Runtime:Connect(HeartbeatService.RenderStepped, function(dt)
            elapsed += dt
            if elapsed < 0.15 then return end
            elapsed = 0
            CurrentViewport = workspace.CurrentCamera or CurrentViewport
            if not CurrentViewport or not inspector then return end
            local target, model = locateTargettedUser()
            Runtime.InventoryTargetName, Runtime.InventoryTargetModel = target, model
            inspector:Update(target, target and pullPlayerLoadout(target) or {})
        end))
        table.insert(activeSignals, Runtime:Connect(CorePlayers.PlayerRemoving, function(player)
            if player == ClientPlayer then shutdownInspector() end
        end))
    end

    GroupBoxes.InvViewerSection:AddToggle('inventoryveiwer', {
        Text = 'Inventory Viewer', 
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(toggledState)
            if toggledState then initializeInspector() else shutdownInspector() end
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.InvViewerSection:AddToggle('teamcheck_inventory', {
        Text = 'Team Check', 
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(toggledState)
            flags.teamcheck_inventory = toggledState
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.InvViewerSection:AddSlider('invscaleslider', {
        Text = 'Inventory Scale', Rounding = 2, Min = 0.5, Max = 3.0, Default = 1.21, 
        Callback = function(sliderValue)
            panelMultiplier = sliderValue
            if inspector then inspector:SetScale(panelMultiplier); repositionPanel() end
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.InvViewerSection:AddSlider('InvViewerPosXSlider', {
        Text = 'Horizontal Offset', Min = -2000, Max = 100, Default = deltaXOffset, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(sliderValue) deltaXOffset = sliderValue; repositionPanel() end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.InvViewerSection:AddSlider('InvViewerPosYSlider', {
        Text = 'Vertical Offset', Min = -100, Max = 1000, Default = deltaYOffset, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(sliderValue) deltaYOffset = sliderValue; repositionPanel() end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Gun mods", function()
    nobob = false
    norecoil = false
    nobobEnabled = false
    local nobobTables = {}
    local swayOriginals, recoilOriginals, instantAimOriginals = {}, {}, {}

    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle('togglenobob',{
        Text = 'No Gun Bob',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            Library:Notify(state and "Gun Bob Removed" or "Gun Bob Restored", 2)
            nobobEnabled = state
            
            for _, tbl in getgc(true) do
                if type(tbl) == 'table' and rawget(tbl, 'BobSpeed') then
                    if state then
                        if not nobobTables[tbl] then nobobTables[tbl] = {
                            BobSpeed = tbl.BobSpeed,
                            BobAmplitudeHorizontal = tbl.BobAmplitudeHorizontal,
                            BobAmplitudeVertical = tbl.BobAmplitudeVertical,
                            MovementOffset = tbl.MovementOffset,
                            CrouchOffset = tbl.CrouchOffset,
                            TransitionRate = tbl.TransitionRate,
                            TransitionRateCrouch = tbl.TransitionRateCrouch,
                            BobPower = tbl.BobPower
                        } end
                        
                        tbl.BobSpeed = 0
                        tbl.BobAmplitudeHorizontal = 0
                        tbl.BobAmplitudeVertical = 0
                        tbl.MovementOffset = Vector3.new()
                        tbl.CrouchOffset = Vector3.new()
                        tbl.TransitionRate = 0
                        tbl.TransitionRateCrouch = 0
                        tbl.BobPower = 0
                    else
                        local saved = nobobTables[tbl]
                        if saved then
                            tbl.BobSpeed = saved.BobSpeed
                            tbl.BobAmplitudeHorizontal = saved.BobAmplitudeHorizontal
                            tbl.BobAmplitudeVertical = saved.BobAmplitudeVertical
                            tbl.MovementOffset = saved.MovementOffset
                            tbl.CrouchOffset = saved.CrouchOffset
                            tbl.TransitionRate = saved.TransitionRate
                            tbl.TransitionRateCrouch = saved.TransitionRateCrouch
                            tbl.BobPower = saved.BobPower
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle('nogunsway',{
        Text = 'No Gun Sway',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
        Library:Notify(state and "Gun Sway Removed" or "Gun Sway Restored", 2)
        if state then
            for _, obj in getgc(true) do
                if type(obj) == "table" and type(rawget(obj, "_positionVelocity")) == "function" then
                    if not swayOriginals[obj] then swayOriginals[obj] = obj._positionVelocity end
                    obj._positionVelocity = function() return Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero end
                end
            end
        else
            for obj, original in pairs(swayOriginals) do obj._positionVelocity = original end
            table.clear(swayOriginals)
        end
    end})

        GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle('norecoiltoggle',{
        Text = 'No Recoil',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            Library:Notify(state and "Recoil Removed" or "Recoil Restored", 2)
            norecoil = state
            if state then
                for _, object in getgc(true) do
                    if type(object) == "table" and rawget(object, "_damper") == 0.75 then
                        if not recoilOriginals[object] then
                            recoilOriginals[object] = { Impulse = object.Impulse, SetPosition = object.SetPosition }
                        end
                        if type(object.Impulse) == "function" then object.Impulse = function() end end
                        if type(object.SetPosition) == "function" then object.SetPosition = function() end end
                    end
                end
            else
                for object, saved in pairs(recoilOriginals) do
                    object.Impulse, object.SetPosition = saved.Impulse, saved.SetPosition
                end
                table.clear(recoilOriginals)
            end
        end,
    })

    local gunModsState = { instantAim = false }

    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle('InstantAimToggle', {
        Text = 'Instant Aim',
        Default = false,
        Tooltip = 'Makes aiming instant with no delay',
        Callback = function(state)
            gunModsState.instantAim = state
            
            if state then
                Library:Notify("Instant Aim On", 3)
                
                for _, v in next, getgc(true) do
                    if type(v) == 'table' then
                        for i, val in next, v do
                            if type(i) == 'string' and string.find(i, 'GunAim') then
                                if type(val) == 'number' then
                                    instantAimOriginals[v] = instantAimOriginals[v] or {}
                                    if instantAimOriginals[v][i] == nil then instantAimOriginals[v][i] = val end
                                    v[i] = 100000000
                                elseif type(val) == 'function' then
                                    instantAimOriginals[v] = instantAimOriginals[v] or {}
                                    if instantAimOriginals[v][i] == nil then
                                        instantAimOriginals[v][i] = { Target = val, Original = hookfunction(val, function() return 100000000 end) }
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                for object, saved in pairs(instantAimOriginals) do
                    for key, original in pairs(saved) do
                        if type(original) == "table" then executorHook(original.Target, original.Original)
                        else object[key] = original end
                    end
                end
                table.clear(instantAimOriginals)
                Library:Notify("Instant Aim Off", 3)
            end
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("World objects", function()
    local LeavesTransparency = 1
    local TrunkTransparency = 0.5
    local LeavesEnabled = false
    local TrunkEnabled = false
    local originalVisibility = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local treeConnection
    local grassTerrain
    local grassDecoration

    local function setTreeVisibility(part, value)
        if originalVisibility[part] == nil then originalVisibility[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier end
        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = value
    end

    local function restorePart(part)
        local value = originalVisibility[part]
        if value ~= nil then
            if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = value end
            originalVisibility[part] = nil
        end
    end

    local function applyPart(part)
        if not part:IsA("MeshPart") then return end
        if part.Name:find("Leaves") then
            if LeavesEnabled then setTreeVisibility(part, LeavesTransparency) else restorePart(part) end
        elseif part.Name:find("Trunk") then
            if TrunkEnabled then setTreeVisibility(part, TrunkTransparency) else restorePart(part) end
        end
    end

    local function getTrees()
        local assets = workspace:FindFirstChild("world_assets")
        local objects = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("StaticObjects")
        return objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Trees")
    end

    local function updateTrees()
        -- Restore disabled categories even if their model moved out of Trees.
        for part in pairs(originalVisibility) do
            if (part.Name:find("Leaves") and not LeavesEnabled)
                or (part.Name:find("Trunk") and not TrunkEnabled) then
                restorePart(part)
            end
        end
        if not (LeavesEnabled or TrunkEnabled) then return end
        local trees = getTrees()
        if not trees then return end
        for _, tree in ipairs(trees:GetChildren()) do
            if tree:IsA("Model") and not tree.Name:match("^Bush") then
                for _, part in ipairs(tree:GetDescendants()) do applyPart(part) end
            end
        end
    end

    local function updateListener()
        if not (LeavesEnabled or TrunkEnabled) then
            if treeConnection then treeConnection:Disconnect(); treeConnection = nil end
        elseif not treeConnection then
            treeConnection = Runtime:Connect(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(part)
                if not part:IsA("MeshPart") then return end
                local trees = getTrees()
                if not trees then return end
                local tree = part.Parent
                while tree and tree.Parent ~= trees do tree = tree.Parent end
                if tree and tree:IsA("Model") and not tree.Name:match("^Bush") then applyPart(part) end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function restoreGrass()
        if grassTerrain then
            sethiddenproperty(grassTerrain, "Decoration", grassDecoration)
            grassTerrain, grassDecoration = nil, nil
        end
    end

    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        if treeConnection then treeConnection:Disconnect(); treeConnection = nil end
        for part in pairs(originalVisibility) do restorePart(part) end
        restoreGrass()
    end)

    GroupBoxes.WorldShit:AddToggle('TrunkTransparencyEnabled', {
        Text = 'Trunk Transparency',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            TrunkEnabled = value
            updateListener()
            updateTrees()
        end
    })
    GroupBoxes.WorldShit:AddSlider('TrunkTransparency', {
        Text = 'Trunk Transparency Amount',
        Min = 0,
        Max = 1,
        Default = 0.5,
        Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value)
            TrunkTransparency = value
            if TrunkEnabled then updateTrees() end
        end
    })
    GroupBoxes.WorldShit:AddToggle('GrassRemover', {
        Text = 'Remove Grass',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            if not value then restoreGrass(); return end
            local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChild("Terrain")
            if not terrain then return end
            if grassTerrain ~= terrain then
                restoreGrass()
                local ok, original = pcall(function()
                    if type(gethiddenproperty) == "function" then return gethiddenproperty(terrain, "Decoration") end
                    return terrain.Decoration
                end)
                assert(ok and type(original) == "boolean", "Remove Grass requires readable Terrain.Decoration to restore it safely")
                grassTerrain, grassDecoration = terrain, original
            end
            sethiddenproperty(terrain, "Decoration", false)
        end
    })
    GroupBoxes.WorldShit:AddToggle('LeavesTransparencyEnabled', {
        Text = 'Leaves Transparency',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            LeavesEnabled = value
            updateListener()
            updateTrees()
        end
    })
    GroupBoxes.WorldShit:AddSlider('LeavesTransparency', {
        Text = 'Leaves Transparency Amount',
        Min = 0,
        Max = 1,
        Default = 1,
        Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value)
            LeavesTransparency = value
            if LeavesEnabled then updateTrees() end
        end
    })
end)
local function SelectPlayerAuraTarget(runtime, world, players, origin, radius, teamCheck, aimPart)
    if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" or type(radius) ~= "number" or radius ~= radius or radius <= 0 then return end
    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    if not localPlayer or localPlayer:GetAttribute("Dead") or localPlayer:GetAttribute("StartLoading") then return end
    local part, point
    local distance = radius
    local seen = {}
    local function consider(player, entity)
        if not player or seen[player] then return end
        seen[player] = true
        if player == players.LocalPlayer or player.Parent ~= players or player:GetAttribute("Dead")
            or player:GetAttribute("StartLoading") or entity.IsDead or entity.Dead then return end
        if teamCheck then
            local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
            local squad = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName")
            if (localPlayer.Team and player.Team == localPlayer.Team)
                or (squad and squad ~= "" and squad == player:GetAttribute("SquadName")) then return end
        end
        local model = entity.WorldModel or entity.Character or player.Character
        if not model or not model.Parent then return end
        local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        local health = model:GetAttribute("Health")
        if type(health) == "number" and health <= 0 then return end
        local character = entity.Character or model
        local targetPart = runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, aimPart or "UpperTorso")
            or runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, "Torso")
            or runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, "LowerTorso")
            or runtime:GetAimPart(character, model, "Head")
        if not targetPart then return end
        local position = runtime:GetAimPosition(targetPart)
        local delta = (position - origin).Magnitude
        if delta > 0.001 and delta <= distance then part, point, distance = targetPart, position, delta end
    end
    for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do consider(entity.Player, entity) end
    for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if not seen[player] then consider(player, {Character = player.Character}) end
    end
    return part, point, part and "Player" or nil
end
local function SelectKillAuraTarget(runtime, world, players, origin, config, teamCheck)
    local part, point, kind
    local distance = config.Distance
    if config.PlayersEnabled then
        part, point, kind = SelectPlayerAuraTarget(runtime, world, players, origin, distance, teamCheck, config.PlayerPart)
        if point then distance = (point - origin).Magnitude end
    end
    if config.Enabled then
