        local assets = world:FindFirstChild("game_assets")
        local entities = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Entities")
        local playerModels = {}
        for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do if player.Character then playerModels[player.Character] = true end end
        for _, entity in pairs(runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            if entity.Player then
                if entity.Character then playerModels[entity.Character] = true end
                if entity.WorldModel then playerModels[entity.WorldModel] = true end
            end
        end
        for _, model in ipairs(entities and entities:GetChildren() or {}) do
            local root, head = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), model:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if playerModels[model] or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or not root or not root:IsA("BasePart")
                or root.CollisionGroup == "PropGroup" or not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") then continue end
            local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local health = model:GetAttribute("Health")
            if (humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0) or (type(health) == "number" and health <= 0) then continue end
            local delta = (root.Position - origin).Magnitude
            if delta <= distance then part, point, kind, distance = head, head.Position, "Zombie", delta end
        end
    end
    return part, point, kind
end
Runtime:Init("Kill aura", function()
    local Config = {
        Enabled = false,
        PlayersEnabled = false,
        PlayerPart = "UpperTorso",
        Distance = 15,
        TracerEnabled = false,
        TracerLifetime = 1,
        TracerThickness = 0.2,
        TracerTransparency = 0,
        TracerColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 150, 255)
    }

    local CharacterState = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("CustomCharacter", 10):WaitForChild("CustomCharacterState", 10))
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

    local activeTracers = {}
    local function clearTracers()
        for dispose in pairs(activeTracers) do dispose() end
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, clearTracers)
    local function createTracer(targetHead)
        if not Config.TracerEnabled or not targetHead or not targetHead.Parent then return end
        local startWorldPos = CaptureKillAuraTraceOrigin(Runtime, LocalPlayer, workspace.CurrentCamera)
        local endWorldPos = Runtime:GetDisplayPart(targetHead).Position
        if typeof(startWorldPos) ~= "Vector3" or typeof(endWorldPos) ~= "Vector3"
            or (endWorldPos - startWorldPos).Magnitude < 0.001 then return end

        local tracerPart = Instance.new("Part")
        tracerPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
        tracerPart.Transparency, tracerPart.Anchored = 1, true
        tracerPart.CanCollide, tracerPart.CanTouch, tracerPart.CanQuery = false, false, false
        tracerPart.CastShadow = false
        tracerPart.CFrame, tracerPart.Parent = CFrame.new(startWorldPos), workspace
        local endPart = tracerPart:Clone()
        endPart.CFrame, endPart.Parent = CFrame.new(endWorldPos), workspace
        local att1, att2 = Instance.new("Attachment"), Instance.new("Attachment")
        att1.Parent, att2.Parent = tracerPart, endPart
        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Attachment0, beam.Attachment1 = att1, att2
        beam.FaceCamera, beam.LightEmission, beam.LightInfluence = true, 1, 0
        local function applyStyle()
            beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(Config.TracerColor)
            beam.Width0, beam.Width1 = Config.TracerThickness, Config.TracerThickness
            beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(math.clamp(Config.TracerTransparency, 0, 1))
        end
        applyStyle()
        beam.Parent = tracerPart
        local connection
        local function dispose()
            if not activeTracers[dispose] then return end
            activeTracers[dispose] = nil
            if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
            tracerPart:Destroy()
            endPart:Destroy()
        end
        activeTracers[dispose] = true
        local elapsed, lifetime = 0, math.max(Config.TracerLifetime, 0.05)
        connection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt)
            elapsed += dt
            if elapsed >= lifetime or not Config.TracerEnabled then dispose(); return end
            applyStyle()
        end)
    end

    local TestMelee
    for _, v in getgc(true) do
        if type(v) == "function" then
            local success, info = pcall(getinfo, v)
            if success and info and info.name == "TestMelee" then
                TestMelee = v
                break
            end
        end
    end

    local activeStates = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local cancelled = {}
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        Config.Enabled, Config.PlayersEnabled = false, false
        for state, saved in pairs(activeStates) do
            if state.GetEyeTransform == saved.Redirected then state.GetEyeTransform = saved.Original end
        end
        table.clear(activeStates)
    end)
    if TestMelee then
        if isfunctionhooked(TestMelee) then restorefunction(TestMelee) end
        local old
        old = hookfunction(TestMelee, newcclosure(function(self, p5, p6, p7)
            if not Runtime.Active or not (Config.Enabled or Config.PlayersEnabled) then return old(self, p5, p6, p7) end
            local state = CharacterState:Get(p5)
            if activeStates[state] then return end
            if not state or type(state.GetEyeTransform) ~= "function" then return old(self, p5, p6, p7) end
            local original = state.GetEyeTransform
            local transform = original(state)
            local target = SelectKillAuraTarget(Runtime, workspace, Players, transform.Position, Config, flags.TeamCheck)
            if not target then return old(self, p5, p6, p7) end
            local function redirected(inst)
                if not (Config.Enabled or Config.PlayersEnabled) or not Runtime.Active then error(cancelled, 0) end
                local eye = original(inst)
                local part, point, kind = SelectKillAuraTarget(Runtime, workspace, Players, eye.Position, Config, flags.TeamCheck)
                if not part then target = nil; return eye end
                target = part
                if kind == "Zombie" then
                    -- Preserve the original zombie aura's hit transform.
                    local delta = point - eye.Position
                    if delta.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return eye end
                    return CFrame.lookAt(point - delta.Unit * 0.5, point)
                end
                return CFrame.lookAt(eye.Position, point)
            end
            activeStates[state] = { Original = original, Redirected = redirected }
            state.GetEyeTransform = redirected
            local results = table.pack(pcall(old, self, p5, p6, p7))
            if state.GetEyeTransform == redirected then state.GetEyeTransform = original end
            activeStates[state] = nil
            if not results[1] then
                if results[2] == cancelled then return end
                error(results[2], 0)
            end
            createTracer(target)
            return table.unpack(results, 2, results.n)
        end))
    end

    local function setAuraEnabled(field, flag, value)
        Config[field] = value and TestMelee ~= nil
        if value and not TestMelee then
            Library:Notify("Native melee function unavailable", 3)
            if Toggles[flag] then Toggles[flag]:SetValue(false) end
            return
        end
    end
    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddToggle("killaura_toggle", {
        Text = "Zombie Kill Aura", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Original zombie aura. Uses the shared radius and tracer settings.",
        Callback = function(value) setAuraEnabled("Enabled", "killaura_toggle", value) end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddToggle("killaura_players", {
        Text = "Player Kill Aura", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Aims normal melee swings at players, including seated players. Torso aim can put the swing through the car body. Radius selects targets; native melee reach still applies.",
        Callback = function(value) setAuraEnabled("PlayersEnabled", "killaura_players", value) end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddDropdown("killaura_player_part", {
        Text = "Player Aura Hit Part", Values = {"Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}, Default = Config.PlayerPart,
        Tooltip = "UpperTorso aims lower than the head for occupied cars. Damage still depends on the game's native melee collision.",
        Callback = function(value)
            if value == "Head" or value == "UpperTorso" or value == "LowerTorso" then Config.PlayerPart = value end
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddSlider("killaura_distance", {
        Text = "Radius Distance", Default = 15, Min = 1, Max = 500, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(v) Config.Distance = v end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddToggle("tracer_toggle", {
        Text = "Hit Tracer", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) 
            Config.TracerEnabled = v
            if not v then clearTracers() end
            Library:Notify(v and "Tracer Enabled" or "Tracer Disabled", 3, v and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0))
        end
    }):AddColorPicker("tracer_color", {
        Default = Config.TracerColor,
        Title = "Tracer Color",
        Callback = function(v)
            Config.TracerColor = v
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddSlider("tracer_lifetime", {
        Text = "Tracer Lifetime", Default = 1, Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(v) Config.TracerLifetime = v end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddSlider("tracer_thickness", {
        Text = "Tracer Thickness", Default = 0.2, Min = 0.05, Max = 1, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(v) Config.TracerThickness = v end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Kill:AddSlider("tracer_transparency", {
        Text = "Tracer Opacity", Default = 0, Min = 0, Max = 1, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(v) Config.TracerTransparency = v end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Anti gameplay paused", function()
    local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local enabled, rootConnection, guiConnection = false, nil, nil
    local function disconnect()
        if rootConnection then rootConnection:Disconnect(); rootConnection = nil end
        if guiConnection then guiConnection:Disconnect(); guiConnection = nil end
    end
    local function attach(gui)
        if guiConnection then guiConnection:Disconnect(); guiConnection = nil end
        if not enabled or not gui then return end
        local function remove(script)
            if enabled and script.Name == "CoreScripts/NetworkPause" then script:Destroy() end
        end
        guiConnection = gui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(script)
            Runtime:Handle("Anti gameplay paused", remove, script)
        end)
        local script = gui:FindFirstChild("CoreScripts/NetworkPause")
        if script then Runtime:Handle("Anti gameplay paused", remove, script) end
    end
    GroupBoxes.Bunker:AddToggle("BunkerRemover", {
        Text = "Bunker", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Removes Roblox's local NetworkPause UI script. Turning off stops removal; removed scripts return after rejoining.",
        Callback = function(value)
            disconnect()
            enabled = value
            if not value then return end
            rootConnection = coreGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(gui)
                if enabled and gui.Name == "RobloxGui" then
                    Runtime:Handle("Anti gameplay paused", attach, gui)
                end
            end)
            attach(coreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui"))
        end,
    })
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() enabled = false; disconnect() end)
end)
Runtime:Init("Bunker doors", function()
    local paths = {
        {"Military_Bunker_POI", "BunkerControl", "HatchBunker", "Door", "DOOR_BLOCK"},
        {"Military_Bunker_POI", "BunkerControl", "HatchBunker", "Door"},
        {"ChineseBunker", "Controller", "EscapeHatch", "Door", "DOOR_BLOCK"},
        {"ChineseBunker", "Controller", "EscapeHatch", "Door", "DOOR_Main"},
        {"ChineseBunker", "Controller", "MainDoors"},
    }
    GroupBoxes.Bunker:AddToggle("BunkerDoorsRemover", {
        Text = "Russian Bunker", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Removes the military bunker hatch and Chinese bunker doors locally. Turn on again to retry streamed-in doors. Removed doors return after rejoining.",
        Callback = function(value)
            if not value then return end
            for _, path in ipairs(paths) do
                -- Handle each door independently so a missing bunker cannot stop the others.
                Runtime:Handle("Bunker doors", function()
                    local assets = workspace:FindFirstChild("world_assets")
                    local objects = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("StaticObjects")
                    local target = objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Buildings")
                    for _, name in ipairs(path) do
                        target = target and target:FindFirstChild(name)
                    end
                    if target then target:Destroy() end
                end)
            end
        end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Freecam", function()
    local run = game:GetService("RunService")
    local cameraService, mouseService
    pcall(function()
        local system = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
        local module = system and system:FindFirstChild("DisableCameraService")
        cameraService = module and CallGameFunction(require, module)
        local mouseModule = system and system:FindFirstChild("FreeMouseService")
        mouseService = mouseModule and CallGameFunction(require, mouseModule)
    end)
    local manager = CreateFreecam(Runtime, workspace, game:GetService("UserInputService"),
        game:GetService("ContextActionService"), game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer,
        cameraService, function()
            if Window.IsOpen then return true end
            if mouseService then
                local ok, free = pcall(mouseService.IsMouseFree, mouseService)
                return ok and free
            end
            return false
        end)
    Runtime.Freecam = manager
    Runtime:Connect(run.PreSimulation, function() manager:HoldCharacter() end)
    Runtime:Connect(run.Heartbeat, function() manager:StepShooting() end)
    local binding = "LokiFreecam_" .. tostring(Runtime)
    run:BindToRenderStep(binding, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function(dt)
        if Runtime.Active then manager:Step(dt) end
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() run:UnbindFromRenderStep(binding); manager:Destroy(); Runtime.Freecam = nil end)
    GroupBoxes.Freecam:AddToggle("FreecamEnabled", {
        Text = "Freecam", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "WASD to fly, Space up, Left Shift down. Your character stays still. Hold left click to shoot from your character toward the freecam crosshair.",
        Callback = function(value) manager:SetEnabled(value) end,
    }):AddKeyPicker("FreecamKeyPicker", {
        Text = "Freecam", Default = "None", Mode = "Toggle", ShowInKeybindsList = true,
        Callback = function() Toggles.FreecamEnabled:SetValue(not Toggles.FreecamEnabled.Value) end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Freecam:AddSlider("FreecamSpeed", {
        Text = "Freecam Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 10, Default = 2.5, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value) manager.Speed = value end,
    })
end)

Runtime:Init("Lighting", function()
    local lightingState = {
        enabled = false,
        hour = 12,
        brightness = 5,
        ambientEnabled = false,
        ambientColor = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
    }

    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local atmosphere = lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")

    local originalLighting = {
        Brightness = lighting.Brightness, TimeOfDay = lighting.TimeOfDay,
        Ambient = lighting.Ambient, OutdoorAmbient = lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        Haze = atmosphere and atmosphere.Haze, Density = atmosphere and atmosphere.Density,
    }
    local function restoreLighting()
        lighting.Brightness, lighting.TimeOfDay = originalLighting.Brightness, originalLighting.TimeOfDay
        if atmosphere then atmosphere.Haze, atmosphere.Density = originalLighting.Haze, originalLighting.Density end
    end
    local function restoreAmbient()
        lighting.Ambient, lighting.OutdoorAmbient = originalLighting.Ambient, originalLighting.OutdoorAmbient
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() restoreLighting(); restoreAmbient() end)
    if atmosphere then
        Runtime:Connect(atmosphere:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Haze"), function()
            if lightingState.enabled then atmosphere.Haze = 0 end
        end)
        
        Runtime:Connect(atmosphere:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Density"), function()
            if lightingState.enabled then atmosphere.Density = 0 end
        end)
    end

    local function updateLighting()
        if lightingState.enabled then
            if atmosphere then atmosphere.Haze, atmosphere.Density = 0, 0 end
            lighting.Brightness = lightingState.brightness
            lighting.TimeOfDay = string.format("%02d:00:00", lightingState.hour)
        end
        if lightingState.ambientEnabled then
            lighting.Ambient = lightingState.ambientColor
            lighting.OutdoorAmbient = lightingState.ambientColor
        end
    end

    Runtime:Connect(lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Brightness"), function()
        if lightingState.enabled and lighting.Brightness ~= lightingState.brightness then
            lighting.Brightness = lightingState.brightness
        end
    end)

    Runtime:Connect(lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TimeOfDay"), function()
        local targetTime = string.format("%02d:00:00", lightingState.hour)
        if lightingState.enabled and lighting.TimeOfDay ~= targetTime then
            lighting.TimeOfDay = targetTime
        end
    end)

    Runtime:Connect(lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Ambient"), function()
        if lightingState.ambientEnabled and lighting.Ambient ~= lightingState.ambientColor then
            lighting.Ambient = lightingState.ambientColor
        end
    end)

    Runtime:Connect(lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("OutdoorAmbient"), function()
        if lightingState.ambientEnabled and lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= lightingState.ambientColor then
            lighting.OutdoorAmbient = lightingState.ambientColor
        end
    end)

    GroupBoxes.Lighting:AddToggle('LightingOverride', {
        Text = 'Custom Time',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            lightingState.enabled = state
            if not state then restoreLighting() end
            updateLighting()
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Lighting:AddSlider('TimeOfDay', {
        Text = 'Time of Day',
        Default = 12,
        Min = 0,
        Max = 23,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(value)
            lightingState.hour = value
            updateLighting()
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Lighting:AddSlider('Brightness', {
        Text = 'Brightness',
        Default = 5,
        Min = 0,
        Max = 10,
        Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value)
            lightingState.brightness = value
            updateLighting()
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Lighting:AddToggle('AmbientOverride', {
        Text = 'Custom Ambient',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            lightingState.ambientEnabled = state
            if not state then restoreAmbient() end
            updateLighting()
        end
    }):AddColorPicker('AmbientColor', {
        Default = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128),
        Title = 'Ambient Color',
        Callback = function(value)
            lightingState.ambientColor = value
            if lightingState.ambientEnabled then
                lighting.Ambient = value
                lighting.OutdoorAmbient = value
            end
        end
    })

    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Sky = Lighting:WaitForChild("Sky", 5)

        ["Purple Nebula"] = { "rbxassetid://159454299", "rbxassetid://159454296", "rbxassetid://159454293", "rbxassetid://159454286", "rbxassetid://159454300", "rbxassetid://159454288" },
