Runtime:Init("Vis check", function()
    local enabled, style, connection = false, "Text", nil
    local text, track, fill
    local progress, lastTarget = 0, nil
    local barLength, cycleTime = 120, 1
    local localPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local function clear()
        if connection then connection:Disconnect(); connection = nil end
        for _, object in pairs({text, track, fill}) do object:Remove() end
        text, track, fill = nil, nil, nil
        progress, lastTarget = 0, nil
    end
    local function update(dt)
        if not enabled then return end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then text.Visible, track.Visible, fill.Visible = false, false, false; return end
        local settings = getgenv().flags
        local selector = Environment.get_closest_target
        local target, _, _, entity
        if selector then target, _, _, entity = selector(settings.FovSize or 120, settings.AimPart or "Head") end
        local status, color, visible = "No Target", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), nil
        if target and target.Parent then
            visible, status = GetDisplayVisibility(Runtime, settings, target, entity, GetLocalVisibilityOrigin(Runtime, localPlayer))
            color = visible and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        else target = nil end
        if target ~= lastTarget then progress = 0 end
        lastTarget = target
        progress = target and (progress + math.max(dt or 0, 0) / cycleTime) % 1 or 0
        local center = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2 + 40)
        local showBar = style == "Loading Bar" and target ~= nil
        text.Position = center
        text.Text, text.Color, text.Visible = status, color, style == "Text"
        track.Position, track.Size, track.Visible = center + Vector2.new(-barLength / 2, 0), Vector2.new(barLength, 6), showBar
        fill.Position, fill.Size = track.Position + Vector2.new(1, 1), Vector2.new((barLength - 2) * progress, 4)
        fill.Color, fill.Visible = color, showBar and progress > 0
    end
    local function start()
        clear()
        if not enabled then return end
        text = Drawing.new("Text")
        text.Center, text.Outline, text.Size, text.Transparency = true, true, 16, 1
        text.Font, text.ZIndex = 2, 50
        track, fill = Drawing.new("Square"), Drawing.new("Square")
        track.Filled, fill.Filled = true, true
        track.Transparency, fill.Transparency = 1, 1
        track.Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
        connection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, update)
        update(0)
    end
    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddToggle("visiblecheck", {
        Text = "Vis Check", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Display visibility from your local character, including in freecam, plus Manipulation visibility.",
        Callback = function(value)
            enabled = value
            if Runtime.VisibilityCache then table.clear(Runtime.VisibilityCache) end
            start()
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddDropdown("VisCheckDisplay", {
        Text = "Vis Check Display", Values = {"Text", "Loading Bar"}, Default = "Text",
        Tooltip = "Text or a repeating bar: red when blocked, green when visible",
        Callback = function(value)
            -- Vitality clears a single-select dropdown when its selected option
            -- is clicked again. Keep a real display selected instead of hiding both.
            style = value == "Loading Bar" and "Loading Bar" or "Text"
            if value ~= style and Options.VisCheckDisplay then Options.VisCheckDisplay:SetValue(style); return end
            if enabled then start() end
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddSlider("VisCheckBarLength", {
        Text = "Vis Check Bar Length", Default = 120, Min = 40, Max = 400, Rounding = 0, Suffix = " px",
        Callback = function(value)
            barLength = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 120, 40, 400)
            if enabled and text then update(0) end
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Aimbot:AddSlider("VisCheckLoadTime", {
        Text = "Vis Check Loading Time", Default = 1, Min = 0.2, Max = 5, Rounding = 1, Suffix = " s",
        Callback = function(value)
            cycleTime = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 1, 0.2, 5)
        end,
    })
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() enabled = false; clear() end)
end)
-- Chroma's resolver corrects remote roots immediately before native character rendering.
Runtime:Init("Server desync resolver", function()
    local enabled, forceRender, resolving, nextResolve = false, false, false, 0
    local localPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local installed = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function correct(records)
        for _, data in pairs(records) do
            if type(data) ~= "table" then continue end
            local player, root, metadata = data.Player, data.RootPart, data.Metadata
            if not player or player == localPlayer or not root or not root.Parent or not data.Initialized
                or type(metadata) ~= "table" or typeof(metadata.CharacterPosition) ~= "Vector3" then continue end
            local car = player:FindFirstChild("Car")
            if car and car.Value then continue end
            -- Keep the native orientation; only the server position needs resolving.
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(metadata.CharacterPosition) * root.CFrame.Rotation
        end
    end
    local function updateRenderPositions(records)
        for _, data in pairs(records) do
            if type(data) ~= "table" then continue end
            local player, root, metadata = data.Player, data.RootPart, data.Metadata
            if not player or player == localPlayer or not root or not root.Parent or not data.Initialized
                or type(metadata) ~= "table" or player:GetAttribute("Dead") or player:GetAttribute("StartLoading") ~= nil then continue end
            local car = player:FindFirstChild("Car")
            if car and car.Value then continue end
            local position = root.Position
            if typeof(position) == "Vector3" and position.X == position.X and position.Y == position.Y and position.Z == position.Z
                and math.abs(position.X) < math.huge and math.abs(position.Y) < math.huge and math.abs(position.Z) < math.huge then
                metadata.CharacterPosition = position
            end
            local charData = player:FindFirstChild("CharData")
            local yaw = charData and charData:FindFirstChild("Yaw")
            local value = yaw and yaw.Value
            if type(value) == "number" and value == value and math.abs(value) < math.huge then
                metadata.CharacterYaw = math.clamp(value, -math.pi, math.pi)
            end
        end
    end
    local function discover()
        local system = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
        local module = system and system:FindFirstChild("CameraService")
        if not module then return end
        local service = CallGameFunction(require, module)
        local camera = CallGameFunction(service.WaitForCamera, service, "LookCamera")
        if not (enabled or forceRender) or not Runtime.Active then return end
        for _, binding in pairs(camera.Bindings.OnPrepare) do
            local action = binding.Action
            if binding.Priority ~= Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 3 or type(action) ~= "function" then continue end
            local entry = installed[action]
            local values = getupvalues(entry and entry.Original or action)
            local records = type(values) == "table" and values[1]
            if type(records) ~= "table" then
                if entry then entry.Records = nil end
                continue
            end
            if entry then entry.Records = records; continue end
            entry = { Records = records }
            entry.Original = hookfunction(action, function(...)
                if (enabled or forceRender) and Runtime.Active and entry.Records then
                    local ok, message = pcall(function()
                        if enabled then correct(entry.Records) end
                        if forceRender then updateRenderPositions(entry.Records) end
                    end)
                    if not ok then Runtime:Report("Server desync resolver", message) end
                end
                return entry.Original(...)
            end)
            installed[action] = entry
        end
    end
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        if not (enabled or forceRender) or resolving or os.clock() < nextResolve then return end
        resolving, nextResolve = true, os.clock() + 1
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, message = pcall(discover)
            resolving = false
            if not ok then Runtime:Report("Server desync resolver", message) end
        end)
    end)
    GroupBoxes.Resolver:AddToggle("ServerDesyncResolver", {
        Text = "Server Desync Resolver", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Uses server metadata for remote character positions; skips players in vehicles",
        Callback = function(value) enabled, nextResolve = value, 0 end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Resolver:AddToggle("ForceRenderPosition", {
        Text = "Force Render Position", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Uses the latest replicated root position and yaw before character rendering. Skips vehicles; network latency still applies.",
        Callback = function(value) forceRender, nextResolve = value, 0 end,
    })
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() enabled, forceRender = false, false end)
end)
Runtime:Init("Server scanner", function()
    local function executeScan()
            local Players = cloneref(game:GetService("Players"))
            local HttpService = cloneref(game:GetService("HttpService"))

            local RawMap = {
                ["Barret50"] = true,
                ["RenelliM4"] = true,
                ["M79"] = true,

                ["SVD"] = true,
                ["AKM"] = true,
                ["Saiga"] = true,
                ["AWM"] = true,
                ["MRAD"] = true,
                ["m110k"] = true,
                ["P90"] = true,
                ["HoneyBadger"] = true,

                ["M249"] = true,
                ["M4A1"] = true,

                ["AltynHelmet"] = true,
                ["RatnikVest"] = true,
                ["VestTier3"] = true,

                ["Mulepack"] = true,
                ["DuffleBag"] = true,
                ["BackpackTier4"] = true,
                ["BackpackTier3"] = true,
            }

            local Map = {}
            for Index, Val in pairs(RawMap) do
                Map[Index:lower()] = Val
            end

            local Attributes = {
                ["Pants"] = "EquipmentPants",
                ["Shirt"] = "EquipmentShirt",
                ["Hat"] = "EquipmentHat",
                ["Vest"] = "EquipmentVest",
                ["Mask"] = "EquipmentMask",
                ["Backpack"] = "EquipmentBackpack",
                ["Shoes"] = "EquipmentShoes",
            }

            local Found = {}
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            for _, Player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if Player == LocalPlayer then
                    continue
                end

                for Name, Attribute in pairs(Attributes) do
                    local Attr = Player:GetAttribute(Attribute)
                    Attr = Attr and HttpService:JSONDecode(Attr) or {}

                    if not Attr.ClassName then
                        continue
                    end

                    local Text = Attr.ClassName:gsub(".item", "")

                    if not Map[Text:lower()] then
                        continue
                    end

                    table.insert(Found, {
                        Player = Player,
                        Gun = Text
                    })

                    local notifText = Player.Name .. " has " .. Text .. "!"
                    if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                        Library:Notify(notifText)
                    end
                end

                local GunInventory = Player:FindFirstChild("GunInventory")

                if not GunInventory then
                    continue
                end

                for _, Slot in pairs(GunInventory:GetChildren()) do
                    local Gun = Slot.Value

                    if not Gun or not Map[Gun.Name:lower()] then
                        continue
                    end

                    local Mag = Slot:FindFirstChild("BulletsInMagazine")
                    local Reserve = Slot:FindFirstChild("BulletsInReserve")

                    if not Mag or not Reserve then
                        continue
                    end

                    local TotalAmmo = Mag.Value + Reserve.Value

                    if Gun.Name:lower() ~= "barret50" and TotalAmmo <= 50 then
                        continue
                    end

                    local displayNameObj = Gun:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
                    local displayName = displayNameObj and displayNameObj.Value or Gun.Name
                    local gunDesc = string.format("%s with %d ammo", displayName, TotalAmmo)

                    table.insert(Found, {
                        Player = Player,
                        Gun = gunDesc
                    })

                    local notifText = string.format("%s has %s (%d ammo)", Player.Name, Gun.Name, TotalAmmo)
                    if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                        Library:Notify(notifText)
                    end
                end
            end

            print(string.format("Found %d things that match", #Found))

            for _, Data in pairs(Found) do
                print(string.format("Player %s has %s", Data.Player.Name, Data.Gun))
            end
            
            if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                Library:Notify("Scan Complete! Found " .. #Found .. " items. Check F9.")
            end
        end

        GroupBoxes.LootScanner:AddButton("Scan Server", function()
            executeScan()
        end)

    GroupBoxes.LootScanner:AddButton("Scan Special Zombies", function()
        task.spawn(function()
            local success, EmberClient = pcall(function() 
                return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):WaitForChild("EmberClientLibrary", 10):WaitForChild("EmberClient", 10):WaitForChild("EmberClient", 10)) 
            end)
            
            if not success then 
                if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                    Library:Notify("Failed to locate EmberClient!")
                else
                    warn("Failed to locate EmberClient!")
                end
                return 
            end

            local NPCSimulatorService = EmberClient:GetService("NPCSimulatorService")
            local FoundAny = false
            
            for _, Zombie in pairs(NPCSimulatorService.NPCs) do
                for _, Item in pairs(Zombie.Equipment) do
                    local ItemClass = Item.ClassName
                    local Skin = Item.SkinOverride
                    
                    if ItemClass:find("Altyn") then
                        local notifText = "Chinese zombie detected: " .. ItemClass:gsub(".item", "")
                        if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                            Library:Notify(notifText)
                        end
                        FoundAny = true
                    elseif Skin and Skin:find("Beret") then
                        local notifText = "Tactical zombie detected: " .. Skin
                        if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                            Library:Notify(notifText)
                        end
                        FoundAny = true
                    end
                end
            end
            
            if not FoundAny then
                if Library and type(Library.Notify) == "function" then
                    Library:Notify("No special zombies detected.")
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
    
end)
Runtime:Init("Movement", function()
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

    local speedhackEnabled = false
    local speedhackSpeed = 0.5
    local speedhackConnection = nil

    local function GetMovementPart()
        for _, child in ipairs(camera:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Size == Vector3.new(2.5, 5, 2.5) then
                return child
            end
        end
    end

    local function ApplySpeedHack()
        if not speedhackEnabled then return end
        if not UserInputService then return end

        local movementPart = GetMovementPart()
        if not movementPart then return end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end

        local moveDirection = Vector3.new()

        -- Flatten camera vectors so looking up/down doesn't kill horizontal movement
        local lookVec = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        lookVec = Vector3.new(lookVec.X, 0, lookVec.Z)
        if lookVec.Magnitude > 0 then lookVec = lookVec.Unit end

        local rightVec = cam.CFrame.RightVector
        rightVec = Vector3.new(rightVec.X, 0, rightVec.Z)
        if rightVec.Magnitude > 0 then rightVec = rightVec.Unit end

        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection + lookVec
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection - lookVec
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection - rightVec
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection + rightVec
        end

        if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
            moveDirection = moveDirection.Unit
            local newVelocity = moveDirection * (25 * speedhackSpeed)

            pcall(function()
                movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                    newVelocity.X,
                    movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,
                    newVelocity.Z
                )
            end)
        end
    end

    local function StartSpeedHack()
        if speedhackConnection then return end
        speedhackConnection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, ApplySpeedHack)
    end

    local function StopSpeedHack()
        if speedhackConnection then
            speedhackConnection:Disconnect()
            speedhackConnection = nil
        end
    end

    GroupBoxes.miscGroup:AddToggle("SpeedHackToggle", {
        Text = "Speed Hack",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            speedhackEnabled = state
            if state then
                StartSpeedHack()
            else
                StopSpeedHack()
            end
        end
    }):AddKeyPicker("SpeedHackKeyPicker", {
        Text = "Speed Hack Key",
        Default = "None",
        Callback = function()
            if Toggles and Toggles.SpeedHackToggle then
                Toggles.SpeedHackToggle:SetValue(not Toggles.SpeedHackToggle.Value)
            end
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.miscGroup:AddSlider("SpeedMultiplierSlider", {
        Text = "Speed Multiplier",
        Default = 5,
        Min = 1,
        Max = 11,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            speedhackSpeed = v / 10
        end
    })

    local levitateEnabled = false
    local levitateHeight = 10
    local levitateSpeed = 5
    local levitateThread = nil

    local function StartLevitate()
        if levitateThread then return end
        levitateThread = task.spawn(function()
            while levitateEnabled do
                local movementPart = GetMovementPart()
                if movementPart then
                    local startPos = movementPart.Position
                    local targetY = startPos.Y + levitateHeight
                    local upVel = levitateSpeed * 7
                    local downVel = levitateSpeed * 10
                    
                    while levitateEnabled and movementPart.Position.Y < targetY do
                        movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                            movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                            upVel,
                            movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                        )
                        task.wait()
                    end

                    movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    task.wait(0.1)

                    while levitateEnabled and movementPart.Position.Y > startPos.Y do
                        movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                            movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                            -downVel,
                            movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                        )
                        task.wait()
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
            levitateThread = nil
        end)
    end

    local function StopLevitate()
        levitateEnabled = false
        levitateThread = nil
    end


    local flightEnabled = false
    local flightSpeed = 5
    local flightConnection = nil

    local function ApplyFlight()
        if not flightEnabled then return end
        if not UserInputService then return end

        local movementPart = GetMovementPart()
        if not movementPart then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end

        local moveDirection = Vector3.new()
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection + cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection - cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection - cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection + cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then
            moveDirection = moveDirection - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        end

        if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
            moveDirection = moveDirection.Unit
            movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = moveDirection * (25 * flightSpeed)
        else
            movementPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end

    local function StartFlight()
        if flightConnection then return end
        flightConnection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, ApplyFlight)
    end

    local function StopFlight()
        if flightConnection then
            flightConnection:Disconnect()
            flightConnection = nil
        end
    end

    GroupBoxes.miscGroup:AddToggle("FlightToggle", {
        Text = "Flight",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            flightEnabled = state
            if state then
                StartFlight()
            else
                StopFlight()
            end
        end
    }):AddKeyPicker("FlightKeyPicker", {
        Text = "Flight Key",
        Default = "None",
        Callback = function()
            if Toggles and Toggles.FlightToggle then
                Toggles.FlightToggle:SetValue(not Toggles.FlightToggle.Value)
            end
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.miscGroup:AddSlider("FlightSpeedSlider", {
        Text = "Flight Speed",
        Default = 3,
        Min = 1,
        Max = 5,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            flightSpeed = v / 2
        end
    })

    local bhop = false
    local bhopConnection = nil

    pcall(function()
        for _, v in getgc() do
            if type(v) == "function" then
                local info = debug.getinfo(v)
                if info and info.name == "updateCharData" then
                    local old
                    old = hookfunction(v, newcclosure(function(p254, p255, p256)
                        if p254 == "Jump" then return end
                        return old(p254, p255, p256)
                    end))
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local function StartBhop()
        if bhopConnection then return end
        
        pcall(function()
            ReplicatedStorage.CustomCharacterConfigs.Configuration.Client.cl_auto_jump.Value = true
        end)
        
        bhopConnection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
            if bhop then
                pcall(function()
                    ReplicatedStorage.CustomCharacterConfigs.Configuration.Client.cl_auto_jump.Value = true
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function StopBhop()
        if bhopConnection then
            bhopConnection:Disconnect()
            bhopConnection = nil
        end
        pcall(function()
            ReplicatedStorage.CustomCharacterConfigs.Configuration.Client.cl_auto_jump.Value = false
        end)
    end

    GroupBoxes.miscGroup:AddToggle("BhopToggle", {
        Text = "Bhop",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            bhop = state
            if state then
                StartBhop()
            else
                StopBhop()
            end
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Attachments", function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

    local ReticleMap = {
        ["ACOG Sight"] = "ACOG",
        ["16× Sniper Scope"] = "SniperScope16xStatic",
        ["8× Sniper Scope"] = "SniperScope8x",
        ["Red Dot Sight"] = "RedDot",
        ["Holographic Sight"] = "Holo",
        ["Reflex Sight"] = "ReflexRifle",
        ["LPVO"] = "LPVOScope",
    }

    local ReticleLabels = {
        "ACOG Sight",
        "16× Sniper Scope",
        "8× Sniper Scope",
        "Red Dot Sight",
        "Holographic Sight",
        "Reflex Sight",
        "LPVO"
    }

    local SlotMap = {
        ["Slot 1"] = 1,
        ["Slot 2"] = 2,
        ["Slot 3"] = 3
    }

    local SlotLabels = { "Slot 1", "Slot 2", "Slot 3" }

    local chosenReticle = ReticleLabels[1]
    local chosenSlot = SlotLabels[1]
    local enableAttachments = false

    setidentity(8)

    GroupBoxes.Attach:AddToggle('Enable_Attachments', {
        Text = "Attachments",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Value)
            enableAttachments = Value
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Attach:AddDropdown('Select_Reticle', {
        Text = "Select Attachment",
        Values = ReticleLabels,
        Default = 1,
        Callback = function(Value)
            chosenReticle = Value
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Attach:AddDropdown('Select_Slot', {
        Text = "Select Slot",
        Values = SlotLabels,
        Default = 1,
        Callback = function(Value)
            chosenSlot = Value
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Attach:AddButton("Apply Attachments", function()
        if not enableAttachments then return end

        local player = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player then return end

        local invFolder = player:FindFirstChild("GunInventory")
        if not invFolder then return end

        local validSlots = {}
        for _, ov in ipairs(invFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if ov:IsA("ObjectValue") and ov.Value then
                table.insert(validSlots, ov)
            end
        end

        local slotIndex = SlotMap[chosenSlot]
        local slotOV = validSlots[slotIndex]
        if not slotOV then return end

        local gunSystemAssets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GunSystemAssets")
        if not gunSystemAssets then return end

        local attachmentsFolder = gunSystemAssets:FindFirstChild("Attachments")
        if not attachmentsFolder then return end

        local function apply(map, label, field)
            if not label then return end
            local assetName = map[label]
            if not assetName then return end
            local asset = attachmentsFolder:FindFirstChild(assetName)
            if not asset then return end
            local ov = slotOV:FindFirstChild(field)
            if not ov then return end
            ov.Value = asset
        end

        apply(ReticleMap, chosenReticle, "AttachmentReticle")
    end)
end)
Runtime:Init("Camera FOV", function()
    local ok, controller = pcall(function()
        local system = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
        local module = system and system:FindFirstChild("CameraFovController")
        return module and require(module)
    end)
    local FovState = { Enabled = false, Value = 90, Controller = ok and controller or nil }

    GroupBoxes.Fov:AddToggle('FovChangerToggle', {
        Text = "FOV Changer", Default = false, Tooltip = "Changes your field of view",
        Callback = function(State)
            FovState.Enabled = State
            if FovState.Controller then
                FovState.Controller.ClientFov = State and FovState.Value or 100
                FovState.Controller:Enable()
                Library:Notify(State and ("FOV Changer Enabled: " .. FovState.Value .. " degrees") or "FOV Changer Disabled", 3)
            else
                Library:Notify("FOV Controller not found!", 3)
            end
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Fov:AddSlider('FovValueSlider', {
        Text = "FOV Value", Default = 90, Min = 1, Max = 120, Rounding = 1, Tooltip = "Field of view in degrees (1-120)",
        Callback = function(Value)
            FovState.Value = Value
            if FovState.Enabled and FovState.Controller then
                FovState.Controller.ClientFov = Value
                FovState.Controller:Enable()
            end
        end
    })
end)
-- BEGIN VEHICLE FLY ORIENTATION
local function CarFlyOrientation(direction, backward)
    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
    local orientation = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, direction)
    if backward then
        -- Pitch down around the car's local X axis after reversing: rear above nose.
        orientation = orientation * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0) * CFrame.Angles(-math.rad(21), 0, 0)
    end
    return orientation
end
-- END VEHICLE FLY ORIENTATION
Runtime:Init("Vehicle movement", function()
    local carFly = {
        enabled = false,
        speed = 120,
        conn = nil,
        backward = false,
        spin = false,
        spinSpeed = 10,
        cachedChassis = nil,
        cachedModel = nil,
        dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),

        attachment = nil,
        linearVelocity = nil,
        alignOrientation = nil,
        vectorForce = nil
    }

    local function get_local_chassis()
        if carFly.cachedChassis and carFly.cachedChassis.Parent then
            local config = carFly.cachedModel and carFly.cachedModel:FindFirstChild("Configuration")
            local driver = config and config:FindFirstChild("Driver")
            if driver and driver:IsA("ObjectValue") and driver.Value == LocalPlayer then
                return carFly.cachedChassis, carFly.cachedModel
            end
        end

        for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            local chassis = obj:FindFirstChild("Chassis")
            local config = obj:FindFirstChild("Configuration")
            if chassis and chassis:IsA("BasePart") and config then
                local driver = config:FindFirstChild("Driver")
                if driver and driver:IsA("ObjectValue") and driver.Value == LocalPlayer then
                    carFly.cachedChassis = chassis
                    carFly.cachedModel = obj
                    return chassis, obj
                end
            end
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    local function setupConstraints(chassis)
        if not chassis then return end
        
        -- Clean up old ones if they exist
        if carFly.attachment then carFly.attachment:Destroy() end
        if carFly.linearVelocity then carFly.linearVelocity:Destroy() end
        if carFly.alignOrientation then carFly.alignOrientation:Destroy() end
        if carFly.vectorForce then carFly.vectorForce:Destroy() end

        -- Create attachment at chassis center
        local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Name = "CarFlyAttachment"
        att.Parent = chassis
        carFly.attachment = att

        -- Direct velocity for Blocky flight
        local lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
        lv.Name = "CarFlyVelocity"
        lv.Attachment0 = att
        lv.MaxForce = math.huge
        lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        lv.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
        lv.Parent = chassis
        carFly.linearVelocity = lv

        local ao = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
        ao.Name = "CarFlyOrientation"
        ao.Attachment0 = att
        ao.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
        ao.MaxTorque = math.huge
        ao.Responsiveness = 200
        ao.Parent = chassis
        carFly.alignOrientation = ao

        local vf = Instance.new("VectorForce")
        vf.Name = "CarFlyGravityCanceller"
        vf.Attachment0 = att
        vf.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
        vf.Force = Vector3.new(0, chassis:GetMass() * workspace.Gravity, 0)
        vf.Parent = chassis
        carFly.vectorForce = vf
    end

    local function removeConstraints()
        if carFly.attachment then carFly.attachment:Destroy(); carFly.attachment = nil end
        if carFly.linearVelocity then carFly.linearVelocity:Destroy(); carFly.linearVelocity = nil end
        if carFly.alignOrientation then carFly.alignOrientation:Destroy(); carFly.alignOrientation = nil end
        if carFly.vectorForce then carFly.vectorForce:Destroy(); carFly.vectorForce = nil end
    end

    local function startCarFly()
        if carFly.conn then return end
        carFly.conn = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
            if not carFly.enabled then return end
            local chassis, model = get_local_chassis()
            if not chassis then return end
            
            if not carFly.attachment or carFly.attachment.Parent ~= chassis then
                setupConstraints(chassis)
            end

            if chassis.Anchored then chassis.Anchored = false end
            
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then return end
            local uis = UserInputService or game:GetService("UserInputService")
            
            local lookVec = cam.CFrame.LookVector
            local moveDir = lookVec
            
            if uis and uis:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
                moveDir = (moveDir - cam.CFrame.RightVector * 0.8).Unit
            elseif uis and uis:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
                moveDir = (moveDir + cam.CFrame.RightVector * 0.8).Unit
            end

            carFly.dir = moveDir
            if carFly.linearVelocity then
                carFly.linearVelocity.VectorVelocity = carFly.dir * carFly.speed
            end
            if carFly.alignOrientation then
                carFly.alignOrientation.CFrame = CarFlyOrientation(carFly.dir, carFly.backward)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function stopCarFly()
        if carFly.conn then carFly.conn:Disconnect(); carFly.conn = nil end
        
        local chassis, _ = get_local_chassis()
        removeConstraints()
        
        if chassis and chassis.Parent then
            chassis.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            chassis.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        carFly.cachedChassis = nil
        carFly.cachedModel = nil
        carFly.dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
    end


    GroupBoxes.CarExploits:AddToggle('CarFlyToggle', {
        Text = 'Vehicle Fly', Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            carFly.enabled = state
            if state then 
                startCarFly()
                Library:Notify("Vehicle Fly Enabled", 2)
            else 
                stopCarFly()
                Library:Notify("Vehicle Fly Disabled", 2) 
            end
        end
    }):AddKeyPicker('CarFlyKeybind', {
        Default = "None", Text = 'Car Fly',
        Callback = function() Toggles.CarFlyToggle:SetValue(not Toggles.CarFlyToggle.Value) end
    })

    GroupBoxes.CarExploits:AddSlider('CarFlySpeed', {
        Text = 'Fly Speed', Default = 120, Min = 10, Max = 200, Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v) carFly.speed = v end
    })

    GroupBoxes.CarExploits:AddToggle('BackwardCarToggle', {
        Text = 'Backward Car', Default = false,
        Callback = function(state) carFly.backward = state end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Long neck", function()
    local gunPlugin = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst").GunSystem.GunController.Events.GunPlugin)
    local charPlugin = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst").CustomCharacter.CharacterController.CharacterPlugin)

    local neckHighEnabled = false
    local neckHighOffset = 3.4
    local currentHeight = nil

    Runtime:Connect(gunPlugin.Signals.SetUberschreibenHohe, function(height)
        currentHeight = height
    end)

    Runtime:Connect(charPlugin.OnPlayerStateChanged, function(state)
        if not neckHighEnabled then return end
        
        if state == "Prone" then
            task.wait()
            currentHeight = (currentHeight or 0) + neckHighOffset
            gunPlugin:SetOverrideCameraHeight(currentHeight)
        else
            currentHeight = (currentHeight or 0) - neckHighOffset
            gunPlugin:SetOverrideCameraHeight(currentHeight == 0 and nil or currentHeight)
        end
    end)

    GroupBoxes.LongNeck:AddToggle('NeckHighToggle', {
        Text = 'Neck Hight',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            neckHighEnabled = state
            if state then
                Library:Notify("Neck High Enabled", 2)
            else
                Library:Notify("Neck High Disabled", 2)
                if currentHeight then
                    currentHeight = nil
                    gunPlugin:SetOverrideCameraHeight(nil)
                end
            end
        end,
    }):AddKeyPicker('NeckHighKeybind', {
        Default = "None",
        Text = 'Neck High',
        Callback = function(value)
            Toggles.NeckHighToggle:SetValue(not Toggles.NeckHighToggle.Value)
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.LongNeck:AddSlider('NeckHighSlider', {
        Text = 'Neck Hieght',
        Min = 1.0,
        Max = 3.4,
        Default = 3.4,
        Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value)
            neckHighOffset = value
        end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Anti aim", function()
    local CustomMeshCharacterModule = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst").GunSystemPlugins.CustomMeshCharacter)

    local aaState = {
        conn = nil,
        repConn = nil,
        cc = nil,
        originalYaw = nil,
        lastUpdate = 0
    }

    local stopYaw
    local function startBackwardsAA()
        if Runtime.UpdateVisualBackwards or aaState.repConn then return end
        
        local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
        local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")
        
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                aaState.cc = require(rep:WaitForChild("CustomCharacter", 10):WaitForChild("CharacterController", 10):WaitForChild("CharacterPlugin", 10))
                if aaState.cc and not aaState.originalYaw then
                    aaState.originalYaw = aaState.cc.SetYaw
                    aaState.cc.SetYaw = function(self, yaw)
                        if flags and flags.backwards_aa then
                            return aaState.originalYaw(self, ((yaw or 0) + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi))
                        end
                        return aaState.originalYaw(self, yaw)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end)
        
        Runtime.UpdateVisualBackwards = function()
            if not flags or not flags.backwards_aa then return end
            
            pcall(function()
                local worldChar = CustomMeshCharacterModule:GetWorldCharacterFromPlayer(lp)
                if not worldChar then return end
                
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam then return end
                
                local camLook = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                local cameraYaw = math.atan2(-camLook.X, -camLook.Z)
                local lockedBackwardsYaw = cameraYaw + math.pi
                
                local pivot = worldChar:GetPivot()
                worldChar:PivotTo(
                    CFrame.new(pivot.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, lockedBackwardsYaw, 0)
                )
            end)
        end
        
        aaState.repConn = Runtime:Connect(rs.Heartbeat, function()
            if not flags or not flags.backwards_aa then return end
            
            local now = tick()
            if now - aaState.lastUpdate < 0.1 then return end
            aaState.lastUpdate = now
            
            pcall(function()
                if lp and lp:FindFirstChild("CharData") and lp.CharData:FindFirstChild("Yaw") then
                    local currentYaw = lp.CharData.Yaw.Value or 0
                    lp.CharData.Yaw.Value = (currentYaw + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function stopBackwardsAA()
        Runtime.UpdateVisualBackwards = nil
        if aaState.repConn then
            aaState.repConn:Disconnect()
            aaState.repConn = nil
        end
        if aaState.originalYaw and aaState.cc then
            aaState.cc.SetYaw = aaState.originalYaw
            aaState.originalYaw = nil
        end
    end

    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, stopBackwardsAA)
    GroupBoxes.AntiAim:AddToggle("BackwardsAA", {
        Text = "MoonWalk",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            flags = flags or {}
            flags.backwards_aa = state
            if state then
                flags.yawspin = false
                if Toggles.YawSpin then Toggles.YawSpin:SetValue(false) end
                if stopYaw then stopYaw() end
                startBackwardsAA()
                pcall(function()
                    if Library and Library.Notify then
                        Library:Notify("MoonWalk Enabled", 3)
                    end
                end)
            else
                stopBackwardsAA()
                pcall(function()
                    if Library and Library.Notify then
                        Library:Notify("MoonWalk Disabled", 3)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })

    local rs, lp = game:GetService("RunService"), game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local yawAngle, yawSpeed, yawConnection1, cc = 0, 12, nil, nil

    local function startYaw()
        if yawConnection1 then return end
        
        task.spawn(function()
            cc = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):WaitForChild("CustomCharacter", 10):WaitForChild("CharacterController", 10):WaitForChild("CharacterPlugin", 10))
            if cc and cc.IsLoaded then
                repeat task.wait() until cc:IsLoaded()
            end
        end)
        
        yawConnection1 = Runtime:Connect(rs.Heartbeat, function(dt)
            if not flags or not flags.yawspin then return end
            yawAngle = (yawAngle + yawSpeed * dt) % (2 * math.pi)
            
            pcall(function()
                if lp and lp:FindFirstChild("CharData") and lp.CharData:FindFirstChild("Yaw") then
                    lp.CharData.Yaw.Value = yawAngle
                end
            end)
        end)
        
        Runtime.UpdateVisualYaw = function()
            if not flags or not flags.yawspin then return end
            
            if cc and cc.SetYaw then
                pcall(cc.SetYaw, cc, yawAngle)
            end
            
            pcall(function()
                local worldChar = CustomMeshCharacterModule:GetWorldCharacterFromPlayer(lp)
                if worldChar then
                    local pivot = worldChar:GetPivot()
                    worldChar:PivotTo(CFrame.new(pivot.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yawAngle, 0))
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    stopYaw = function()
        if yawConnection1 then yawConnection1:Disconnect(); yawConnection1 = nil end
        Runtime.UpdateVisualYaw = nil
        yawAngle = 0
    end

    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() stopYaw() end)

    GroupBoxes.AntiAim:AddToggle("YawSpin", {
        Text = "SpinBot",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            flags = flags or {}
            flags.yawspin = state
            if state then
                if Toggles.BackwardsAA then Toggles.BackwardsAA:SetValue(false) end
                startYaw()
            else stopYaw() end
        end,
    })

    GroupBoxes.AntiAim:AddSlider("SpinSpeed", {
        Text = "SpinBot Speed",
        Default = 12,
        Min = 1,
        Max = 30,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(value) yawSpeed = value end
    })

    local visualYawBinding = "LucidVisualYaw_" .. tostring(Runtime)
    rs:BindToRenderStep(visualYawBinding, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 1, function()
        if not Runtime.Active then return end
        if Runtime.UpdateVisualBackwards then
            local ok, reason = pcall(Runtime.UpdateVisualBackwards)
            if not ok then Runtime:Report("Visual backwards yaw", reason) end
        end
        if Runtime.UpdateVisualYaw then
            local ok, reason = pcall(Runtime.UpdateVisualYaw)
            if not ok then Runtime:Report("Visual yaw", reason) end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() rs:UnbindFromRenderStep(visualYawBinding) end)

    local aa = { enabled = false, mode = "Up", pitch = 0, phase = 0, tweenPitch = math.rad(89) }

    GroupBoxes.AntiAim:AddToggle('anti_aim_toggle', {
        Text = 'Anti Aim',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            aa.enabled = state
            aa.phase, aa.tweenPitch = 0, math.rad(89)
            Runtime.AntiAimEnabled = state
            Runtime.UseRenderedBarrel = aa.enabled and aa.mode == "Tweak"
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.AntiAim:AddDropdown('anti_aim_mode', {
        Text = 'Mode',
        Default = 'Up',
        Values = {'Up', 'Down', 'Tweak'},
        Callback = function(value)
            aa.mode = value
            aa.phase, aa.tweenPitch = 0, math.rad(89)
            Runtime.UseRenderedBarrel = aa.enabled and aa.mode == "Tweak"
        end
    })

    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        Runtime.UseRenderedBarrel, Runtime.AntiAimEnabled = nil, nil
    end)
    -- Sample one sine-eased tween pose per animation frame; controller reads stay consistent.
    Runtime:Connect(rs.PreAnimation, function(dt)
        if not aa.enabled or aa.mode ~= "Tweak" then return end
        aa.phase, aa.tweenPitch = AdvanceTweakPitch(aa.phase, dt or 0, flags and flags.jitter_speed)
    end)

    local old
    old = hookfunction(getrawmetatable(game).__index, function(self, key)
        if not aa.enabled or key ~= "CFrame" then return old(self, key) end
        if not checkcaller() and self == workspace.CurrentCamera then
            local cf = old(self, key)
            
            if aa.enabled then
                local calling_script = getcallingscript()
                if calling_script and calling_script.Name == "CharacterController" then
                    local m = aa.mode
                    if m == "Up" then
                        return CameraAlignedPitch(cf, math.rad(89))
                    elseif m == "Down" then
                        return CameraAlignedPitch(cf, -math.rad(89))
                    elseif m == "Pitch" then
                        return CameraAlignedPitch(cf, aa.pitch)
                    elseif m == "Tweak" then
                        return CameraAlignedPitch(cf, aa.tweenPitch)
                    end
                end
            end
            return cf
        end
        return old(self, key)
    end)

    GroupBoxes.AntiAim:AddSlider('Speed', {
        Text = "Speed",
        Default = 1,
        Min = 0.1,
        Max = 1,
        Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value)
            flags = flags or {}
            flags.jitter_speed = value
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Staff scanner", function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local Lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    local StaffScannerEnabled, scannerChoice = false, "9an2s scanner"
    local GroupId, NotificationDelay = 3441839, 5
    local scanLowRanks, reportEnabled = false, false

    local StaffRanks = {
        {min = 5, max = 5, name = "Wiki Team"},
        {min = 6, max = 6, name = "Bug Squasher"},
        {min = 7, max = 7, name = "Contributor"},
        {min = 8, max = 8, name = "Alpha Tester"},
        {min = 9, max = 9, name = "Beta Tester"},
        {min = 9, max = 9, name = "Sigma Tester"},
        {min = 90, max = 90, name = "Content Creator"},
        {min = 98, max = 98, name = "Trial Moderator"},
        {min = 99, max = 99, name = "Moderator"},
        {min = 100, max = 100, name = "Senior Moderator"},
        {min = 150, max = 150, name = "Administrator"},
        {min = 151, max = 151, name = "Contractor"},
        {min = 152, max = 152, name = "Map Developer"},
        {min = 198, max = 198, name = "Internal Staff"},
        {min = 199, max = 199, name = "Senior Administrator"},
        {min = 250, max = 250, name = "Developer"},
        {min = 253, max = 253, name = "Lead Developer"},
        {min = 254, max = 254, name = "Co-Owner"},
        {min = 255, max = 255, name = "Owner"}
    }

    local function GetValidStaffRole(rank)
        for _, data in ipairs(StaffRanks) do
            if rank >= data.min and rank <= data.max then
                if rank == 9 and scannerChoice == "james scanner" then return "Beta / Sigma Tester" end
                return data.name
            end
        end
        if scannerChoice == "james scanner" and rank >= 98 then return "Rank " .. tostring(rank) end
    end
    local soundChoice, soundVolume = "Neverlose", 1
    local StaffSounds = {
        Neverlose = "rbxassetid://8726881116", TF2 = "rbxassetid://8255306220",
        Gamesense = "rbxassetid://4817809188", Rust = "rbxassetid://1255040462",
        Bubble = "rbxassetid://85730811347567", Quake = "rbxassetid://1455817260",
        ["Classic Alert"] = "rbxassetid://885589466",
    }
    local soundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
    local alertSound
    local seen, epoch = {}, 0
    local addedConnection, removedConnection
    local function prepareAlert()
        local id = StaffSounds[soundChoice]
        if not id then return end
        if not alertSound then
            alertSound = Instance.new("Sound")
            alertSound.Name = "LokiStaffJoinAlert"
            alertSound.Parent = soundService
        end
        alertSound.SoundId, alertSound.Volume = id, soundVolume
        return alertSound
    end
    local function preloadAlert()
        local sound = prepareAlert()
        if not sound then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function() game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync({ sound }) end)
        end)
    end
    local function playAlert()
        if soundVolume <= 0 then return end
        local sound = prepareAlert()
        if not sound then return end
        sound:Stop()
        soundService:PlayLocalSound(sound)
    end
    local function inspect(player, joining)
        if player == Lp or seen[player] then return end
        local admission, generation, choice = {}, epoch, scannerChoice
        seen[player] = admission
        local function current()
            return StaffScannerEnabled and generation == epoch and seen[player] == admission and player.Parent == Players
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local rank
            for attempt = 1, 3 do
                if not current() then return end
                local ok, result = pcall(function()
                    if choice == "james scanner" then return player:GetRankInGroupAsync(GroupId) end
                    return player:GetRankInGroup(GroupId)
                end)
                if not current() then return end
                if ok and type(result) == "number" then rank = result; break end
                if attempt < 3 then task.wait(attempt * 0.5) end
            end
            if not rank then seen[player] = nil; return end
            if choice == "james scanner" and rank < 98 and not scanLowRanks then return end
            local role = GetValidStaffRole(rank)
            if not role then return end
            if rank >= 98 and (choice == "9an2s scanner" or joining) then
                Runtime:Call("Staff alert sound", playAlert)
            end
            local label = player.Name
            if choice == "james scanner" and player.DisplayName ~= player.Name then
                label = player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")"
            end
            Library:Notify(string.format("[%s] %s %s as %s!", choice, label,
                joining and "joined" or "is here", role), NotificationDelay)
        end)
    end

    -- James's report/mod notices use only the remotes available to this client.
    local reportConnections, boundRemotes = {}, {}
    local reportEpoch, lastNotice, lastNoticeAt = 0, nil, 0
    local remoteNames = { CGamesModerationNotices = true, CGamesModerationNoticeAction = true, CGamesMuteNotice = true }
    local function reportsActive()
        return reportEnabled and scannerChoice == "james scanner"
    end
    local function playerName(id)
        local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(id)
        if player then
            return player.DisplayName ~= player.Name and (player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")") or player.Name
        end
        local ok, name = pcall(Players.GetNameFromUserIdAsync, Players, id)
        return ok and type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and (name .. " [" .. id .. "]") or ("UserId " .. id)
    end
    local function field(payload, ...)
        for _, key in ipairs({...}) do
            if payload[key] ~= nil then return payload[key] end
        end
    end
    local function summarize(payload)
        if type(payload) ~= "table" then return "[Mod] " .. tostring(payload), false end
        local kind = field(payload, "Kind", "kind", "Type", "type", "Action", "action", "Notice", "notice")
        local targetId = field(payload, "TargetUserId", "targetUserId", "UserId", "userId", "TargetId", "targetId", "PlayerId", "playerId")
        local reporterId = field(payload, "ReporterUserId", "reporterUserId", "ReporterId", "reporterId", "FromUserId", "fromUserId", "AuthorUserId", "authorUserId")
        local actorId = field(payload, "ActorUserId", "actorUserId", "ModeratorUserId", "moderatorUserId", "StaffUserId", "staffUserId")
        local reason = field(payload, "Reason", "reason", "Message", "message", "Description", "description", "Text", "text")
        local aboutMe = targetId == Lp.UserId or payload.IsLocal == true or payload.Self == true
        local parts = { "[Mod]" }
        if type(kind) == "string" and kind ~= "" then table.insert(parts, string.lower(kind)) end
        if type(actorId) == "number" then table.insert(parts, "by " .. playerName(actorId)) end
        if type(reporterId) == "number" and type(targetId) == "number" then
            table.insert(parts, playerName(reporterId) .. " → " .. playerName(targetId))
        elseif aboutMe then table.insert(parts, "(you)")
        elseif type(targetId) == "number" then table.insert(parts, playerName(targetId)) end
        if reason then table.insert(parts, tostring(reason)) end
        if #parts == 1 then
            local keys = {}
            for key in pairs(payload) do table.insert(keys, tostring(key)) end
            table.sort(keys)
            table.insert(parts, "keys: " .. table.concat(keys, ", "))
        end
        return table.concat(parts, " "), aboutMe
    end
    local function onNotice(...)
        if not reportsActive() then return end
        local generation, args = reportEpoch, table.pack(...)
        local payload = args[1]
        for index = 1, args.n do
            if type(args[index]) == "table" then payload = args[index]; break end
        end
        local text, aboutMe = summarize(payload)
        -- Name lookups can yield while the user switches scanner or unloads.
        if generation ~= reportEpoch or not reportsActive() then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if text == lastNotice and now - lastNoticeAt < 2 then return end
        lastNotice, lastNoticeAt = text, now
        Library:Notify(text, aboutMe and math.max(7, NotificationDelay) or NotificationDelay)
    end
    local function disconnectReports()
        reportEpoch += 1
        for _, connection in ipairs(reportConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        for _, connection in pairs(boundRemotes) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(reportConnections)
        table.clear(boundRemotes)
        lastNotice, lastNoticeAt = nil, 0
    end
    local function updateReports()
        disconnectReports()
        if not reportsActive() then return end
        local function bind(remote)
            if not remoteNames[remote.Name] or boundRemotes[remote] then return end
            local signal
            if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then signal = remote.OnClientEvent
            elseif remote:IsA("BindableEvent") then signal = remote.Event end
            if signal then boundRemotes[remote] = Runtime:Connect(signal, onNotice) end
        end
        table.insert(reportConnections, Runtime:Connect(ReplicatedStorage.ChildAdded, bind))
        table.insert(reportConnections, Runtime:Connect(ReplicatedStorage.ChildRemoved, function(remote)
            local connection = boundRemotes[remote]
            if connection then connection:Disconnect(); boundRemotes[remote] = nil end
        end))
        for _, remote in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetChildren()) do bind(remote) end
    end
    local function disconnect()
        if addedConnection then addedConnection:Disconnect(); addedConnection = nil end
        if removedConnection then removedConnection:Disconnect(); removedConnection = nil end
        table.clear(seen)
        if alertSound then alertSound:Stop() end
    end
    local function restart()
        epoch += 1
        disconnect()
        if not StaffScannerEnabled then return end
        preloadAlert()
        addedConnection = Runtime:Connect(Players.PlayerAdded, function(player) inspect(player, true) end)
        removedConnection = Runtime:Connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(player) seen[player] = nil end)
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do inspect(player, false) end
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        StaffScannerEnabled, reportEnabled = false, false
        epoch += 1
        disconnect()
        disconnectReports()
        if alertSound then alertSound:Destroy(); alertSound = nil end
    end)
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddDropdown("StaffScannerType", {
        Text = "Scanner", Values = { "9an2s scanner", "james scanner" }, Default = "9an2s scanner",
        Callback = function(value)
            scannerChoice = value
            restart()
            updateReports()
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddToggle("StaffScanner", {
        Text = "Staff Scanner", Default = false,
        Callback = function(state) StaffScannerEnabled = state; restart() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddToggle("JamesScanLowRanks", {
        Text = "James: Include Low Ranks", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Include testers, contributors, wiki staff and content creators with james scanner.",
        Callback = function(state)
            scanLowRanks = state
            if scannerChoice == "james scanner" then restart() end
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddToggle("JamesReportNotifier", {
        Text = "James: Report / Mod Notifier", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Show available moderation notices while james scanner is selected.",
        Callback = function(state) reportEnabled = state; updateReports() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddDropdown("StaffJoinSound", {
        Text = "Mod Detection Sound", Values = { "Neverlose", "TF2", "Gamesense", "Rust", "Bubble", "Quake", "Classic Alert", "Off" },
        Default = "Neverlose", Callback = function(value)
            soundChoice = value
            if alertSound then alertSound:Stop() end
            if StaffScannerEnabled then preloadAlert() end
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddSlider("StaffSoundVolume", {
        Text = "Mod Sound Volume", Min = 0, Max = 3, Default = 1, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value)
            soundVolume = value
            if alertSound then alertSound.Volume = value end
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddSlider("StaffNotificationSpeed", {
        Text = "Notification Duration", Default = 5, Min = 1, Max = 10, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value) NotificationDelay = value end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Mod detector", function()
    local detector = CreateModeratorDetector(Runtime, game:GetService("Players"), function(text, duration)
        Library:Notify(text, duration)
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() detector:Destroy() end)
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddToggle("moddtc", {
        Text = "Mod Detector", Default = true,
        Tooltip = "Alerts for players in group 3441839 with moderator rank 99 or higher.",
        Callback = function(value) detector:SetEnabled(value) end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddSlider("moddtc_duration", {
        Text = "Mod Alert Duration", Min = 1, Max = 10, Default = 5, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value) detector.Duration = value end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Player notifiers", function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local function addNotifier(flag, label, event, action)
        local enabled, connection = false, nil
        local function disconnect()
            if connection then connection:Disconnect(); connection = nil end
        end
        table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
            enabled = false
            disconnect()
        end)
        GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddToggle(flag, {
            Text = "Player " .. label .. " Notifier", Default = false,
            Callback = function(state)
                enabled = state
                disconnect()
                if not state then return end
                connection = Runtime:Connect(event, function(player)
                    if not enabled or player == Players.LocalPlayer then return end
                    Library:Notify(string.format("[%s Notifier] %s has %s the game!",
                        label, player.Name, action), 4)
                end)
            end,
        })
    end
    addNotifier("JoinNotifier", "Join", Players.PlayerAdded, "joined")
    addNotifier("LeaveNotifier", "Leave", Players.PlayerRemoving, "left")
end)
Runtime:Init("Player HUD", function()
    local noAltynHudEnabled = false
    local originalGasMaskUI = nil

    local function removeGasMaskUI()
        pcall(function()
            local gasMaskUI = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.GameUI.BackgroundUI.GasMaskUI
            if gasMaskUI and not originalGasMaskUI then
                originalGasMaskUI = gasMaskUI:Clone()
                gasMaskUI:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function restoreGasMaskUI()
        pcall(function()
            if originalGasMaskUI then
                local backgroundUI = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.GameUI.BackgroundUI
                if backgroundUI and not backgroundUI:FindFirstChild('GasMaskUI') then
                    local restored = originalGasMaskUI:Clone()
                    restored.Parent = backgroundUI
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    GroupBoxes.Player:AddToggle("noaltynhud", {
        Text = "No Altyn UI",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            noAltynHudEnabled = state
            if state then
                removeGasMaskUI()
                task.spawn(function()
                    while noAltynHudEnabled and task.wait(0.5) do
                        removeGasMaskUI()
                    end
                end)
            else
                restoreGasMaskUI()
            end
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Server name", function()
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")

    local function getServerNameLabel()
        if not PlayerGui then return nil end
        local gameUI = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("GameUI")
        local perfStats = gameUI and gameUI:FindFirstChild("PerformanceStats")
        if not perfStats then return nil end

        return perfStats:FindFirstChild("ServerName")
    end

    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddToggle("ServerSpooferToggle", {
        Text = "Server Spoofer",
        Default = false
    })

    GroupBoxes.ServerScanner:AddInput("ServerNameInput", {
        Text = "Server Name",
        Default = "",
        Placeholder = "Type server name...",
        Numeric = false,
        Finished = false
    })

    local spoofConnection
    Toggles.ServerSpooferToggle:OnChanged(function()
        if Toggles.ServerSpooferToggle.Value then
            spoofConnection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
                local nameLabel = getServerNameLabel()
                local nameVal = Options.ServerNameInput and Options.ServerNameInput.Value or ""
                
                if nameLabel and nameVal ~= "" then
                    nameLabel.Text = "SERVER: " .. nameVal -- Fixed '+' to '..'
                end
            end)
        else
            if spoofConnection then
                spoofConnection:Disconnect()
                spoofConnection = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end)
Runtime:Init("Silent reload", function()
    local silentreloadconnection = nil

    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle('SilentReloadToggle', {
        Text = 'Silent Reload',
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if silentreloadconnection then 
                silentreloadconnection:Disconnect()
                silentreloadconnection = nil 
            end 
            if v then 
                silentreloadconnection = Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
                    LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
                    local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                    local gunSystem = rs and rs:FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
                    local eventFolder = gunSystem and gunSystem:FindFirstChild("Event")
                    local gunEvent = eventFolder and eventFolder:FindFirstChild("GunEvent")
                    local reloadStart = gunEvent and gunEvent:FindFirstChild("GunReloadStart")
                    
                    if reloadStart then
                        reloadStart:FireServer()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Sounds / bullet tracers", function()
    local flags = getgenv().flags
    local clearBulletTracers
    FLAGS = FLAGS or {}
    FLAGS.misc = FLAGS.misc or {}
    FLAGS.misc.HitmarkerEnabled = false
    FLAGS.misc.SelectedHitSound = "Neverlose"
    FLAGS.misc.FireSoundEnabled = false
    FLAGS.misc.SelectedFireSound = "Raygun"

    FLAGS.misc.BeamEnabled = false
    FLAGS.misc.BeamColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 120, 255) 
    FLAGS.misc.BeamWidth = 0.5
    FLAGS.misc.BeamLifetime = 0.5
    FLAGS.misc.BeamTransparency = 0
    FLAGS.misc.BeamEndWidth = 0.5
    FLAGS.misc.BeamBrightness = 1
    FLAGS.misc.BeamLightEmission = 1
    FLAGS.misc.BeamLightInfluence = 0
    FLAGS.misc.BeamSegments = 10
    FLAGS.misc.BeamFaceCamera = true
    FLAGS.misc.BeamTexture = "Streak"
    FLAGS.misc.BeamTextureMode = "Stretch"
    FLAGS.misc.BeamTextureLength = 1
    FLAGS.misc.BeamGlowEnabled, FLAGS.misc.BeamGlowStrength = false, 0.6

    local HitSounds = {
        ["Neverlose"] = "rbxassetid://8726881116",
        ["TF2"] = "rbxassetid://8255306220",
        ["Gamesense"] = "rbxassetid://4817809188",
        ["Rust"] = "rbxassetid://1255040462",
        ["Bubble"] = "rbxassetid://85730811347567",
        ["Quake"] = "rbxassetid://1455817260",
        ["Among-Us"] = "rbxassetid://7227567562",
        ["Ding"] = "rbxassetid://2868331684",
        ["Minecraft"] = "rbxassetid://6361963422",
        ["Blackout"] = "rbxassetid://3748776946",
        ["Osu!"] = "rbxassetid://7151989073",
        ["HEADSHOT"] = "rbxassetid://18362692980",

    local FireSounds = {
        ["Raygun"] = "rbxassetid://131340586597360",
        ["Uwu"] = "rbxassetid://139219844119994",
        ["Laser 1"] = "rbxassetid://137510557013265",
        ["Laser 2"] = "rbxassetid://127358374154057",

    for name, soundId in pairs(HitSounds) do if not FireSounds[name] then FireSounds[name] = soundId end end

    for name, _ in pairs(HitSounds) do
        table.insert(hitSoundNames, name)

    table.sort(hitSoundNames)

    local fireSoundNames = {}
    for name, _ in pairs(FireSounds) do

    table.sort(fireSoundNames)

    GroupBoxes.Sounds:AddToggle("HitmarkerToggle", {

        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Sounds:AddToggle("FireSoundToggle", {
        Text = "Custom Fire Sounds",
        Default = FLAGS.misc.FireSoundEnabled,

    local customSounds = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local playSoundEffect = function(soundId)
        local sound = Instance.new('Sound')
        customSounds[sound] = true
        sound.SoundId = soundId
        sound.Volume = 1
        sound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        Runtime:Once(sound.Ended, function()
            sound:Destroy()
        end)
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 10)
        sound:Play()
    end

    local cachedWeapons, nextWeaponRefresh, weaponService = {}, 0, nil
    local function worldWeaponModels()
        if os.clock() < nextWeaponRefresh then return cachedWeapons end
        nextWeaponRefresh = os.clock() + 0.25
        table.clear(cachedWeapons)
        if not weaponService then
            local guns = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
            local module = guns and guns:FindFirstChild("WeaponWorldModelService")
            if module then local ok, value = pcall(CallGameFunction, require, module); if ok and type(value) == "table" then weaponService = value end end
        end
        if weaponService and type(weaponService.GetSelectedWeapons) == "function" then
            local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            local rigs, seen = {}, {}
            local function add(rig) if rig and rig.Parent and not seen[rig] then seen[rig] = true; table.insert(rigs, rig) end end
            add(player.Character)
            for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
                if entity.Player == player then add(entity.WorldModel); add(entity.Character) end
            end
            for _, rig in ipairs(rigs) do
                local ok, selected = pcall(CallGameFunction, weaponService.GetSelectedWeapons, weaponService, player, rig)
                if ok and type(selected) == "table" then
                    for _, record in ipairs(selected) do
                        local model = record.WeaponModel and record.WeaponModel.Instance
                        if model then table.insert(cachedWeapons, model) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return cachedWeapons
    end
    local function replacePlayedSound(self)
        if customSounds[self] then return end
        if self.ClassName == "Sound" then
            local parentName = self.Parent and self.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
            local soundName = self.Name:lower()
            local fullName = ""
            local current = self
            while current and current ~= game do
                fullName = current.Name .. "." .. fullName
                current = current.Parent
            end
            fullName = fullName:lower()

            if FLAGS.misc.FireSoundEnabled then
                local checked, isLocal = pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
                    local owned = IsLocalFireSound(self, localPlayer, workspace.CurrentCamera, nil)
                    if not owned then
                        owned = IsLocalFireSound(self, localPlayer, workspace.CurrentCamera, Runtime:GetEntities(), worldWeaponModels())
                    end
                    return owned
                end)
                if not checked then Runtime:Report("Fire sound ownership", isLocal) end
                if checked and isLocal then
                    local customId = FireSounds[FLAGS.misc.SelectedFireSound] or FireSounds["Raygun"]
                    local played, message = pcall(playSoundEffect, customId)
                    if played then
                        pcall(function() self:Stop() end)
                        return
                    end
                    Runtime:Report("Local fire sound", message)
                end
            end

            if FLAGS.misc.HitmarkerEnabled then
                if fullName:find("defaulthitmarker") or parentName:find("hit") or soundName:find("hit") then
                    local soundId = HitSounds[FLAGS.misc.SelectedHitSound] or HitSounds["Neverlose"]
                    local played, message = pcall(playSoundEffect, soundId)
                    if played then pcall(function() self:Stop() end)
                    else Runtime:Report("Hit sound", message) end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local inFlight = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local function playThrough(original, self, ...)
        if inFlight[self] or not Runtime.Active or not (FLAGS.misc.FireSoundEnabled or FLAGS.misc.HitmarkerEnabled) then return original(self, ...) end
        inFlight[self] = true
        local returned = table.pack(pcall(original, self, ...))
        if returned[1] then
            local ok, message = pcall(replacePlayedSound, self)
            if not ok then Runtime:Report("Custom sounds", message) end
        end
        inFlight[self] = nil
        if not returned[1] then error(returned[2], 0) end
        return table.unpack(returned, 2, returned.n)
    end
    local oldNamecall
    oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
        if getnamecallmethod() ~= "Play" then return oldNamecall(self, ...) end
        return playThrough(oldNamecall, self, ...)
    end)
    local probe = Instance.new("Sound")
    local oldPlay
    oldPlay = hookfunction(probe.Play, function(self, ...) return playThrough(oldPlay, self, ...) end)
    probe:Destroy()

    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

    local activeBulletTracers = {}
    local tracerBinding = "LucidBulletTracers_" .. tostring(Runtime)
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(tracerBinding, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 3, function(dt)
        for _, update in pairs(activeBulletTracers) do update(dt) end
    end)
    clearBulletTracers = function()
        for dispose in pairs(activeBulletTracers) do dispose() end
    end
    local function createCustomTracer(startWorldPos, endWorldPos)
        if not FLAGS.misc.BeamEnabled then return end
        local tracerPart = Instance.new("Part")
        tracerPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
        tracerPart.Transparency, tracerPart.Anchored = 1, true
        tracerPart.CanCollide, tracerPart.CanTouch, tracerPart.CanQuery = false, false, false
        tracerPart.CastShadow = false
        tracerPart.CFrame, tracerPart.Parent = CFrame.new(startWorldPos), workspace
        local endPart = tracerPart:Clone()
        endPart.CFrame, endPart.Parent = CFrame.new(endWorldPos), workspace
        local att1, att2 = Instance.new("Attachment"), Instance.new("Attachment")
        att1.Parent, att2.Parent = tracerPart, endPart
        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Attachment0, beam.Attachment1 = att1, att2
        local glow = CreateTracerGlow(beam, tracerPart)
        local scoped = type(Runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin) == "function" and Runtime.GetScopedVisualOrigin(0.06)
