        local scopedAtFire = typeof(scoped) == "Vector3"
        local function updateAppearance()
            local appearance = FLAGS.misc
            if scopedAtFire then
                appearance = table.clone(appearance)
                appearance.BeamWidth = math.max(appearance.BeamWidth, 0.08)
                appearance.BeamEndWidth = math.max(appearance.BeamEndWidth, 0.04)
                appearance.BeamBrightness = math.max(appearance.BeamBrightness, 3)
                appearance.BeamLightEmission, appearance.BeamLightInfluence = 1, 0
                appearance.BeamFaceCamera, appearance.BeamTexture = true, "Solid"
            end
            ApplyTracerAppearance(beam, appearance)
            glow:Update(appearance)
        end
        updateAppearance()
        beam.Parent = tracerPart
        local function dispose()
            if not activeBulletTracers[dispose] then return end
            activeBulletTracers[dispose] = nil
            glow:Destroy()
            tracerPart:Destroy()
            endPart:Destroy()
        end
        local elapsed, lifetime = 0, math.max(FLAGS.misc.BeamLifetime, 0.05)
        activeBulletTracers[dispose] = function(dt)
            LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
            elapsed += dt
            if elapsed >= lifetime or not FLAGS.misc.BeamEnabled then dispose(); return end
            updateAppearance()
        end
        -- Finishing an expired/toggled-off trace never spawns a delayed duplicate.
        return function(endpoint)
            if activeBulletTracers[dispose] and typeof(endpoint) == "Vector3" then endPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(endpoint) end
        end
    end
    Runtime.BeginBulletTrace = createCustomTracer
    Runtime.BulletTrace = createCustomTracer
    Runtime.BulletTraceActive = FLAGS.misc.BeamEnabled
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        clearBulletTracers()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(tracerBinding)
        Runtime.BeginBulletTrace, Runtime.BulletTrace, Runtime.BulletTraceActive = nil, nil, false
    end)

end)
Runtime:Init("Crosshair", function()

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)

    local function removeDefaultCrosshairs()
        local defaultNames = {"Crosshair", "CrosshairGui"}
        for _, name in ipairs(defaultNames) do
            local gui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(name)
            if gui then
                gui:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    removeDefaultCrosshairs()
    Runtime:Connect(PlayerGui.ChildAdded, function(child)
        if child.Name == "Crosshair" or child.Name == "CrosshairGui" then
            task.defer(function()
                child:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end)

    FLAGS.misc = FLAGS.misc or {}
    FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair or {
        Enabled = true,
        Type = "Cross",
        Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 128, 255), 
        Size = 12,
        Thickness = 2,
        Gap = 4,
        SpinSpeed = 180,
    }
    local crosshair = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair
    MergeDefaults(crosshair, {
        Color2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 180), AnimationMode = "Off",
        AnimationSpeed = 1, PulseAmount = 0.25, SpinEnabled = false,
        LockEnabled = false,
    })
    if crosshair.Type == "Classic (+)" then crosshair.Type = "Cross" end

    local crosshairToggle = GroupBoxes.Cross:AddToggle("CustomCrosshairEnabled", {
        Text = "Custom Crosshair",
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Enabled,
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Enabled = v
        end
    })
    local crosshairSettings = crosshairToggle:AddSettings()
    crosshairSettings:AddLabel("Primary Color"):AddColorPicker("CustomCrosshairColor", {
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Color,
        Title = "Crosshair Color",
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Color = v
        end
    })

    crosshairSettings:AddLabel("Secondary Color"):AddColorPicker("CustomCrosshairColor2", {
        Default = crosshair.Color2, Title = "Secondary Color",
        Callback = function(value) crosshair.Color2 = value end,
    })
    crosshairSettings:AddDropdown("CustomCrosshairAnimation", {
        Text = "Animation", Values = { "Off", "Color Wave", "Pulse", "Wave + Pulse" }, Default = crosshair.AnimationMode,
        Callback = function(value) crosshair.AnimationMode = value end,
    })
    crosshairSettings:AddSlider("CustomCrosshairAnimationSpeed", {
        Text = "Animation Speed", Default = crosshair.AnimationSpeed, Min = 0.1, Max = 10, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value) crosshair.AnimationSpeed = value end,
    })
    crosshairSettings:AddSlider("CustomCrosshairPulseAmount", {
        Text = "Pulse Amount", Default = crosshair.PulseAmount, Min = 0, Max = 0.75, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value) crosshair.PulseAmount = value end,
    })
    crosshairSettings:AddToggle("CustomCrosshairSpinEnabled", {
        Text = "Spin", Default = crosshair.SpinEnabled,
        Callback = function(value) crosshair.SpinEnabled = value end,
    })

    crosshairSettings:AddDropdown("CustomCrosshairType", {
        Values = {"Cross", "Three Lines", "Dot", "Circle", "Swastika"},
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Type,
        Text = "Crosshair Style",
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Type = v
        end
    })

    crosshairSettings:AddToggle("CustomCrosshairLock", {
        Text = "Follow Looked-at Player", Default = crosshair.LockEnabled,
        Callback = function(value) crosshair.LockEnabled = value end,
    })
    crosshairSettings:AddSlider("CustomCrosshairSize", {
        Text = "Size",
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Size,
        Min = 2,
        Max = 40,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Size = v
        end
    })

    crosshairSettings:AddSlider("CustomCrosshairThickness", {
        Text = "Thickness",
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Thickness,
        Min = 1,
        Max = 8,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Thickness = v
        end
    })

    crosshairSettings:AddSlider("CustomCrosshairGap", {
        Text = "Center Gap",
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Gap,
        Min = 0,
        Max = 20,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.Gap = v
        end
    })

    crosshairSettings:AddSlider("CustomCrosshairSpin", {
        Text = "Spin Speed",
        Default = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.SpinSpeed,
        Min = 0,
        Max = 720, 
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(v)
            FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair.SpinSpeed = v
        end
    })

    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

    local crosshairLines = {}
    for i = 1, 16 do
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Visible = false
        table.insert(crosshairLines, line)
    end

    local centerDot = Drawing.new("Circle")
    centerDot.Visible = false
    centerDot.Filled = true
    centerDot.NumSides = 12

    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local lockedPart
    local function collectTargetParts()
        local parts, seen, knownPlayers = {}, {}, {}
        local function add(player, character, model, dead)
            if not player then return end
            knownPlayers[player] = true
            if player == localPlayer or dead or player:GetAttribute("Dead") then return end
            if flags.teamcheck_inventory then
                local squad = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName")
                if (localPlayer.Team and player.Team == localPlayer.Team)
                    or (squad and squad == player:GetAttribute("SquadName")) then return end
            end
            character = character or model
            if not character then return end
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
            local health = character:GetAttribute("Health")
            if type(health) == "number" and health <= 0 then return end
            local head = (model and model:FindFirstChild("Head")) or character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head and head:IsA("BasePart") and head.Parent and not seen[head] then
                seen[head] = true
                table.insert(parts, head)
            end
        end
        local list = Runtime:GetEntities()
        if list then
            for _, entity in pairs(list) do
                add(entity.Player, entity.Character, entity.WorldModel, entity.IsDead or entity.Dead)
            end
        end
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if not knownPlayers[player] then add(player, player.Character) end
        end
        return parts
    end

    local targetGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.1)
    local targetParts = {}
    local currentRotation, animationTime = 0, 0

    Runtime:Connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function(dt)
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        local cfg = FLAGS.misc.CustomCrosshair
        
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cfg.Enabled or not camera then
            lockedPart = nil
            Runtime.CrosshairTarget = nil
            for _, line in ipairs(crosshairLines) do line.Visible = false end
            centerDot.Visible = false
            return
        end

        local center = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
        local crosshairPos = center
        if cfg.LockEnabled then
            if targetGate:Ready(os.clock()) then targetParts = collectTargetParts() end
            -- Follow the displayed inventory player, while rechecking the FOV every frame.
            local _, inventoryModel = GetInventorySelection(Runtime)
            local point
            lockedPart, point = SelectCrosshairTarget(camera, targetParts, center,
                flags.FovSize or 120, flags.TargetClosestPlayers, localPlayer, inventoryModel)
            if lockedPart then crosshairPos = point end
        else
            lockedPart = nil
            targetParts = {}
            targetGate.Next = -math.huge
        end
        Runtime.CrosshairTarget = lockedPart
        animationTime += dt
        if cfg.SpinEnabled and cfg.SpinSpeed > 0 then
            currentRotation = (currentRotation + cfg.SpinSpeed * dt) % 360
        else currentRotation = 0 end
        local drawColor, drawSize, rad = CrosshairAppearance(cfg, animationTime, currentRotation)

        for _, line in ipairs(crosshairLines) do line.Visible = false end
        centerDot.Visible = false


        if cfg.Type == "Cross" or cfg.Type == "Three Lines" then
            for i, dir in ipairs(CrosshairDirections(cfg.Type)) do
                local line = crosshairLines[i]
                local rotatedDir = dir
                if cfg.SpinEnabled and cfg.SpinSpeed > 0 then
                    local cos, sin = math.cos(rad), math.sin(rad)
                    rotatedDir = Vector2.new(dir.X * cos - dir.Y * sin, dir.X * sin + dir.Y * cos)
                end

                local innerOffset = rotatedDir * cfg.Gap
                local outerOffset = rotatedDir * (cfg.Gap + drawSize)

                line.From = crosshairPos + innerOffset
                line.To = crosshairPos + outerOffset
                line.Thickness = cfg.Thickness
                line.Color = drawColor
                line.Visible = true
            end

        elseif cfg.Type == "Dot" then
            centerDot.Filled = true
            centerDot.Position = crosshairPos
            centerDot.Radius = cfg.Thickness * 1.5 * (drawSize / cfg.Size)
            centerDot.Color = drawColor
            centerDot.Visible = true

        elseif cfg.Type == "Circle" then
            centerDot.Position = crosshairPos
            centerDot.Radius = drawSize
            centerDot.Thickness = cfg.Thickness
            centerDot.Color = drawColor
            centerDot.Filled = false
            centerDot.Visible = true

        elseif cfg.Type == "Swastika" then
            local numSegments = 4
            local lineIndex = 1
            for i = 0, numSegments - 1 do
                local angle = rad + (i * (math.pi / 2))
                local p1 = crosshairPos + Vector2.new(math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)) * cfg.Gap
                local p2 = crosshairPos + Vector2.new(math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)) * (cfg.Gap + drawSize)
                local perpAngle = angle + math.pi / 2
                local p3 = p2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(perpAngle), math.sin(perpAngle)) * (drawSize * 0.6)

                if crosshairLines[lineIndex] and crosshairLines[lineIndex + 1] then
                    local l1 = crosshairLines[lineIndex]
                    local l2 = crosshairLines[lineIndex + 1]

                    l1.From = p1
                    l1.To = p2
                    l1.Thickness = cfg.Thickness
                    l1.Color = drawColor
                    l1.Visible = true

                    l2.From = p2
                    l2.To = p3
                    l2.Thickness = cfg.Thickness
                    l2.Color = drawColor
                    l2.Visible = true

                    lineIndex = lineIndex + 2
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)
-- BEGIN FAST FIRE
Runtime:Init("Fast fire", function()
    local input = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local run = game:GetService("RunService")
    local enabled, focused, busy, resolving = false, true, false, false
    local interval, nextShot, nextResolve, generation = 0.10, 0, 0, 0
    local gunPlugin, gunModule
    local function currentModule()
        local system = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
        local controller = system and system:FindFirstChild("GunController")
        local events = controller and controller:FindFirstChild("Events")
        return events and events:FindFirstChild("GunPlugin")
    end
    local function resolve(module)
        if resolving then return end
        resolving, nextResolve = true, os.clock() + 0.5
        local version = generation
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, plugin = pcall(function()
                if module then return CallGameFunction(require, module) end
            end)
            resolving = false
            if version ~= generation or not enabled or not Runtime.Active then return end
            if ok and type(plugin) == "table" then gunModule, gunPlugin = module, plugin
            else gunModule, gunPlugin = nil, nil end
            if not ok then Runtime:Report("Fast Fire loading", plugin) end
        end)
    end
    local function reset(state)
        generation += 1
        enabled, nextShot, nextResolve = state, 0, 0
        gunModule, gunPlugin = nil, nil
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() reset(false) end)
    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle("FastFireEnabled", {
        Text = "Fast Fire", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Repeats native firing while left click is held, up to 10 shots per second; waits for the gun system if it is still loading",
        Callback = function(state) reset(state) end,
    })
    Runtime:Connect(input.WindowFocused, function() focused = true end)
    Runtime:Connect(input.WindowFocusReleased, function() focused = false end)
    Runtime:Connect(run.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not enabled then return end
        local now, module = os.clock(), currentModule()
        -- Resolve while enabled even before clicking, and reacquire replaced controllers.
        if module ~= gunModule then gunPlugin = nil end
        if now >= nextResolve then resolve(module) end
        if flags.RageBotEnabled or Runtime.FreecamActive or busy or not gunPlugin or not focused or Window.IsOpen
            or input:GetFocusedTextBox() or not input:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) or now < nextShot then return end
        -- Read the current dispatch method: controller initialization can replace it
        -- after the module was first required or after respawning.
        local request = gunPlugin.Functions and gunPlugin.Functions.AttemptShootGun
        local owner, callback = gunPlugin, gunPlugin.AttemptShootGun
        if request and type(request.Invoke) == "function" then owner, callback = request, request.Invoke end
        if type(callback) ~= "function" then nextResolve = 0; return end
        busy = true
        local version = generation
        local ok, message = pcall(CallGameFunction, callback, owner)
        busy = false
        if version ~= generation or not enabled then return end
        if ok then
            if message ~= false then nextShot = os.clock() + interval end
        else
            gunPlugin, gunModule, nextResolve = nil, nil, os.clock() + 0.5
            Runtime:Report("Fast Fire retry", message)
        end
    end)
end)
-- END FAST FIRE

Runtime:Init("Triggerbot", function()
    local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local Workspace = workspace

    local clickDelay = 0.08
    Runtime.TriggerDelay = clickDelay
    local fovRadius = 300
    local triggerEnabled = false
    local fovColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

    local fovCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
    fovCircle.Visible = false
    fovCircle.Thickness = 1
    fovCircle.NumSides = 64
    fovCircle.Filled = false
    fovCircle.Transparency = 1

    local function IsTeammate(player)
        if not player then return true end
        if player == LocalPlayer then return true end

        local success1, mySquad = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        local success2, theirSquad = pcall(function() return player:GetAttribute("SquadName") end)
        if success1 and success2 and mySquad and theirSquad and mySquad == theirSquad then
            return true
        end

        local successRank1, myRank = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        local successRank2, theirRank = pcall(function() return player:GetRankInGroup(3441839) end)
        if successRank1 and successRank2 and myRank and theirRank and myRank > 0 and theirRank > 0 then
            return true
        end
        
        return false
    end

    local function IsDead(v)
        if not v then return true end
        
        local player = v.Player
        if player then
            local success, deadAttr = pcall(function()
                return player:GetAttribute("Dead")
            end)
            if success and deadAttr then
                return true
            end
        end
        
        local successTable, deadAttrTable = pcall(function() return v.IsDead or v.Dead end)
        if successTable and deadAttrTable then return true end

        local character = v.Character or (v.WorldModel and v.WorldModel.Parent)
        if character then
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                return true
            end

            local successHealth, health = pcall(function() return character:GetAttribute("Health") end)
            if successHealth and health and health <= 0 then
                return true
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local function get_entity_list()
        return Runtime:GetEntities()
    end

    local cachedRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    cachedRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    cachedRayParams.IgnoreWater = true
    local cachedIgnore = {}

    local function IsVisible(targetPart, OriginPos, extraIgnore)
        if not targetPart or not targetPart.Parent then return false, nil end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        OriginPos = OriginPos or (camera and camera.CFrame.Position) or Vector3.new()
        
        table.clear(cachedIgnore)
        if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character then
            table.insert(cachedIgnore, LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        if camera then table.insert(cachedIgnore, camera) end
        if typeof(extraIgnore) == 'table' then
            for _, v in ipairs(extraIgnore) do 
                if typeof(v) == 'Instance' then 
                    table.insert(cachedIgnore, v) 
                end 
            end
        elseif typeof(extraIgnore) == 'Instance' then 
            table.insert(cachedIgnore, extraIgnore) 
        end
        
        cachedRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = cachedIgnore
        
        local direction = targetPart.Position - OriginPos
        if direction.Magnitude == 0 then return true, nil end
        
        local result = workspace:Raycast(OriginPos, direction, cachedRayParams)
        if not result then return true, nil end
        if result.Instance and result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetPart.Parent) then return true, result end
        return false, result
    end

    local function GetClosestTarget(aimPartName)
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return nil end

        local list = get_entity_list()
        if not list then return nil end

        local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        local maxDist = fovRadius
        local targetPart, targetEntity = nil, nil

        for _, v in pairs(list) do
            local player = v.Player
            if player and player ~= LocalPlayer and not IsTeammate(player) and not IsDead(v) then
                local character = v.Character
                local worldmodel = v.WorldModel

                if character and worldmodel then
                    local part = Runtime:GetAimPart(character, worldmodel, aimPartName)

                    if part then
                        local sp, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                        if onScreen then
                            local dist = (Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
                            if dist < maxDist then
                                maxDist = dist
                                targetPart, targetEntity = part, v
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return targetPart, targetEntity
    end

    local triggerBusy = false
    local lastShotTick = 0
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if triggerEnabled then
            local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            fovCircle.Position = mousePos
            fovCircle.Radius = fovRadius
            fovCircle.Color = fovColor
            fovCircle.Visible = true
        else
            fovCircle.Visible = false
        end

        if flags.RageBotEnabled or Runtime.FreecamActive or Window.IsOpen or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
            or not triggerEnabled or triggerBusy then return end

        if (tick() - lastShotTick) < clickDelay then return end

        pcall(function()
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then return end

            local targetHead, entity = GetClosestTarget("Head")
            if not targetHead then return end
            if flags.aimbot.exploits.manipulation.enabled then
                if not Runtime.FireManipulatedTarget then return end
                triggerBusy = true
                local ok, accepted = pcall(Runtime.FireManipulatedTarget, targetHead, entity)
                triggerBusy = false
                if ok and accepted then lastShotTick = tick() end
                if not ok then Runtime:Report("Manipulation Triggerbot", accepted) end
                return
            end

            local isVis = false
            local visible, rayResult = IsVisible(targetHead, camera.CFrame.Position)
            
            if visible then
                isVis = true
            else
                if rayResult and rayResult.Position then
                    local headSize = typeof(targetHead.Size) == "Vector3" and targetHead.Size.Magnitude or 2
                    local threshold = math.max(2, headSize * 1.5)
                    if (rayResult.Position - targetHead.Position).Magnitude <= threshold then
                        isVis = true
                    end
                end
            end

            if isVis then
                lastShotTick = tick()
                VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(0.01)
                    VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
                end)
            end
        end)
    end)

    GroupBoxes.Trigger:AddToggle("TriggerBotToggle", {
        Text = "Trigger Bot",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(state)
            triggerEnabled = state
        end
    }):AddKeyPicker("TriggerBotKeyPicker", {
        Text = "TriggerBot Key",
        Default = "None",
        Callback = function()
            if Toggles and Toggles.TriggerBotToggle then
                Toggles.TriggerBotToggle:SetValue(not Toggles.TriggerBotToggle.Value)
            end
        end
    }):AddColorPicker("TriggerBotFOVColor", {
        Text = "FOV Color",
        Default = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        Callback = function(color)
            fovColor = color
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Trigger:AddSlider("TriggerBotDelay", {
        Text = "Shot Delay",
        Default = 0.08,
        Min = 0.01,
        Max = 0.5,
        Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value)
            clickDelay = value
            Runtime.TriggerDelay = value
        end
    })

    GroupBoxes.Trigger:AddSlider("TriggerBotFOVRadius", {
        Text = "FOV Radius",
        Default = 300,
        Min = 50,
        Max = 1500,
        Rounding = 0,
        Callback = function(value)
            fovRadius = value
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Zombie ESP", function()
    Players = Players or game:GetService("Players")
    LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    RunService = game:GetService('RunService')
    
    specialZombieESP = specialZombieESP or {
        Enabled = false,
        ShowName = true,
        ShowDistance = false,
        Color1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251),
        Size = 14,
        RenderDistance = 10000,
        Active = {},
        Font = 2,
    }
    
    local Functions = {}
    do
        function Functions:FadeOutOnDist(drawingsList, distance, maxDistance)
            local transparency = math.max(0.1, 1 - (distance / maxDistance))
            for _, obj in ipairs(drawingsList) do
                obj.Transparency = transparency
            end
        end
    end

    local function cleanupZombieData(data)
        if data and data.charDrawings then
            for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do
                pcall(function() obj:Remove() end)
            end
            data.charDrawings = {}
        end
    end

    local function getSpecialZombieInfo(zombie)
        for _, item in pairs(zombie.Equipment or {}) do
            local itemClass = item.ClassName or ""
            local skin = item.SkinOverride or ""
            
            if itemClass:find("Altyn") then
                return "Altyn Helmet", item
            elseif skin:find("Green") and skin:find("Beret") then
                return "Green Beret", item
            elseif skin:find("Black") and skin:find("Beret") then
                return "Black Beret", item
            end
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    local npcGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.25)
    local NPCSimulatorService
    local function updateSpecialZombieESP()
        Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera or Camera
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local InitializerPos = (char and char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position) or Camera.CFrame.Position
        
        if npcGate:Ready(os.clock()) then
            if not NPCSimulatorService then
                local success, service = pcall(function()
                    local client = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"):WaitForChild("EmberClientLibrary", 10):WaitForChild("EmberClient", 10):WaitForChild("EmberClient", 10))
                    return client:GetService("NPCSimulatorService")
                end)
                if not success then return end
                NPCSimulatorService = service
            end
            if not NPCSimulatorService then return end
            local currentActiveZombies = {}
        
            for _, zombie in pairs(NPCSimulatorService.NPCs or {}) do
                local labelName, matchedItem = getSpecialZombieInfo(zombie)
            
                if labelName and zombie.Character then
                    local rootPart = zombie.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or zombie.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    if rootPart then
                        currentActiveZombies[zombie] = {
                            zombie = zombie,
                            part = rootPart,
                            labelName = labelName,
                            charDrawings = specialZombieESP.Active[zombie] and specialZombieESP.Active[zombie].charDrawings or {},
                            lastText = specialZombieESP.Active[zombie] and specialZombieESP.Active[zombie].lastText or ""
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
        
            -- Clean up removed zombies
            for zombie, data in pairs(specialZombieESP.Active) do
                if not currentActiveZombies[zombie] then
                    cleanupZombieData(data)
                    specialZombieESP.Active[zombie] = nil
                end
            end
        
            specialZombieESP.Active = currentActiveZombies
        
        end

        -- Render updates
        for zombie, data in pairs(specialZombieESP.Active) do
            local part = data.part
            local dist = (part.Position - InitializerPos).Magnitude
            local displayDist = dist / 2.56
            
            if specialZombieESP.Enabled and dist <= specialZombieESP.RenderDistance then
                local targetWorldPos = part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                local pos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetWorldPos)
                
                if onScreen then
                    local text = ''
                    if specialZombieESP.ShowName or specialZombieESP.ShowDistance then
                        if specialZombieESP.ShowName then
                            text = data.labelName
                        end
                        if specialZombieESP.ShowDistance then
                            local distText = string.format('[%.0fm]', displayDist)
                            text = (text ~= '' and (text .. ' ' .. distText)) or distText
                        end
                    end
                    
                    if text == '' then
                        for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do obj.Visible = false end
                    else
                        local label = data.charDrawings[1]
                        if not label then
                            label = Drawing.new("Text")
                            label.Center, label.Outline = true, true
                            label.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                            data.charDrawings[1] = label
                        end
                        label.Visible = true
                        label.Size, label.Font = specialZombieESP.Size, specialZombieESP.Font
                        label.Text, label.Position = text, Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y)
                        label.Color = specialZombieESP.Color1
                        Functions:FadeOutOnDist(data.charDrawings, dist, specialZombieESP.RenderDistance)
                    end
                else
                    for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do obj.Visible = false end
                end
            else
                for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings) do obj.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end
    
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if specialZombieESP.Enabled and (specialZombieESP.ShowName or specialZombieESP.ShowDistance) then
            updateSpecialZombieESP()
        else
            for _, data in pairs(specialZombieESP.Active) do
                for _, obj in ipairs(data.charDrawings or {}) do
                    obj.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    GroupBoxes.ZombieESP:AddToggle("SpecialZombieNameToggle", {
        Text = 'Special Zombie Name',    
        Default = false,    
        Callback = function(state)
            specialZombieESP.ShowName = state
            specialZombieESP.Enabled = specialZombieESP.ShowName or specialZombieESP.ShowDistance
            if not specialZombieESP.Enabled then
                for _, data in pairs(specialZombieESP.Active) do
                    cleanupZombieData(data)
                end
            end
        end,    
    }):AddColorPicker("SpecialZombieESPColor1", {
        Title = 'Zombie Color 1',    
        Default = specialZombieESP.Color1,    
        Callback = function(value)
            specialZombieESP.Color1 = value
        end,    
    })


    GroupBoxes.ZombieESP:AddToggle('SpecialZombieDistanceToggle', {
        Text = 'Special Zombie Distance',    
        Default = false,    
        Callback = function(state)
            specialZombieESP.ShowDistance = state
            specialZombieESP.Enabled = specialZombieESP.ShowName or specialZombieESP.ShowDistance
            if not specialZombieESP.Enabled then
                for _, data in pairs(specialZombieESP.Active) do
                    cleanupZombieData(data)
                end
            end
        end,    
    })

    GroupBoxes.ZombieESP:AddSlider('SpecialZombieESPRenderDistance', {
        Text = 'Special Zombie ESP Distance',    
        Min = 50,    
        Max = 10000,    
        Default = specialZombieESP.RenderDistance,    
        Rounding = 0,    
        Callback = function(value)
            specialZombieESP.RenderDistance = value
        end,    
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Hand offsets", function()
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local offsets = CreateHandOffsets(Runtime, function()
        local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("GunSystemAssets")
        return assets and assets:FindFirstChild("GunData")
    end)
    local elapsed = 0
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
        elapsed += dt
        if elapsed < 0.5 then return end
        elapsed = 0
        if offsets.Enabled then offsets:Refresh() end
    end)
    GroupBoxes.HandOffsets:AddToggle("viewmodel_offset", {
        Text = "Hand Offset Changer", Default = false,
        Callback = function(value) offsets:SetEnabled(value) end,
    })
    for _, axis in ipairs({"X", "Y", "Z"}) do
        GroupBoxes.HandOffsets:AddSlider("viewmodel_offset_" .. axis:lower(), {
            Text = "Hand Offset " .. axis, Min = -5, Max = 5, Default = 0, Rounding = 1,
            Callback = function(value) offsets:SetAxis(axis, value) end,
        })
    end
end)
Runtime:Init("Chams", function()
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local world = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    local Flags = {
        Weapon = false, Arms = false, Body = false, Car = false, Player = false,
        WeaponColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251), ArmColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251),
        BodyColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251), CarColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 255),
        PlayerColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 128, 255),
        WeaponMaterial = "ForceField", ArmMaterial = "ForceField", BodyMaterial = "ForceField", CarMaterial = "ForceField",
        Brightness = 0.65, HeadTransparency = 0, CarTransparency = 0,
        GunGlow = false, GunGlowStrength = 0.6, GunGlowWidth = 0.04,
    }
    local Saved, CarSaved, Sources = {}, {}, {}
    local ViewModelColors = {}
    local gunGlow = CreateGunGlow(world)
    local GunPlugin, pluginModule, nextPluginRetry = nil, nil, 0
    local pluginConnections = {}
    local worldServices, nextWorldServiceRetry = {}, 0
    local alive = true
    local ApplyChams, ApplyCarChams

    local function dimColor(color, factor)
        return Color3.new(math.clamp(color.R * factor, 0, 1), math.clamp(color.G * factor, 0, 1), math.clamp(color.B * factor, 0, 1))
    end
    local function resolvemat(name)
        return Enum.Material[name] or Enum.Material.ForceField
    end
    local function path(root, names)
        for _, name in ipairs(names) do root = root and root:FindFirstChild(name) end
        return root
    end
    local function disconnect(connections)
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(connections)
    end
    local function connect(signal, callback, connections)
        if not signal or type(signal.Connect) ~= "function" then return end
        local connection = Runtime:Connect(signal, callback)
        if connections then table.insert(connections, connection) end
        return connection
    end
    local function changedAsset(object)
        local part = object.Parent
        local saved = part and (Saved[part] or CarSaved[part])
        if saved then saved.AssetsDirty = true end
    end
    local function source(root)
        local entry = Sources[root]
        if entry then entry.Used = true; return entry.Parts end
        entry = {Parts = {}, Connections = {}, Used = true}
        Sources[root] = entry
        local function added(object)
            if object:IsA("BasePart") then entry.Parts[object] = true else changedAsset(object) end
        end
        added(root)
        for _, object in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do added(object) end
        connect(root.DescendantAdded, added, entry.Connections)
        connect(root.DescendantRemoving, function(object)
            if object:IsA("BasePart") then entry.Parts[object] = nil end
        end, entry.Connections)
        return entry.Parts
    end
    local function collectAssets(part, saved)
        for _, object in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
            if not saved.Assets[object] then
                if object:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or object:IsA("Decal") or object:IsA("Texture") then
                    saved.Assets[object] = {Parent = part}
                elseif object:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                    saved.Assets[object] = {TextureId = object.TextureId}
                end
            end
        end
        saved.AssetsDirty = false
    end
    local function remember(part, store)
        local saved = store[part]
        if not saved then
            saved = {Color = part.Color, Material = part.Material, MaterialVariant = part.MaterialVariant,
                Transparency = part.Transparency, Assets = {}, AssetsDirty = true}
            if part:IsA("MeshPart") then saved.TextureID = part.TextureID end
            store[part] = saved
        end
        if saved.AssetsDirty then collectAssets(part, saved) end
        return saved
    end
    local function reset(part, store)
        local saved = store[part]
        if not saved then return end
        store[part] = nil
        pcall(function()
            part.Color, part.Material, part.MaterialVariant = saved.Color, saved.Material, saved.MaterialVariant
            part.Transparency = saved.Transparency
            if saved.TextureID ~= nil then part.TextureID = saved.TextureID end
        end)
        for object, original in pairs(saved.Assets) do
            pcall(function()
                if original.Parent then
                    if object.Parent == nil and original.Parent.Parent then object.Parent = original.Parent end
                elseif object.Parent then object.TextureId = original.TextureId end
            end)
        end
    end
    local function setCham(part, style, store)
        local saved = remember(part, store)
        for object, original in pairs(saved.Assets) do
            if original.Parent then
                if object.Parent == part then object.Parent = nil end
            elseif object.Parent and object.TextureId ~= "" then object.TextureId = "" end
        end
        if saved.TextureID ~= nil and part.TextureID ~= "" then part.TextureID = "" end
        if part.MaterialVariant ~= "" then part.MaterialVariant = "" end
        if part.Color ~= style.Color then part.Color = style.Color end
        if part.Material ~= style.Material then part.Material = style.Material end
        local transparency = style.Transparency
        if transparency == nil then transparency = saved.Transparency end
        if part.Transparency ~= transparency then part.Transparency = transparency end
    end
    local function style(color, material, transparency, brightness)
        material = resolvemat(material)
        return {Color = material == Enum.Material.Neon and dimColor(color, brightness or Flags.Brightness) or color,
            Material = material, Transparency = transparency}
    end
    local function pluginQuery(name)
        if not GunPlugin then return end
        local method = GunPlugin[name]
        if type(method) == "function" then
            local ok, result = pcall(method, GunPlugin)
            if ok and result ~= nil then return result end
        end
        local query = GunPlugin.Functions and GunPlugin.Functions[name]
        if query and type(query.Invoke) == "function" then
            local ok, result = pcall(query.Invoke, query)
            if ok then return result end
        end
    end
    local function validModel(model)
        return typeof(model) == "Instance" and model.Parent and (model:IsA("Model") or model:IsA("BasePart") or model:IsA("Tool"))
    end
    local function getViewModels()
        local models = {}
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return models end
        local function add(model)
            if not validModel(model) then return end
            local ancestor = model
            while ancestor do
                if ancestor:IsA("ViewportFrame") or (ancestor:IsA("Camera") and ancestor ~= camera) then return end
                ancestor = ancestor.Parent
            end
            for _, existing in ipairs(models) do
                if model == existing or model:IsDescendantOf(existing) then return end
            end
            for index = #models, 1, -1 do
                if models[index]:IsDescendantOf(model) then table.remove(models, index) end
            end
            table.insert(models, model)
        end
        add(camera:FindFirstChild("CameraModel"))
        add(camera:FindFirstChild("CurrentWeapon"))
        add(pluginQuery("GetGunViewModel"))
        return models
    end
    local function getWorldCharacter()
        local model = pluginQuery("GetWorldCharacter")
        if validModel(model) then
            for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character == model then return localPlayer.Character end
            end
            for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
                if entity.Player and entity.Player ~= localPlayer and (entity.Character == model or entity.WorldModel == model) then
                    return localPlayer.Character
                end
            end
            return model
        end
        for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            if entity.Player == localPlayer then return entity.WorldModel or entity.Character or localPlayer.Character end
        end
        return localPlayer.Character
    end
    local function getWorldWeapons()
        local rigs, weapons, seenRigs, seenWeapons = {}, {}, {}, {}
        local function addRig(rig)
            if validModel(rig) and not seenRigs[rig] then seenRigs[rig] = true; table.insert(rigs, rig) end
        end
        local function addWeapon(model)
            if validModel(model) and not seenWeapons[model] then seenWeapons[model] = true; table.insert(weapons, model) end
        end
        addRig(getWorldCharacter())
        addRig(localPlayer.Character)
        for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
            if entity.Player == localPlayer then addRig(entity.WorldModel); addRig(entity.Character) end
        end
        local meshes = worldServices.CustomMeshCharacter
        if meshes and type(meshes.GetWorldCharacterFromPlayer) == "function" then
            local ok, rig = pcall(meshes.GetWorldCharacterFromPlayer, meshes, localPlayer)
            if ok then addRig(rig) end
        end
        local service = worldServices.WeaponWorldModelService
        for _, rig in ipairs(rigs) do
            if service and type(service.GetSelectedWeapons) == "function" then
                local ok, selected = pcall(service.GetSelectedWeapons, service, localPlayer, rig)
                if ok and type(selected) == "table" then
                    for _, record in ipairs(selected) do
                        local model = record.WeaponModel
                        addWeapon(model and model.Instance)
                    end
                end
            end
            addWeapon(rig:FindFirstChild("Weapon"))
            addWeapon(rig:FindFirstChild("CurrentWeapon"))
            for _, child in ipairs(rig:GetChildren()) do if child:IsA("Tool") then addWeapon(child) end end
        end
        return weapons
    end
    local function isArm(part, root)
        local current = part
        while current and current ~= root do
            local name = current.Name:lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", "")
            name = name:gsub("left", ""):gsub("right", ""):gsub("upper", ""):gsub("lower", ""):gsub("fore", "")
            if name == "skin" or name == "shirt" or name == "arm" or name == "arms" or name == "hand" or name == "hands" then return true end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function helper(part, viewmodel)
        local name = part.Name:lower()
        return (name == "part" and not viewmodel) or name:find("root") or name:find("hitbox") or name:find("collider")
            or part:GetAttribute("FakeHead") or part:GetAttribute("LokiSkinProxy")
            or part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxCollider") or part:GetAttribute("LokiHitboxVisual")
    end
    local function bodyPart(part, root)
        if helper(part) then return false end
        local current = part
        while current and current ~= root do
            local name = current.Name:lower()
            if current:IsA("Tool") or name:find("weapon") or name:find("gun") or name:find("hold") then return false end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return true
    end
    local function getWrapper()
        return path(localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"), {"GameUI", "Menu", "Inventory", "Equipment", "Wrapper"})
    end
    local function getWeaponViewports(wrapper)
        local viewports = {}
        for _, slot in ipairs(wrapper and wrapper:GetChildren() or {}) do
            if slot.Name == "CharacterViewer" then continue end
            local activator = slot:FindFirstChild("Activator")
            for _, viewport in ipairs(activator and activator:GetChildren() or {}) do
                if viewport:IsA("ViewportFrame") then table.insert(viewports, viewport) end
            end
        end
        return viewports
    end
    local function reconcile(wanted, store)
        for part in pairs(store) do if not wanted[part] or not part.Parent then reset(part, store) end end
        for part, appearance in pairs(wanted) do if part.Parent then setCham(part, appearance, store) end end
    end
    ApplyChams = function()
        local wanted = {}
        local weaponStyle = style(Flags.WeaponColor, Flags.WeaponMaterial)
        local armStyle = style(Flags.ArmColor, Flags.ArmMaterial)
        local bodyStyle = style(Flags.BodyColor, Flags.BodyMaterial)
        local viewColors, glowParts = {}, {}
        if Flags.Weapon or Flags.Arms then
            for _, vm in ipairs(getViewModels()) do
                for part in pairs(source(vm)) do
                    if not part.Parent or helper(part, true) then continue end
                    if isArm(part, vm) then
                        if Flags.Arms then wanted[part] = armStyle; viewColors[part] = armStyle end
                    elseif Flags.Weapon then wanted[part] = weaponStyle; viewColors[part] = weaponStyle; glowParts[part] = true end
                end
            end
        end
        if Flags.Weapon then
            for _, weapon in ipairs(getWorldWeapons()) do
                for part in pairs(source(weapon)) do
                    if part.Parent and not helper(part, true) and not isArm(part, weapon) then
                        wanted[part] = weaponStyle
                        viewColors[part] = weaponStyle
                        glowParts[part] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local wc = Flags.Body and getWorldCharacter()
        if wc and wc.Parent then
            for part in pairs(source(wc)) do
                if part.Parent and bodyPart(part, wc) and not wanted[part] then wanted[part] = bodyStyle end
            end
            local head = FindRealHead(wc)
            if head then wanted[head] = style(Flags.BodyColor, Flags.BodyMaterial, Flags.HeadTransparency) end
        end
        local wrapper = (Flags.Body or Flags.Weapon) and getWrapper()
        local cv = wrapper and wrapper:FindFirstChild("CharacterViewer")
        if Flags.Body and cv then
            for part in pairs(source(cv)) do if part.Parent and not helper(part) then wanted[part] = bodyStyle end end
        end
        if Flags.Weapon then
            for _, vp in ipairs(getWeaponViewports(wrapper)) do
                for part in pairs(source(vp)) do if part.Parent and not helper(part) then wanted[part] = weaponStyle end end
            end
        end
        reconcile(wanted, Saved)
        ViewModelColors = viewColors
        gunGlow:Sync(glowParts, Flags.Weapon and Flags.WeaponMaterial == "Neon" and Flags.GunGlow and Flags.GunGlowStrength > 0)
        gunGlow:Update(Flags.WeaponColor, Flags.GunGlowStrength, Flags.GunGlowWidth, world.CurrentCamera)
    end
    local carSkip = {ZombieBumper=true, SMOKE=true, detail_mesh=true, CollisionHelper=true, AntiRollBarPart=true,
        SteeringRack=true, Knuckle=true, Mount=true, DriveSeat=true, PassengerSeat=true, Chassis=true, Cylinder003=true}
    ApplyCarChams = function()
        local wanted = {}
        local carOV = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Car")
        local car = carOV and carOV:IsA("ObjectValue") and carOV.Value
        if Flags.Car and validModel(car) then
            local appearance = style(Flags.CarColor, Flags.CarMaterial, Flags.CarTransparency, 0.65)
            for part in pairs(source(car)) do
                if part.Parent and not carSkip[part.Name] then wanted[part] = appearance end
            end
        end
        reconcile(wanted, CarSaved)
    end

    local playerChams = CreateLocalChams(nil)
    local function ApplyPlayerChams()
        local desired, seen = {}, {}
        local function add(player, model, dead)
            if not player or player == localPlayer then return end
            seen[player] = true
            if not Flags.Player or dead or player:GetAttribute("Dead") or not model or not model.Parent then return end
            local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
            if getgenv().flags.teamcheck_esp then
                local squad = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SquadName")
                if (localPlayer.Team and player.Team == localPlayer.Team) or (squad and squad == player:GetAttribute("SquadName")) then return end
            end
            desired[model] = {Color = Flags.PlayerColor, Material = Enum.Material.ForceField, Transparency = 0.3,
                Filter = function(part) return not helper(part) end}
        end
        if Flags.Player then
            for _, entity in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do add(entity.Player, entity.WorldModel or entity.Character, entity.IsDead or entity.Dead) end
            for _, player in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do if not seen[player] then add(player, player.Character) end end
        end
        playerChams:Sync(desired)
    end
    local function connectPluginSignals()
        disconnect(pluginConnections)
        local function apply() if alive then ApplyChams() end end
        for _, name in ipairs({"OnGunChange", "OnRespawn", "OnSpectatingPlayerChanged"}) do
            local callback = GunPlugin and GunPlugin[name]
            local signal
            if type(callback) == "function" then
                local ok, result = pcall(callback, GunPlugin)
                if ok then signal = result end
            end
            signal = signal or (GunPlugin and GunPlugin.Signals and GunPlugin.Signals[name])
            connect(signal, apply, pluginConnections)
        end
        connect(GunPlugin and GunPlugin.Signals and GunPlugin.Signals.SetWorldCharacter, apply, pluginConnections)
    end
    local function resolvePlugin()
        local now = os.clock()
        if now >= nextWorldServiceRetry then
            nextWorldServiceRetry = now + 1
            local first = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")
            for name, names in pairs({
                WeaponWorldModelService = {"GunSystem", "WeaponWorldModelService"},
                CustomMeshCharacter = {"GunSystemPlugins", "CustomMeshCharacter"},
            }) do
                if not worldServices[name] then
                    local module = path(first, names)
                    if module then
                        local ok, service = pcall(require, module)
                        if ok and type(service) == "table" then worldServices[name] = service end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if now < nextPluginRetry then return end
        nextPluginRetry = now + 1
        local module = path(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"), {"GunSystem", "GunController", "Events", "GunPlugin"})
        if module == pluginModule and GunPlugin then return end
        if not module then
            if GunPlugin then GunPlugin = nil; pluginModule = nil; disconnect(pluginConnections) end
            return
        end
        local ok, plugin = pcall(require, module)
        if ok and type(plugin) == "table" then GunPlugin, pluginModule = plugin, module; connectPluginSignals() end
    end
    local carReference, carConnection
    local function watchCar()
        local current = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("Car")
        if current == carReference then return end
        if carConnection then carConnection:Disconnect(); carConnection = nil end
        carReference = current
        if current and current:IsA("ObjectValue") then carConnection = connect(current.Changed, ApplyCarChams) end
    end
    local function pruneSources()
        for root, entry in pairs(Sources) do
            if not entry.Used or not root.Parent then disconnect(entry.Connections); Sources[root] = nil end
        end
    end
    local function refresh()
        if not alive then return end
        for _, entry in pairs(Sources) do entry.Used = false end
        if Flags.Weapon or Flags.Arms or Flags.Body then resolvePlugin() end
        watchCar()
        ApplyChams()
        ApplyCarChams()
        ApplyPlayerChams()
        pruneSources()
    end

    local function removeLegacyGlow(effect)
        if effect:IsA("BloomEffect") and effect.Name == "LucidNeonChamsGlow" then effect.Enabled = false; effect:Destroy() end
    end
    local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    for _, effect in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do removeLegacyGlow(effect) end
    connect(lighting.ChildAdded, removeLegacyGlow)
    local cameraConnection
    local function watchCamera()
        if cameraConnection then cameraConnection:Disconnect(); cameraConnection = nil end
        local camera = world.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            for _, effect in ipairs(camera:GetChildren()) do removeLegacyGlow(effect) end
            cameraConnection = connect(camera.ChildAdded, removeLegacyGlow)
        end
        refresh()
    end
    connect(world:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"), watchCamera)
    connect(localPlayer.CharacterAdded, refresh)
    connect(localPlayer.ChildAdded, function(child) if child.Name == "Car" then watchCar(); ApplyCarChams() end end)
    connect(localPlayer.ChildRemoved, function(child) if child == carReference then watchCar(); ApplyCarChams() end end)
    local renderBinding = "LucidGunChamColors"
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(renderBinding, Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function()
        if not alive then return end
        for part, appearance in pairs(ViewModelColors) do
            if not part.Parent then ViewModelColors[part] = nil; continue end
            if part.Material ~= appearance.Material then part.Material = appearance.Material end
            if part.Color ~= appearance.Color then part.Color = appearance.Color end
            if part.MaterialVariant ~= "" then part.MaterialVariant = "" end
            local saved = Saved[part]
            if saved and saved.TextureID ~= nil and part.TextureID ~= "" then part.TextureID = "" end
        end
        gunGlow:Update(Flags.WeaponColor, Flags.GunGlowStrength, Flags.GunGlowWidth, world.CurrentCamera)
    end)
    local elapsed = 0
    connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
        elapsed += dt
        if elapsed < 0.3 then return end
        elapsed = 0
        if Flags.Weapon or Flags.Arms or Flags.Body or Flags.Car or Flags.Player or next(Saved) or next(CarSaved) then refresh() end
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        alive = false
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(renderBinding)
        table.clear(ViewModelColors)
        gunGlow:Clear()
        disconnect(pluginConnections)
        for _, entry in pairs(Sources) do disconnect(entry.Connections) end
        table.clear(Sources)
        for part in pairs(Saved) do reset(part, Saved) end
        for part in pairs(CarSaved) do reset(part, CarSaved) end
        playerChams:Clear()
    end)

    local function controls(toggleId, colorId, materialId, label, enabled, colorKey, materialKey)
        GroupBoxes.Chams:AddToggle(toggleId, {
            Text = label, Default = false,
            Callback = function(value) Flags[enabled] = value; refresh() end,
        }):AddColorPicker(colorId, {
            Title = label .. " Color", Default = Flags[colorKey],
            Callback = function(value) Flags[colorKey] = value; refresh() end,
        })
        GroupBoxes.Chams:AddDropdown(materialId, {
            Text = label .. " Material", Values = {"Neon", "ForceField", "Plastic"}, Default = Flags[materialKey],
            Callback = function(value) Flags[materialKey] = value; refresh() end,
        })
    end
    controls("GunChamsToggle", "GunChamsColorPicker", "GunChamsMaterial", "Weapon Chams", "Weapon", "WeaponColor", "WeaponMaterial")
    GroupBoxes.Chams:AddToggle("GunGlowToggle", {
        Text = "Gun Glow (Neon)", Default = false,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.GunGlow = value; refresh() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Chams:AddSlider("GunGlowStrength", {
        Text = "Gun Glow Strength", Default = 0.6, Min = 0, Max = 1, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.GunGlowStrength = value; refresh() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Chams:AddSlider("GunGlowWidth", {
        Text = "Gun Glow Width", Default = 0.04, Min = 0.01, Max = 0.2, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.GunGlowWidth = value; refresh() end,
    })
    controls("ArmChamsToggle", "ArmChamsColor", "ArmChamsMaterial", "Arm Chams", "Arms", "ArmColor", "ArmMaterial")
    controls("body_chams_toggle", "body_chams_color", "BodyChamsMaterial", "Body Chams", "Body", "BodyColor", "BodyMaterial")
    controls("CarChamsToggle", "CarChamsColor", "CarChamsMaterial", "Car Chams", "Car", "CarColor", "CarMaterial")
    GroupBoxes.Chams:AddSlider("ChamNeonBrightness", {
        Text = "Neon Dimmer", Default = Flags.Brightness, Min = 0, Max = 2, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.Brightness = value; refresh() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Chams:AddSlider("body_head_transparency", {
        Text = "Head Transparency", Default = Flags.HeadTransparency, Min = 0, Max = 1, Rounding = 1,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.HeadTransparency = value; refresh() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.Chams:AddSlider("CarChamsTransparency", {
        Text = "Car Chams Transparency", Default = Flags.CarTransparency, Min = 0, Max = 1, Rounding = 2,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.CarTransparency = value; refresh() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.PlayerESP:AddToggle("PlayerChamsToggle", {
        Text = "Player Chams", Default = false,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.Player = value; refresh() end,
    }):AddColorPicker("PlayerChamsColor", {
        Title = "Player Chams Color", Default = Flags.PlayerColor,
        Callback = function(value) Flags.PlayerColor = value; refresh() end,
    })
    watchCamera()
end)
Runtime:Init("Loot ESP", function()
    local miscFolder
    local function getMiscFolder()
        local assets = workspace:FindFirstChild("world_assets")
        local objects = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("StaticObjects")
        return objects and objects:FindFirstChild("Misc")
    end
    local storage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local gunAssets = storage:WaitForChild("GunSystemAssets", 10)
    local gunDataFolder = gunAssets and gunAssets:WaitForChild("GunData", 10)
    assert(gunDataFolder, "GunData unavailable for Loot ESP; reload after the game loads")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
    local studsPerMeter = 3.5461
    local taggedItems = {}
    local taggedPending = {}
    local gunByHandle = {}
    local BaseItem, gameRequire
    local function moduleAt(root, names)
        for _, name in ipairs(names) do root = root and root:FindFirstChild(name) end
        return root
    end
    local function loadLootMetadata()
        if BaseItem and gameRequire then return true end
        local base = moduleAt(storage, { "EmberSharedLibrary", "GameShared", "Item", "ConstructorItems", "BaseItem" })
        local loader = moduleAt(storage, { "EmberSharedLibrary", "EmberShared", "Require" })
        if not base or not loader then return false end
        local identity = getthreadidentity()
        setthreadidentity(2)
        local ok, itemModule, requireModule = pcall(function() return require(base), require(loader) end)
        setthreadidentity(identity)
        if ok then BaseItem, gameRequire = itemModule, requireModule end
        return ok
    end


    flags.GunESPEnabled = flags.GunESPEnabled or false

    if flags.GunNameEnabled == nil then flags.GunNameEnabled = true end
    flags.GunNameColor = flags.GunNameColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)

    if flags.GunDistanceEnabled == nil then flags.GunDistanceEnabled = true end
    flags.GunDistanceColor = flags.GunDistanceColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)

    flags.RepairKitEnabled = flags.RepairKitEnabled or false
    flags.RepairKitColor = flags.RepairKitColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)

    flags.RepairKitDistanceEnabled = flags.RepairKitDistanceEnabled or false
    flags.RepairKitDistanceColor = flags.RepairKitDistanceColor or Color3.fromRGB(255, 218, 251)

    flags.GunRenderDistance = flags.GunRenderDistance or 10000

    local meleeBlacklist = {
        AxeFire = true,
        ChemLight = true,
        Cleaver = true,
        CombatKnife = true,
        Crowbar = true,
        HandSaw = true,
        Shovel = true,
        KitchenKnife = true,
        GrenadeSmokeMakeshift = true,
        GrenadeSmoke = true,
        AxeMakeshift = true,
        Cuffs = true,
        Fists = true,
        GenericItemThrow = true,
        Generic_consumable = true,
        GrenadeFrag = true,
        GrenadeGas = true,
        GrenadeMolotov = true,
        GrenadePipebomb = true,
        Snowball = true,
        ThrowableBottle = true,
        Wrench = true,
        SteakKnife = true
    }

    local weaponESP = {
        Active = {}
    }

    local repairKitESP = {
        Active = {}
    }

    local scannedGuns = setmetatable({}, {
        __mode = "k"
    })

    local scannedRepairKits = setmetatable({}, {
        __mode = "k"
    })

    local gunDataMeshInfos = {}

    local function getMeshPartsInfo(model)
        local info = {}

        for _, child in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("MeshPart") then
                local childNames = {}

                for _, childObject in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                    table.insert(childNames, childObject.Name)
                end

                table.sort(childNames)

                table.insert(info, {
                    Name = child.Name,
                    MeshId = child.MeshId,
                    ChildrenNames = childNames
                })
            end
        end

        table.sort(info, function(a, b)
            return a.Name < b.Name
        end)

        return info
    end

    local function meshPartsMatch(a, b)
        if #a ~= #b then
            return false
        end

        for i = 1, #a do
            if a[i].Name ~= b[i].Name
                or a[i].MeshId ~= b[i].MeshId then

                return false
            end

            local c1 = a[i].ChildrenNames
            local c2 = b[i].ChildrenNames

            if #c1 ~= #c2 then
                return false
            end

            for j = 1, #c1 do
                if c1[j] ~= c2[j] then
                    return false
                end
            end
        end

        return true
    end

    local function getChildrenNames(model)
        local names = {}

        for _, child in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
            table.insert(names, child.Name)
        end

        table.sort(names)

        return names
    end

    local function childrenNamesMatch(a, b)
        if #a ~= #b then
            return false
        end

        for i = 1, #a do
            if a[i] ~= b[i] then
                return false
            end
        end

        return true
    end

    local function getDescendantsNames(model)
        local names = {}

        for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            table.insert(names, descendant.Name)
        end

        table.sort(names)

        return names
    end

    local function descendantsNamesMatch(a, b)
        if #a ~= #b then
            return false
        end

        for i = 1, #a do
            if a[i] ~= b[i] then
                return false
            end
        end

        return true
    end

    for _, folder in ipairs(gunDataFolder:GetChildren()) do
        local worldModel = folder:FindFirstChild("WorldModel")

        if worldModel then
            gunDataMeshInfos[folder.Name] = getMeshPartsInfo(worldModel)
        end
    end

    local function checkModelAgainstGunData(model)
        if not model:IsA("Model") then
            return nil
        end

        local meshInfo = getMeshPartsInfo(model)

        if #meshInfo > 0 then
            for name, referenceInfo in pairs(gunDataMeshInfos) do
                if meshPartsMatch(meshInfo, referenceInfo) then
                    return name
                end
            end
        else
            local childNames = getChildrenNames(model)
            local candidates = {}

            for name in pairs(gunDataMeshInfos) do
                local gunData = gunDataFolder:FindFirstChild(name)

                if gunData then
                    local worldModel = gunData:FindFirstChild("WorldModel")

                    if worldModel then
                        local referenceChildren = getChildrenNames(worldModel)

                        if childrenNamesMatch(
                            childNames,
                            referenceChildren
                        ) then
                            table.insert(candidates, name)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if #candidates == 1 then
                return candidates[1]
            elseif #candidates > 1 then
                local descendantNames = getDescendantsNames(model)

                for _, name in ipairs(candidates) do
                    local gunData = gunDataFolder:FindFirstChild(name)

                    if gunData then
                        local worldModel = gunData:FindFirstChild("WorldModel")

                        if worldModel then
                            if descendantsNamesMatch(
                                descendantNames,
                                getDescendantsNames(worldModel)
                            ) then
                                return name
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return nil
    end

    local function createGunBillboard(model, gunName, taggedItem)
        if weaponESP.Active[model] then
            local data = weaponESP.Active[model]
            if taggedItem then data.taggedItem, data.name, data.RemovedTag = taggedItem, gunName, false end
            return data
        end

        local handle = model:IsA("BasePart") and model or model:FindFirstChild("Handle", true)
            or model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if handle and gunByHandle[handle] then
            local data = gunByHandle[handle]
            if taggedItem then data.taggedItem, data.name, data.RemovedTag = taggedItem, gunName, false end
            return data
        end

        if not handle or not handle:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end

        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "GunESP"
        billboard.Adornee = handle
        billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 60)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
        billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
        billboard.Enabled = false
        billboard.Parent = handle

        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        nameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        nameLabel.TextSize = 14
        nameLabel.Visible = false
        nameLabel.Parent = billboard

        local distanceLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        distanceLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        distanceLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20)
        distanceLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        distanceLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        distanceLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        distanceLabel.TextSize = 14
        distanceLabel.Visible = false
        distanceLabel.Parent = billboard

        local data = {
            billboard = billboard,
            nameLabel = nameLabel,
            distanceLabel = distanceLabel,
            nameChars = {},
            distanceChars = {},
            model = model,
            handle = handle,
            name = gunName,
            taggedItem = taggedItem
        }

        weaponESP.Active[model] = data
        gunByHandle[handle] = data

        return data
    end

    local function createRepairKitBillboard(part)
        if repairKitESP.Active[part] then
            return repairKitESP.Active[part]
        end

        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "RepairKitESP"
        billboard.Adornee = part
        billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 40)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
        billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
        billboard.Enabled = false
        billboard.Parent = part

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        label.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        label.TextSize = 14
        label.Visible = false
        label.Parent = billboard

        local distanceLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        distanceLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        distanceLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
        distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20)
        distanceLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        distanceLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        distanceLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        distanceLabel.TextSize = 14
        distanceLabel.Visible = false
        distanceLabel.Parent = billboard

        local data = {
            billboard = billboard,
            label = label,
            distanceLabel = distanceLabel,
            nameChars = {},
            distanceChars = {},
            part = part
        }

        repairKitESP.Active[part] = data

        return data
    end

    local repairMeshId = "rbxassetid://10058182223"

    local function scanGun(model)
        if not model:IsA("Model") then
            return
        end

        if scannedGuns[model] then
            return
        end

        local gunName = checkModelAgainstGunData(model)

        if gunName and not meleeBlacklist[gunName] then
            if createGunBillboard(model, gunName) then scannedGuns[model] = true end
        end
    end

    local function scanRepairKit(object)
        if scannedRepairKits[object] then
            return
        end

        if not object:IsA("MeshPart") then
            return
        end

        if object.Name ~= "Main" then
            return
        end

        if object.MeshId ~= repairMeshId then
            return
        end

        scannedRepairKits[object] = true

        createRepairKitBillboard(object)
    end

    -- GROUND_ITEMS persists independently of the nearby decorative gun models.
    -- Resolve names through the same CId metadata used in the attached source.
    local definitionCache = {}
    local function scanTaggedItem(item)
        if not item.Parent or not CollectionService:HasTag(item, "GROUND_ITEMS") then taggedPending[item] = nil; return end
        if taggedItems[item] then taggedPending[item] = nil; return end
        if not loadLootMetadata() then taggedPending[item] = os.clock() + 1; return end
        local cid = item:GetAttribute("CId")
        local moduleName = BaseItem.CIDMap and BaseItem.CIDMap[cid]
        if not moduleName then taggedPending[item] = os.clock() + 1; return end
        local info = definitionCache[moduleName]
        if not info then
            local identity = getthreadidentity()
            setthreadidentity(2)
            local ok, result = pcall(function() return gameRequire:GetModuleByName(moduleName) end)
            setthreadidentity(identity)
            if ok then info = result; definitionCache[moduleName] = result end
        end
        if type(info) ~= "table" then taggedPending[item] = os.clock() + 1; return end
        taggedPending[item] = nil
        local root = (item:IsA("BasePart") or item:IsA("Model")) and item or item.Parent
        if not root or not (root:IsA("BasePart") or root:IsA("Model")) then return end
        if info.ItemType == "Weapon" and info.AmmoType and flags.GunESPEnabled then
            local name = info.DisplayName or moduleName:gsub("%.item$", "")
            taggedItems[item] = createGunBillboard(root, name, item)
        elseif flags.RepairKitEnabled and root:IsA("MeshPart") and root.MeshId == repairMeshId then
            local data = createRepairKitBillboard(root)
            data.taggedItem, data.RemovedTag = item, false
            taggedItems[item] = data
        end
    end
    local function scanTagged()
        if not (flags.GunESPEnabled or flags.RepairKitEnabled) then return end
        for _, item in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("GROUND_ITEMS")) do
            if not taggedItems[item] then taggedPending[item] = 0 end
        end
    end
    Runtime:Connect(CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("GROUND_ITEMS"), function(item)
        if flags.GunESPEnabled or flags.RepairKitEnabled then taggedPending[item] = 0 end
    end)
    Runtime:Connect(CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("GROUND_ITEMS"), function(item)
        taggedPending[item] = nil
        local data = taggedItems[item]
        if data then
            if data.taggedItem == item then data.taggedItem = nil; data.RemovedTag = true end
            data.billboard.Enabled = false
            taggedItems[item] = nil
        end
