    end)
    local function isTracked(object, data)
        if data.RemovedTag then return false end
        if data.taggedItem then
            return data.taggedItem.Parent and CollectionService:HasTag(data.taggedItem, "GROUND_ITEMS")
        end
        return miscFolder and object:IsDescendantOf(miscFolder)
    end
    local function scan()
        if not miscFolder then return end
        if flags.GunESPEnabled then
            for _, model in ipairs(miscFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                scanGun(model)
            end
        end

        if flags.RepairKitEnabled then
            for _, object in ipairs(miscFolder:GetDescendants()) do
                scanRepairKit(object)
            end
        end
    end

    local function hideCharacters(list)
        for _, object in pairs(list or {}) do
            object.Visible = false
        end
    end


    local function hideGunData(data)
        data.billboard.Enabled = false
        data.nameLabel.Visible = false
        data.distanceLabel.Visible = false

        hideCharacters(data.nameChars)
        hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)
    end

    local function hideRepairData(data)
        data.billboard.Enabled = false
        data.label.Visible = false
        data.distanceLabel.Visible = false

        hideCharacters(data.nameChars)
        hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)
    end

    local function updateGunESP()
        Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera or Camera

        if not Camera then
            return
        end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local origin = root
            and root.Position
            or Camera.CFrame.Position

        for model, data in pairs(weaponESP.Active) do
            if not model.Parent or not isTracked(model, data)
                or not data.handle
                or not data.handle.Parent then

                if data.billboard then
                    pcall(function()
                        data.billboard:Destroy()
                    end)
                end

                hideCharacters(data.nameChars)
                hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)

                weaponESP.Active[model] = nil
                gunByHandle[data.handle] = nil
                if data.taggedItem then taggedItems[data.taggedItem] = nil end
                scannedGuns[model] = nil
                continue
            end

            local distance = (data.handle.Position - origin).Magnitude / studsPerMeter

            if not flags.GunESPEnabled
                or distance > flags.GunRenderDistance then

                hideGunData(data)
                continue
            end

            local nativeRange = flags.GunRenderDistance * studsPerMeter
            if data.billboard.MaxDistance ~= nativeRange then data.billboard.MaxDistance = nativeRange end
            local yOffset = 0
            local hasContent = false

            if flags.GunNameEnabled then
                local text = "[" .. data.name .. "]"

                hideCharacters(data.nameChars)

                data.nameLabel.Text = text
                data.nameLabel.TextColor3 = flags.GunNameColor
                data.nameLabel.TextSize = 14
                data.nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, yOffset)
                data.nameLabel.Visible = true

                yOffset = yOffset + 15
                hasContent = true
            else
                data.nameLabel.Visible = false
                hideCharacters(data.nameChars)
            end

            if flags.GunDistanceEnabled then
                local distanceText = string.format(
                    "[%d M]",
                    math.floor(distance)
                )

                hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)

                data.distanceLabel.Text = distanceText
                data.distanceLabel.TextColor3 = flags.GunDistanceColor
                data.distanceLabel.TextSize = 14
                data.distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, yOffset)
                data.distanceLabel.Visible = true

                hasContent = true
            else
                data.distanceLabel.Visible = false
                hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)
            end

            data.billboard.Enabled = hasContent
        end

        for part, data in pairs(repairKitESP.Active) do
            if not part.Parent or not isTracked(part, data) then
                if data.billboard then
                    pcall(function()
                        data.billboard:Destroy()
                    end)
                end

                hideCharacters(data.nameChars)
                hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)

                repairKitESP.Active[part] = nil
                if data.taggedItem then taggedItems[data.taggedItem] = nil end
                scannedRepairKits[part] = nil
                continue
            end

            local distance = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude / studsPerMeter

            if not flags.RepairKitEnabled
                or distance > flags.GunRenderDistance then

                hideRepairData(data)
                continue
            end

            local nativeRange = flags.GunRenderDistance * studsPerMeter
            if data.billboard.MaxDistance ~= nativeRange then data.billboard.MaxDistance = nativeRange end
            local yOffset = 0
            local hasContent = false

            if flags.RepairKitEnabled then
                local text = "[Repair Kit]"

                hideCharacters(data.nameChars)

                data.label.Text = text
                data.label.TextColor3 = flags.RepairKitColor
                data.label.TextSize = 14
                data.label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, yOffset)
                data.label.Visible = true

                yOffset = yOffset + 15
                hasContent = true
            else
                data.label.Visible = false
                hideCharacters(data.nameChars)
            end

            if flags.RepairKitDistanceEnabled then
                local distanceText = string.format(
                    "[%d M]",
                    math.floor(distance)
                )

                hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)

                data.distanceLabel.Text = distanceText
                data.distanceLabel.TextColor3 = flags.RepairKitDistanceColor
                data.distanceLabel.TextSize = 14
                data.distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, yOffset)
                data.distanceLabel.Visible = true

                hasContent = true
            else
                data.distanceLabel.Visible = false
                hideCharacters(data.distanceChars)
            end

            data.billboard.Enabled = hasContent
        end
    end

    local pendingGuns = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local folderConnections = {}
    local function queueGun(model)
        if not model or not model:IsA("Model") or pendingGuns[model] or scannedGuns[model] then return end
        pendingGuns[model] = true
        task.delay(0.1, function()
            pendingGuns[model] = nil
            if flags.GunESPEnabled and miscFolder and model:IsDescendantOf(miscFolder) then scanGun(model) end
        end)
    end
    local function added(object)
        if flags.RepairKitEnabled then scanRepairKit(object) end
        if not flags.GunESPEnabled then return end
        local model = object
        while model and model ~= miscFolder do
            if model:IsA("Model") then queueGun(model) end
            model = model.Parent
        end
    end
    local function bindMiscFolder()
        local folder = getMiscFolder()
        if folder == miscFolder then return end
        for _, connection in ipairs(folderConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(folderConnections)
        miscFolder = folder
        if not folder then return end
        table.insert(folderConnections, Runtime:Connect(folder.DescendantAdded, added))
        table.insert(folderConnections, Runtime:Connect(folder.DescendantRemoving, function(object)
            scannedGuns[object], scannedRepairKits[object] = nil, nil
            local model = object.Parent
            while model and model ~= miscFolder do
                if model:IsA("Model") then scannedGuns[model] = nil end
                model = model.Parent
            end
        end))
        scan()
    end
    bindMiscFolder()
    scanTagged()
    Runtime:Connect(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(object)
        if object.Name == "Misc" then bindMiscFolder() end
    end)
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
        for _, connection in ipairs(folderConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(taggedPending)
        table.clear(taggedItems)
        table.clear(gunByHandle)
        table.clear(definitionCache)
    end)

    -- BillboardGui follows its adornee natively; only text and distance need refreshing.
    local lootLabelGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.1)
    local lootESPVisible = false
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        LPH_ATTRIBUTES(VM(NONE))
        if not (flags.GunESPEnabled or flags.RepairKitEnabled) then
            if lootESPVisible then
                for _, data in pairs(weaponESP.Active) do hideGunData(data) end
                for _, data in pairs(repairKitESP.Active) do hideRepairData(data) end
                lootESPVisible = false
            end
            return
        end
        lootESPVisible = true
        if lootLabelGate:Ready(os.clock()) then
            local count, now = 0, os.clock()
            for item, retryAt in pairs(taggedPending) do
                if now < retryAt then continue end
                scanTaggedItem(item)
                count += 1
                if count >= 4 then break end
            end
            updateGunESP()
        end
    end)

    GroupBoxes.GunESP:AddToggle("GunESPEnabled", {
        Text = "Enable Gun ESP",
        Default = flags.GunESPEnabled,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.GunESPEnabled = State

            if State then
                task.spawn(function()
                    bindMiscFolder()
                    scanTagged()
                    if miscFolder then
                        for _, model in ipairs(miscFolder:GetDescendants()) do scanGun(model) end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })

    local GunNameToggle = GroupBoxes.GunESP:AddToggle("GunNameEnabled", {
        Text = "Gun Name",
        Default = flags.GunNameEnabled,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.GunNameEnabled = State
        end
    })

    GunNameToggle:AddColorPicker("GunNameColor", {
        Default = flags.GunNameColor,
        Title = "Gun Name Color 1",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.GunNameColor = Value
        end
    })



    local GunDistanceToggle = GroupBoxes.GunESP:AddToggle("GunDistanceEnabled", {
        Text = "Gun Distance",
        Default = flags.GunDistanceEnabled,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.GunDistanceEnabled = State
        end
    })

    GunDistanceToggle:AddColorPicker("GunDistanceColor", {
        Default = flags.GunDistanceColor,
        Title = "Gun Distance Color 1",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.GunDistanceColor = Value
        end
    })



    local RepairKitToggle = GroupBoxes.GunESP:AddToggle("RepairKitEnabled", {
        Text = "Repair Kit",
        Default = flags.RepairKitEnabled,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.RepairKitEnabled = State

            if State then
                task.spawn(function()
                    bindMiscFolder()
                    scanTagged()
                    if miscFolder then
                        for _, object in ipairs(miscFolder:GetDescendants()) do scanRepairKit(object) end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })

    RepairKitToggle:AddColorPicker("RepairKitColor", {
        Default = flags.RepairKitColor,
        Title = "Repair Kit Color 1",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.RepairKitColor = Value
        end
    })



    local RepairKitDistanceToggle = GroupBoxes.GunESP:AddToggle("RepairKitDistanceEnabled", {
        Text = "Repair Kit Distance",
        Default = flags.RepairKitDistanceEnabled,
        Callback = function(State)
            flags.RepairKitDistanceEnabled = State
        end
    })

    RepairKitDistanceToggle:AddColorPicker("RepairKitDistanceColor", {
        Default = flags.RepairKitDistanceColor,
        Title = "Repair Kit Distance Color 1",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.RepairKitDistanceColor = Value
        end
    })



    GroupBoxes.GunESP:AddSlider("GunRenderDistance", {
        Text = "Render Distance",
        Default = flags.GunRenderDistance,
        Min = 50,
        Max = 10000,
        Rounding = 0,
        Suffix = "m",
        Callback = function(Value)
            flags.GunRenderDistance = Value
        end
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Shot locations", function()
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    local notifications, blipsEnabled, hooked = false, false, false
    local mapImage, blipUtil, blipContainer, blipTemplate
    local labels, gridLabels, mapConnections, pending = {}, {}, {}, {}
    local settings = { Duration = 5, CombineDistance = 257, MinimumDistance = 0 }
    local blips = CreateShotBlips(localPlayer, function(position)
        if not (blipTemplate and blipContainer and blipUtil) then return end
        local root = blipTemplate:Clone()
        local label = root:FindFirstChild("TextLabel", true)
        if not label then root:Destroy(); return end
        root.Name = "LokiShotBlip"
        root.Position = blipUtil:ToBlipSpace(position)
        root.Visible = true
        root.Parent = blipContainer
        local visuals = {}
        local objects = root:GetDescendants()
        table.insert(objects, root)
        for _, object in ipairs(objects) do
            local values = {}
            if object:IsA("GuiObject") then values.BackgroundTransparency = object.BackgroundTransparency end
            if object:IsA("TextLabel") then
                values.TextTransparency, values.TextStrokeTransparency = object.TextTransparency, object.TextStrokeTransparency
            elseif object:IsA("ImageLabel") or object:IsA("ImageButton") then values.ImageTransparency = object.ImageTransparency
            elseif object:IsA("UIStroke") then values.Transparency = object.Transparency end
            if next(values) then table.insert(visuals, { Object = object, Values = values }) end
        end
        return { Root = root, Label = label, Shadow = label:FindFirstChild("Dropshadow"), Visuals = visuals }
    end, os.clock, settings)
    local function clearMap()
        blips:Clear()
        for _, connection in ipairs(mapConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(mapConnections)
        table.clear(labels)
        table.clear(gridLabels)
        mapImage, blipUtil, blipContainer, blipTemplate = nil, nil, nil, nil
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, clearMap)
    local function readGameModule(module)
        local identity = getthreadidentity()
        setthreadidentity(2)
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        setthreadidentity(identity)
        if not ok then error(result) end
        return result
    end
    local function currentMap()
        local gui = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        local ui = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("GameUI")
        return ui and ui:FindFirstChild("Map")
    end
    local function findMap()
        clearMap()
        local map = currentMap()
        mapImage = map and map:FindFirstChild("MapImage", true)
        local module = map and map:FindFirstChild("BlipUtil", true)
        if not mapImage or not module then return end
        local ok, result = pcall(readGameModule, module)
        if not ok then Runtime:Report("Shot locations map", result); return end
        blipUtil = result
        blipContainer = mapImage:FindFirstChild("Blips")
        local templates = map.Parent and map.Parent:FindFirstChild("TemplateAssets")
        blipTemplate = templates and templates:FindFirstChild("BlipSmall")
        local function remember(label)
            if not label:IsA("TextLabel") and not label:IsA("TextButton") then return end
            local parent, insideImage = label.Parent, false
            while parent and parent ~= map do
                if parent.Name == "Blips" or parent.Name == "Templates" or parent.Name == "TemplateAssets" then return end
                if parent == mapImage then insideImage = true end
                parent = parent.Parent
            end
            if parent ~= map then return end
            gridLabels[label] = true
            if insideImage then labels[label] = true end
        end
        for _, label in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do remember(label) end
        table.insert(mapConnections, Runtime:Connect(map.DescendantAdded, remember))
        table.insert(mapConnections, Runtime:Connect(map.DescendantRemoving, function(label)
            labels[label], gridLabels[label] = nil, nil
        end))
    end
    local weaponFilter = CreateShotWeaponFilter()
    local function resolveShotWeapon(data)
        local name = ShotWeaponName(data.WeaponName) or ShotWeaponName(data.WeaponData) or ShotWeaponName(data.Weapon)
        if name then return name end
        local shooter = data.Shooter
        if typeof(shooter) == "Instance" and shooter:IsA("Player") then
            local entity
            for _, candidate in pairs(Runtime:GetEntities() or {}) do
                if type(candidate) == "table" and candidate.Player == shooter then entity = candidate; break end
            end
            name = ReadIndicatorGun(shooter, entity)
            if name ~= "Unknown" and name ~= "Fists" then return name end
        end
    end
    local alert = CreateShotObserver(localPlayer, function() return notifications end, function(position)
        return DescribeShotLocation(position, mapImage, blipUtil, labels, gridLabels)
    end, function(text) Library:Notify(text, 5) end, function(data) return data.ShotWeaponName end)
    local function observer(data)
        if not notifications and not blipsEnabled then return end
        if type(data) ~= "table" or data.Shooter == localPlayer then return end
        -- UI/map work runs on our heartbeat rather than inside native firing.
        if #pending < 256 then
            local snapshot = table.clone(data)
            -- Resolve before the shooter can swap weapons or mutate WeaponData.
            snapshot.ShotWeaponName = resolveShotWeapon(data)
            table.insert(pending, snapshot)
        end
    end
    local function ensureHook()
        if hooked then return end
        local storage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local guns = storage:FindFirstChild("GunSystem")
        local remote = guns and guns:FindFirstChild("Remote")
        local module = remote and remote:FindFirstChild("GunFire.routeschema")
        if not module then return end
        local ok, cleanup = pcall(function()
            local info = getinfo or (debug and debug.getinfo) or function(callback) return debug.info(callback, "s") end
            local upvalues = getupvalues or (debug and debug.getupvalues)
            return CallGameFunction(ObserveShotAction, readGameModule(module), observer, hookfunction, upvalues, info)
        end)
        if ok then table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, cleanup); hooked = true
        else Runtime:Report("Shot locations hook", cleanup) end
    end
    local function refresh()
        if not notifications and not blipsEnabled then table.clear(pending); clearMap(); return end
        ensureHook()
        findMap()
    end
    GroupBoxes.ShotAlerts:AddToggle("ShotLocationAlerts", {
        Text = "Shot Location Notifications", Default = false,
        Tooltip = "Reports other players’ shots only for the guns selected below.",
        Callback = function(value) notifications = value; refresh() end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ShotAlerts:AddToggle("ShotBlips", {
        Text = "Shot Blips", Default = false,
        Callback = function(value) blipsEnabled = value; refresh() end,
    })
    local weaponNames, seenWeapons = {}, {}
    local function addWeaponName(value)
        local name = ShotWeaponName(value)
        if name and not seenWeapons[name] then seenWeapons[name] = true; table.insert(weaponNames, name) end
    end
    local assets = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("GunSystemAssets")
    local gunData = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("GunData")
    if gunData then
        for _, weapon in ipairs(gunData:GetChildren()) do addWeaponName(weapon) end
    end
    for _, name in ipairs({"AK47", "AKM", "AR15", "ASVAL", "AWM", "Barret50", "DesertEagle", "DesertEagleGold", "DoubleBarrelShotgun", "DoubleBarrelShotgunShort", "FAL", "FNX45", "Famas", "G36k", "Glock", "HoneyBadger", "HuntingRifle", "M110K", "M1911", "M249", "M4A1", "M40A1", "M79", "M9", "MCX", "MK14", "MK18", "MK4", "MK47", "MP5", "MRAD", "Makarov", "MosinNagant", "Mossberg", "P226", "P90", "PKM", "RenelliM4", "RevolverNew", "SCAR", "SKS", "SPAS12", "SVD", "Saiga", "Scrap_SMG", "Scrap_Sniper", "Shotgun", "ShotgunMakeshift", "Sporter", "Tec9", "UMP45", "UZI"}) do addWeaponName(name) end
    table.sort(weaponNames)
    table.insert(weaponNames, "Unknown Weapon")
    GroupBoxes.ShotAlerts:AddDropdown("ShotTrackedGuns", {
        Text = "Tracked Guns", Values = weaponNames, Multi = true, Default = {},
        Tooltip = "Only selected guns produce shot notifications and map blips. Empty selection shows no shots. Your own shots are hidden.",
        Callback = function(value)
            weaponFilter:SetSelection(value)
            table.clear(pending)
            blips:Clear()
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.ShotAlerts:AddSlider("ShotBlipDuration", {
        Text = "Blip Duration", Default = settings.Duration, Min = 2, Max = 30, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "s",
        Callback = function(value) settings.Duration = value end,
    })
    local gate, mapGate = CreateRefreshGate(0.1), CreateRefreshGate(1)
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        if not notifications and not blipsEnabled then return end
        local shots = pending
        pending = {}
        for _, data in ipairs(shots) do
            Runtime:Handle("Shot location notification", function()
                if data.Shooter == localPlayer then return end
                local weapon = data.ShotWeaponName
                if not weaponFilter:Matches(weapon) then return end
                data.ShotWeaponName = weapon
                alert(data)
                if blipsEnabled then
                    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    blips:Add(data, camera and camera.CFrame.Position)
                end
            end)
        end
        if mapGate:Ready(os.clock()) then
            ensureHook()
            local map = currentMap()
            local templates = map and map.Parent and map.Parent:FindFirstChild("TemplateAssets")
            if not mapImage or not blipUtil or not map or map:FindFirstChild("MapImage", true) ~= mapImage
                or (blipsEnabled and (mapImage:FindFirstChild("Blips") ~= blipContainer
                    or not templates or templates:FindFirstChild("BlipSmall") ~= blipTemplate)) then findMap() end
        end
        if blipsEnabled and blipUtil and #blips.Entries > 0 and gate:Ready(os.clock()) then
            blips:Update(function(position) return blipUtil:ToBlipSpace(position) end)
        end
    end)
end)

Runtime:Init("Target indicator", function()
    local players = game:GetService("Players")
    local localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
    local enabled, panel = false, nil
    local visibilitySettings = { Enabled = true }
    local gate = CreateRefreshGate(0.2)
    local function update()
        if not enabled then return end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local entities = Runtime:GetEntities()
        local player, head, entity = SelectIndicatorTarget(camera, localPlayer, entities,
            players:GetPlayers(), getgenv().flags, Runtime.CrosshairTarget)
        if not panel and (player or Window.IsOpen) then
            panel = CreateTargetIndicator(Vitality, function() return Window.IsOpen end, visibilitySettings)
        end
        if not player then if panel then panel:Update(nil) end; return end
        local origin = GetLocalVisibilityOrigin(Runtime, localPlayer)
        if not origin then panel:Update(nil); return end
        local visible = Runtime.IsTargetVisible(head, entity, origin)
        panel:Update(player, (head.Position - origin).Magnitude / 2.56, ReadIndicatorGun(player, entity), visible)
    end
    table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function() if panel then panel:Destroy() end end)
    GroupBoxes.TargetIndicator:AddToggle("TargetIndicatorEnabled", {
        Text = "Target Indicator", Default = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            enabled = value
            gate.Next = -math.huge
            if not value then if panel then panel.Root.Instance.Visible = false end; return end
            update()
        end,
    })
    GroupBoxes.TargetIndicator:AddToggle("TargetIndicatorVisCheck", {
        Text = "Visible Status", Default = true,
        Tooltip = "Lithium visibility status: player name, visible/not visible and distance",
        Callback = function(value)
            visibilitySettings.Enabled = value
            update()
        end,
    })
    Runtime:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
        if enabled and gate:Ready(os.clock()) then update() end
    end)
end)

Runtime:Init("Watermark", function()
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    Library:SetWatermarkVisibility(true)
    local FrameTimer = tick()
    local FrameCounter = 0
    local FPS = 60
    local StartTime = tick()
    local gameName = "Aftermath"
    pcall(function() gameName = Vitality:GetGameName() or gameName end)

    local executorName = "Unknown"
    pcall(function()
        if identifyexecutor then
            executorName = identifyexecutor()
        elseif getexecutorname then
            executorName = getexecutorname()
        end
    end)

    Runtime:Connect(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
        FrameCounter += 1
        local now = tick()
        local elapsed = now - FrameTimer
        if elapsed < 1 then return end
        FPS = FrameCounter / elapsed
        FrameTimer, FrameCounter = now, 0

        local ping = 0
        pcall(function()
            ping = math.floor(game:GetService('Stats').Network.ServerStatsItem['Data Ping']:GetValue())
        end)

        local playerCount = #game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()
        local elapsedSeconds = math.floor(tick() - StartTime)
        local minutes = math.floor(elapsedSeconds / 60)
        local seconds = elapsedSeconds % 60
        local timerFormatted = string.format("%02d:%02d", minutes, seconds)

        Library:SetWatermark(('%s | %s | %s fps | %s ms | Players: %s | Time: %s'):format(
            gameName,
            executorName,
            math.floor(FPS),
            ping,
            playerCount,
            timerFormatted
        ))
    end)
end)
-- END FEATURE ENGINE
local exportedTarget = Environment.get_closest_target
local exportedBarrel = Environment.get_gun_barrel_position
local exportedRestore = Environment.RestoreBulletSims
table.insert(Runtime.Cleanups, function()
    if exportedTarget ~= PreviousExports.Target and Environment.get_closest_target == exportedTarget then
        Environment.get_closest_target = PreviousExports.Target
    end
    if exportedBarrel ~= PreviousExports.Barrel and Environment.get_gun_barrel_position == exportedBarrel then
        Environment.get_gun_barrel_position = PreviousExports.Barrel
    end
    if exportedRestore ~= PreviousExports.Restore and Environment.RestoreBulletSims == exportedRestore then
        Environment.RestoreBulletSims = PreviousExports.Restore
    end
end)

-- A newer execution may have unloaded this startup while a game module was yielding.
if not Runtime.Active then return end
Runtime:Init("No spread control", function()
    GroupBoxes.GunMods:AddToggle("NoSpread", {
        Text = "No Spread", Default = flags.NoSpread,
        Tooltip = "Requires Enable Aimbot. Uses the native packet's shot seed and pellet count",
        Callback = function(value) flags.NoSpread = value end,
    })
end)
Runtime:Init("Settings", function() Window:InitSettings() end)
if not Runtime.Active then return end
Library:Notify(next(Runtime.FailedFeatures) and "Lucid loaded with unavailable features; check Console" or "Lucid Loaded", 4)
return Window
